  01.为了雪山的庄严和父母的期望

  01. For the solemnity of the snow-capped mountains and the expectations of parents

  一

  One人们常常问我，你发表处女作是哪一年？我说，是l987年，那一年我已经三十五周岁了。人们就“啊”了一声，不再说什么，但表情里含了疑惑：早些年你干吗去了？

  People often ask me, what year did you publish your debut novel? I said, it was l987, when I was thirty-five years old. People just said "ah" and didn't say anything more, but there was a doubt in their expressions: Why did you go in the early years?在写作以前，我在遥远的西藏当兵，学的是医务。我在白衣战士的那条战线上，当到了内科主治医师的位置。假如不是改了行，就当到了副主任，您现在到医院看我的门诊，就要挂三块钱一个的号1了。

  Before writing, I was a soldier in distant Tibet and studied medicine. I was on the front line of the white warriors and became the attending physician of internal medicine. If you don't change your career, you will become the deputy director, and you will have to hang up the number 1 of three yuan a piece when you go to the hospital to see my outpatient clinic.一个女人，更具体地说，是一个医术很好颇有人缘的女大夫，在已过了“而立之年”的沉稳日子里，为什么要弃医从文，拿起生疏的文学之笔开始艰难的跋涉？

  A woman, more specifically, a popular female doctor with good medical skills, in the calm days of "standing in the year", why should she abandon medicine and follow literature, pick up an unfamiliar literary pen and start a difficult trek?在许多孤寂写作的深夜，我对着苍天自问。

  In the late night of many lonely writings, I asked myself to the sky.我不知道。

  I do not know.但是我感到一个苍凉而喑哑的声音，在寒冷的西部呼唤我。

  But I felt a desolate and hoarse voice calling me in the cold West.你既然来到了这里，你就要让世人知道这里。

  Since you are here, you must let the world know about it.他说。带着无上的权威。

  He said. With supreme authority.我没有办法抗拒。你可以违背一个人的意志，但是你不能违背一座雪山。

  I couldn't resist. You can go against a person's will, but you can't go against a snowy mountain.这就是昆仑山啊。我们民族最伟大的峰峦。

  This is Kunlun Mountain. The greatest peak of our nation.不管文化古籍里怎样考证，说传说中的昆仑山是现如今的什么什么山，我总认为它不是一座具体的山，而是一个象征。想想那时候，交通工具多么不便，又没有精确的地图，指南针还没有发明出来。古人绝不可能把山与山的分野搞得条块分明。他们只有对着西部广袤的隆起兴叹，在落日辉煌的余晖里，勾勒云霭中浮动着鬼斧神工的宫殿……于是他们把无数神奇的传说附丽其上，敷衍出最雄伟的想象。那里有九条尾巴的天神把守的天宫，那里有直插云霄的天稻，每一粒谷子都是鸡蛋大的玉石……

  No matter how it is verified in ancient cultural books, I always think that the legendary Kunlun Mountain is what it is today, but a symbol. Think about how inconvenient transportation was at that time, there was no accurate map, and the compass had not yet been invented. The ancients could never have made the division between mountains clear. They could only sigh at the vastness of the west, and in the glorious afterglow of the setting sun, they outlined the palace floating in the clouds...... So they attached countless magical legends to it, perfunctory the most majestic imagination. There is the heavenly palace guarded by the nine-tailed heavenly god, and there is the heavenly rice that reaches straight into the sky, and every grain of grain is a jade the size of an egg......无独有偶。在印度辽阔的恒河平原上，更为优雅的神话野火般流传。赤足的人们向西眺望，看到皑皑的冰峰劈裂云霄。他们认为有超凡入圣的法力统治其上，于是说那里是佛祖居住的地方……

  Coincidentally. In the vast Ganges plains of India, more elegant myths spread like wildfire. Barefoot people looked west and saw the white ice peaks splitting the sky. They believed that there was a transcendent magic power ruling over it, so they said that it was where the Buddha lived......两大古老种族神秘的目光交会于此——这就是地球上最高耸的原野——藏北高原。

  The mysterious eyes of two ancient races meet here - this is the highest wilderness on earth - the northern Tibetan plateau.当我十六岁的时候，离开北京，穿上军装。火车不断地向西向西，到了新疆的乌鲁木齐。又换上汽车向西向西。在茫茫戈壁上奔跑了六天以后，到达南疆重镇喀什。这一次汽车不是向地面上的哪个方向行驶了，而是向“天上”爬去。又经历了六天无与伦比的颠簸，我作为藏北某部队第一批五个女兵当中的一员，到达了共和国这块最高的土地。

  When I was sixteen, I left Beijing and put on my military uniform. The train continued to go west to Urumqi, Xinjiang. Then I changed to the car and headed west. After running on the vast Gobi for six days, he arrived at Kashgar, an important town in southern Xinjiang. This time the car did not drive in any direction on the ground, but crawled "into the sky". After six days of unparalleled turbulence, I arrived at the highest land of the Republic as one of the first five female soldiers of a certain unit in northern Tibet.这块土地是喜马拉雅山、冈底斯山和喀喇昆仑山聚合的地方，平均高度在海拔五千米以上，它有一个奇怪的名字，叫作“阿里”。

  This land is the convergence of the Himalayas, Gangdis and Karakoram Mountains, with an average altitude of more than five thousand meters above sea level, and it has a strange name called "Ali".没有人知道“阿里”是什么意思。我曾经问过博学的藏学家，也没能给一个明晰的回答，只是说这个词语可能属于一个早已消亡了的语系。于是我就沿用了一个我在阿里搜集到的民间传说：阿里的意思是“我的”。

  No one knows what "Ali" means. I once asked a learned Tibetan scholar, but I couldn't give a clear answer, just saying that this word may belong to a language family that has long died out. So I followed a folklore I collected from Ali: Ali means "mine".“我的”什么呢？我的高原？我的山川？我的牦牛和我的盐巴？我的清澈的湖泊和险恶的风暴？不知道。人类的远祖用我们不懂的语言，为我们留下了一道永恒的谜。也许在先民们眼中，所有的一切都是有灵性的，他们都在呼喊着“我的”。

  What is "my"? My plateau? My mountains and rivers? My yak and my salt? My crystal clear lakes and treacherous storms? I don't know. The distant ancestors of mankind left us an eternal mystery in a language we did not understand. Perhaps in the eyes of the ancestors, everything was spiritual, and they were all shouting "mine".我小的时候，学习很好。语文好，数学也好。语文老师说我以后可以当个记者，数学老师则说我以后可以上清华大学，成为一个女数学家。我回到家里，很高兴地把这些话学给妈妈。没想到，她训斥我说，这都是老师逗你玩的，你不要相信别人说你如何好的话。

  When I was young, I studied well. Chinese is good, and mathematics is also good. The Chinese teacher said that I could be a journalist in the future, and the math teacher said that I could go to Tsinghua University in the future and become a female mathematician. I returned home and happily learned these words from my mother. Unexpectedly, she reprimanded me and said that this is all the teacher's trick to tease you, don't believe what others say about you.我挺伤心的，从此对别人的夸奖总是半信半疑。我不知这习惯到底好不好，但它使我在荣誉面前天生地镇静起来。比如我的作文被老师批过“5+”的分数，但是小小的我丝毫不骄傲，因为我知道那是她逗我玩的。

  I was very sad, and I was always skeptical about other people's praises from now on. I don't know if this habit is good or not, but it makes me naturally calm in the face of honor. For example, my composition was graded by the teacher with a score of "5+", but I was not proud at all because I knew that she was teasing me.我小学毕业后考进了北京外国语学院附属学校。据说是很难考的，录取率只有几百分之一，而且女生录取得很少，只及总数的四分之一。在我这个年纪的北京人，都会记得当时每年一度的北京外语学校招生，是怎样地惊动京城。

  After graduating from elementary school, I was admitted to the Affiliated School of Beijing Chinese Language University. It is said that it is difficult to take the exam, the admission rate is only a few percent, and the admission rate for girls is very small, only a quarter of the total. Beijingers my age will remember how the annual enrollment of Beijing Foreign Language School alarmed the capital.我考上了，妈妈难得地高兴了一回。但是我已经养成了宠辱不惊的脾气，并没有特别地兴奋。

  I was admitted, and my mother was very happy. But I had developed a temper of not being frightened, and I wasn't particularly excited.在外语学校读书的时候，我的成绩依然很好。我现在还保存着一张当时的成绩单，所有的科目平时都是5分，期末考试都是“优”。我后来在军队院校军医专业学习的时候，每次考试也都是第一。由于一贯的优异，使我在内心深处看不起在校学习这件事。你想啊，上边有老师喋喋不休在讲，周围有同学可研讨，你什么事都没有，一门心思学那点前人遗下的知识，你要是还学不好，不是太说不过去了吗？

  When I was studying at a foreign language school, my grades were still very good. I still have a transcript from that time, all subjects are usually 5 points, and the final exam is "excellent". Later, when I studied military medicine in military academies, I was the first in every exam. Because of my consistent excellence, I look down on studying at school in my heart. You think, there are teachers chattering above, there are classmates around you to discuss, you have nothing, you are focused on learning the knowledge left by your predecessors, if you still can't learn well, isn't it too unreasonable?我在外语学校最大的收获，是见了一个比较大的世面，读了不少的书。退回去三十多年，许多社会名流的孩子已经在“反帝反修”的同时，孜孜不倦地开始学习外语。我们这所学校干部子女的密集程度，大概超过了京城的任何一所学校。我的父亲是军队的一位正师级干部，但相比之下，我只能算作平民子弟。由于我优异的学习成绩，使我保持了一种有尊严的生活态度。我得以近距离地观察到真正的“贵族”气派，看到它的华贵，也看到它的羸弱。

  The biggest gain I gained from foreign language school was that I saw a relatively large world and read a lot of books. After more than 30 years of regression, many children of celebrities have already begun to learn foreign languages tirelessly while "anti-imperialism and anti-revision". The density of cadres and children in our school is probably more than that of any school in the capital. My father was a division-level cadre in the army, but in comparison, I could only be counted as a civilian child. Due to my excellent academic performance, I have maintained a dignified attitude towards life. I was able to observe the true "aristocratic" style up close, see its luxury, and see its weakness.读了许多课外书，则得益于“文化大革命”的停课。我们学校里有一个很大的图书馆，平日里我们是没有机会读小说的。功课压得非常紧，老师原本要求我们夜里说梦话都用外语的。现在一停课，大松心了，快活无比。只是图书馆里的书可不是无偿看的，看一本，要写出一篇批判文章。

  After reading many extracurricular books, he benefited from the suspension of classes during the "Cultural Revolution". We have a large library in our school, and we don't have the opportunity to read novels on weekdays. The homework was very tight, and the teacher originally asked us to speak in a foreign language at night. Now that classes are suspended, I am very relieved and extremely happy. It's just that the books in the library are not free to read, and you have to write a critical article to read a book.刚开始大伙觉得这个交易做得来，不就是看完之后胡乱照着报纸抄点革命词语就能交差了吗？于是大家都去借，并相约看完了自己的那本以后，彼此交换。这样各人写一篇批判稿，就可以看几本好小说，不是太合算了吗？

  At first, everyone thought that this deal could be done, and after reading it, they could just copy some revolutionary words in the newspaper. So everyone went to borrow it, and after reading their own book, they exchanged it with each other. In this way, each person can write a critical manuscript and read a few good novels, isn't it too cost-effective?但实践的结果并不美妙。很多人书看了，但批判稿久久写不出来，时间长了，就失去了继续借书的资格。我也不愿意写大批判文章，你想啊，都是世界名著，看的时候，对大师们佩服得五体投地，书皮一合上，就要批判他们，这是一件多么残酷的事情！但管图书馆的小个子老师很严厉，交不了稿，你就不要想从她的手里再借出一张纸。为了阅读大师们的作品，我只有硬起头皮来批判大师们。

  But the results of practice are not wonderful. Many people read books, but they couldn't write critical manuscripts for a long time, and after a long time, they lost their qualifications to continue borrowing books. I don't want to write big critical articles, you think, they are all world masterpieces, when I read them, I admire the masters to the ground, and as soon as the book cover is closed, I have to criticize them, what a cruel thing this is! But the little teacher in charge of the library is very strict and can't turn in the manuscript, so don't think about borrowing another piece of paper from her. In order to read the works of the masters, I can only bite the bullet to criticize the masters.道理虽说明白了，但写的时候，心痛如绞。我终于想出了一个两全其美的办法。比如看完《复活》，我就在纸上写：以下部分暴露出列夫·托尔斯泰的资产阶级人道主义倾向……然后我开始大段地抄录老托尔斯泰的原文，抄得很仔细，连一个标点都不错过……

  Although the truth is clear, when I wrote it, my heart ached. I finally came up with a way to get the best of both worlds. For example, after reading "Resurrection", I wrote on paper: The following part exposes Leo Tolstoy's bourgeois humanitarian tendencies...... Then I began to transcribe the original text of the old Tolstoy in large paragraphs, copying it very carefully, not missing a single punctuation......还书的时候心情好忐忑，生怕小个子老师看出什么。没想到，她连连表扬我的认真，原来她是只看标题，看字迹是否整齐，看篇幅的长短，并不在意你写的是什么。

  When I returned the book, I was in a very uneasy mood, for fear that the little teacher would see something. Unexpectedly, she praised my seriousness again and again, it turned out that she only looked at the title, whether the handwriting was neat, the length of the page, and didn't care what you wrote.只有我一个人坚持借书写批判稿了，同屋的同学开始央求我，要我看完了书暂不要还，让大家都传着看一看。我当然不能拒绝，只是有的人看得很慢，已经过了好多天了，你问她看完了没有，她还说没完。知道书看到半截被人夺走的苦处，我不好意思催，只得耐心地等。但看惯了书的人，就像大烟瘾，是很难忍得住的。我就在下次借书的时候想办法——连借带偷。图书馆的小老师对我已是十分地信任了，每次我来借书，她不跟着，让我自己在书架里挑。

  I was the only one who insisted on borrowing the critical manuscript, and my classmates in the same room began to beg me not to return the book for the time being, so that everyone could pass it on. Of course I can't refuse, but some people watch it very slowly, it has been many days, you ask her if she has finished watching, and she still says it is not finished. Knowing that the book saw half of the suffering of being taken away, I was embarrassed to urge it, so I had to wait patiently. But people who are used to reading books, like a big smoking addiction, are hard to bear. The next time I borrow a book, I will find a way - borrow and steal. The little teacher in the library already trusts me very much, and every time I come to borrow books, she doesn't follow me and asks me to pick them from the bookshelf.我们的图书馆是一座建立于20世纪初期的西式楼房，窗户很高很小，像旧时的教堂。加上书架遮挡了大部分的阳光，走道幽暗深邃。这真是一个作案的好场所。我在书架里转啊转，看到一本好书，就夹在胳肢窝的衣服里……这样几圈下来，双臂就像机械的木偶，动也不敢动了。最后僵硬地走到老师跟前，只把手里抱着的书登记。

  Our library is a Western-style building built in the early 20th century, with tall and small windows, like an old church. In addition, the bookshelves block most of the sunlight, and the aisles are dark and deep. This is really a good place to commit crimes. I was spinning around the bookshelf and saw a good book, and I put it in my clothes in my arm socket...... After a few laps like this, the arms were like mechanical puppets, and they didn't dare to move. Finally, he walked up to the teacher stiffly and only registered the book he was holding in his hand.这样我看好几本书，只需写一本书的大批判稿，不但减轻了手的负担，加快了看书的速度，更重要的是减轻了心灵的负担。

  In this way, I am optimistic about a few books, and I only need to write a big critical draft of a book, which not only reduces the burden on my hands, speeds up the speed of reading, but more importantly, reduces the burden on my soul.但还书的时候，气氛挺吓人的。借的时候，只图一时快活，完全忘记是从哪个犄角旮旯掏出来的书，可还的时候一定要归位。小老师是很认真的，一旦她发现大量的图书放错了地方，怀疑到我身上，我的秘密书库就会被彻底摧毁了，损失不堪设想。我谨慎地控制着偷书的数量，严格地完璧归赵。每次还书时候，都恐惧万分。身上夹带着好几本书，像个沉重的孕妇，还要等着小老师验收批判文章，心中狂跳不止。待老师那里过了关，急急钻进书架的峡谷，拼命回想上次取书的位置，冷汗涔涔。好不容易放了回去，刚轻松了一秒钟，又贪婪地开始了新一轮的夹带……

  But when returning the book, the atmosphere was quite scary. When borrowing, I only want to be happy for a while, completely forgetting which corner the book was taken out from, but when I return it, I must return it to its place. The little teacher is very serious, once she finds that a large number of books are misplaced and suspects me, my secret library will be completely destroyed, and the loss will be unimaginable. I carefully controlled the number of books stolen and returned them to Zhao strictly. Every time I return the book, I am extremely afraid. Carrying several books on her body, like a heavy pregnant woman, she still had to wait for the little teacher to accept the critical article, and her heart was beating wildly. After passing the teacher's place, I hurriedly got into the canyon of the bookshelf, desperately recalling the last time I picked up the book, and broke out in a cold sweat. Finally put it back, and after just a second of relaxation, it greedily started a new round of entrainment......同学们坐享其成，却全然不体谅我的苦衷，轮到我要还书了，她们就耍赖，说还没看完呢。我说，那你们也得给我一个时间，你们不能老这么耽误我呀。她们就说，要不这样吧，书你现在就可以拿走，但是你得把书中的故事讲给我们听。

  The classmates sat back and enjoyed their success, but they didn't understand my hardships at all, and when it was my turn to return the book, they played tricks, saying that they hadn't finished reading it yet. I said, then you have to give me a time, you can't delay me like this all the time. They said, otherwise, you can take the book now, but you have to tell us the story in the book.于是，在“文化大革命”最激烈的年代，在北京城内一所古老的校舍里，每逢夜深人静，在一间住着八个女孩的房间里，就会传出娓娓的话语，中外文学大师的智慧，像月光清冷地笼罩着我们，伴我们走进悠远的梦乡。

  Therefore, in the most intense years of the "Cultural Revolution", in an old school building in Beijing, in the dead of night, in a room where eight girls lived, eloquent words would come out, and the wisdom of Chinese and foreign literary masters would envelop us like moonlight, accompanying us into a distant dreamland.

  为了给同学们讲得不露破绽，我读原著的时候就格外地认真。几十年过去了，我的一位现已在美国定居的朋友，说她至今记着我给她讲过的《笑面人》，而且拒绝看雨果的原著。她说，毕淑敏在那个夏夜所讲的《笑面人》是世界上最好的《笑面人》，我从来没有听过比这再好的故事了。

  In order to tell my classmates without revealing any flaws, I read the original work very seriously. Decades later, a friend of mine who has now settled in the United States said that she still remembers what I told her about "The Smiling Man" and refused to read Hugo's original book. She said that the "Smiling Man" that Bi Shumin told on that summer night was the best "Smiling Man" in the world, and I had never heard a better story than this.我对这个评价淡然一笑。我知道，这是她在怀念自己的少年时代。

  I smiled indifferently at this evaluation. I know that this is her nostalgia for her youth.二

  Two我从北京来到西藏的阿里当兵，严酷的自然环境将我震撼。所有的日子都充满严寒，绿色已成为遥远而模糊的记忆。

  I came to Ali from Beijing to Tibet to serve as a soldier, and the harsh natural environment shocked me. All the days were filled with bitter cold, and green had become a distant and vague memory.吃的是脱水菜，像纸片一样干燥的洋葱皮，在雪水的浸泡下，膨胀成赭色的浆团。炒或熬以后，一种辛辣而令人懊恼的气味充斥军营。即使在日历上最炎热的夏季，你也绝不可以脱下棉衣，否则夜里所有的关节就会嘎嘎作响。

  What I ate was dehydrated vegetables, onion skins that were as dry as paper pieces, and when soaked in snow water, they swelled into ochre slurry. After stir-frying or boiling, a pungent and annoying smell fills the barracks. Even in the hottest summer on the calendar, you should never take off your cotton coat, otherwise all the joints will rattle at night.由于缺乏维生素，我的嘴唇像兔子一样裂开了，讲话的时候就会有红红的血珠掉下来。这是很不雅的事情，我就去问老医生怎样才能治好嘴唇？医生想了半天说，你要大量地吃维生素。我说吃啦，每天都吃一大把，足足有二十多片呢！可我的嘴唇为什么还是长不拢？医生说那就是你说话太多了，紧紧地闭一个星期嘴巴，你的嘴唇就长好了。我说，那可不行，我是卫生员的班长，就算跟伙伴可以不说话，跟病人也是要讲话的……老医生表示爱莫能助。

  Due to the lack of vitamins, my lips cracked like rabbits, and red blood droplets fell when I spoke. This is a very indecent thing, so I asked the old doctor how to cure his lips? The doctor thought for a long time and said that you should take a lot of vitamins. I said eat, eat a lot every day, more than 20 pieces! But why can't my lips still grow together? The doctor said that you talk too much, close your mouth tightly for a week, and your lips will grow. I said, that's not good, I'm the squad leader of the hygienist, even if I can not talk to my partner, I have to talk to the patient...... The old doctor said that love could not help.后来我的嘴唇还是我自己给治好的。夜里睡觉的时候，用胶布把自己的嘴巴粘起来，强迫裂开的口子靠在一起。白天撕开照常讲话。坚持了一段时间，后来就好了。

  Later, my lips were healed by myself. When sleeping at night, glue your mouth with tape and force the cracked openings to come together. Tear open during the day and speak as usual. I held on for a while, and then it got better.由于缺氧，我的指甲猛烈地凹陷下去，像一个搅拌咖啡的小勺。年轻的女孩就是爱斗嘴，有一天，女卫生员争论起来谁的指甲凹得最厉害，最后决定用注射器针头往指甲坑里注水，一滴滴往下灌，水的滴数多而不流者为胜。记得我得了第一。好像是贮藏了十几滴水吧，凝聚得圆圆的，像一颗巨大的露珠，乖乖地趴在我的指甲上。

  Due to lack of oxygen, my nails sink violently, like a small spoon for stirring coffee. One day, the female hygienist argued about whose nails were the most sunken, and finally decided to use a syringe needle to pour water into the nail pit, drop by drop, and the one with more drops of water would win. I remember I won the first place. It seems to have stored more than a dozen drops of water, condensed into a circle, like a huge dewdrop, lying obediently on my fingernails.我是一个优秀的卫生员。有一天，我在军报上看到了一个叫作“毕淑敏”的人写的一首诗，就轻轻地笑了一下。我知道我的名字很大众，全中国从八岁到八十岁的女人，有许多叫这个名字。但是我的姓是比较少的。现在有了一个同名同姓的人写了一首诗，觉得很亲切，就很仔细地读。

  I am an excellent hygienist. One day, I saw a poem written by a man named "Bi Shumin" in the military newspaper, and I smiled softly. I know that my name is very popular, and there are many women in China from the age of eight to eighty who have this name. But my last name is relatively few. Now there is a person with the same name and surname who wrote a poem, and I feel very kind, so I read it carefully.一读之下，我吃了一惊。因为这首诗是我写的。但是千真万确，我没有向任何一家报刊投过稿。

  When I read it, I was surprised. Because I wrote this poem. But to be honest, I have not submitted my manuscript to any newspaper.我不知道这是怎么一回事，也没有人负责向我解释。时间一长，我就把它忘了。但是军邮车下次上高原的时候（由于道路封山，邮车很长时间才上来一趟），报社给我寄来了一个黄色封面的采访本，我才得以确认那首诗是我的作品，这个本子就是稿费了。我用这个本子记了许多有关解剖和生理方面的知识。

  I don't know what this is going on, and no one is responsible for explaining it to me. After a long time, I forgot about it. But the next time the military mail car went to the plateau (due to the road closure, the mail car took a long time to come up), the newspaper sent me an interview book with a yellow cover, and I was able to confirm that the poem was my work, and this book was the manuscript fee. I used this notebook to record a lot of knowledge about anatomy and physiology.一个很偶然的机会，政治部的一位干事对我说，你的那首诗，充满了鲜血和死亡的意识，真不像一个十几岁的女孩子写的。

  By chance, an officer of the Political Department said to me, "Your poem is full of blood and the consciousness of death, and it really doesn't look like it was written by a teenage girl."我恍然大悟说，噢！原来我的那首诗是你给我投到报社去的啊？

  I suddenly realized and said, Oh! It turns out that you sent me to the newspaper for that poem?他说，不是他。

  He said, not him.他这才告诉我，军报的一位记者到阿里高原采访。高原反应像重量级的拳击手，毫不留情地击倒了他，第二天他就下山返回平原了。但记者很忠于职守，就在高原的这仅有的一天里，挣扎着看了一些单位的黑板报，摘了一些作品带回去，我的小诗也在其中。回去以后，别人的都没选中，只发了我的那一首……

  He then told me that a reporter from the military newspaper went to the Ali Plateau for an interview. Altitude sickness, like a heavyweight boxer, knocked him down mercilessly, and the next day he went down the mountain and returned to the plains. But the reporter was very loyal to his duties, and in this only day on the plateau, he struggled to read the blackboard newspapers of some units, picked up some works to take back, and my little poems were among them. After going back, none of the others chose it, only my ......我不知道自己随手涂抹的句子还有这样的经历，但幼时妈妈的教育使我绝不大惊小怪。我没有看见自己的作品变成铅字的喜悦，只认为这是一个巧合。不会再有第二个记者匆匆下山，不会再有人看上我的小诗……

  I didn't know that I had such an experience with the sentences I scribbled, but my mother's education when I was young made me never fuss. I didn't see the joy of seeing my work turn into lead letters, I just thought it was a coincidence. There will be no second reporter rushing down the mountain, and no one will take a fancy to my poems......我继续专心地学习医学知识，一点也没有因此想投稿搞创作什么的。

  I continued to concentrate on learning medical knowledge, and I didn't want to contribute to creation or anything because of this.当了几年兵，我回家探亲。我的父亲很郑重地同我谈到了那首诗，说他很高兴。

  After a few years of being a soldier, I went home to visit relatives. My father spoke to me about the poem very solemnly, saying that he was very happy.我从小是一个乖孩子，愿意使自己的父母快活。但我还是没想到写作，只感到一种隐隐约约的愿望在内心起伏。

  I grew up as a well-behaved child who was willing to make my parents happy. But I still didn't think about writing, I only felt a faint desire rising and falling in my heart.我在藏北高原当了十一年的兵，把自己最宝贵的青年时代留在了冰川与雪岭之间。

  I served as a soldier on the northern Tibetan plateau for eleven years, leaving my most precious youth between glaciers and snowy mountains.我曾经背负武器、红十字箱、干粮、行军帐篷跋涉在无人区，也曾骑马涉过冰河给藏族老乡送医药。

  I once trekled through no man's land with weapons, red cross boxes, dry food, and marching tents, and also rode horses across glaciers to deliver medicine to Tibetan villagers.我曾在万古不化的寒冰上，铺一张雨布席地而眠，初次这样露营时，我想醒来身体还不得泊在一片汪洋之中？我真是高估了人的微薄热量，黎明当我掀开雨布查看时，只见雪原依旧，连个人形的凹陷都没有。除了双膝凝固般的疼痛，一切都很正常。

  I once slept on the eternal ice, spreading a raincloth on the ground, and when I first camped like this, I thought I woke up and my body couldn't be parked in the ocean? I really overestimated the meager heat of people, and when I lifted the raincloth to check at dawn, I saw that the snowfield was still there, not even a depression in the human figure. Except for the coagulating pain in both knees, everything was normal.攀越海拔六千多米的高山时，心脏在胸膛炸成碎片，仿佛要随着急遽的呼吸迸溅出嘴巴。仰望云雾缭绕的顶峰，俯视脚下深不可测的渊薮，只有十七岁的我，第一次想到了死。我想这样爬上去太苦难了，干脆装作一失足，掉下悬崖……没有人会发现我是故意这样做的，在如此险恶的行军中，死人的事经常发生。我牺牲于军事行动，也要算作小小的烈士，这样我的父母也会有一份光荣……我把一切都周密地盘算好了，只需找一块陡峻的峭壁实施自戕的方案。不一会儿，地方选好了。那是一处很美丽的山崖，天像纯蓝墨水一样浓郁地蓝着，有凝然不动的苍鹰像图钉似的进苍天。这里的积雪比较薄，赭色的山岩像礁石一般浮出雪原（我知道要找一块山石狰狞的地方下手，否则叫厚雪一垫，很可能功亏一篑）……

  When climbing a mountain at an altitude of more than 6,000 meters, his heart exploded into pieces in his chest, as if it was about to burst out of his mouth with rapid breathing. Looking up at the cloudy peak and looking down at the unfathomable abyss under my feet, I was only seventeen years old and thought of death for the first time. I thought it was too painful to climb up like this, so I simply pretended to stumble and fell off the cliff...... No one will find out that I did it on purpose, and in such a sinister march, dead things happen often. I sacrificed myself in military operations, and I must also be counted as a small martyr, so that my parents will also have an honorable ...... I calculated everything carefully, just find a steep cliff to carry out the plan of suicide. After a while, the place was chosen. It was a very beautiful cliff, the sky was as blue as pure blue ink, and there were still goshawks entering the sky like thumbpins. The snow here is relatively thin, and the ochre-colored rocks emerge from the snowfield like reefs (I know to find a hideous place to start, otherwise it will be called a thick snow mat, and it is likely to fall short)......一切都策划好了，但是我遇到了最大的困难。我的脚不听我的指挥，想让右脚腾空，可是它紧紧地用脚趾抠住毛皮鞋底儿，鞋底儿粘在酷寒的土地上，丝毫不肯像我计划的那样飞翔而起……我转而命令左脚，它倒是抬起来了，可它不是向下滑动，而是挣扎着向上挪去……青春的机体不服从我的死亡指令，各部分零件出于本能居然独自求生……那一瞬我苦恼至极，生也不成，死也不成，生命为何如此苛待于我？

  Everything was planned, but I encountered the biggest difficulty. My foot did not obey my commands, and wanted to let my right foot take off, but it tightly pinched the fur sole with its toes, and the sole stuck to the cold land, refusing to fly as I had planned...... I turned to command my left foot, and it lifted up, but instead of sliding down, it struggled to move up...... The youthful body did not obey my death orders, and the parts survived alone out of instinct...... At that moment, I was extremely distressed, I couldn't live or die, why did life treat me so harshly?一个老兵牵着咻咻吐白汽的马走过来，他是负责后卫收容的。他说，曼巴2，拉着我的马尾巴吧，它会把你带到山顶。我看了一眼马毛被汗湿成一绺绺的军马，背上驮着掉队者的背包和干粮，已是不堪重负。

  A veteran came over with a horse spitting white steam, and he was in charge of the rearguard's containment. He said, Mamba 2, pull my horse's tail, and it will take you to the top of the mountain. I glanced at the horse's horse, which was soaked with sweat into strands of horse hair, and was already overwhelmed with backpacks and dry food for stragglers on its backs.不。我不。我说。

  No. I don't. I say.老兵痛惜地看着我说，你是不是怕它扬起后蹄踢了你？放心吧，它没有那个劲儿了。在这么陡的山上，它再累也不敢踢你。只要它的蹄子一松劲儿，就得滚到谷里去。它是老马了，懂得这个利害。你就大胆地揪它的尾巴吧。

  The veteran looked at me with regret and said, are you afraid that it will raise its hind hooves and kick you? Don't worry, it doesn't have that energy anymore. On such a steep mountain, no matter how tired it is, it won't dare to kick you. As long as its hooves loosen, it has to roll into the valley. It is an old horse and understands the stakes. Just boldly grab its tail.我迟疑着，久久没有揪那条马尾。

  I hesitated, not pulling the ponytail for a long time.不是害怕马。甚至也不是怜悯马。

  Not afraid of horses. Not even a pity horse.我在考虑自己的尊严。

  I was thinking about my dignity.

  一个战士，揪着马尾巴攀越雪山，这是不是比死还让人难堪？我的意志做出一个回答，生存的本能做出另一个回答。

  A soldier, holding a horse's tail and climbing over a snow-capped mountain, is this more embarrassing than death? My will gives one answer, and my survival instinct gives another.意志在本能面前屈服，我伸出手，揪住了马尾巴……

  Giving in to instinct, I reached out and grabbed the horse's tail......我看到许多年轻的生命永远地留在了万水千山之间。他们发生过悲凉或欣喜的故事，被呼啸的山风卷得毫无痕迹。

  I saw many young lives forever left between thousands of rivers and mountains. They have had stories of sadness or joy, swept away by the howling mountain wind without a trace.我为一个二十岁的班长换过尸衣，脱下被血染红的军装，清理他口袋里的遗物。他兜里装着几块水果糖，纸都磨光了，糖块像一只只斑驳的小乌龟，沾着他的血迹……我一点都不害怕，因为我的兜里也有和他一样的水果糖，这件小小的物品使我觉得他是兄弟。

  I changed the corpse clothes of a twenty-year-old squad leader, took off his blood-stained military uniform, and cleaned up the relics in his pocket. He had a few pieces of fruit candy in his pocket, and the paper was polished, and the sugar cubes were like mottled little turtles, stained with his blood...... I wasn't afraid at all, because I had the same fruit candy in my pocket as him, and this little item made me feel like a brother.我们把他肚子上覆盖的瓷碗取下来。碗里扣着的，是他流出的肠子。敌人的子弹贯穿了他的腹腔，肠管已经变得像铁管一样坚硬，没有办法再填回他的肚子里去了。

  We took off the porcelain bowl covering his stomach. What was buckled in the bowl was his intestines. The enemy's bullets pierced his abdominal cavity, and his intestines had become as hard as iron pipes, and there was no way to fill them back into his stomach.我们给他换上崭新的军装，把风纪扣严严实实地系好。除了他的腰间因为流出的肠子，扎了皮带也显得有些臃肿，真是一个精干的小战士呢。

  We put on a brand new military uniform and fastened the discipline tightly. In addition to the intestines that flowed out of his waist, he also looked a little bloated with a belt, and he was really a capable little warrior.趁人不注意，我在他的衣兜里又放上了几块水果糖。我不敢让别人知道，因为老兵们一定要嘲笑我的。但我真的觉得这个班长需要这几块水果糖。糖是我特意挑的，每一块的糖纸都很完整，硬挺地支棱着，像一种干燥的翅果。

  While no one was paying attention, I put a few more pieces of fruit candy in his pocket. I don't dare to let others know, because the veterans must laugh at me. But I really think this squad leader needs these pieces of fruit candy. I specially picked the sugar, and each piece of sugar paper is very intact, stiffly and stiff, like a kind of dried winged fruit.那个小兵被安葬在阿里高原，距今已经有二十多年了。我想他身边的冻土，有一小块一定微微发甜。他在晴朗的月夜，也许会尝一尝吧？

  The soldier was buried on the Ali Plateau more than 20 years ago. I think there must be a small piece of frozen soil around him that must be slightly sweet. He might taste it on a clear moonlit night, right?三

  Three1980年我转业到北京，在一家工厂的卫生所当医生，后来当了所长。结婚、生子、操持家务……一个女人来到这个世界上该做的事情，我都很认真地做了。贤妻良母好医生，这是人们众口一词的评价。

  In 1980, I moved to Beijing and worked as a doctor in a factory health center, and later became the director. Marriage, childbearing, housekeeping...... What a woman should do when she comes into this world, I do it very seriously. A good wife and a good mother are good doctors, this is the evaluation of people.对一个三十岁的女医生来说，你还需要什么？

  What more do you need for a thirty-year-old female doctor?按说是不需要什么了，我应该安安静静地沿着命运已经勾勒的轨道，盘旋下去。

  It is said that I don't need anything, I should quietly hover along the track that fate has outlined.我虽然从小生活在北京，对北京的一草一木都那样熟悉，此次归来，我却不再是过去的那个我了。怀里揣了那么多藏北的风雪，它们强烈地撞击着我的心脏。我对这个巨大的都市，开始了新的审视。我到过这个国家最偏远最荒凉的地方，在横贯整个中国的旅行中，我知道了它的富饶与贫瘠。我在妖娆的霓虹灯中行走，身旁会突然显现白茫茫的雪原。在文明的喧哗与躁动之间，我倾听到遥远的西部有一座山在虎啸龙吟……

  Although I have lived in Beijing since I was a child and am so familiar with every plant and tree in Beijing, this time I return, I am no longer the same me I used to be. With so many winds and snows in northern Tibet in my arms, they hit my heart strongly. I began a new look at this huge city. I have been to the most remote and desolate places in this country, and in my travels across China, I have learned its richness and barrenness. I was walking in the enchanting neon lights, and a vast white snowfield suddenly appeared beside me. Between the noise and restlessness of civilization, I heard a mountain roaring in the far west......我的父亲有一天对我说，我看你是可以写一点东西的，你为什么不写呢？

  My father said to me one day, I think you can write something, why don't you write?我的父亲是一个很聪明的人，而且在文学艺术方面有很好的天赋。只是由于他们那一代人所处的环境，使他戎马一生，始终未能从事文学。我从他的目光里看到了期望，我决定一试。

  My father was a very intelligent man and had a good talent for literature and art. It was only because of the environment in which their generation lived that he was never able to engage in literature throughout his life. I saw the expectation in his gaze and I decided to give it a try.一个微茫的希望在远方磷火般地闪动。我想用我的笔，告诉世人一些风景和故事。我想让我的父母惊喜。

  A faint hope flashed like phosphorus in the distance. I want to use my pen to tell the world some scenery and stories. I want to surprise my parents.于是在一个普通的日子，我铺开一张洁白的纸。那是在深夜的内科值班室，轮到我值班，恰好没有病人。日光灯管发出咝咝的叫声，四周一片寂静。记忆在蛰伏了多少年后苏醒，将高原的生命与鲜血铺陈于我面前。

  So on an ordinary day, I spread out a piece of white paper. It was in the internal medicine duty room late at night, and it was my turn to be on duty, and there happened to be no patients. The fluorescent lamps sizzled, and there was silence all around. The memory awakened after many years of dormancy, spreading the life and blood of the plateau in front of me.我在高耸的雪山上开始了我为医的生涯，雪山也将它的身影，倾泻于我的笔端。
I started my career as a doctor on the towering snow-capped mountains, and the snow-capped mountains also poured out their figures on the tip of my pen.

未知

  02.到西藏去

  02. Go to Tibet

  小小的年纪，告别了父母，到一个遥远而陌生的地方去，本应该是很伤心的。妈妈到火车站送我的时候，险些哭了。但我心中充满了快乐，到西部去，到高原去，真是一次空前的冒险啊！

  At a young age, it should have been very sad to say goodbye to my parents and go to a distant and unfamiliar place. When my mother came to the train station to see me off, I almost cried. But my heart is full of happiness, going to the west, to the plateau, what an unprecedented adventure!从北京坐上火车，一直向西向西。窗外的景色，由密集的村落演变成空旷的荒野。气候越来越干燥，人烟越来越稀少，绿色逐渐被荒凉的戈壁滩所代替。三天三夜之后，我们这群女孩子到达了新疆的乌鲁木齐。在这里要进行最后的体检，才能决定谁可以到海拔五千米以上的西藏去。

  Take the train from Beijing and go all the way west to the west. The view outside the window evolves from a dense village to an empty wilderness. The climate is getting drier and sparsely populated, and the green is gradually replaced by the desolate Gobi Desert. After three days and three nights, our group of girls arrived in Urumqi, Xinjiang. A final medical examination is required here to decide who can go to Tibet at an altitude of more than 5,000 meters.我的身体一向很好，但这次医生说我的小便化验不正常，要是过几天复查还不合格的话，就要把我退回北京。

  I have always been in good health, but this time the doctor said that my urine test was not normal, and if the re-examination was still unqualified in a few days, I would be sent back to Beijing.这不是“出师未捷身先死”吗？我的探险还没有开始，难道就要这么狼狈地打道回府啦？

  Isn't this "dying before the army succeeds"? My expedition hasn't started yet, am I going to go home in such a hurry?我一定要想出一个办法！

  I must come up with a way!我的目光停留在一个同我最要好的女孩子身上。

  My eyes were fixed on a girl who was the best with me.我悄悄地把她扯到一个僻静的地方，对着她的耳朵说，你说，我们是不是好朋友啊？

  I quietly pulled her to a secluded place and said in her ear, you say, are we good friends?她说，当然是啦。你怎么想起问这个不成问题的问题？

  She said, of course. How do you think of asking this question that is not a problem?我说，既然是好朋友，我向你借一样东西，你一定是借的啦？

  I said, since we are good friends, I borrowed something from you, you must have borrowed it?她一扭头嚷起来，什么东西呀？咱们的东西都是统一发的，我有的，你都有啊！

  She turned her head and shouted, what is it? Our things are all sent uniformly, I have them, you have them!我一把捂住她的嘴说，干吗这么大声？是不是太小气不想借给我？实话说吧，我跟你借的这样东西，对你是一点用处都没有的，但对我的好处就大了！

  I covered her mouth and said, why are you so loud? Are you too stingy and don't want to lend it to me? To be honest, the thing I borrowed from you is of no use to you, but it is of great benefit to me!"她说，那是什么宝贝呀？

  She said, what kind of baby is that?我说，是尿啊！

  I said, it's urine!我把我的打算告诉她，复查的时候把她的尿当成我的标本送上去。她刚开始吓了一跳，然后，很犹豫地说，这不是骗人吗？我说，要是我复查不合格，到不了西藏，被退回北京，我们俩就再也见不到面了，更甭提做朋友了。她想了想，答应了。

  I told her my plan and sent her urine as my specimen during the review. She was startled at first, and then, hesitantly, said, isn't this a lie? I said, if I fail the review, I can't go to Tibet, and I'm sent back to Beijing, the two of us will never see each other again, let alone be friends. She thought about it and agreed.好不容易挨到了复查的那一天，没想到是通知我一个人单独到医院的检查科去。在卫生间里，我拈着盛标本的小瓶子，急得直掉泪。我真想到水龙头那儿，接一点自来水送上去，或者干脆把眼泪送上去化验，那就绝对没问题了。可是，我不敢。你想啊，化验员用的是显微镜，还不一下子就发现我的花招了？万般无奈之下，只好把自己的“标本”交上去了。

  Finally, on the day of the review, I didn't expect to be notified to go to the hospital's examination department alone. In the bathroom, I was holding a small bottle containing the specimen, and I was so anxious that I burst into tears. I really want to go to the faucet, take a little tap water, or simply send tears for testing, then there is absolutely no problem. However, I don't dare. Do you think, the laboratory technician uses a microscope, and he discovered my tricks all at once? In desperation, he had to hand over his "specimen".等待结果的日子，我和我的好朋友都充满了悲哀，以为我们必定分手了。

  On the days of waiting for the results, my good friends and I were filled with sadness, thinking that we must break up.不可思议的是，这一次的化验结果完全正常。

  Incredibly, the test results were completely normal this time.我终于和我的好朋友一道，踏上了遥远的奔赴西藏的道路。

  Finally, together with my good friend, I embarked on the long road to Tibet.

  我们告别了乌鲁木齐，在广阔的戈壁滩与高原上坐了整整十二天的汽车，到达了白雪皑皑的世界屋脊。我在那里待了十年。

  We bid farewell to Urumqi and took a twelve-day car ride on the vast Gobi Desert and plateau to reach the snow-capped roof of the world. I stayed there for ten years.后来，我把这一段有惊无险的遭遇和我的计谋，讲给一位老医生听，口气中充满了得意。没想到，他皱着眉说，幸好你本身的体检合格了。要知道，西藏高原缺氧，氧气只有海平面的一半。要是你的小便有问题，就说明你的肾脏有问题；要是你的肾脏真的有病，又用别人的标本蒙混过关，那是很危险的。

  Later, I told an old doctor about this thrilling encounter and my plan, and my tone was full of pride. Unexpectedly, he frowned and said, fortunately, your own physical examination has passed. You know, the Tibetan plateau lacks oxygen, and the oxygen is only half of the sea level. If you have problems urinating, it means that there is a problem with your kidneys; If your kidneys are really sick and you use other people's specimens to get by, it is very dangerous.我承认他的话很对，但也仍旧很佩服当年那两个十几岁的少女，我们为了友谊和理想，真是很勇敢呢！而且不服气地想，西藏人的肾脏，就个个都是铁打的了？我在高原见过不少肾脏有病的人，活得也很快乐啊！
I admit that he is right, but I still admire the two teenage girls back then, we are really brave for friendship and ideals! And I was not convinced to think, the kidneys of Tibetans are all ironed? I have seen many people with kidney disease on the plateau, and they are living happily!

未知

  03.绿色皮诺曹

  03. Green Pinocchio

  我从小就很想当兵，最主要的动机是喜欢绿色。小时候，每逢妈妈要给我买衣服，我就大叫，要绿的。妈妈生起气来，说，你也不看看自己，毛衣毛裤围巾手套都是绿色，再套上一件绿外衣，活像一只青蛙！我低头一瞧，说，哪怕就是像只绿豆蝇，我也还要绿衣服。

  I have wanted to be a soldier since I was a child, and the main motivation is to like green. When I was a child, whenever my mother wanted to buy me clothes, I would shout for green. Mom got angry and said, you don't look at yourself, sweaters, sweaters, scarves, and gloves are all green, and put on a green coat, like a frog! I looked down and said, even if I look like a mung bean fly, I still want green clothes.当兵多好啊！从此，可以名正言顺地一年到头穿绿衣服，再也没人说你一句闲话。可那时候要当女兵也挺难的，想当的人太多了，僧多粥少。听说男兵和女兵的比例是千分之二点五，也就是说，征一千名男兵，才要两个半女兵，女兵简直像空气中的惰性气体。身体检查严格极了，差不多和当女飞行员同样标准。幸好我那时身高一百七十厘米，两眼裸视力二点零还有富余，心、肝、脾、肺、肾全像刚从工厂造出来一样合格，属于特等甲级身体，经过了一轮又一轮的淘汰，我终于过五关斩六将，拿到了入伍通知书。

  How good it is to be a soldier! From then on, you can legitimately wear green clothes all year round, and no one will gossip about you again. But at that time, it was quite difficult to be a female soldier, there were too many people who wanted to be women, and there were more monks and less porridge. I heard that the ratio of male soldiers to female soldiers is 2.5 per thousand, that is to say, for 1,000 male soldiers, only two and a half female soldiers are needed, and female soldiers are like inert gas in the air. The physical examination is extremely strict, almost the same standard as being a female pilot. Fortunately, I was 170 centimeters tall at that time, with a naked vision of 2.0 and surplus, and my heart, liver, spleen, lungs, and kidneys were as qualified as if they had just been made from the factory, and they belonged to the special Class A body.我几乎不相信自己的好运气，连连问妈妈，您说，事情到了这个份儿上，还会有令人悲痛的变化吗？

  I hardly believed in my good luck, and asked my mother again and again, do you think there will be sad changes when things get to this point?妈妈说，不会吧。你就把通知书放在枕头底下，安心睡个好觉。

  Mom said, no. You just put the notice under your pillow and get a good night's sleep.我说，没穿上绿衣服之前，我可放心不下。

  I said, before I put on the green clothes, I can't rest assured.妈妈说，要变，你穿上军服还会让你脱下，担心也没有用。解放军应该是说话算话的。

  Mom said, if you want to change, you will take it off if you put on a military uniform, and it is useless to worry. The People's Liberation Army should be able to speak and count.发衣服的时候，穿着五颜六色家常衣服的新兵，排成一队，依次从司务长面前走过。司务长像大商场的成衣售货员，眯起眼睛打量着走过的小伙子和姑娘，大声地说，帽子二号……衣服三号……蹲在一旁的上士，就像老鹰抓小鸡一样，手疾眼快取出相应号码的衣物，把衬衣铺在最下面，其余所有东西都堆在上面，一时间好似平地起了一座绿色的小山，然后麻利地把衬衣的两条袖子抻出来，把它们打个结，怀抱里就塞满了崭新的衣物。领了军衣的人，就快乐地抱着这个绿色的半截人，走进一间密闭的小屋。再走出来的时候，就是一个英姿勃勃的兵了。

  When distributing clothes, recruits wearing colorful homely clothes lined up and walked past the secretary in turn. The secretary was like a ready-to-wear salesperson in a big shopping mall, squinting his eyes at the young men and girls walking by, and said loudly, hat number two...... Clothing number three...... The sergeant squatting on the side, like an eagle catching a chicken, quickly took out the clothes with the corresponding number, spread the shirt at the bottom, and piled everything else on it, as if a green hill had risen on the ground for a while, and then quickly stretched out the two sleeves of the shirt, tied them into a knot, and stuffed the arms with brand new clothes. The person who received the military uniform happily hugged the green half of the man and walked into a closed hut. When he came out again, he was a heroic soldier.好不容易轮到我的时候，司务长目测了一下，自言自语说，这个兵啊，长得不合尺寸。穿一号的小，穿特号的又大……

  When it was finally my turn, the secretary looked at it and said to himself, this soldier is not the size. Wearing a small size and a large size wearing a special size......我赶紧说，您甭为难。我要特号的。

  I hurriedly said, don't be embarrassed. I want a special number.司务长说，咦？女孩子都愿意穿得比较秀气，你这个兵倒奇怪。发给你特号的衣服，到时候裤腿踩到脚底下，窝窝囊囊，一不留神摔个大马趴，可别怪我。

  The director said, huh? Girls are willing to dress more delicately, but you are strange. The clothes I gave you a special number, when the trouser legs stepped under the soles of your feet, you were squishy, and you fell on a big horse if you didn't pay attention, don't blame me.我忙说，不怪不怪，绝不找你。我妈说过，衣服是会缩水的，当然是大点好了。裤腿长了可以裁，要是短了，就得自己找布接，多不合算！

  I hurriedly said, no blame, no blame, I will never look for you. My mother said that clothes will shrink, of course, it is better to be bigger. If the trouser legs are long, they can be cut, but if they are short, you have to find a cloth connection yourself, which is not cost-effective!司务长说，看不出来，你小小年纪，还挺会过日子的。好吧，依你，给特号。

  The director said, I can't see that you are young and can live quite well. Well, it's up to you, give a special number.

  我欢天喜地地去换衣服，一试之下，特号衣服果然名不虚传，上衣还凑合，裤子好像是给跳高运动员预备的，腿长无比。我把裤脚挽起来两折，自觉比较利索了，抱着旧衣服正准备从更衣小屋往外走，先换好军衣的一个女孩端详着我说，你像一个打鱼的。

  I happily went to change my clothes, and after a try, the special clothes really lived up to their name, the top was still made, and the pants seemed to be prepared for high jumpers, and the legs were extremely long. I rolled up my trousers in half, feeling more neat, holding my old clothes and was about to walk out of the changing room, a girl who had changed into a military uniform looked at me and said, you look like a fisherman.我看了她一眼，屋里光线不好，看不清眉眼，只觉得军装好像是特地比量她的身材做的，妥帖极了。我愤愤地说，你的意思是我不像一个兵？

  I glanced at her, the light in the room was not good, I couldn't see her eyebrows clearly, I just felt that the military uniform seemed to be specially made to measure her figure, which was very appropriate. I said indignantly, you mean I don't look like a soldier?她轻轻笑笑，露出雪白的牙说，你还是像一个兵的，只不过是个邋遢兵。

  She smiled softly, showed her snow-white teeth and said, you are still like a soldier, but a sloppy soldier.她的口气很老练，虽然军装同我一样没钉领章，军龄倒好像已有一百年。我没好气儿地说，兵工厂的人太没有节约观念了，裤子做得这么大，使人穿上像皮诺曹。

  Her tone is very sophisticated, although the military uniform is the same as mine, but the military experience seems to be a hundred years. I said angrily that the people in the arsenal had no concept of thrift, and the pants were made so big that people wore them like Pinocchio.她说，皮诺曹是谁？是咱们一块儿当女兵的吗？我叫小如，你叫什么？

  She said, who is Pinocchio? Are we here to be female soldiers? My name is Xiaoru, what is your name?我说，你就叫我小毕好了。咱们就甭理那个姓皮的家伙了，反正三言两语也说不清它的来历，还是讨论这条讨厌的裤子吧。我想把它剪掉一截，哪儿有剪刀？

  I said, you can call me Xiao Bi. Let's ignore the guy surnamed Pi, anyway, I can't tell where it came from in a few words, let's discuss these pesky pants. I want to cut it off, where are the scissors?小如说，剪了不好。一剪子下去倒是痛快，以后要是觉得短了，或者你再长个儿了，就没法补救了。不到万不得已，还是别干一锤子买卖的事。

  Xiaoru said, it's not good to cut it. It's fun to cut it down, but if you feel short in the future, or if you grow taller, you can't remedy it. Unless it is a last resort, it is better not to do a hammer deal.我不耐烦了，说，你倒是想得蛮周到，可大道理以后慢慢说，现在要解决的问题是，我怎么走出这间房子？

  I became impatient and said, you are quite thoughtful, but the truth will be slowly said later, and the problem to be solved now is, how can I get out of this house?小如笑起来，说，真是个急性子。一条裤子少说要穿一年，可你连这么几分钟时间都不愿等，活该你像那个姓皮的。

  Xiaoru laughed and said, what an impatient person. A pair of pants will take a year to wear, but you don't even want to wait for so many minutes, you deserve to be like that one surnamed Pi.想起木偶皮诺曹的狼狈样，我只好安静下来，听小如的主意。

  Thinking of the embarrassment of the puppet Pinocchio, I had to quiet down and listen to Xiaoru's idea.小如不说话，往外走。我说，你干吗去？

  Xiaoru didn't speak and walked out. I said, why are you going?她说，我去找司务长借针线。

  She said, I went to the secretary to borrow a needle and thread.我忙拦住说，使不得。

  I hurriedly stopped and said, no way.小如说，为什么呢？

  Xiaoru said, why?我苦着脸说，你不知道，我刚才跟司务长夸了口的，说衣服大了和他没关系。现在你去求他，不是太丢我的面子吗！

  I said with a bitter face, you don't know, I just boasted to the secretary, saying that the clothes are big and have nothing to do with him. Now you go and beg him, isn't it too much of a shame for me!"小如说，你就放心好了。

  Xiaoru said, don't worry.我竖起耳朵听外面小如和司务长的对话。小如说话的声调带一点乡下口音，但是很甜，好像那种高高地长在地里的玉米秸，清凉而柔韧。她说，司务长，借我一根细细的针、一条长长的线，好吗？

  I pricked up my ears to listen to the conversation between Xiaoru and the secretary outside. Xiaoru's tone has a slight country accent, but it is very sweet, like the corn stalks growing high in the ground, cool and flexible. She said, Director, borrow me a thin needle and a long thread, okay?硬邦邦的司务长好像被糖醋过了，声音变得软绵绵，说，针啊有有，只不过又粗又大，你就凑合着使吧，留神别扎了手。只是你要针线干什么？

  The hard secretary seemed to have been sweet and sour, and his voice became soft, saying, there are needles, but they are thick and big, you can make do, be careful not to prick your hands. But what do you want needle and thread for?缝衣服啊。

  Sew clothes.缝什么衣服？司务长立刻警觉起来。

  What clothes to sew? The secretary immediately became alert.缝你发给我们的衣服啊。小如很机智地回答。

  Sew the clothes you sent us. Xiaoru replied very wittily.我发给你们的衣服都是新的，哪里用得着缝？莫不是有什么破损的地方，你拿来，我给你换。然后再找被服厂的人理论。司务长很负责地说。

  The clothes I sent you are all new, where can you use sewing?" Could it be that there is something damaged, you bring it, and I will replace it for you." Then find the people in the quilt factory to theorize. The secretary said responsibly.小如笑笑，说，没那么严重。我只不过是想把衣服改一改。

  Xiaoru smiled and said, it's not that serious. I just want to change my clothes.司务长如临大敌，严肃起来，说，你是新兵，我是老兵，必要的规矩要告诉你。军装是不能任意改的，大家是个统一的整体。

  The secretary was like facing a great enemy, became serious, and said, you are a recruit, I am a veteran, and I want to tell you the necessary rules. Military uniforms cannot be changed arbitrarily, everyone is a unified whole.小如不理这一套，说，衣服太肥了，你总不能让我们一甩袖子，就像舞台上唱戏的青衣啊。

  Xiao Ru ignored this set and said, the clothes are too fat, you can't let us throw off our sleeves, just like the green clothes singing on the stage.司务长嘿嘿笑着说，袖子改得太瘦了，打靶的时候弯不过肘子来，小心吃鸭蛋。

  The secretary smiled and said, the sleeves have been changed too thin, and you can't bend your elbows when shooting targets, so be careful to eat duck eggs.小如说，鸭蛋多了就腌起来呗，腌得蛋黄流红油，就着馒头吃，香死个人！

  Xiaoru said, if there are too many duck eggs, marinate them, marinate the yolk with red oil, and eat it with steamed buns, which is fragrant to death!司务长说不过小如，就把针线给了小如。小如进了屋，拿过我的裤子，开始飞针走线，一会儿就把裤腿改得熨熨帖帖。我穿上后，举手投足，再不拖泥带水。

  The director couldn't say anything to Xiaoru, so he gave Xiaoru the needle and thread. Xiaoru entered the house, took my pants, and began to fly needles, and after a while, she changed her trouser legs to iron and iron. After I put it on, I gesture and don't drag my feet again.我说，小如，谢谢你。

  I said, Xiaoru, thank you.小如说，不必谢，我们乡下的女孩子，从小就要学会使针线，要不长大了，没人娶你做媳妇。

  Xiaoru said, no need to thank you, our girls in the countryside have to learn to use needles and threads since they were young, or if they grow up, no one will marry you as a daughter-in-law.

  我说，哎呀呀，像你这样的一手好活计，岂不是说媒的要挤破门！像我这样的，只好像个坏橘子一般，剩在筐里没人要了。

  I said, oh, if you are a good job, doesn't it mean that the matchmaker wants to squeeze the door! People like me are just like a bad orange, and no one wants them left in the basket.小如说，小声点，这种玩笑还是少开的好。你知道吗？当兵的时候是不准谈恋爱的。

  Xiaoru said, be quiet, it's better to make less jokes. Do you know? When you are a soldier, you are not allowed to fall in love.我连忙闭了嘴，要晓得为穿上这套绿衣服，我是多么费尽心机，哪能稀里糊涂地就叫人打发回家了。

  I quickly closed my mouth, knowing how hard I had tried to put on this green dress, how could I send someone home in a daze.等我们走出密闭的小屋时，司务长看了看我的裤子，叹了口气说，你是特号的身子一号的腿。

  When we walked out of the closed cabin, the secretary looked at my pants, sighed and said, you are the leg of the special body No. 1.我听了怒火中烧，这意思不就是我身子长腿短吗？哪个女孩子爱听这种话！我狠狠地瞪了他一眼，可惜司务长正瞧着别的地方，对我的愤怒没反应。不管怎么说，从今天开始，我成为一个真正的兵了。

  I was furious when I heard this, doesn't that mean that I have a long body and short legs? What girl loves to hear this kind of thing! I glared at him fiercely, but unfortunately the secretary was looking elsewhere and did not respond to my anger. In any case, from today onwards, I became a real soldier.

未知

  04.白云剪裁的衣服

  04. Clothes cut by white clouds

  河莲个儿矮，像个敦实的土丘。司务长低估了她的胖，给了一套正二号的军装。河莲勉强把自己装了进去，觉得憋得慌，大叫起来，说上衣的第二颗扣子压迫了心脏，喘不过气来。司务长只好给她去换副号衣服。

  He Lian is short, like a solid mound. The secretary underestimated her fatness and gave her a military uniform of number two. Ha Lian barely put herself in, feeling so panicked that she screamed, saying that the second button of her shirt pressed her heart and she couldn't breathe. The secretary had no choice but to change her sub-number clothes.军衣的型号挺奇怪，号数愈大的尺寸愈小。比如正五号衣服，中学生都能穿，但要是正一号，就得一米八以上的个头才撑得起来。当然，这讲的是标准身材，要是你长得比较圆滚，就得穿副号军装。副号的意思，是长度同正号一样，宽窄要肥出许多。女孩子一般都很忌讳副号。你想啊，军装为了行军打仗的方便，本来就宽宽大大，再一“副”，就更没款没型了。但河莲是个敢想敢说的女孩，她才不会为了别人的眼睛，让自己的心肺受委屈。

  The model of the military uniform is quite strange, the larger the number, the smaller the size. For example, No. 5 clothes can be worn by middle school students, but if they are No. 1, they have to be more than 1.8 meters tall to hold up. Of course, this is about the standard figure, if you are relatively round, you have to wear a secondary military uniform. The meaning of the secondary number is that the length is the same as the regular number, and the width is much fatter. Girls are generally very taboo about sub-numbers. You think, for the convenience of marching and fighting, military uniforms are originally wide and wide, and if they are "secondary", they will be even more stylish. But Ha Lian is a girl who dares to think and speak, so she will not let her heart and lungs be wronged for the eyes of others.正号军装是大路货，后勤部门保证供应。副号属于稀少品种，司务长颇费了一番心思，恨不能跟后勤部门说河莲胖得像个孕妇，才算领来一套副二号的衣服。

  The regular military uniform is a big road, and the logistics department guarantees the supply. The deputy number is a rare breed, and the secretary took a lot of effort, wishing he could tell the logistics department that He Lian was as fat as a pregnant woman, so he could get a set of clothes for deputy number two.试穿之后，河莲大为满意。不仅她的心脏跳动正常，这套衣服还有许多妙不可言的好处。一般衣服都是军绿色，好像夏天的松树林，这种独特的颜色有一个雄赳赳的名字，叫作“国防绿”。河莲的副号却是安宁的黄绿色，好像秋风扫过的草原，温暖而朴素。普通的衣服都是平纹布，河莲的衣服却是“人字呢”的。虽说它不是真正的呢子，只是布的纹路互相交叉，好像一行行一排排细密的“人”字，故而得了这样一个考究的名字，但看起来要比平纹布挺括得多。最最重要的是，河莲的军装是四个兜的！

  After trying it on, Ha Lian was very satisfied. Not only does her heart beat normally, but this outfit has many wonderful benefits. The general clothes are military green, like pine forests in summer, and this unique color has a majestic name, called "national defense green". The subtitle of He Lian is a peaceful yellow-green, like a grassland swept by the autumn wind, warm and simple. Ordinary clothes are plain fabric, but Ha Lian's clothes are "herring". Although it is not a real wool, it is just that the lines of the cloth intersect with each other, like rows of fine "people" characters, so it got such a sophisticated name, but it looks much more crisp than plain weave. The most important thing is that Ha Lian's military uniform is four pockets!没有当过兵的人，不知道衣兜的重要性。它除了装东西之外，更是一个标志。战士服只在胸前有两个口袋，提升了干部，才能穿有四个口袋的上衣。口袋因此成了某种地位的象征。不过女兵喜欢四个兜的衣服，倒不是势利的缘故。因为胸高，随身又总有些小零碎儿，比如手绢、钢笔什么的要经常带着，若衣服下摆没有兜，只得都塞在胸前，鼓鼓囊囊，像藏了一窝鸽子，显得很不利落。

  People who have never been soldiers do not know the importance of clothing pockets. In addition to holding things, it is also a sign. The soldier's uniform only has two pockets on the chest, and only after the cadre is promoted can a top with four pockets be worn. The pocket thus became a status symbol. However, female soldiers like clothes with four pockets, not because of snobbery. Because of the high chest, there are always some small bits and pieces with you, such as handkerchiefs, pens, etc., if there is no pocket on the hem of the clothes, you have to stuff them all in front of your chest, bulging, like hiding a nest of pigeons, which is very unfavorable.副号有这么多优越性，大家都去找司务长要求换军装。司务长火了，说没见过这么难缠的兵！婆婆妈妈的，谁要是不想干了，就向后转，回家去，爱穿什么穿什么！

  The sub-number has so many advantages that everyone went to the secretary to ask for a change of uniform. The secretary was angry and said that he had never seen such a difficult soldier! Mother-in-law's, if anyone doesn't want to do it, turn back, go home, and wear whatever you like!话说到如此凶狠的份儿上，我们只好乖乖地穿正号衣服。河莲独自乐了没几天，发现人字呢也有弊病。洗衣的时候，刚把衣服泡在脸盆里，就有浑黄的汤沁出来。刚开始，河莲以为衣服格外脏，就拼命搓，搓得两个手掌像红萝卜一样。洗了几水之后，正号衣服还像葱叶一般绿，河莲的副号军衣已泛出菜心般的黄。

  Speaking of such ferocity, we had to obediently wear the right clothes. He Lian had fun alone for a few days, and found that the herringbone also had drawbacks. When washing, as soon as I soaked the clothes in the basin, a yellow soup came out. At first, Ha Lian thought that her clothes were particularly dirty, so she rubbed them desperately, rubbing her palms like carrots. After washing a few waters, the legitimate clothes were still as green as green onion leaves, and He Lian's sub-number military uniform had turned yellow like cabbage.一天，果平大惊小怪地喊起来，河莲，要是敌机轰炸，第一个阵亡的肯定是你！

  One day, Guo Ping shouted in a fuss, He Lian, if the enemy plane bombs, you will definitely be the first to die!我们大吃一惊，不知果平为何发此恶毒咒语。

  We were taken aback and didn't know why Guohing cast this vicious spell.果平说，你们想啊，我们都有绿色伪装，只有河莲的衣服像经了霜的野草，还不一下就被发现了？

  Guo Ping said, you think, we all have green camouflage, only He Lian's clothes are like frosted weeds, and they are discovered in a hurry?河莲脑子快，立即反驳说，依我看，还不知谁第一个为国捐躯呢！没准儿正是你们这些国防绿。

  He Lian was quick-witted, and immediately retorted, in my opinion, I don't know who was the first to sacrifice his life for the country! Maybe it's you national defense greens.所有穿正号军装的都不干了，定要河莲说个清楚。

  All those wearing regular military uniforms are gone, and He Lian must make it clear.河莲不慌不忙地说，要是春夏季节开仗，大地一片翠绿，自然你们的衣服是最好的保护色。可要是秋天呢？丰收在望，落叶满地，到处都是金黄，肯定是我的衣服伪装性更好。

  He Lian said calmly, if the war starts in spring and summer, the earth will be green, and naturally your clothes will be the best protective color. But what if it's autumn? The harvest is in sight, the fallen leaves are all over the ground, and there is gold everywhere, it must be that my clothes are better camouflaged.大家你看看我、我看看你，不得不承认河莲的话有几分道理，只好自我解嘲道，反正我们也不是敌人的参谋长，谁知道仗哪会儿打？要是春夏开战，河莲你就留在后方做饭。要是秋天开战，河莲你就一个人打冲锋。

  Everyone, look at me, I look at you, I have to admit that He Lian's words have some truth, so I have to laugh at myself, anyway, we are not the enemy's chief of staff, who knows when the battle will be fought? If there is a war in spring and summer, Ha Lian you will stay in the rear to cook. If there is a war in the fall, Ha Lian will charge alone.河莲也不理我们，只是更起劲儿地洗军装，盆子里倒进一大堆洗衣粉，激起的泡沫，好像有一百只大螃蟹愤怒地吞云吐雾。她还专拣大太阳当头的日子，在外面晒衣服。这样，没用多长时间，副号不断褪色，最后简直变成白的了。

  He Lian ignored us, but washed his military uniform even harder, poured a lot of laundry detergent into the basin, and the foam stirred up seemed to be a hundred big crabs angrily swallowing clouds and mist. She also chose a day when the sun was shining to dry her clothes outside. In this way, it didn't take long for the sub-number to fade and finally turn white.古代有句俗话叫：男要俏，一身皂；女要俏，一身孝。

  There is an ancient saying: Men should be pretty, and they should be dressed in soap; Women should be beautiful and filial.

  关于“皂”到底是什么色，我们争论了好长时间，基本上统一了意见，认定是一种近乎月亮和蓝天混合在一起的颜色。关于“孝”，倒是没有什么争论的，就是医院里没有染上血的棉花颜色了。河莲在黎明的晨光里，背对着太阳走向我们的时候，白衣白裤，好像云彩剪裁做成的军装。

  We argued for a long time about what color "soap" was, and basically unified our opinion, believing that it was a color that was almost a mixture of the moon and the blue sky. There is no debate about "filial piety", that is, there is no blood-stained cotton color in the hospital. When He Lian walked towards us with her back to the sun in the morning light of dawn, she was dressed in white clothes and white pants, like a military uniform made of clouds.正号们充满嫉妒之心，果平甚至痛下决心，要在一年之内，把自己吃成一个大胖子，明年就可名正言顺地领人字呢副二号了。

  The regular numbers were full of jealousy, and Guo Ping even made up his mind to eat himself into a big fat man within a year, and next year he would be able to rightfully lead the second pair of people.看着果平像北京填鸭似的大吃特吃，小如提醒她，人字呢因为染料不过关，属淘汰产品，已经不生产了。河莲领的是库底子，谁知明年会怎样？若是你辛辛苦苦吃成相扑手模样，明年的副号已变成国防绿，你岂不白胖了一回？

  Seeing Guoping eating like a Peking duck, Xiaoru reminded her that the herringbone is no longer produced because the dye is not up to par and is an obsolete product. He Lian is led by Kudizi, who knows what will happen next year? If you work hard to become a sumo wrestler, next year's sub-number has become national defense green, won't you gain weight in vain?果平这才放慢了胡吃海塞的速度。

  Only then did Guo Ping slow down the speed of eating Haisai.我问河莲，你把衣服洗得这样白，是否准备冬天打仗的时候，一个人趴在雪地上，狙击敌人？你不要闹个人英雄主义，要知道，冬天的伪装并不难办，只要每个人披上一条白床单，任你火眼金睛也发现不了埋伏。

  I asked He Lian, you wash your clothes so white, are you preparing to fight in winter, lying on the snow alone and sniping the enemy? Don't make personal heroism, you know, winter disguise is not difficult, as long as everyone wears a white sheet, you can't find an ambush even if you are hot.河莲说，你以为我是孤胆英雄？你不穿这衣服，不知它的毛病。特别不经脏，刚穿一两天，袖口就黑得像套了一圈猴皮筋，抹了机油似的，所以，我就老得洗。

  Ha Lian said, do you think I am a lone hero? If you don't wear this dress, you don't know what's wrong with it. It's very dirty, and after just wearing it for a day or two, the cuffs are as black as a circle of monkey rubber bands and oiled, so I'm old and washable.练习匍匐前进，连长一个鱼跃，趴到草丛中，泥土四溅。女孩子虽然酷爱干净，但连长这般身先士卒，也就只好奋不顾身地扑过去，手脚并用，在粗糙的草叶上敏捷地爬行。草汁和着汗水涂抹在脸上，人好像流了绿色的血。

  Practice crawling forward, and the company leader jumped a fish, lying down in the grass, and the mud was splashed. Although the girl loves to be clean, the company commander took the lead so much that she had to pounce on it, using her hands and feet, and crawling agilely on the rough blades of grass. Grass juice and sweat were smeared on the face, and the person seemed to have green blood.所有的人都趴下了，唯有河莲笔直地站在那里。

  Everyone fell down, except for He Lian standing straight there.你为什么不卧倒？连长的好奇更大于震怒，在他当兵若干年的历史中，还从未看到过一个面对命令敢于不趴下的士兵。

  Why don't you lie down? The company commander's curiosity was greater than anger, and in his several years of military service, he had never seen a soldier who dared not lie down in the face of orders.我的衣服颜色浅，趴在这样的泥土里，再也洗不干净了。河莲理直气壮。

  My clothes are light-colored, lying in such dirt, and I can no longer wash them. He Lian is justified and strong.是衣服重要还是胜利重要？如果在战场上，你不卧倒，衣服可能始终干净，但你的小命就没有啦！连长声色俱厉。

  Is it the clothes or the victory that matters? If you don't lie down on the battlefield, your clothes may always be clean, but your life will be gone! The company commander's voice and expression were fierce.我是傻子吗？到了打仗的时候，我自然知道生命比衣服更重要。炮声一响，我就像邱少云一样趴在地上，纹丝不动。河莲才不吃他那一套，有板有眼地回答。我们都忍不住笑起来。

  Am I a fool? When it came time to fight, I naturally knew that life was more important than clothes. As soon as the cannon sounded, I lay on the ground like Qiu Shaoyun, motionless. He Lian didn't eat his set, and replied with a clear eye. We all couldn't help but laugh.连长大怒，认为河莲没有战斗观念，目无上级，给了她一个队前警告。看得出，河莲非常不服，但是有什么办法呢？一个小兵，而且是个新兵，哪里有你说话的份儿！我们顿生兔死狐悲之心，希望自己快快地老起来，满脸皱纹，穿破十套军装，就有了倚老卖老的资格。比如我们的班长，都是通信部队来的老兵，她们可以自由自在地打闹和嗑瓜子，连长皱皱眉，一声也不敢吭。

  The company commander was furious, thinking that He Lian had no concept of combat and had no superiors, and gave her a warning in front of the team. It can be seen that He Lian is very dissatisfied, but what can be done? A small soldier, and a recruit, where is your part to speak! We suddenly felt sad and sad, hoping that we would grow old quickly, with wrinkles on our faces and wearing ten sets of military uniforms, and we would have the qualifications to rely on the old and sell the old. For example, our squad leaders are all veterans from the communication unit, and they can play and eat melon seeds freely, but the company commander frowned and did not dare to say a word.由于不断地卧倒，草绿色军装很快变成灰黑，勤快的人隔两天洗一回，使它勉强保持着衣服的本色。我是个懒虫，心想反正洗了也是脏，不洗也是脏，索性由它脏着好了。好在也不是我一个人不成嘴脸，大家基本上都是暗无天日。

  Due to the constant lying down, the grass green military uniform quickly turned gray and black, and diligent people washed it every two days, so that it barely maintained the true color of the clothes. I was a lazy person, thinking that it would be dirty if I washed it anyway, and it would be dirty if I didn't wash it, so I just let it get dirty. Fortunately, I'm not alone, everyone is basically dark.一天连长看到我，咧着嘴说，我从来没有看到过像你这么脏的女兵。

  One day, the company commander saw me and grinned and said, I have never seen a female soldier as dirty as you.我说，这是节约啊。

  I said, this is saving.连长很奇怪，说，脏衣服比干净的衣服更耐磨吗？我当了这么多年兵，从没听说过。

  The company commander was very strange, saying, is dirty clothes more wear-resistant than clean clothes? I have been a soldier for so many years and have never heard of it.我说，每天都洗衣服，要用掉多少洗衣粉和肥皂？多少时间？多少力气？搭在铁丝上，水珠会让铁丝生锈，日子久了，铁丝还可能会被压断……只要不洗衣服，这些岂不都省了？

  I said, how much laundry detergent and soap do you use to wash clothes every day? How much time? How much strength? On the wire, water droplets will rust the wire, and over time, the wire may be crushed...... As long as you don't wash your clothes, won't these be saved?连长第一次听到这种逻辑，气得咻咻喘，可一时也没话好说。但他似乎怀恨在心，在紧接下来的射击训练中，故意不指导我和河莲。别人托着枪练习瞄准，连长会耐心地趴在旁边，从瞄准镜中观察他们的动作是否符合要领，矫正他们有毛病的动作。走到我和河莲身旁，他总是淡淡地说，你们俩还需要辅导啊？都是很见过世面的老兵了，一个知道战斗英雄邱少云，一个是节约模范，到了靶场上，打个优秀是没说的了。

  The company commander heard this logic for the first time, and he was so angry that he couldn't say anything for a while. But he seemed to hold a grudge and deliberately did not instruct me and Ha Lian in the next shooting training. When others practice aiming with guns, the company commander will patiently lie next to them, observe whether their movements are in line with the essentials from the scope, and correct their faulty movements. Walking next to me and He Lian, he always said lightly, do you two still need tutoring? They are all veterans who have seen the world very well, one knows the combat hero Qiu Shaoyun, and the other is a model of thrift, and when he arrives at the shooting range, there is nothing to say about playing excellently.我和河莲苦着脸。多倒霉啊，刚当新兵，就和顶头上司结下冤仇。我使劲儿打了一下军衣的下襟，好像它是一个有生命的小动物。所有的麻烦，都是衣服惹出来的。当然啦，结果是除了军衣冒出一股尘土以外，疼的还是我的手和肚子。

  He Lian and I made bitter faces. How unlucky, as soon as he became a recruit, he made a grudge against his immediate boss. I tapped the hem of my military uniform vigorously, as if it were a living animal. All the trouble is caused by clothes. Of course, the result was that in addition to a cloud of dust coming out of the military uniform, it was my hands and stomach that hurt.晚饭后，河莲和我坐在葡萄架下商量，连长这么恨我们，怎么办呢？要不然，我从此不洗衣服，尽快把白军装穿成黑的，连长是不是就会笑口常开？河莲手托着腮帮，好像牙疼般地说。

  After dinner, He Lian and I sat under the grape trellis and discussed, what should we do if the company commander hates us so much? Otherwise, if I don't wash my clothes and wear the white military uniform black as soon as possible, will the company commander smile all the time? He Lian held her cheek in her hand and said as if she had a toothache.我没好气儿地答，做梦吧！我的衣服倒是黑的，可连长还不是耿耿于怀？关键是我们顶撞了他。俗话说，连长连长，半个皇上。咱们再怎么赔笑脸，也没法挽回影响啦。

  I replied angrily, dream! My clothes are black, but isn't the company commander worried about it? The point is that we contradicted him. As the saying goes, the company commander is half the emperor. No matter how much we lose our smiling faces, we can't undo the impact.河莲倔强地说，你猜，连长现在最希望我们干什么？

  He Lian said stubbornly, guess what the company commander wants us to do most now?我把葡萄藤卷曲的须子含在嘴里嚼着，苦涩的清水像小水枪一样滋在舌头上，酸得人打寒战。我说，他最巴望着咱俩在射击场上吃鸭蛋吧。

  I chewed the curly whiskers of the grapevines in my mouth, and the bitter water nourished my tongue like a small water gun, souring and shivering. I said, he is most looking forward to the two of us eating duck eggs on the shooting range.河莲说，英雄所见略同。我们现在只有用行动证实自己是个好兵。要不，就会被人指着脊梁骨耻笑。

  He Lian said that the hero's vision is roughly the same. We can only prove that we are good soldiers with our actions. Otherwise, you will be pointed at the spine and laughed at.

  人们多以为爱可以给人以力量，其实，憋着一口气的劲头更是大得可怕。我和河莲从此抓紧一切时间练习瞄准，每天趴在地上，胳膊肘磨破了皮，脖子上永远淌着几条透明的蚯蚓。口中念念有词，把射击要领背得像父母的名字一样熟，看到任何物体，想的都是“三点成一线”的口诀。至于军装，再不去理它，脏得简直没法提，活似两个卖炭翁。

  People often think that love can give people strength, but in fact, the momentum of holding a breath is even more terrifying. From then on, He Lian and I seized all the time to practice aiming, lying on the ground every day, our elbows worn out of the skin, and a few transparent earthworms always flowing around our necks. He muttered words in his mouth, memorized the essentials of shooting as familiar as his parents' names, and when he saw any object, he thought of the mantra of "three points into a line". As for the military uniform, if you don't pay attention to it, it is so dirty that you can't mention it, like two charcoal sellers.连长还是不理我们。好在射击要领也不是他的专利，班长和其他人也可以指导我们。再有什么不明白的，我和河莲就自己揣摩，争取自学成才。

  The company commander still ignored us. Fortunately, the essentials of shooting are not his patent, and the squad leader and others can also guide us. If there is anything we don't understand, He Lian and I will figure it out by ourselves and strive to be self-taught.实弹射击的时候到了。靶场上的气氛很森严，掩体里等待报靶的士兵戴着亮闪闪的钢盔，在远处神出鬼没。二百米开外的半身胸环靶，在阳光下好似幻影。我不由得紧张，手心像攥了两把糨糊，黏黏糊糊。我看看河莲，她倒一副胸有成竹的模样。我也定了心，心想到了这个关头，你腿肚子发软，只会把事情弄得更糟，索性豁出去拼了。

  It's time for live ammunition. The atmosphere on the shooting range was very strict, and the soldiers waiting for the target in the bunker wore shiny steel helmets and were elusive in the distance. The half-body chest ring target 200 meters away looks like a phantom in the sun. I couldn't help but be nervous, my palms seemed to be holding two handfuls of paste, sticky. I looked at He Lian, and she looked confident. I also made up my mind, thinking that at this juncture, your legs and stomach are weak, which will only make things worse, so I simply give up and fight.枪声响起来。我的第一感觉，是它绝没有想象中的响亮，只相当于一个中等二踢脚崩出的动静。对真枪实弹声音的失望，使我的心很快宁静下来。偷眼看看连长，他似乎比我们还要紧张，目光炯炯地注视着一个个进入射位的女兵。每逢射手扣扳机的时候，他颊上的肌肉就会跳动一下，令人猜到他是牙关紧咬。

  Gunshots rang out. My first feeling was that it was by no means as loud as I imagined, just equivalent to the movement of a medium second kick. The disappointment of the sound of real guns and live ammunition quickly calmed my heart. Stealing a glance at the company commander, he seemed to be more nervous than us, staring brightly at the female soldiers who entered the shooting position. Whenever the shooter pulls the trigger, the muscles on his cheek jump, making people guess that he is clenching his teeth.我打了个“良好”。说不上很理想，但我已殚精竭虑。

  I typed "good". It's not ideal, but I've done my best.河莲平时的眼神不怎么好，没想到九发子弹竟打出了八十六环的优秀成绩，特别是她前八发子弹，居然是发发命中十环，简直是个神枪手。唯一美中不足的是，最后一枪，不知是何差池，江郎才尽，只中了六环。

  He Lian's eyes are usually not very good, but she didn't expect that nine bullets would hit an excellent result of eighty-six rings, especially her first eight bullets, which actually hit ten rings, which was simply a sharpshooter. The only fly in the ointment is that the last shot, I don't know what kind of mistake it was, Jiang Lang was exhausted, and only hit the sixth ring.不管怎么说，河莲为自己大大地挣回了面子。当连长向她走来的时候，我们就直直地盯着连长，看他对这个自己不喜欢但创造出优异成绩的刺儿头兵，如何反应。

  In any case, He Lian earned a lot of face for herself. When the company commander walked towards her, we stared straight at the company commander to see how he reacted to this assassin who didn't like him but created excellent results.连长仿佛什么事也不曾发生过的样子，对河莲说，要是你最后一枪打得再从容些，就能得满环，也许我会为你报个功呢。可惜了。

  The company commander seemed to have never happened, and said to He Lian, if you shoot the last shot more calmly, you can get a full ring, maybe I will repay you. It's a pity.河莲刚查完自己的靶纸，不服气地说，我这最后一枪，端端正正地打到了敌人的脑袋瓜上。我看这报靶的环数定得不科学。若打到右胸偏上的位置，按规定就是八环，可谁都知道，那地方离心脏远着呢，并不一定会置人于死地。我的这个六环，正中人的太阳穴，明摆着，一枪就能取了人性命。

  As soon as He Lian finished checking her target paper, she said unconvincedly, "My last shot hit the enemy's head neatly." I think the number of rings of this target is unscientific. If it hits the upper position of the right chest, it is the eighth ring according to the regulations, but everyone knows that the place is far from the heart, and it may not necessarily put people to death. My six rings, right in the temple, are clearly one shot can take a person's life.我们一听，都觉得河莲说得有理，且看连长如何答对。

  When we heard this, we all felt that He Lian was reasonable, and let's see how the company commander answered correctly.连长微微一笑说，河莲，没想到，你还有一套打不准的理论。可是我问你，瞄准的时候，你瞄的是敌人的脑袋还是敌人的胸脯？

  The company commander smiled slightly and said, He Lian, I didn't expect that you still have a set of inaccurate theories. But let me ask you, when aiming, are you aiming at the enemy's head or the enemy's chest?河莲说，连长你这个问题难不倒我。瞄准的要领是准星、缺口和胸环靶的下沿正中呈一条直线，当然是胸脯了……

  He Lian said, company commander, you can't trouble me. The key to aiming is that the crosshair, notch and chest ring target are in a straight line in the middle of the lower edge, of course, the chest......连长用一个坚决的手势，制止了河莲略带卖弄的背诵。他可不想听一个新兵，把自己烂熟于心的拿手好戏再演练一遍。好了，你既然瞄准的是敌人的肚子，结果子弹却打到了头上，就算敌人躺倒了，也是瞎猫碰到了死耗子，没什么可吹的。很可能下次你瞄的是敌人的天灵盖，打到的却是脚指头。连长说。

  The company commander used a resolute gesture to stop He Lian's slightly ostentatious recitation. He didn't want to listen to a recruit and rehearse his favorite tricks again. Well, since you are aiming at the enemy's stomach, but the bullet hits the head, even if the enemy lies down, it is a blind cat that touches the dead mouse, and there is nothing to brag. It is likely that next time you will aim at the enemy's heavenly spirit cover, but you will hit your toes. The company commander said.大家笑起来。我真替河莲抱不平，但连长的话驳不倒。可怜河莲本是功高盖世的英豪，此刻倒成了大家的笑料。

  Everyone laughed. I really feel sorry for He Lian, but the company commander's words cannot be refuted. Poor He Lian was originally a hero with great merits, but now she has become everyone's laughingstock.实弹训练结束后，有两天的休整。我和河莲把自己的军衣都洗了，天哪，水黑如墨，沉淀了半盆的泥沙。看见我泼水的人直嚷：快去叫老农！这样的肥水，可以浇两亩好地。

  After the live ammunition training, there are two days of rest. He Lian and I washed all our military uniforms, oh my God, the water was as black as ink, and half a basin of sediment was deposited. The person who saw me splashing water shouted: Go and call the old farmer! Such fertilizer water can water two acres of good land.我们耐心地等着太阳把湿军装晒干。洁净的衣服重新穿在身上的时候，令人有一种脱胎换骨的感觉。我们俩你看看我、我看看你，好像不认识似的。我的军装绿如橄榄，河莲的衣服恢复了白云的颜色。

  We patiently waited for the sun to dry our wet military uniforms. When clean clothes are put back on the body, people feel reborn. You two look at me, I look at you, as if we don't know each other. My military uniform is as green as olives, and Ha Lian's clothes have regained the color of white clouds.连长走过来说，现在这个样子嘛，我这个当连长的面子上也有光。不管怎么说，你俩是我带过的最邋遢最不听话的新兵了。不过，幸好还不算太笨。
The company commander came over and said, now that it looks like this, I also have light on my face as a company commander. In any case, you two are the most sloppy and disobedient recruits I have ever led. Fortunately, though, it's not too stupid.

未知

  05.走，到阿里去

  05. Let's go, go to Ali

  新兵训练要结束了，分配就在眼前。大家心里都关心这事，可表面上显得很淡漠，没心没肺地打打闹闹。因为你要是特别表现出对去向的关注，别人会觉得你挑肥拣瘦，思想有问题。领导知道了，没准儿会特地把你分到一个倒霉的单位，制裁一下你呢。

  Recruit training is coming to an end, and assignments are just around the corner. Everyone was concerned about this matter in their hearts, but on the surface they seemed very indifferent, fighting heartlessly. Because if you show special attention to where you are going, others will think that you are picking the fat and thin, and you have a problem with your thinking. If the leader knows, he may specially assign you to an unlucky unit and sanction you.我对这事想得比较简单，希望做一个通信兵。女兵基本上只有两个工种可挑——卫生员和电话员。卫生员要给病人端屎端尿，我一想就心中作呕。要是当着病人的面吐起来，是多么尴尬的事！通信兵就比较安稳，每天打交道的无非是塞绳和电线，都是不会说话的哑巴，当然省心了。

  I think about this relatively simply, I hope to be a signal soldier. Female soldiers basically have only two types of jobs to choose from - hygienists and telephone operators. The hygienist wanted to give the patient feces and urine, and I felt nauseous when I thought about it. How embarrassing it would be to vomit in front of the patient! The signal soldiers are relatively stable, and they deal with nothing more than plugged ropes and wires every day, and they are all mute people who can't speak, of course, they don't have to worry.墙上有一幅油画，叫“我是海燕”，一个英姿勃勃的女兵，在漫天风雨中攀上高耸的电线杆，维修线路。狂风卷起她漆黑的短发，因为淋了水，橡胶雨衣显出乌鸦羽毛一般油亮的光泽，随风飘荡……她高喊着“我是海燕”，这既是一句线路修复之后的联络用语，也充满了勇敢的象征意味，使我年轻的心激荡万分。油画的技术如何，我不知道，但暴风雨中的女通信兵成了我的青春偶像。我想，要是我当通信兵，力争比她干得还棒。打仗时，我会用两手把线路接通，让进攻的命令通过我的身体传达到火线，立个功给大家看。

  There is an oil painting on the wall called "I am Haiyan", a heroic female soldier climbing a towering telephone pole in the wind and rain to repair the lines. The wind swept up her short black hair, and because of the water, the rubber raincoat showed the shiny luster of a crow's feather, fluttering in the wind...... She shouted "I am Haiyan", which was not only a contact phrase after the line was repaired, but also full of brave symbolism, which made my young heart excited. I don't know how the technique of oil painting is, but the female signal soldier in the storm became my youth idol. I think if I become a signal soldier, I will strive to do better than her. When fighting, I will connect the line with both hands, so that the command to attack is transmitted to the line of fire through my body, and I will make a meritorious service for everyone to see.在树林里，小如悄悄凑近我的耳朵说，这次有五个名额，分到阿里去。

  In the woods, Xiaoru quietly approached my ear and said that there were five places this time, and they were assigned to Ali.我从这一句话里听出了两个问题：阿里是哪儿？你从谁那儿听说的？

  I heard two questions from this sentence: Where is Ali? Who did you hear about it from?小如拢拢耷拉到眼前的头发说，阿里是西藏的一个地方，听说海拔有五千多米呢，高寒缺氧，还有好多地方根本就没有人去过，号称“无人区”。

  Xiao Ru gathered his hair in front of him and said that Ali is a place in Tibet, I heard that the altitude is more than 5,000 meters, the high cold and lack of oxygen, and there are many places that no one has been to at all, known as "no man's land".我吓得抽了一口凉气说，既然是无人区，要我们去干什么？

  I was so frightened that I took a breath and said, since it's a no-man's land, what do you want us to do?小如说，普通人当然没有了，但有国防军啊。听说那里以前从来没有女兵，这次是头一回。

  Xiaoru said, of course ordinary people don't have it, but there is a national defense force. I heard that there had never been female soldiers there before, and this was the first time.我说，你的情报还挺详细，哪儿来的？道听途说还是你自己编的？

  I said, your information is quite detailed, where did it come from? Hearsay or did you make it up yourself?小如说，你还挺高看我的，这样机密的消息，我就是蒙着头想它个三天三夜，也编不出。是连长告诉我的。

  Xiaoru said, you think highly of me, such a confidential news, I just cover my head and think about it for three days and three nights, and I can't make it up. It was the company commander who told me.我大吃一惊，说看连长那个严肃样，恨不能把我们都当成射击胸靶，怎会把兵家大事透露给你？

  I was surprised, saying that looking at the company commander's serious look, I wish I could treat us all as shooting targets, how could I reveal the major events of the military family to you?小如说，这事对你我是大事，对连长来说，不过小菜一碟。经他的手，把多少新兵送往四面八方啊。这是我给他洗衣服的时候，随口问来的。

  Xiaoru said, this matter is a big deal for you and me, but for the company commander, it is just a piece of cake. How many recruits were sent to all directions through his hands. This is what I asked casually when I was washing his clothes.我的疑问更大了，说，小如，你再说一遍，你给谁洗衣服？

  My question was even bigger, saying, Xiaoru, say it again, who do you wash clothes for?给连长啊。小如清清楚楚地重复。

  To the company commander. Xiao Ru repeated clearly.你为什么要给连长洗衣服呢？他难道是个残疾人，自己没有手吗？我很纳闷，惊奇中又很不以为然，看不起她巴结领导。

  Why do you want to wash the company commander? Is he a disabled person who has no hands? I was very puzzled, surprised and dissatisfied, and looked down on her flattering the leader.小如坦然地说，每天训练回来，一身泥一身土的，谁像你似的，那么懒，帽子脏得像炸油饼的锅盖也不洗。我可天天要洗的，要不睡不着觉。好几次遇到连长，他一个男人家，洗衣的时候笨手笨脚，肥皂泡溢了一地。帮一下呗，顺手的活儿。在家的时候，我也净帮着我哥。

  Xiaoru said frankly that when he came back from training every day, he was covered in mud and dirt, who was like you, so lazy, and his hat was as dirty as a fried pancake and didn't wash the lid. I have to wash it every day, or I can't sleep. Several times I met the company commander, who was a man's family, clumsy when doing laundry, and soap bubbles overflowed all over the floor. Help, it's a convenient job. When I was at home, I also helped my brother.我大笑起来，原来你把连长当成了哥，他就向你透露军情。

  I laughed, it turned out that you regarded the company commander as a brother, and he revealed the military situation to you.小如说，没事闲聊呗，话赶话地就说到那儿了。

  Xiaoru said, it's okay to chat, and that's it.我说，请继续刺探下去，特别是通信兵和卫生兵的比例问题。

  I said, please continue to spy on the issue, especially the ratio of signal soldiers and medics.小如说，你干吗特别关心这个呢？

  Xiaoru said, why are you particularly concerned about this?我说，我讨厌卫生员这个行当，一天到晚遇见的不是病人就是死人，反正都是些没有笑容的脸，晦气啊。而且从根本上来说，我是一个缺乏同情心的人，所以，我不想穿白大褂。

  I said, I hate the hygienist, all day long I meet either patients or dead people, anyway, they are all faces without smiles, bad luck. And fundamentally, I'm a person who lacks compassion, so I don't want to wear a white coat.小如反驳我说，当个医生多么好！治好了一个病人，人家全家都感谢你，会记你一辈子的。

  Xiaoru retorted to me, how good it is to be a doctor! If you cure a patient, the whole family will thank you and will remember you for the rest of your life.我说，你怎么光想好事？就不想想，若给人家治死了，全家都恨你，也许到海枯石烂。

  I said, why do you just think about good things? Don't think about it, if you are killed by others, the whole family will hate you, and maybe the sea will rot.小如说，为什么光想坏事？再说，你就不会把本事练得精点，别把人家给治死吗？

  Xiaoru said, why just think about bad things? Besides, you won't practice your skills more refined, don't you kill people?我说，天有不测风云啊。再说，人总是要死的，这是伟人说的……

  I said, there are unforeseen circumstances. Besides, people always have to die, this is what the great man said......我俩正拌嘴，果平跑过来说，你们躲在犄角旮旯，是不是正说我的坏话呢？背人没好事。

  We were arguing, and Guo Ping ran over and said, you are hiding in the corner, are you talking bad about me? There is no good thing to carry people.我们大叫冤枉。果平嘻嘻一笑说，既然不是说我的坏话，就把正说的话告诉我吧。要不我不信。

  We shouted that we were wronged. Guo Ping smiled and said, since you are not speaking ill of me, tell me what you are saying. Otherwise, I don't believe it.我看着小如。消息的主要来源是小如，不能喧宾夺主。小如是个好脾气，虽然她不想把消息散布得人人皆知，但考虑到友谊至上，还是把所有的情报都告诉了果平。

  I looked at Xiao Ru. The main source of the news is Xiaoru, which cannot be overpowered. Xiaoru is a good-natured person, although she doesn't want to spread the news to everyone, but considering the supremacy of friendship, she still told Guo Ping all the information.我以为果平会激动得捶胸顿足，没想到她一撇嘴说，就这个啊，早嚷破天了。

  I thought Guo Ping would be so excited that she beat her chest and stomped her feet, but she curled her lips and said, that's it, I've already shouted at the sky.我这才明白，有些消息的传播，是不需要“海燕”的。

  Only then did I understand that the spread of some news does not require "Haiyan".果平接着说，连分配中卫生兵和通信兵的比例是九比一，也已是公开的秘密。

  Guo Ping went on to say that the ratio of medical soldiers and signal soldiers in the company is 9 to 1, which is an open secret.好像有千吨陨铁自九天坠下，正好砸到我的头上。我揪着果平说，你这话当真？

  It seemed that a thousand tons of meteorite fell from the Nine Heavens and hit my head. I grabbed Guo Ping and said, are you serious?果平说，向毛主席保证！

  Guo Ping said, I promise Chairman Mao!这是一句极有威力的誓言，我再也无法怀疑它的准确性。

  This is a very powerful oath, and I can no longer doubt its accuracy.小如沉静地说，看来，只有极少数的幸运儿才能当上海燕，绝大多数都是小白鸽啦。

  Xiaoru said calmly, it seems that only a very small number of lucky people can be petrels, and the vast majority are little white pigeons.小白鸽是小说《林海雪原》中女卫生员的爱称。果平说，悲痛欲绝！我本来想若是一半对一半的比例，不哼不哈地等着，也许就会分我到通信站。没想到，事实这般残酷！
