  The little white pigeon is the nickname of the female hygienist in the novel "Lin Hai Snow Field". Guo Ping said, grief-stricken! I originally thought that if I waited for a half-to-half ratio, maybe I would be assigned to the communication station. I didn't expect the facts to be so cruel!完啦！我彻底绝望，近在咫尺就有竞争者。我简直想变成老鹰，把小白鸽抓走几只。

  It's over! I was completely desperate, there were competitors close at hand. I wanted to turn into an eagle and take away a few little white pigeons.河莲走过来说，这次分配最艰苦的地方是阿里。越是艰苦越光荣，我想写一份血书，你们谁与我同甘共苦？

  He Lian came over and said that the hardest part of this assignment was Ali. The more difficult it is, the more glorious it is, I want to write a blood book, which of you will share the joys and sorrows with me?

  果平说，哈！我只是在小说和电影里才看到血书什么的，没想到，真有人打算这么做！太棒了，我的血和你流在一起！

  Go back quickly. I said to Xiaoru.这儿的雨和内地的雨不一样。我家乡的雨，很细很小，牛毛一般。你要是不留意，好像觉不出来似的。但它的后劲儿很大，你在雨中走一会儿，全身的衣服都会湿透，阴冷会一直沁到骨头缝里。这儿，雨来得很猛，可是这一颗雨滴和那一颗雨滴之间，隔得很远，简直能跑一只骆驼呢！小如说。

  The company commander asked us to continue to report the numbers. He changed his approach slightly, no longer dividing us into groups of one or two, but asked everyone to report one by one, and then ordered the people to take a big step forward, like the day chosen when the countryside rushed to the market. At this time, there were significantly fewer people stepping forward, as if the crops had passed the seedlings, and some seedlings were eaten by voles, and there was only one person far away.人们越发莫名其妙，连长当然不做任何解释。他按照自己的预定方针，继续发布命令，让站在队伍最前列的那排人，按一定规律报数，然后命令逢到某个特定号码的人向前迈步……几番操作下来，剩下的人越来越少，大家的好奇心也越来越强烈了。

  People became more and more inexplicable, and the company commander certainly did not give any explanation. According to his predetermined policy, he continued to issue orders, asking the people standing at the front of the line to report the number according to a certain rule, and then ordered the person who met a certain number to step forward...... After several operations, there were fewer and fewer people left, and everyone's curiosity became stronger and stronger.现在，站在最前列的只有五个女孩子了。我很想看看都是谁，可是不行。连长的目光像探照灯一样盯着我们，只要你稍微拧一下脖子，立刻就会被他发现。

  Now, there are only five girls at the forefront. I really wanted to see who it was, but I couldn't. The company commander's eyes are staring at us like a searchlight, and as long as you twist your neck a little, you will be immediately discovered by him.连长走到我们面前，对着我们五个人，也对着操场上所有的女兵说，现在我宣布，站在最前列的这五名，光荣地被选为第一批奔赴西藏阿里的女战士。这是她们的光荣，也是我们所有人的荣耀。让我们以热烈的掌声，欢送她们走上共和国最高的国土……

  The company commander walked up to us, said to the five of us, and to all the female soldiers on the playground, and now I announce that the five who stand at the forefront have been honorably selected as the first female soldiers to go to Ali, Tibet. This is their glory and the glory of all of us. Let us send them off to the highest land of the Republic with warm applause......掌声暴风雨般地响起来，缠绕我们许久的问号，就被连长用这样宿命的方式，三下五除二地解决了。

  Applause rang out like a storm, and the question mark that had entangled us for a long time was solved by the company commander in such a fateful way.连长接着用毫无感情色彩的语调，念出其余人的分配名单，对谁都是一视同仁。

  The company commander then read out the allocation list of the rest of the people in an emotionless tone, treating everyone equally.直到这时，我才有胆量偷偷斜了旁边一眼，哈！果平、小如、河莲都和我并排站着，还有一个瘦弱的小姑娘，站在队伍的尾巴上，她叫苏鹿鹿。

  Only then did I have the courage to secretly glance to the side, ha! Guo Ping, Xiao Ru, and He Lian were all standing side by side with me, and there was a thin little girl standing at the tail of the team, her name was Su Lulu.和朋友们在一起的狂喜，冲散了我不愿当卫生员的愁云。况且，我也想通了，即使我不被分配到西藏去，也很难保证能当上海燕。听天由命吧，也许我的命里注定，必须要在工作中见到许多呻吟的人。不管怎么说，就算上班的时候愁眉苦脸，下班以后可以和伙伴们开心一乐，也该知足啊。

  The ecstasy of being with my friends dispelled my sorrow that I didn't want to be a hygienist. Moreover, I also figured out that even if I was not assigned to Tibet, it would be difficult to guarantee that I would be a petrel. Leave it to fate, maybe it is destined that I must see many moaning people at work. In any case, even if you are sad when you go to work, you can have fun with your friends after get off work, and you should be content.解散以后，大家立刻把我们几个围起来，充满好奇之情，好像此刻的我们已和大家有了显著的不同。

  After the disbandment, everyone immediately surrounded a few of us, full of curiosity, as if we were significantly different from everyone at this moment.我大叫，不要这样对我们虎视眈眈好不好？好像我们不是要到阿里去，是从阿里已经绕回一圈似的。

  I yelled, don't look at us like this, okay? It seems that we are not going to Ali, but have already circled back from Ali.大家就笑起来说，毕竟你们是要到那么遥远的一个地方，仿佛去另一个星球。到了那里，千万记得要给我们写信啊。

  Everyone laughed and said, after all, you are going to such a distant place, as if you are going to another planet. When you get there, remember to write to us.我说，你们那么多人，我怎么写得过来？等我以后当了作家，写一本书，你们大家传着看吧。

  I said, how can I write it with so many of you? When I become a writer and write a book, you can read it.大家就笑个不停，说这个家伙多么会吹牛啊。

  Everyone laughed non-stop, saying how good this guy was at bragging.连长走过来，大家的笑声立刻消失了，等着听他的指示。连长不看大家，单对我们五个说，现在，你们已经是西藏阿里边防部队医院的战士了，我们已经用电报通知了那里，那边工作很忙，要求你们立即上山。

  The company commander came over, and everyone's laughter immediately disappeared, waiting to listen to his instructions. The company commander didn't look at everyone, and only said to the five of us, now, you are already soldiers from the Ali Border Guard Hospital in Tibet, and we have notified there by telegram, and the work over there is very busy, and I ask you to go up the mountain immediately.我小声嘟囔了一声，为什么不用电话呢，那可比电报要快得多啊。

  I muttered softly, why not use a phone, it's much faster than a telegram.连长看着我，说，那里不通电话。我们只能用最简练的词句，把最多的内容用无线电波传递上去。

  The company commander looked at me and said, there is no phone there. We can only use the most concise words and phrases to transmit the most content on the radio waves.大家都不由自主地吐了吐舌头。连长并不理睬我们的惊讶，也不看大家，只是对着我们五个人说，上山的路途艰难而遥远，你们要做好充分的思想准备。为了领导方便，你们要选出一个班长来。

  Everyone couldn't help but stick out their tongues. The company commander ignored our surprise and didn't look at everyone, but just said to the five of us that the road up the mountain is difficult and long, and you must be fully mentally prepared. For the convenience of the leader, you have to choose a squad leader.大家面面相觑。自当兵以来，凡事都是领导指定，今日为何民主起来？

  Everyone looked at each other. Since becoming a soldier, everything has been designated by the leader, why is it democratic today?河莲最先说出我们的心里话，选什么？连长看着谁合适，就让谁当呗！

  He Lian was the first to say what was in our hearts, what to choose? The company commander sees whoever is suitable, let him be whoever he wants!一向说一不二的连长破天荒地缓缓说道，从现在开始，我已不再是你们的连长，你们已经完成了新兵的训练课目，就要走上工作岗位。希望你们能够记住这一段岁月，它是你们军旅生涯的开端。

  The company commander, who has always been unambiguous, said slowly, from now on, I am no longer your company commander, you have completed the training courses of recruits, and you are about to go to work. I hope you can remember this period of time, it was the beginning of your military career.

  大家的鼻子就有些酸，感觉到分手就在眼前。想想连长虽说严厉、偏心，但也有可敬可爱的地方。比如这一次分配，就并没有利用自己手中的权力做什么特意安排。他宁可用一种概率的方法来决定大家的命运。

  Everyone's nose was a little sore, and they felt that the breakup was just around the corner. Think about it, although the company commander is strict and partial, he also has respectable and lovely things. For example, this time he did not use the power in his hands to make any special arrangements. He would rather use a probabilistic method to decide everyone's fate.我们伤感了一会儿，才发觉班长的人选问题并没有随着心情的变化而解决。小如最先打破沉寂，说，我看就选小毕吧。

  We were sad for a while before we realized that the problem of the squad leader was not solved with the change of mood. Xiaoru was the first to break the silence and said, I think I will choose Xiao Bi.我吓得大喊，不同意！不同意！

  I was so scared that I cried out that I didn't agree! Disagree!大家齐刷刷地问我，为什么？

  Everyone asked me in unison, why?我说，谁不知道班长是军队里最小的官啊，当不当的，实在也说明不了是否进步。可吃苦在前，享受在后，身先士卒是第一位的。我这个人，从骨子里就比较怕苦怕累，要是有别人给我做了榜样，带领着我向前，基本上还算一个服从命令的兵。要是想让我冲锋在前地起到某种表率作用，实事求是地说，我做不到。

  I said, who doesn't know that the squad leader is the smallest officer in the army, and if he is not appropriate, it really can't tell whether he has progressed. But hardship is first, enjoyment is later, and taking the lead is the first. If someone else sets an example for me and leads me forward, I am basically a soldier who obeys orders. If I want to charge forward and play some kind of role model, to be honest, I can't do it.大伙看我这副不堪重任的样子，也就不勉强我。但总得有个班长啊，连长等得不耐烦了，直搓手掌。我说，我提个人，你们可不能说我有私心。好不好？

  Everyone saw that I was overwhelmed, so they didn't force me. But there must be a squad leader, the company commander waited impatiently and rubbed his palms. I said, I mention someone, you can't say that I am selfish. Good no good?

  大家说，真啰唆。没人议论你，快提吧。

  Everyone said, it's really instigative. No one is talking about you, bring it up.我说，刚才小如提名我当班长，现在我再提她，好像有点互相吹捧的意思。我可真的是出于公心地认为，小如是班长的合适人选。她温柔细心，组织纪律性强，关心爱护同志，还爱给别人洗衣服……

  I said, just now Xiaoru nominated me as the class president, and now I mention her again, as if I am praising each other. I really think out of public conscience that Xiaoru is the right candidate for the squad leader. She is gentle and careful, has strong organizational discipline, cares for her comrades, and loves to wash clothes for others......大家笑起来，说同意同意，就小如啦！

  Everyone laughed and said they agreed, just like that!连长大手一挥，宣布说，奔赴西藏阿里的女兵班现在组建完成，还是由小毕担任临时班长。

  The company commander waved his hand and announced that the female soldier squad going to Ali, Tibet, had now been formed, and Xiao Bi would serve as the temporary squad leader.走，到阿里去！我们五个女孩手拉起手。

  Let's go, go to Ali! The five of us girls held hands.
