  上山了。

  Up the mountain.我们五个——小如、果平、河莲、鹿鹿和我，有幸成为西藏阿里的第一批女兵，开始向雪山之巅进发。

  The five of us—Xiaoru, Guoping, Helian, Lulu, and me—were honored to be the first female soldiers of Ali in Tibet and began to march to the top of the snow-capped mountains.一个炎热的早晨，我们坐上了从平原到西藏去的军用大卡车。大车厢里载了许多麻袋，内装大米。坐在麻袋上，把脚像芭蕾舞演员一般竖起，插进麻袋的缝隙。汽车摇摇晃晃地在布满石子的路上向山上爬，像一只笨拙的绿毛龟。

  One hot morning, we got into a large military truck from the plains to Tibet. The large carriage was loaded with many sacks of rice. Sit on the sack, stand up like a ballerina, and insert it into the gap of the sack. The car staggered up the hill on the gravel road, like a clumsy green turtle.人人脑袋上方，笼罩着一片绿色。不是天的颜色，是汽车篷布笼罩的效果。我们大呼憋死了，要求同行的老兵批准揭开这顶盖子，看看外面的风景。

  Above everyone's head, there is a green shroud. It's not the color of the sky, it's the effect of the car tarpaulin. We screamed and suffocated, asking the veterans who were traveling with us to approve the lifting of the lid and see the scenery outside.透过篷布上的窟窿，你们尽管看，看个够。针尖大的窟窿能透过斗大的风。没听人说吗？眼皮是世界上最大的物件，你只要睁着眼，有什么看不到的？同行的老兵懒洋洋地说。他是下山治病的，听说病还没治好，工作紧张，要他上山，所以，他闷闷不乐，一副苦大仇深的样子。新兵连长把我们几个女兵交给他，委托照应，他好像不堪重负的毛驴，又被人强压了一捆柴火，愤愤地不爱理人。

  Through the hole in the tarpaulin, you can see enough. The hole with the tip of the needle can pass through the big wind. Didn't you hear from anyone? The eyelids are the largest object in the world, as long as you open your eyes, what can't you see? The veteran said lazily. He went down the mountain to treat his illness, and when he heard that the disease had not been cured, he was intense at work, so he was sullen and looked bitter and hateful. The recruit company commander handed over a few of our female soldiers to him and entrusted them with care, he was like an overwhelmed donkey, and he was forced to be pressed with a bundle of firewood, and he was indignantly ignoring people.我们只好像预备行窃的小偷一样，每人揪住篷布上的一个小孔，尽力向外张望。汽车颠簸着，大米麻袋不停地上下蹿动，好像一尊浑身长着硬颗粒的庞然大物，不甘心驮人，一有机会就想把我们从它背上掀下来。我被晃得肠胃错位，说，一会儿你们谁帮我一下？我打算改造一下座位，用几袋大米摞成沙发模样，虽说硌屁股，肯定比现在舒服得多。

  We were like thieves preparing to steal, each of us grabbed a small hole in the tarpaulin and tried our best to look out. The car was bumpy, and the rice sacks kept jumping up and down, like a behemoth covered in hard particles, unwilling to carry people, and wanted to lift us off its back at the first opportunity. I was shaken so that my stomach was misaligned, and I said, who of you will help me for a while? I plan to remodel the seat and use a few bags of rice to make it look like a sofa, although the buttocks are definitely much more comfortable than now.同病相怜的女兵们精神一振，都说我主意不错。

  The female soldiers who were sick and sympathetic to each other cheered up and said that I had a good idea.胡说！老兵斥我。

  Nonsense! The veteran reprimanded me.怎么啦？我不服气。

  What's wrong? I am not convinced.你找死啊！上山的路，奇险无比，咱是摸着阎王鼻子走钢丝，你还想舒服？到时候一个急转弯，你的麻袋沙发砸下来，屁股倒是不硌了，整个人成了米粉肉！老兵慢吞吞地说着刻毒的话。

  You are looking for death! The road up the mountain is extremely dangerous, we are walking on a tightrope with the nose of the King of Hell, do you still want to be comfortable? When the time comes, a sharp turn, your sack sofa will smash down, your butt will not be flat, and the whole person will become rice noodles! The veteran slowly said harsh words.想想也是。我讨了个没趣，只得乖乖地坐着重新张望。车外是一片青翠的原野，有薄荷样的清凉味道弥漫在裹着黄沙的空气中。

  Think about it. I was bored, so I had to sit obediently and look again. Outside the car is a verdant field, with a cool mint-like smell filling the air wrapped in yellow sand.要走几天，才能到目的地啊？有人问。

  How many days do you have to walk to reach your destination? Someone asked.大家都默不作声，车里能回答这个问题的，只有一个人。可是此刻他眯缝着眼，好像已经昏过去了。

  Everyone was silent, and there was only one person in the car who could answer this question. But at this moment he squinted his eyes, as if he had passed out.要是没什么意外的话，也就是说，不翻车，不遇上暴风雪，司机不得急病，车子不抛锚……六天。过了好久，当我们对获知答案基本绝望的时候，老兵瓮声瓮气地回答。

  If there is no accident, that is, if the car does not roll over, does not encounter a snowstorm, the driver must not be suddenly ill, and the car will not break down...... Six days. After a long time, when we were basically desperate to learn the answer, the veteran replied angrily.天哪，要走那么远的路！那还不到外国啦？要是能快点就好了，到了我就能给我妈妈写信了。鹿鹿说。她是我们之中最小的，肯定想家了。

  Oh my God, it's been such a long journey! That's not a foreign country? If only I could hurry, I would be able to write to my mother when I arrived. Lulu said. She is the youngest of us and must be homesick.老兵突然睁开眼，说，车走得那么快，有什么好的？还是慢点好，抓紧时间，好好看看，好好闻闻吧。他说得很认真，像是在传授什么秘诀。

  The veteran suddenly opened his eyes and said, what's so good about the car going so fast? It's better to slow down, hurry up, take a good look, and smell it. He spoke seriously, as if he was teaching some secret.我们四处乱瞧，耸动鼻子，但除了山峦和扑面的尘土以外，没发现什么特别的好味道。只好请教他，你让我们看什么闻什么呢？

  We looked around and shook our noses, but we found nothing particularly good except for the mountains and the dust that hit our faces. I had to ask him, what do you let us see and smell?看地。闻气。老兵很简略地说。

  Look at the ground. Smell the breath. The veteran said briefly.地有什么好看的呢？每个人都在地上生活了十几年，地就像我们的身体，早就熟透了。现在我们巴望的是早早到陌生的高原上去。至于空气，不就是一种无色无味风一样流动的东西吗？它无时无刻不在陪伴着我们，鼻子里嘴巴里胸膛中都充满了它，从我们一出生就与之相伴了。

  What is so beautiful about the land? Everyone has lived on the earth for more than ten years, and the earth is like our body, which has long been ripe. Now what we hope to do is to go to the unfamiliar plateau early. As for air, isn't it just a colorless and odorless thing that flows like wind? It is with us all the time, the nose, mouth, chest are full of it, and it has been with us since we were born.不得要领，只得继续请教傲慢的老兵。老兵这一回很健谈，好像一直在等着教育我们的机会，马上就要开始爬山了，当然，是汽车在爬，不是我们爬。但是都一样，你会觉得路在我们面前立起来，汽车像个铁猴子攀登。爬得高了，氧气就慢慢稀薄了，好像空气和冰雪有不共戴天的仇恨，雪多的地方，空气就越来越少。

  I didn't get to the point, so I had to continue to consult the arrogant veterans. The veteran was very talkative this time, as if he had been waiting for the opportunity to educate us, and was about to start climbing the mountain, of course, it was the car that was climbing, not us. But all the same, you will feel that the road stands in front of us, and the car climbs like an iron monkey. As you climb higher, the oxygen slowly becomes thinner, as if there is a hatred between air and ice and snow, and where there is more snow, the air becomes less and less.空气少了，是一种什么滋味呢？是不是就像感冒时，鼻子里堵满了鼻涕的感觉？大家纷纷议论。

  What does it feel like to have less air? Is it like the feeling of a stuffy nose when you have a cold? Everyone talked about it.不是那么回事。比起来，感冒就太舒服了。缺氧的感觉，就像有人掐住你的脖子，然后用鞭子赶着你在玻璃罩子里跑。你拼命张大了嘴呼吸，可是肺永远是空的……老兵若有所思地说。

  Not so so. In comparison, colds are too comfortable. The feeling of lack of oxygen, like someone grabbing your neck and driving you through the glass cover with a whip. You desperately open your mouth wide to breathe, but your lungs are always empty...... The veteran said thoughtfully.这真是太可怕了。我们一个个煞白着脸，好像在听一个从地狱里回来的人讲旅游经历。

  It's terrible. We all looked pale, as if we were listening to a person returning from hell talk about his travel experience.老兵是个很奇怪的人，当我们满不在乎的时候，他就吓唬我们。我们真的害怕了，他又变得大大咧咧。

  The veteran is a very strange person, and when we are full of care, he scares us. We were really scared, and he became carefree again.我告诉你们一个治缺氧的好办法吧，百治百灵的……他很神秘地说。

  Let me tell you a good way to treat hypoxia, Baizhi Bailing's ...... He said mysteriously.啊，我知道的。一定是吸氧气了。鹿鹿的家里有从医的根底，抢先说道。

  Ah, I know. It must be oxygen. Lulu's family has a foundation in medicine, and he said first.老兵有些泄气，但他很快恢复了指点江山的气概，说，你那是洋法子。荒山野岭的，到哪儿去找氧气筒？我说的是土方子，偏方治大病，你们知不知道？

  The veteran was a little discouraged, but he quickly regained his spirit of pointing out the country and said, you are a foreign method. In the barren mountains and wilderness, where can I find an oxygen tank? I'm talking about earthworks, folk remedies to treat serious diseases, do you know?我们怕他一生气，就不讲了，忙狠狠地瞪小鹿，齐声说，知道知道，偏方治大病。

  We were afraid that he would be angry, so we stopped talking, and hurriedly glared at the deer fiercely, saying in unison, knowing, knowing, folk remedies to cure serious diseases.老兵这才告诉我们，治缺氧最好的办法是——用背包带，喏，就是你们捆行李的那种，把自己的头紧紧地缠起来。记住，一定要用那根宽带子，窄的不管事。

  Only then did the veteran tell us that the best way to treat hypoxia is to use a backpack strap, the kind you tie up your luggage with, and wrap your head tightly. Remember, be sure to use that wide band, narrow and nothing.我们目瞪口呆，果平第一个战战兢兢地说，那还不得把人勒死了？

  We were stunned, and Guo Ping was the first to say tremblingly, then you can't strangle people to death?老兵大不耐烦，说，我让你勒的是太阳穴那个位置，又没让你勒脖子，怎么就会死了！

  The veteran was impatient and said, I asked you to strangle the temple, and I didn't let you strangle my neck, how could I die!大家想想也是，河莲说，是不是勒成日本浪人那副模样？

  Everyone think about it, Ha Lian said, is it like a Japanese ronin?老兵说，日本浪人什么样，我没见过。反正这个法子治好了许多缺氧头痛的兵，信不信由你们。

  The veteran said, I have never seen what Japanese ronin looks like. Anyway, this method has cured many soldiers with hypoxic headaches, believe it or not.我们赶快说，信信！

  Let's say quickly, believe in it!说话间，汽车马达发出很怪异的声响，好像是发动机得了肺炎，吭哧吭哧直咳嗽。老兵警觉地说，这就是开始爬达坂了。平原已经一去不复返。

  As he spoke, the car motor made a very strange sound, as if the engine had pneumonia, and he coughed. The veteran said alertly, this is the beginning of climbing the slope. The plains are gone.我们从墨绿色的汽车篷布缝隙，注视着越退越远的平原，意识到一种巨大的变化就要出现了。

  We looked at the plain that was receding further and further away from the gaps in the dark green car tarpaulin, and realized that a great change was about to appear.老兵谆谆告诫我们，今天到了兵站的时候，你们一定不可以跳下车就撒腿跑。因为身体根本不适应高原，你一剧烈活动，心脏的负担突然加重，它受不了，就罢工了。你就永远睡在第一个兵站了。

  The veteran earnestly warned us that when you arrive at the military station today, you must not jump out of the car and run away. Because the body is not adapted to the plateau at all, when you are vigorous, the burden on the heart suddenly increases, it can't stand it, and it strikes. You will sleep forever in the first military station.尽管老兵的口气很平稳，我们还是吓得不敢大口喘气。河莲似乎连笑也很节省气力，再不像往日那样哈哈个不停，只是小小地抿着嘴，好像旧时代的小姐。她不放心地说，如果背包带勒头不管事，怎么办呢？老兵很干脆地说，那就成烈士呗。阿里这地方就这点好，不管你是因为什么原因死的，只要牺牲在高原，就算是正经八百的烈士。说起来也有道理，要不是保家卫国，谁到这天边似的地方来呢。

  Despite the veteran's steady tone, we were too scared to take a deep breath. He Lian seemed to be very energy-saving even when she smiled, and she didn't stop laughing like before, she just pursed her lips slightly, like a young lady from the old era. She said uneasily, what if the backpack strap doesn't matter? The veteran said simply, then he will become a martyr. Ali is this good place, no matter what reason you died, as long as you died on the plateau, even if you are a serious martyr. It makes sense, if it weren't for defending the country, who would come to this place like the edge of the sky.我们都不想小小的年纪就成为烈士，因此，就很注意保养自己，大家话也不敢多说，软软地靠在大米袋子上，生怕一个微小的举动，消耗掉体内宝贵的氧气，悲惨地成了第一个用背包带勒头的人。

  None of us want to become martyrs at a young age, so we pay great attention to taking care of ourselves, and we don't dare to say much, leaning softly on the rice bag, for fear that a small gesture will consume the precious oxygen in the body, and tragically become the first person to strangle our heads with a backpack.缺氧有一种轻度的麻醉作用，像喝了酒似的，晕晕乎乎。初次体验这种感觉的我们，以为它是晕车呢，并不在意。只是原来观看景色的眼皮，好像被糊了一层透明胶纸，你什么都可以看到，却觉得遥远而虚假。刚开始是冷漠地眯起眼帘，后来干脆昏昏欲睡，仿佛被人施了武林中的“麻骨松筋散”，大脑一片空白。

  Hypoxia has a mild anesthetic effect, like drinking alcohol, dizzy. When we first experienced this feeling, we thought it was motion sickness and didn't care. It's just that the eyelids of the original view seem to be pasted with a layer of transparent tape, and you can see everything, but it feels distant and false. At first, he squinted his eyes indifferently, but then he simply became drowsy, as if he had been given the "numb bones and loose tendons" in the martial arts, and his brain was blank.

  到啦到啦！老兵喊起来。

  It's here, it's here! The veteran shouted.我们一惊，今天怎么过得这么快？老兵说，第一天登山的路，料到大伙都不习惯，特地安排得短些。以后甭想这么舒服了，晓行夜宿，早上摸着星星出兵站，晚上揣着月亮进兵站。对了，这还是在车子不闹脾气的好运气下。要是出了故障，另当别论，也许在冰达坂上蹲上个三天两宿，也正常。

  We were surprised, why did today pass so quickly? The veteran said that on the first day, he expected that everyone would not be used to it, so he specially arranged it shorter. In the future, I don't want to be so comfortable, I will stay at night, feel the stars in the morning and enter the military station with the moon in the evening. By the way, this is still under the good luck of not losing a temper in the car. If something goes wrong, it's another matter, maybe squatting on Bingda Slope for three days and two nights is normal.老兵有个爱好，特别喜欢说不吉利的话，好像能从中感到极大的乐趣。

  Veterans have a hobby, especially like to say unlucky things, as if they can feel great fun from it.河莲撇撇嘴。那没说出来的话，我们都听到了——吓唬人呗！

  He Lian curled her lips. We all heard the unspoken words - scary!老兵不傻，看出了我们的不以为然。他撩开篷布，一指兵站后面的小山，说，看到了吗？

  The veterans are not stupid and see that we are not impressed. He lifted the tarpaulin, pointed to the hill behind the military station, and said, do you see it?兵站这个名字，很有点烽烟缭绕的边塞感，想象中该是庞大的屯兵之地，发生过“增兵减灶”之类的惊险故事。哪怕是军棋上的兵站，也有些不凡。谁一躲进去，就可避免炸弹的袭击。军长、司令也常常在内休养生息。可眼前的这几间低矮的小平房，冒着袅袅的炊烟，和普通的民居差不多，实在让人难以生出英武之感。至于兵站后面的小山，要不是老兵特意提示，根本就没人注意。一路上，这种貌不惊人的山梁，大约经过了几万座。

  The name of the military station is a bit of a sense of border fortification, and it should be a huge garrison in the imagination, and there have been thrilling stories such as "increasing troops and reducing stoves". Even the military station on military chess is a bit extraordinary. Whoever hides in will avoid the bomb attack. Commanders and commanders also often recuperate inside. But the few low bungalows in front of me, with the smoke of cooking, are similar to ordinary houses, which is really difficult to give birth to a sense of heroism. As for the hill behind the military station, if it weren't for the veteran's special reminder, no one would have paid attention at all. Along the way, this kind of unamazing-looking mountain beam passed by about tens of thousands.看到了。大家应付老兵说。

  See. Everyone said to the veteran.看到什么啦？老兵穷追不舍，好像诲人不倦的老师，课堂上提问没完成作业的差生。

  What do you see? Veterans chase relentlessly, like tireless teachers, poor students who ask questions in class and have not completed their homework.看到一座普普通通的山。我们懒懒地答道。

  See an ordinary mountain. We replied lazily.谁让你们看山了？我让你们看的是山上的东西。老兵有些火了，脸皱得像汽车轮胎。

  Who let you see the mountain? I let you see things on the mountain. The veteran was a little angry, his face wrinkled like a car tire.山上还有东西？我们很吃惊，幸好我们都是刚验过身体的新兵，视力绝对是雏鹰般敏锐，很快就看到了小山坡上的确有一些隆起的小土包，好像还有凋零的白花。

  Is there something else on the mountain? We were surprised, fortunately we were all recruits who had just undergone physical examinations, and our eyesight was definitely as sharp as a young eagle, and we soon saw that there were indeed some raised small earthen bags on the hillside, as if there were withered white flowers.知道那是什么东西吗？坟。是一些像你们一样年轻、第一次上山的兵，没经验，觉得高原也没有什么了不起的，天是一样地蓝，水是一样地清。他们不听招呼，低估了高原的杀伤力。有人因为憋了一泡尿，下了车就跑，啪，摔倒了，再也没起来，永远留在高原上了。从今天开始，你们在上山的每一个兵站后面，都会看到一片铺满白雪的墓地。今天才是高原的边角，雪山的第一级台阶。假如你们要想在高原上活下去，必须对高原毕恭毕敬。你瞧不起它，它就让你拿命来向它赔不是。记住了吗？老兵这一席话，说得我们开始对他佩服得五体投地。

  Do you know what that thing is? Grave. They are some soldiers who are young like you and are going up the mountain for the first time, inexperienced, and feel that there is nothing great about the plateau, the sky is the same blue, and the water is the same clear. They did not listen to greetings and underestimated the lethality of the plateau. Some people ran out of the car because they held back their urine, snapped, fell, never got up again, and stayed on the plateau forever. Starting today, behind every military station you go up the mountain, you will see a cemetery covered with snow. Today is the corner of the plateau, the first step of the snow-capped mountains. If you want to live on the plateau, you must be respectful to the plateau. If you look down on it, it will let you take your life to compensate it. Remember? The veteran's words made us admire him so much that we began to admire him.老兵率先下了车，铁拐李似的，走得极慢。我们按照他的样子，像旧社会的小脚女人，一步迈不了三寸。

  The veteran got out of the car first, like an iron crutch, walking extremely slowly. We follow his image, like a woman with small feet in the old society, who can't take a step three inches.西部夜幕落得晚，这天行程也短，此刻太阳在很高的山上悬挂着，像一只金羽毛的火鸟，灿烂而冷漠。果平说，啊，我对高原的第一个感觉是寂静，第二个感觉是寒冷，第三个感觉是空旷，第四个感觉是……

  The night in the west fell late, and the itinerary was short, and at this moment the sun hung on the high mountains, like a firebird with golden feathers, brilliant and indifferent. Guo Ping said, ah, my first feeling of the plateau is silence, the second feeling is cold, the third feeling is emptiness, and the fourth feeling is ......老兵不屑地说，这里才三千多米，你就那么多的感觉。要是到了阿里，足有六千多米，你还不得弄个十来八条的感觉，累不累啊？

  The veteran said disdainfully, it's only more than 3,000 meters here, and you have so many feelings. If you arrive in Ali, there are more than 6,000 meters, you still have to make a feeling of ten or eight, are you tired?果平仿佛被人塞了一脖子雪，立时没了说话的情绪。我们慢慢走到食堂，默不作声地开始吃饭。主食是大米饭，菜肴因为一下来了这么多人，兵站措手不及，不及准备，就倒了半盆酱油，说用这个拌米饭，很好吃的。

  Guo Ping seemed to have been stuffed with snow in his neck, and immediately lost the mood to speak. We slowly walked to the cafeteria and started eating in silence. The staple food is rice, and the dish was caught off guard by so many people at once, and before they could prepare, they poured half a pot of soy sauce, saying that it was delicious to mix with rice.我心说，这玩意儿黑不溜秋咸不啦唧的，倒在米饭里，能咽得下去吗？

  I thought to myself, this thing is black and salty, can it be swallowed when poured into rice?嘿！真奇怪，舌头一上了高原，好像也发生了奇妙的变化，竟然完全分辨不出食物的味道。米饭吃到嘴里，像一粒粒长着刺的锯末。酱油汁把米饭渗透到发红发黑的地步，也不觉咸，好像搅拌进去的是一种无味的特殊颜料。不过，胃比舌头可捣蛋多了，刚吃第一口，就想吐。

  Hey! It's really strange, as soon as the tongue goes up to the plateau, it seems to have undergone a wonderful change, and it can't distinguish the taste of food at all. When the rice is eaten in the mouth, it is like sawdust with thorns. The soy sauce sauce penetrates the rice to the point of turning red and black, and it doesn't feel salty, as if it is stirred in a special odorless pigment. However, the stomach is much more tricky than the tongue, and as soon as I take the first bite, I want to vomit.看我们眉头紧锁不动筷子，老兵大口咽着饭说，知道了吧，这就是高原的厉害了。它会变魔术。从现在开始，你们要放弃在平原上的许多怪毛病。吃东西，不是为了舌头，而是为了肚子，为了脑袋，为了胳膊腿……一句话，为了能在高原上好好地活下去，你必须得吃。别理舌头那个家伙，听它的，你什么也不想吃。更别理胃那个软溜溜的没骨气的玩意儿，它想吐，你愣吃，它也没法，吃进去就是胜利。

  Seeing that we frowned and couldn't move our chopsticks, the veteran swallowed his food and said, you know, this is the power of the plateau. It works magic. From now on, you will give up the many strange problems of the plains. Eat not for the tongue, but for the stomach, for the head, for the arms and legs...... In a word, in order to survive well on the plateau, you have to eat. Ignore the tongue guy, listen to it, you don't want to eat anything. Don't pay attention to the soft and spineless thing in the stomach, it wants to vomit, you are stunned to eat, it can't eat, eating it is victory.我们像吃毒药似的，每人填了半碗饭。甭管老兵怎么用眼光督战，还是义无反顾地撤离饭桌，到各自房间睡觉。躺进冷硬如铁的被子时，我最后一个动作是看了看宽背包带放在哪儿。

  We were like poison, each filling half a bowl of rice. Regardless of how the veterans supervised the battle with their eyes, they still evacuated the dinner table and went to sleep in their respective rooms without hesitation. When I lay down on the quilt, which was as cold as iron, my last action was to look at where the wide backpack strap was placed.咳，也不知道明天早上，我还会不会在阳光下醒来？要是就这样“烈士”了，倒也不算太难受。我想着，很快睡着了。

  Cough, I don't know if I will wake up in the sun tomorrow morning? If he is a "martyr" like this, it is not too uncomfortable. I thought about it and fell asleep quickly.第二天起来的时候，没什么独特的倒霉感觉，我甚至都有点失望了，高原不过如此。

  When I got up the next day, there was no unique feeling of bad luck, and I was even a little disappointed, just like the plateau.但很快，我就知道自己小瞧了高原。它用大智若愚的绵长内力，慢慢地持久地消耗着我们，当到达海拔六千米的界山达坂时，猛地一变脸，发动了全面的攻击。

  But soon, I knew I underestimated the plateau. It used its long internal force to consume us slowly and persistently, and when it reached Jieshan Daban at an altitude of 6,000 meters, it suddenly changed its face and launched an all-out attack.胸膛里吸进的好像不再是空气，而是一种黏糊糊的金属，沉重而压抑。肋骨好像变成了八脚章鱼，紧紧地箍着肺，让它没法像平日那般自由扩张。脑袋里装满了打火石，摇一下就金星乱冒。眼珠子胀得难受，恨不能把它抠出来，用冰凉的雪水擦擦四周，再安回狭小的眼眶。每个人都嘴唇青紫，好像刚刚吃完玫瑰香葡萄，葡萄皮没吐干净。

  It seemed that what was sucked in in the chest was no longer air, but a slimy metal, heavy and oppressive. The ribs seemed to have turned into an eight-legged octopus, tightly clamping the lungs, preventing it from expanding freely as usual. The head is full of flint, and when you shake it, Venus will appear. The eyeballs were swollen uncomfortably, and I wished I could pick it out, wipe it around with cold snow water, and then put it back into the narrow eye sockets. Everyone's lips were blue, as if they had just eaten rose grapes, and the grape skin was not spit out.

  恰好这时，由于海拔太高，气压太低，汽车也犯了高原病，水箱开锅了，呼呼直冒热气，像个火车头。司机只好停车，到远处去背雪，赶快给发高烧的汽车降温，让它歇息一会儿才可继续赶路。

  At this time, due to the high altitude and low air pressure, the car also suffered from altitude sickness, and the water tank turned on and steamed up, like a locomotive. The driver had to stop and go to a distant place to carry the snow, quickly cool down the car with a high fever, and let it rest for a while before continuing on the road.我们像些八十岁的老婆婆，颤颤巍巍地爬下车。虽然一上一下又要消耗不少体力，喘似多年的老气管炎病人，我们还是要站在雪地上透透风。

  We are like eighty-year-old women, trembling and climbing out of the car. Although it takes a lot of physical strength to go up and down, and we still have to stand on the snow to ventilate the air.

  无垠的雪原环绕着我们。五个女孩互相搀扶着，站在巨大的高原中央，惊讶它无比的美丽和壮观。天蓝得让人误以为是深不可测的海底，一朵白云像沉睡千年的珊瑚礁，凝然不动地沉没在空中，喜马拉雅鹰像热带鱼一般翩翩而过，黑翅掀起的气流，使山影像浸在水里的绸缎般抖动不止。陡峭的山峰戴着白雪的桂冠，安然地屹立着，好像在打坐，思索着人世间的难题。在偏戴着的帽子顶端，镶着钻石般的冰川，阳光照耀下，折射出的无数根银线，几乎要把人的双眼刺瞎。精灵般的野马，用花瓣一样的蹄子，把山石敲打出紫色的火星，似岚气顺着山脊蜿蜒攀升，只把一条乱甩的尾巴，留在跟踪它的眼光里……

  Endless snowfields surround us. Five girls supported each other and stood in the middle of the huge plateau, amazed at its incomparable beauty and spectacle. The sky is so blue that people mistake it for an unfathomable seabed, a white cloud sinks into the air like a coral reef that has been dormant for thousands of years, the Himalayan eagle flutters like a tropical fish, and the air currents raised by the black wings make the mountain image shake like satin immersed in water. The steep peaks stand peacefully with snow-white laurel crowns, as if meditating, thinking about the problems of the world. At the top of the hat he was wearing, there was a diamond-like glacier, and under the sunlight, countless silver threads were refracted, almost blinding people's eyes. The elf-like wild horse, with its petal-like hooves, knocked the rocks out of purple sparks, and climbed up along the ridge like mist, leaving only a flicking tail in the eyes that followed it......我们呆呆地看着，缺氧使我们变傻，恍惚间觉得自己到了月亮背面，虽然极端荒凉，但美得令人不可思议。

  We watched blankly, the lack of oxygen made us stupid, and in a trance we felt that we were on the far side of the moon, which was extremely desolate but incredibly beautiful.果平掐掐自己的腮帮子，说，咦，我怎么不觉得疼？这是在梦里吧？

  Guo Ping pinched his cheeks and said, hey, why don't I feel pain? This is in a dream, right?河莲很有经验地说，因为太冷，你脸上的肉都变成木板了，所以感觉不出疼。你可换种方式，比如用牙咬咬舌头，狠一点，才会见效果。

  He Lian said with great experience that because it was too cold, the flesh on your face turned into a wooden board, so you didn't feel any pain. You can change the way, such as biting your tongue with your teeth, and be more ruthless, to see the effect.果平“呸”了她一口说，我宁愿相信自己是到了火星，也不愿把舌头咬出血。

  Guo Ping "bah" her and said, I would rather believe that I was on Mars than bite my tongue and bleed.河莲做出很无辜的样子说，我在脑子缺氧的情况下，还替你想出这样有效的办法，而你，真是不识好人心！

  He Lian made a very innocent look and said, I came up with such an effective method for you when my brain was deprived of oxygen, and you really don't know good people!什么事都怕说，本来每个人都头痛欲裂，以为别人没感觉，就不好意思呻吟叫唤。现在有人开了头，大家就同仇敌忾地叫起苦来。

  Everyone was afraid to say anything, and everyone had a headache, thinking that others didn't feel it, so they were embarrassed to moan. Now that someone has started, everyone will cry out in anger with the enemy.鹿鹿的头上早已绑了背包带，因为用力过大，额头勒得像个细腰葫芦，嘴巴被扯到耳朵根，好像她无时无刻不在嘲笑谁。她说，还偏方治大病呢，我的脑袋都捆成炸药包了，一点用也没有。

  Lu Lu had already tied a backpack strap to her head, because of the excessive force, her forehead was strangled like a slender-waisted gourd, and her mouth was pulled to the base of her ears, as if she was laughing at someone all the time. She said, "I also have folk remedies to treat serious diseases, and my head is tied up in explosive bags, which is of no use at all."果平说，真想把肺从肚子里掏出来，邮寄到平原去，让家里人给灌饱了氧气，再寄回来。

  Guo Ping said that he really wanted to take the lungs out of his stomach and mail them to the plains, so that his family could fill them with oxygen and send them back.河莲说，那可得挂号。要是万一寄丢了，你不就成了有心没肺的人了？

  He Lian said, you have to register. If you lose it, won't you become a heartless person?沉稳的小如说，我有一个设想……

  The calm Xiaoru said, I have an idea......大家就都很感兴趣地凑过来，要知道在这里冒出来的设想，很有可能是世界上最高级的。别的地方海拔哪儿有这么高！

  Everyone came over with great interest, knowing that the ideas that emerged here were likely to be the most advanced in the world. Where else is the altitude so high!小如说，我想制造一种氧气压缩片。小小的，白白的，很洁净的样子。含在嘴里，甜甜的，用舌头一抿，就有清凉的氧气从牙缝中源源不断地冒出来。呼吸到肺里，肺就像海上的风帆一般，张开来，像白蝴蝶一样，所有缺氧的难受就都消失了。

  Xiaoru said, I want to make an oxygen compression tablet. Small, white, very clean. Holding it in your mouth, sweet, with a spurse of your tongue, cool oxygen continues to come out from between your teeth. Breathing into the lungs, the lungs are like sails on the sea, opening up, like white butterflies, and all the discomfort of lack of oxygen disappears.我们听着，都无限神往地舔着嘴唇……可惜啊，嘴里翻腾的都是昨晚上的酱油泡米饭滋味，小如的氧气压缩片只是一个梦。

  We listened and licked our lips with infinite fascination...... It's a pity that all that is churning in my mouth is the taste of soy sauce-soaked rice from last night, and Xiaoru's oxygen compression tablets are just a dream.老兵不知道什么时候走了过来，听了我们的谈话，说，氧气可以压缩到瓶子里，关键时刻真的能救命呢。压成片，没听说过。就是能行，也不能做。太危险了。比如，你兜里装了许多氧气片，要是经过炉子旁边，会呼地一下烧起来，爆炸起火……

  The veteran didn't know when he came over, listened to our conversation, and said that oxygen can be compressed into a bottle, and it can really save lives at critical moments. Pressed into pieces, I have never heard of it. Even if it can be done, it cannot be done. It's too dangerous. For example, if you have a lot of oxygen sheets in your pocket, if you pass by the stove, it will burn and explode......我们掐着自己的太阳穴，困难地思索着老兵的话，在高原上，神经的传导也像蜗牛一般磨蹭。半晌之后，我们在心里强烈地反驳他：老兵，你也太没点想象力了。难道不能在氧气压缩片的外面，裹上一层保护用的红色糖衣，让它像巧克力豆一般美丽吗？揣着它穿过火焰的时候，至多是外皮有一点发黏，并不会影响使用。需要的时候含在嘴里，轻微的香甜过去之后，糖衣融化完，就一定会有带着薄荷味儿的氧气，像雨后森林的风一般，源源涌出。

  We pinched our temples and thought about the veteran's words with difficulty, and on the plateau, the conduction of nerves also rubbed like snails. After a while, we strongly refuted him in our hearts: Veteran, you are too unimaginative. Can't you wrap a protective red icing coating on the outside of the oxygen compression tablet to make it as beautiful as a chocolate chip? When carrying it through the flame, at most the skin is a little sticky, which will not affect the use. Hold it in your mouth when you need it, and after the slight sweetness passes, after the sugar coating melts, there will definitely be oxygen with the smell of mint, like the wind in the forest after the rain, gushing out.

未知

  07.黑白拂尘

  07. Black and white dust

  抵达阿里，我们受到了热烈的欢迎。头几天，领导上照顾我们，说是不安排工作，让安心休息以适应高原。我们住在医院最暖和的房子里，清闲得像一群公主。

  Arriving in Ali, we were warmly welcomed. In the first few days, the leader took care of us, saying that they would not arrange work and let them rest in peace to adapt to the plateau. We lived in the warmest house in the hospital, as leisurely as a group of princesses.一天早上，我走出房门，突然看到一个奇怪的庞然大物卧在雪地上，目光炯炯地面对着我。它眼若铜铃，身披长毛，威风凛凛地凝视远方，丝毫也不把寒冷放在心上，好像身下不是皑皑的白雪，而是温暖的丝绵。它一动也不动，仿佛一堵古老残破的褐色城墙。长而弯曲的犄角，散发着不可抗拒的威严。

  One morning, I walked out of the door and suddenly saw a strange behemoth lying on the snow, its eyes staring at me. Its eyes were like copper bells, covered in long hair, staring majestically into the distance, and it did not take the cold to heart at all, as if it was not white snow under it, but warm silk. It didn't move, like an old and dilapidated brown wall. The long, curved horns exude irresistible majesty.天哪！这是什么？我小声喊道。原本是想大叫的，只是突然想到若是一下子惊动了这猛兽，它还不得用舌头把我卷上天空，然后掉下来摔成一摊肉泥！声音就在喉咙里飞快地缩小，最后成了恐惧的嘟囔。

  Oh, my God! What is it? I whispered. I originally wanted to scream, but suddenly thought that if I alarmed this beast all of a sudden, it wouldn't have to roll me up into the sky with my tongue, and then fall down and fall into a pile of meat paste! The voice quickly shrank in his throat, and finally became a muttering of fear.声音虽弱，但受了惊吓的慌张劲儿还是成色十足。河莲一边用牙刷捅着腮帮子，一边吐着泡沫从屋里走出来说，一大清早，你瞎叫什么呀？好像撞见了鬼？

  Although the voice was weak, the frightened panic was still full of color. He Lian poked her cheeks with a toothbrush, while spitting out foam and walked out of the house and said, early in the morning, what are you talking about? It seems like you have bumped into a ghost?我战战兢兢地指给她看，说，比鬼可怕多了。鬼是轻飘飘的，可它比一百个鬼都有劲儿！

  I pointed to her tremblingly and said, "It's much more terrifying than a ghost." The ghost is light, but it is more energetic than a hundred ghosts!河莲顺着我的手指看去，眼光触到怪物，大叫了一声，哎哟，我的妈呀，肯定是牛魔王闯到咱们家来啦！说罢，吐着牙膏沫子逃向别处。

  He Lian followed my finger, her eyes touched the monster, and she shouted, oh, my mother, it must be the Bull Demon King who broke into our house! After saying that, he spat out toothpaste foam and fled elsewhere.本来我想河莲会给我壮个胆，没想到她临阵脱逃。我偷着瞅了一眼怪物，只见它的大眼睛很温驯地瞄着我们的小屋，并没有露出恼火的神色。过了半天，它沉重地眨了一下眼皮，就又悠然自得地注视远方去了。

  Originally, I thought He Lian would give me courage, but I didn't expect her to escape. I stole a glance at the monster, only to see its big eyes staring at our cabin very meekly, without showing an annoyed look. After a long time, it blinked its eyelids heavily, and then looked into the distance leisurely.我屏住气，悄悄地走近它。只见它浑身上下都是尺把长的棕黑毛，好像裹着一件硕大的蓑衣，连海碗大的蹄子上方也长满了毛，像毛靴一样把自己保护得严严实实，难怪它对酷寒无动于衷，没准儿觉得像乘凉一般舒服呢。连它的尾巴也不同寻常，不似水牛、黄牛的，只是小小的一绺儿，在屁股后面抽抽打打地赶蚊蝇，好像苍蝇拍一样。这家伙的尾巴是蓬蓬松松的一大把，好像一只同样颜色的小松鼠顽皮地蹲在它身后。我正看得带劲儿，它突然不耐烦起来，挺起胸膛，大大地张开嘴巴，我看到雪白的牙齿和红红的舌头，一股淡黄色的热气喷涌而出，好像它的嘴巴是一个即将爆发的火山口……

  I held my breath and quietly approached it. I saw that it was covered with brown and black hair all over its body, as if it was wrapped in a huge raincoat, and even the top of the hooves of the sea bowl was covered with hair, protecting itself tightly like woolen boots, no wonder it was indifferent to the cold, and maybe it felt as comfortable as enjoying the shade. Even its tail is unusual, unlike that of buffalo and scalpers, just a small strand, whipping behind its buttocks to drive away mosquitoes and flies, like fly swatters. The guy's tail is a large fluffy handful, like a small squirrel of the same color squatting playfully behind it. I was watching it vigorously, it suddenly became impatient, puffed out its chest, opened its mouth wide, and I saw snow-white teeth and a red tongue, a pale yellow heat gushed out, as if its mouth was a crater about to erupt......更可怕的事还在后面，从它粗大得像水桶一般的喉咙里，发出了震撼山峦的吼叫。

  More terrible things were yet to come, and from its throat, which was as thick as a bucket, there was a roar that shook the mountains.我被这叫声吓呆了，不仅仅是因为它的声音大，像它这么大的体积，吼声震天是意料中的事。令人惊异的是它的叫声太像猪了，好像宇宙间有一大群猪八戒，接受了统一的口令，齐声高歌。

  I was stunned by this cry, not only because of its loud voice, but because of its size, it was expected to roar to shake the sky. What is amazing is that its cry is too much like a pig, as if there is a large group of pig Bajie in the universe, accepting a unified password and singing in unison.我看着发出猪叫的怪物，它也很得意地看着我，好像在说，对，就是我在叫。怎么样啊？真正的猪也没我叫得像吧？

  I looked at the monster that was barking, and it looked at me proudly, as if to say, yes, I was screaming. How is it? The real pig doesn't look like me, right?震耳欲聋的猪叫声把老蓝给引出来了。老蓝是医院里最老的医生，有一种爷爷的风度。他一看我和怪物对峙的局面，忙打了一声奇怪的呼哨。那怪物好像听到了同伴的召唤，慢慢爬起来，恋恋不舍地看了我们一眼，向远处的深山走去。

  The deafening pig cry lured Lao Lan out. Lao Lan is the oldest doctor in the hospital, and he has a grandfather's demeanor. When he saw the situation where I was confronting the monster, he hurriedly blew a strange whistle. The monster seemed to hear the call of its companions, slowly got up, glanced at us reluctantly, and walked towards the deep mountains in the distance.老蓝说，你这个女娃胆忒大，知道它是什么吗？

  Lao Lan said, you girl is bold, do you know what it is?我说，知道。它是野猪。

  I said, I know. It is a wild boar.老蓝说，错啦！它要是野猪，你还能安安生生地在这儿跟我耍贫嘴？它是牦牛！

  Lao Lan said, wrong! If it is a wild boar, can you still play with me here in peace?" It's a yak!

  我说，野牦牛？

  I said, wild yak?老蓝说，它是家牦牛，你没看它挺和气的，我一发出牧人的信号，它就找自己的伙伴去了？野牦牛的脾气要比它大得多，一不高兴，就会用犄角把你的肚子顶出两个透明的窟窿。

  Lao Lan said, it is a domestic yak, you didn't see that it is quite kind, as soon as I sent out the signal of the shepherd, it went to find its own partner? The wild yak has a much bigger temper than it, and when it is unhappy, it will use its horns to push your belly out of two transparent holes.我说，老蓝你没搞错吧？它的叫声分明是猪啊。我小的时候，在我姥姥家住过，猪圈就在窗户根底下，每天不是公鸡打鸣报告天亮，而是猪像闹钟一样准时把我叫醒。我可以证明，我们平常说猪是懒惰的动物，真是冤枉了它。猪是很勤快的，起得可早了……

  I said, Lao Lan, you are not mistaken, are you? Its cry is clearly a pig. When I was a child, I lived in my grandmother's house, and the pigsty was under the window, and every day it was not the rooster that crowed to report dawn, but the pig woke me up on time like an alarm clock. I can attest that we usually say that pigs are lazy animals, and we are really wronged about it. Pigs are very diligent and get up early......老蓝不耐烦地打断了我的啰唆，说我在西藏喝过的雪水，比你过的河都多。你看见过长角的猪吗？

  Lao Lan interrupted my insistence impatiently, saying that I had drunk more snow water in Tibet than you had crossed the river. Have you ever seen a pig with horns?我一下子傻了眼。是啊，古今中外，还真没听说过猪长角。

  I was dumbfounded. Yes, in ancient and modern China and abroad, I have never heard of pig horns.老蓝说，牦牛是一种特殊的牛，老在寒冷的高原住着，它们身上的毛就越长越长，恨不能拖拉到地上，变成一件毛大氅。它的叫声像猪，老乡就给它起了一个好听的小名，叫作“猪声牛”，其实，它和猪没有一点关系，是地地道道的牛科反刍动物。别看牦牛长得挺吓人，其实，它的脾气最好，而且特别能吃苦耐劳。早年间西藏没有公路不通汽车的时候，牦牛就是最主要的运输工具，被人赞为“高原之舟”，和骆驼属一个级别的。牦牛奶也很好喝，颜色是淡黄的，营养价值特别高。牦牛的肉也很好吃，因为它经常跋山涉水的，瘦肉多，一点也不腻。它的毛非常结实，细的可以用来纺线织牦牛绒的衣服，暖和极了。粗的毛可以搓绳子，擀毡，制帐篷……牦牛简直浑身都是宝。对了，它的油更是好东西，能打出上好的酥油茶，那个香啊……还有牦牛血，提神壮胆……

  Lao Lan said that yaks are a special kind of cow, always living on the cold plateau, the hair on their bodies is growing longer and longer, and they can't drag it to the ground and become a big cloak. Its cry is like a pig, and the fellow gave it a nice nickname, called "Pig Sound Cow", in fact, it has nothing to do with pigs, it is an authentic bovid ruminant. Don't look at the yak looking scary, in fact, it has the best temper and is particularly hardworking. In the early years, when Tibet had no roads and cars, yaks were the most important means of transportation, and were praised as "plateau boats", which belonged to the same level as camels. Yak milk is also very delicious, the color is light yellow, and the nutritional value is particularly high. The meat of the yak is also very tasty, because it often trekkes mountains and rivers, has a lot of lean meat, and is not greasy at all. Its fur is very strong, and the fine ones can be used to spin yak wool clothes, which are extremely warm. Coarse wool can be used to rub ropes, roll felt, and make tents...... The yak is simply full of treasures. By the way, its oil is even better, it can make good butter tea, that fragrant...... There is also yak blood, which is refreshing and courageous......老蓝说得得意起来，有滋有味地咂摸着，好像酥油茶抹了一嘴唇。

  Lao Lan said proudly, smacking it with taste, as if butter tea had wiped his lips.我刚开始听得很起劲儿，到了后来，忍不住说，老蓝，你怎么老说吃牦牛的事啊，都是高原上的生物，多不容易啊，为什么不让牦牛越养越多，漫山遍野？

  I listened very energetically at first, but later, I couldn't help but say, Lao Lan, why do you keep talking about eating yaks, they are all creatures on the plateau, how difficult it is, why not let the yaks raise more and more, all over the mountains?老蓝说，你这个女娃的想法怪。牦牛养得太多了，你让它们吃什么？高原上只有很少的地方能长草，牦牛的舌头一舔过去，地上就秃了。

  Lao Lan said, you girl has strange ideas. Yaks are raised too much, what do you let them eat? There are only a few places on the plateau where grass can grow, and when the yak's tongue licks it, the ground is bald.想想也是，我只好为牦牛的命运叹了一口气。

  Thinking about it, I had to sigh for the fate of the yak.这时河莲走来，说，那个可怕的家伙跑了？

  At this time, He Lian came over and said, that terrible guy ran away?我说，河莲，如果发生了战争，我断定你是个叛徒。

  I said, Ha Lian, if there is a war, I will conclude that you are a traitor.河莲说，你可冤枉了我！你以为老蓝是自发来的吗？那是我呼叫来的援军，我陪着你死守有什么用？还是老高原有办法。这是机动灵活的战略战术啊！

  He Lian said, you have wronged me! Do you think Lao Lan came spontaneously? That's the reinforcements I called, what's the use of me staying with you?" There is still a way for the old plateau. This is a mobile and flexible strategy and tactics!老蓝趁我们俩斗嘴的工夫，回到自己的房间。当他再次出现的时候，手里多了一柄雪白的拂尘。它长丝垂地，根根都像精心锻造的银线笔直刚硬，拂动晨风，令人有飘飘欲仙之感。

  Lao Lan took advantage of the bickering between the two of us and returned to his room. When he reappeared, there was a snow-white duster in his hand. Its filaments hang down to the ground, and the roots are straight and hard like carefully forged silver threads, blowing the morning breeze, making people feel like they are floating.

  我和河莲看傻了，觉得老蓝一下子变成了观音菩萨的化身，手持拂尘，仙风道骨，超然脱俗。

  He Lian and I were dumbfounded, and felt that Lao Lan suddenly became the incarnation of Guanyin Bodhisattva, holding a duster, with a fairy style and bones, transcendent and refined.老蓝当然还是那个倔老头儿的模样，关键是他手中的那柄拂尘，像精彩的道具，让老蓝摇身一变，使人耳目一新。

  Of course, Lao Lan still looks like the stubborn old man, the key is that the duster in his hand is like a wonderful prop, which makes Lao Lan transform and make people refreshed.您这个东西是干什么用的？河莲问。

  What is this thing for? He Lian asked.老蓝得意地一挥拂尘，轻盈地旋转了一下，原先聚在一起的银丝，就像一把白绸伞，缓缓地张开了翅膀，绽成一朵白莲花，在初升的太阳照耀下，晶莹剔透，神奇极了。

  Lao Lan waved the dust proudly and spun lightly, and the silver threads that had been gathered together were like a white silk umbrella, slowly spreading their wings and blooming into a white lotus, which was crystal clear and magical under the rising sun.我和河莲还没来得及表达惊叹，老蓝就把这美丽的白伞高高举起，重重地抽在自己身上，于是，一股黄烟从老蓝油脂麻花的棉袄上腾起，好像在他身上爆炸了一颗手榴弹。高原上的风沙大，大家都是“满面尘灰烟火色”，衣服更成了沙尘的大本营。这柄拂尘好像鸡毛掸子，把灰沙从衣服布丝的缝隙里驱赶出来，抖在空气中，化成呛人的气流，随着寒风远去。老蓝用短短的胳膊挥着长长的银丝，围着自己圆柱形的身体，反复抽打着，直到把浑身打扫得如同河滩上一块干净的鹅卵石。

  Before He Lian and I could express our amazement, Lao Lan raised this beautiful white umbrella high and slammed it on himself, so a puff of yellow smoke rose from Lao Lan's grease twist cotton jacket, as if a grenade had exploded on him. The wind and sand on the plateau are large, and everyone is "covered in dust and fireworks", and their clothes have become the base camp of sand and dust. This duster is like a feather duster, driving the gray sand out of the gaps in the cloth of the clothes, shaking in the air, turning into a choking airflow, and going away with the cold wind. Lao Lan waved a long silver wire with his short arms, surrounded his cylindrical body, and whipped repeatedly until he cleaned his whole body like a clean pebble on the river beach.老蓝表演结束后，看着我们说，怎么样？

  After the performance of Lao Lan, he looked at us and said, how is it?这是从哪儿搞来的？河莲不理老蓝的问话，追问感兴趣的话题。

  Where did this come from? He Lian ignored Lao Lan's question and asked about the topic of interest.老蓝说，是牦牛的尾巴啊。

  Lao Lan said, it's the tail of a yak.我和河莲惊得几乎跳起来，说，牦牛的尾巴能做拂尘？

  He Lian and I were so shocked that we almost jumped up and said, can a yak's tail be a duster?老蓝说，正是。你们不是亲眼见了吗！

  Lao Lan said, exactly. Didn't you see it with your own eyes!我们又问，哪里有白牦牛啊？

  We asked again, where are the white yaks?老蓝得意起来，说，白牦牛就像白蛇白猿一样，非常稀少。我在西藏多年，只碰见过一头白牦牛，浑身上下像是雪捏的。

  Lao Lan became proud and said that white yaks are like white snakes and white apes, very rare. I have been in Tibet for many years, and I have only met a white yak, which looks like snow all over its body.你就把它的尾巴活活给割下来了？我战战兢兢地说。

  You cut off its tail alive? I said tremblingly.不是我给割下来的。是我让牧民在这头牦牛老死的时候，把它的尾巴给我留下来，做个纪念。老蓝很认真地更正。

  I didn't cut it off. It was I who asked the herdsmen to leave its tail to me as a souvenir when this yak died of old age. Lao Lan corrected it very seriously.我从老蓝手里接过牦牛尾巴做成的拂尘，它仿佛有神奇的法力，扑打出那么多的灰尘，自己还是洁白如雪。想到它曾是一头巨大生物的尾巴，每一根银丝都好像具有灵性，在阳光下抖得像琴弦，我不禁肃然起敬。

  I took the dust made of the yak tail from Lao Lan's hand, it seemed to have magical power, fluttering so much dust, and I was still as white as snow. I couldn't help but be in awe at the thought that it was once the tail of a huge creature, and each silver thread seemed to have spirituality, shaking like strings in the sun.我央告老蓝，你去对牧民说说，让他们也送我一条牦牛尾巴。

  I beg Lao Lan, you go and tell the herdsmen and let them give me a yak tail too.老蓝说，一个女娃，勤洗着点衣服，身上哪有那么多土？实在脏了，找条手巾拍打拍打就是。一头牦牛只有一条尾巴，拂尘，难搞着呢。

  Lao Lan said, a girl, washing some clothes frequently, how can there be so much soil on her body? It's really dirty, just find a handkerchief and pat it. A yak has only one tail, dusting, and it is difficult to deal with it.我说，我不是要拿它掸土，是要把它挂在墙上。

  I said, I don't want to dust it off, I want to hang it on the wall.老蓝说，干啥？当画？

  Lao Lan said, what are you doing? Should I paint?我说，留个纪念。以后我回了家，会指着它对别人说，知道这是什么吗？它是牦牛啊！一个尾巴就这样震撼人心，要是整个现出原形，庞大得会让你腿肚子朝前。

  I said, keep a souvenir. When I go home in the future, I will point to it and say to others, do you know what it is? It's a yak! A tail is so shocking that if the whole thing shows its original shape, it will be so huge that it will make your legs and stomach forward.老蓝说，你这么一说，我这个白牦牛尾巴也不用它掸土了。牦牛毛虽然很结实，也是掉一根少一根。掸土时再精心，也免不了伤了它。从今往后，我就把这牦牛尾巴当宝贝藏起来。探亲的时候拿出来，人家还以为我是从南海观音那儿借来的呢！

  Lao Lan said, if you say this, my white yak tail doesn't need to dust off it. Although the yak hair is very strong, it also sheds one less strand. No matter how careful you are when dusting the soil, you will inevitably hurt it. From now on, I will hide this yak tail as a treasure. When I took it out when I visited relatives, people thought I borrowed it from Guanyin in the South China Sea!河莲一撇嘴说，谁那么傻！仔细闻闻，您这个掸子，牛毛味儿大着呢！

  He Lian curled her lips and said, who is so stupid! Smell carefully, you duster, the smell of cow hair is big!老蓝听了，真就把牦牛尾巴托到鼻子跟前，像猎犬那样闻个不止。我和河莲哈哈大笑起来，因为雪白长须挂在他的下巴上，太像唱戏的老生了。

  When Lao Lan heard this, he really put the yak's tail to his nose and sniffed it like a hound. Ha Lian and I laughed because the snow-white beard hung on his chin, too much like an old singer.老蓝说，嗯，是有点膻气。怪我当时洗得不干净。

  Lao Lan said, well, it's a bit smelly. Blame me for not washing it cleanly at that time.河莲凑过去说，老蓝，我给你再洗洗怎么样？用我洗头发使的胰子，保证让您的牦牛尾巴从此香得跟茉莉花似的。

  He Lian leaned over and said, Lao Lan, how about I wash you again? Use the pancreas I wash my hair to ensure that your yak tail will smell like jasmine from now on.老蓝摆手说，那倒不必，东西还是天然味儿的好。你这个女娃心眼儿多，手脚勤快。不过，我看你是个无利不起早的人，说吧，有什么要求我办的事？

  Lao Lan waved his hand and said, that's not necessary, things still taste good. You girl has a lot of heart and eyes, and your hands and feet are diligent. However, I think you are a person who can't afford to make a profit, tell me, what do you want me to do?"河莲说，老蓝你真是火眼金睛，怎么一下就把我看穿了呢？我要办的事一点也不复杂，就是你给小毕搞牦牛尾巴的时候，顺便给我也剁下一绺儿。

  He Lian said, Lao Lan, you are really hot-eyed, why did you see through me all at once? What I want to do is not complicated at all, that is, when you make a yak tail for Xiao Bi, by the way, chop a strand for me as well.我说，河莲，你怎么抢我的？

  I said, Ha Lian, how did you rob me?河莲说，不是抢，是分个二分之一到三分之一的，无伤大雅。

  He Lian said that it was not a robbery, but a half to a third, which was harmless.我说，我的牦牛尾巴被你砍去一半，只剩下电话线粗细的一小撮儿，成什么样子？人家没准儿以为是马尾巴呢！

  I said, my yak tail was cut off in half by you, leaving only a small handful of the thickness of the telephone line, what did it look like? People might think it's a ponytail!河莲说，那就叫老蓝多给我们弄些就是了。

  He Lian said, then ask Lao Lan to get us more.老蓝气得说，谁答应你们啦？还闹起分赃不均！

  Lao Lan said angrily, who promised you? There is also an uneven distribution of stolen goods!我们又赶快哄他说，咱们换工吧。你若是给我们搞来了牦牛尾巴，我们就给你洗衣服。

  We quickly coaxed him again, let's change jobs. If you get us a yak tail, we will wash your clothes.老蓝脸色像夏天的雪山，有了一丝暖气，说，那好吧。一根牦牛尾巴合一件衣服。

  Lao Lan's face was like a snow-capped mountain in summer, with a hint of heating, and he said, okay. A yak tail is combined into one dress.我和河莲大惊失色，说老蓝你太黑！一柄拂尘少说也有几千根牦牛毛，这样洗下去，十个手指头还不搓得露出骨头来！

  He Lian and I were shocked and said that Lao Lan is too dark! A duster has thousands of yak hairs, to say the least, and if you wash it like this, ten fingers won't rub their bones out!老蓝微笑着说，我的意思是，我给你们每人一柄拂尘，你们只需为我洗一件衣服即可。

  Lao Lan smiled and said, I mean, I'll give each of you a duster, you only need to wash one piece of clothing for me.我很惭愧，觉得自己以小人之心度君子之腹。河莲到底深谋远虑，说您让我们洗的那件衣服，该不会是皮大衣吧？

  I am ashamed and feel that I have the heart of a villain to cross the belly of a gentleman. Ha Lian was far-sighted, saying that the clothes you asked us to wash should not be a fur coat, right?老蓝说，普通的外衣，就是脖领上的油泥稍厚了些。

  Lao Lan said that the ordinary coat is slightly thicker in the oil sludge on the neck.事情就这么说定了。老蓝是个说话算话的人，当我们催他把外衣赶快送来时，他总是不好意思地说，牦牛尾巴还没搞到，还是以物易物好，我不喜欢拖欠。

  That's it. Lao Lan is a man of words, and when we urge him to send his coat quickly, he always says embarrassedly, the yak tail hasn't been obtained yet, it's better to barter, I don't like to be in arrears.一天，老蓝提着麻袋来了，往地上一倒，一团黑白夹杂的毛发滚到地上。河莲说，天哪，简直像谋杀案里的人头。

  One day, Lao Lan came with a sack and fell to the ground, and a ball of black and white hair rolled to the ground. Ha Lian said, oh my God, it looks like a human head in a murder case.老蓝说，这就是牦牛尾巴，剩下的事我就不管了，你们俩自己分吧，互相谦让着点，别打起来。

  Lao Lan said, this is the tail of a yak, I don't care about the rest, you two divide it yourself, be humble to each other, don't fight.河莲说，老蓝你没有搞错吧，这团毛黑白相间像围棋子似的，是牦牛尾还是荷兰黑白花的奶牛尾巴？

  He Lian said, Lao Lan, you are not mistaken, this ball of black and white hair is like a Go piece, is it a yak tail or a cow tail with Dutch black and white flowers?

  老蓝说，你想得美！娇气的荷兰奶牛若还能在这海拔五千米的高原活着，挤出的就不是牛奶，而是牛骨髓了。这是地地道道的牦牛尾。

  Lao Lan said, you think beautifully! If the squeamish Dutch cow can still live on this plateau at an altitude of 5,000 meters, it will not be milk, but cow bone marrow will be squeezed out. This is an authentic yak tail.河莲说，那为什么不是白的？

  He Lian said, why isn't it white?老蓝说，我不是跟你们讲过了吗，纯白牦牛极其少见，这种黑白交叉的也不多，算稀有品种呢。最大路的货是褐色的，还有黑的，没掸灰呢就显出脏，不好看。

  Lao Lan said, didn't I tell you, pure white yaks are extremely rare, and there are not many black and white crosses, which is a rare breed. The goods on the biggest road are brown, and there are black ones, and if they are not dusted, they will look dirty and not good-looking.我们只得谢谢他，然后自己开始洗涤和分割牦牛尾巴。

  We had to thank him and start washing and dividing the yak tail ourselves.先用清水泡，再用碱水反复搓洗，最后用洗发膏加工，在阳光下晾干。直到抖开时每一根尾丝都滑如琴弦，柔顺地搭在我们的胳膊上，像一道奇特的瀑布。

  Soak it in water, then scrub it repeatedly with alkaline water, and finally process it with shampoo and dry it in the sun. Until it is shaken off, each tail silk is as smooth as a string, and it rests smoothly on our arms, like a peculiar waterfall.河莲说，它黑的黑、白的白，好似中老年人的头发。虽说是珍稀品种，终是不大好看。我想，咱们能不能把黑白两色分开，一个人专要黑的，另一人专要白的。要知道有一句谚语说，单纯就是美。

  He Lian said that it is black and white, like the hair of middle-aged and elderly people. Although it is a rare variety, it is not very good-looking after all. I wondered, can we separate black and white, one person wants black, and the other wants white. You must know that there is a proverb that simply is beautiful.我晓得河莲是很有谋略的，赶忙先下手为强说，那我要白的，你要黑的。

  I knew that He Lian was very strategic, so I hurriedly said first, then I want white, you want black.河莲说，我想出的主意，却被你占了先。好吧，谁让我年纪比你大呢，让你一回吧。

  He Lian said, the idea I came up with was preempted by you. Well, who made me older than you, let you go once.我们于是找来外科专用的有齿镊子，一根根地从牦牛尾皮上往下拽毛。河莲把黑色的归成一堆，我把白色的拢在一起。尾毛长得很牢实，像一根根长针扎进皮里，拔起来挺费力气的。但是一想起我们每人将有一把纯色的拂尘，我们干得还是很起劲儿，一边干一边聊天。

  We then found surgical toothed tweezers and pulled the hair down from the yak tail skin one by one. He Lian put the black ones together, and I put the white ones together. The tail hair grows very firmly, like long needles stuck into the skin, and it takes a lot of effort to pull it out. But when we thought that each of us would have a solid color duster, we still worked very hard, chatting while working.你说人的头发，除了黑的白的以外，还有灰白的。牦牛尾毛要么油黑，要么雪白，怎么就没个中间色的呢？我说。

  You say that human hair, in addition to black and white, is also gray and white. The yak tail hair is either oily black or snow-white, why is there no middle color? I say.人的头发从黑变白，是渐渐老了呗。这头黑白相间的牦牛，是天生的，所以不变灰。河莲解释。

  People's hair turns from black to white, which is gradually getting older. This black and white yak is born, so it does not turn gray. Ha Lian explained.我说，这头牦牛并不老，就死了。想起这个，我心里有点难过。

  I said, this yak is not old, it is dead. Thinking of this, I feel a little sad.河莲说，牦牛死了，尾巴留给我们。它的尾巴那么美丽地活着，它就没死。

  He Lian said that the yak is dead, and the tail is left to us. Its tail is so beautiful that it is not dead.我说，人死了以后，也该有点美丽的东西留在世上啊。

  I said, after a person dies, there should be something beautiful left in the world.河莲说，是啊。我们一定要给人间留点什么，才不算白活过。

  He Lian said, yes. We must leave something for the world so that we don't live in vain.正说着，我突然发现了一个致命的问题——牦牛毛拔下来以后，我们有什么法子，再把它做成一柄拂尘？

  As I was talking, I suddenly discovered a fatal problem - after the yak hair is pulled out, what can we do to make it into a duster?普通的拂尘制作工艺很简单，把长着牛毛的尾皮，直接钉在一根木柄上，在木柄上画点花草，再涂上一层清漆，就大功告成了。可是脱离了皮的毛，怎么钉在木柄上？

  The ordinary dusting process is very simple, nail the tail skin with cow hair directly to a wooden handle, draw some flowers and plants on the wooden handle, and then apply a layer of varnish, and you're done. But how can the hair that is separated from the skin nail to the wooden handle?也许在特殊的工厂里，可以把单根的毛发，用强力的胶水粘到布或皮革上。但在荒凉的高原，我们没有任何办法！

  Perhaps in special factories it is possible to glue individual hairs to cloth or leather with strong glue. But on the desolate plateau, we have no way!河莲捶胸顿足，懊悔自己智者千虑，有此一失。不过，她很快恢复了镇静，说，事已至此，我们只有一个办法。

  He Lian pounded her chest and stomped her feet, regretting that she had made such a mistake by thinking a thousand times as a wise man. However, she quickly regained her composure and said, "It's over, we have only one way."我忙问，什么办法？

  I hurriedly asked, what can be done?她一字一句地说，把所有揪下的尾毛，都扔了。

  She said word by word, throwing away all the tail hairs she had pulled.我说，这算什么办法呢？

  I said, what is this method?河莲说，而且永远不对别人说。咱们实在太蠢了。

  Ha Lian said, and never to others. We are so stupid.我们沿着狮泉河走，把撕下的牛尾毛，挽成两个大大的毛圈，抛进清澈的河水。它们像两位黑发与白发美女的遗物，打着旋儿飘荡着，半个环浸入水里，半个环挂满阳光和风，好像水下有两只巨手托举着它们，缓缓地浮沉，漂向远方。

  We walked along the Shiquan River, rolled the torn oxtail hair into two large terry rings, and threw it into the clear river. They are like the relics of two black-haired and white-haired beauties, floating in a spiral, half of the ring is immersed in the water, and the half ring is full of sunlight and wind, as if two giant hands are holding them underwater, slowly floating and sinking, drifting into the distance.由于失误，剩下的牦牛尾巴再裁成两份，就比较单薄了。我们只有在木柄上多下功夫，精心打磨，请了画画最好的人，为我们各画了一幅雪山风景。别人见了，都说我们的牦牛拂尘，小是小了一点，但十分精致。

  Due to a mistake, the remaining yak tail was cut into two parts, which was relatively thin. We only put a lot of effort into the wooden handle, carefully polished it, and invited the best painter to paint a snow-capped mountain landscape for us. When others saw it, they all said that our yak dusted off, which was a little smaller, but very exquisite.心情总算好起来。河莲突然又叫道，糟了！

  The mood finally improved. He Lian suddenly shouted again, oh no!我摸着胸口说，河莲你别一惊一乍的，我算叫你吓怕了。又有什么糟糕事？

  I touched my chest and said, Helian, don't be surprised, I think I'm scaring you. What's wrong with that?河莲说，我们俩的牦牛尾巴是来自同一条牦牛，不但颜色是一样的，连毛发的根数都几乎相等，木柄也是同一个人画的，除了咱们两个以外，别人怎么能分清哪个是你的、哪个是我的？

  He Lian said that the yak tails of the two of us are from the same yak, not only the color is the same, even the number of hair roots is almost the same, and the wooden handle is also drawn by the same person.我说，哈！这算什么事啊。你忘了咱们俩有一个巨大的区别了？

  I said, ha! What is this? Have you forgotten that there is a huge difference between the two of us?河莲说，是什么？

  He Lian said, what is it?我说，你家在南方，我家在北方，我们以后把牛尾拂尘挂在自己家的墙上，隔了十万八千里，哪里会弄混！

  I said, your home is in the south, my family is in the north, we will hang the oxtail duster on the wall of our house in the future, 108,000 miles apart, where will we mix up!

  河莲说，我真是糊涂了。这世上是没有两头一模一样的牦牛的，像我们俩这种黑白相间的拂尘，注定也只有这两柄。以后，无论我们到了什么地方，都会记得这头牦牛，都会记得我们一起度过的时光。
He Lian said, I'm really confused. There are no two identical yaks in this world, and black and white dusters like the two of us are destined to only have these two. In the future, no matter where we go, we will remember this yak and the time we spent together.

未知

  08.“回”字形银饰

  08. "Hui" zigzag silver jewelry

  头发和女孩有着不解的缘分。

  Hair and girls have an indissoluble fate.果平梳的是长辫子，她的头发可真好，在被雪山冰川反射的强烈阳光下，会发出蓝缎子似的闪光，让人以为她在头发里偷偷抹了纯蓝钢笔水，秀发才能幻化出这样美丽的色彩，羡慕死人了。

  Guo Ping combed long braids, her hair is really good, under the strong sunlight reflected by the snow-capped mountain glacier, it will emit a flash like blue satin, making people think that she secretly smeared pure blue pen water in her hair, and her hair can transform into such a beautiful color, envious of death.小如人长得很甜，特别是右嘴角上方生着一个深深的酒窝，在她笑的时候，里面放一颗圆圆的药片，会妥帖地跟着她的笑容旋转，一定不会掉出来。可惜她的头发不争气，又稀又黄，好像大旱之年贫瘠山坡上的三类苗。

  Xiao Ruren is very sweet, especially there is a deep dimple above the right corner of her mouth, and when she smiles, a round pill is placed in it, which will rotate properly with her smile and will definitely not fall out. It's a pity that her hair is not up to the task, thin and yellow, like three types of seedlings on a barren hillside in a year of drought.河莲的头发和她的长相一样，居中。就是说，不怎么好也不怎么坏，发质不黑也不黄，数目不多也不少，发际不高也不低，整个是沧海一粟芸芸众生的代表。

  Ha Lian's hair is the same as her appearance, in the center. That is to say, it is neither good nor bad, the hair quality is neither black nor yellow, the number is neither too much nor less, the hairline is neither high nor low, and the whole is a drop in the sea of representatives of all living beings.不过，除了女孩子自己，没人知道我们的头发是什么样。这是一个大大的秘密。当兵的人不能把头发露在帽子外面，好像那是一些见不得人的东西。军规要求把每一根头发都藏在军帽里面，据说是为了打仗时行动方便。我总想不通，打仗嘛，较量的是武器和智慧，关毛茸茸、乱蓬蓬的头发什么事？

  However, no one knows what our hair looks like, except for the girls themselves. This is a big secret. Soldiers cannot show their hair outside their hats, as if it were something shameful. Military regulations require that every hair be hidden in a military cap, which is said to be for ease of movement during war. I can't figure it out, war, it's about weapons and wisdom, what's the matter with hairy and messy hair?我从小剪短发，关于头发的军规，对我的影响倒是不怎么严重，甚至还有好处。不管发型如何杂草丛生，只要把像个鸭蛋壳似的帽子往脑袋上一扣，就像罩上了变魔术的黑斗篷，没人知道里面是啥货色。你尽可以瞒天过海地三天不梳头，让头发自由自在地乱成鸟窝。当然啦，你要在帽子的边缘下些功夫，尽可能地把所有不听指挥、张牙舞爪预备伺机蹿出帽圈的发丝严格围困起来，使它们不得擅自行动。这个过程说起来简单，真正做起来有一定难度。短发不易将整个帽子填满，虚虚囊囊的空帽袋，就像装泡沫塑料的盒子，一遇大风，很容易飞走。

  I have cut my hair short since I was a child, and the military regulations on my hair have not had a serious impact on me, and it is even beneficial. No matter how overgrown the hairstyle is, as long as the hat like a duck eggshell is buckled on the head, it is like a black cloak that is magical, and no one knows what color is inside. You can hide it from the sky and not comb your hair for three days, and let your hair be free into a bird's nest. Of course, you have to put some effort into the edge of the hat, and try to tightly encircle all the hairs that do not obey the command and are ready to jump out of the hat circle as much as possible, so that they cannot act without authorization. This process is simple to say, but it is difficult to do it in practice. Short hair is not easy to fill the entire hat, and the empty hat bag is like a box containing styrofoam, which is easy to fly away in the event of a strong wind.帽子被刮跑，真是一件可怕的事情。灾难在眨眼间降临，根本没有任何先兆，仿佛空气中有一根魔杖，轻轻一挑，久存反叛之心的帽子，就像优秀的三级跳远选手，听到了比赛的口令，兴奋而轻盈地一跃，嗖地一个腾挪就蹦上了屋檐的高度。它还算讲义气，略微停留一下，转过身来看你一眼，算是和往日的主人依依不舍地告个别。接下来的动作就是跃上云端，风筝一般义无反顾地向着蓝天飞去，寻找无拘无束的自由去了。最后一个姿势简直优美绝伦，腾云驾雾地在半空中翻着跟头，飞快地旋转着，越来越远，越来越小，像哪吒的风火轮，凝成一个黑点，消失在雪山背后。

  It was a terrible thing to have the hat scraped off. Disaster came in the blink of an eye, without any warning at all, as if there was a magic wand in the air, and with a light lift, the hat of rebellion for a long time was like an excellent triple jumper, hearing the command of the competition, he jumped excitedly and lightly, and jumped to the height of the eaves with a whoosh. It's also considered loyal, pause for a moment, turn around to look at you, and say goodbye to the former master reluctantly. The next action was to jump into the clouds, and the kite flew towards the blue sky without hesitation, looking for unrestrained freedom. The last posture was simply beautiful, somersaulting in mid-air, spinning rapidly, getting farther and farther away, smaller and smaller, like Nezha's hot wheels, condensed into a black dot, and disappeared behind the snow-capped mountains.这种干脆利索的丢失，还算痛快的。最可恨的是帽子和你逗着玩，并不是一开始就飞得无影无踪，好让你干脆死了心。它装作漫不经心地在地上散步，不急不缓，距离你始终只有一步之遥，诱你快步去追。每次在胜利即将到手的一瞬，它仿佛被咒语保佑，猛地往旁一闪，打一个滚，灵巧地逃开了你的手指尖。你不灰心，继续追下去，帽子就像一个小偷，躲躲藏藏又机智无比，在你就要把它追捕归案的时候，旱地拔葱一跃而起，飘悠悠迂回到一侧，成功地躲避了缉拿。你若追得狠了，它干脆耍开了无赖，专往陡峭的山壁或险恶的河面上跑，滴溜溜地好似滚动的圆盘，让你眼睁睁地看着它逍遥法外，无可奈何。

  This kind of neat loss is quite pleasant. The most hateful thing is that the hat plays with you, not flying without a trace from the beginning, so that you simply give up. It pretends to walk carelessly on the ground, unhurried, and is always one step away from you, tempting you to chase quickly. Every time at the moment when victory is about to be in hand, it seems to be blessed by a spell, flashing aside suddenly, rolling, and deftly escaping your fingertips. You don't lose heart, continue to chase, the hat is like a thief, hiding and witty, when you are about to hunt it down, the dry land jumps up, drifts back to the side, and successfully evades arrest. If you chase it hard, it will simply play away the scoundrel and run on the steep mountain wall or the treacherous river, dripping like a rolling disc, making you watch it go unpunished, helpless.

  司务长，我的帽子丢了。因为每人只有单、棉帽各一顶，丢了就没有替换的了，只好马上报告，以便补发。

  Director, I lost my hat. Because each person only has a single and a cotton hat, there is no replacement if they are lost, so they have to report immediately so that they can be reissued.帽子怎么又丢了？司务长不耐烦，这已是今天上午第三个要求补发帽子的女兵了。

  Why did you lose your hat again? The secretary was impatient, and this was the third female soldier to ask for a reissue of her hat this morning.叫大风刮跑了。小如如实汇报。今天外面的风特别大，山都给吹得摇晃起来了。小如补充说明，以求得司务长的同情。

  The wind blew away. Small as reporting truthfully. The wind outside was particularly strong today, and the mountains were shaking. Supplementary explanations are provided to gain the sympathy of the secretary.司务长被补发帽子的申请搅得手忙脚乱，没好气儿地说，风大有什么稀奇的？这里一年只刮两次风。一次是从一月一号到六月三十号。下一次是从七月一号到十二月三十一号。别人都不怕，就你们这几个女兵事多，要是打起仗来，还不得把枪都丢了？被服库又不是你们家的小皮箱，丢了手心向上就领新的，你们倒方便！照这样下去，军需仓库就要底儿朝天啦！

  The secretary was in a hurry by the application for a reissue of the hat, and said angrily, what's so strange about Feng Da? It winds here only twice a year. The first time was from January 1 to June 30. The next one is from July 1 to December 31. Others are not afraid, just you female soldiers have a lot of things, if you start a war, you can't lose all your guns? The quilt garage is not your small suitcase, and if you lose your palm, you will get a new one, which is convenient for you! If this continues, the munitions warehouse will be upside down!要依我的性子，就得和司务长吵起来。我就说，哼！仓库也不是你们家开的，帽子是被风抢走的，你有本事，找风发脾气好了。

  If you want to follow my temperament, you have to quarrel with the secretary. I said, hum! The warehouse is not opened by your family, the hat was snatched away by the wind, you have the ability, it's good to lose your temper with the wind.小如比我有涵养多了，她微微一笑，酒窝就在面颊上旋起来，缓缓地说，司务长，今天的风力足有十级，我们也没长飞毛腿，也不是会翻筋斗云的孙悟空，哪能追得上风啊？

  Xiaoru is much more cultivated than me, she smiled slightly, her dimples swirled up on her cheeks, and said slowly, Secretary, today's wind is enough to be level ten, we don't have scuds, and we are not Sun Wukong who can somersault clouds, how can we catch up with the wind?司务长的脸色好看了一点，说，你们也太笨了，怎么连自己头上三寸之地的一顶帽子也看不住？

  The secretary's face looked a little better, and said, you are too stupid, why can't you even look at a hat three inches above your head?说得我们不好意思。想想也是，都是一样的人，怎么人家的帽子就服服帖帖地粘在脑瓜上，偏我们的帽子好像是属车轱辘的，总是跑个不停。直着身子挨完司务长的训，领了新帽子回到宿舍，小如一声不吭。

  We're sorry to say it. Think about it, they are all the same people, why do people's hats stick to their heads obediently, but our hats seem to be wheeled, always running non-stop. After receiving the training of the director straight, he received a new hat and returned to the dormitory, Xiao Ru said nothing.我说，还难过呢？我有法子报复这个爱耷拉驴脸的司务长。人吃五谷杂粮，我就不信他不生病。等他躺在床上的时候，就是你我的天下了。别看他现在闹得欢，那会儿就再逞不了强。让我们一齐诅咒他得一场不轻不重的病吧！咱们就可以板起脸，狠狠地训他一顿了。

  I said, are you still sad? I have a way to take revenge on this director who loves to droop his donkey's face. When a person eats grains, I don't believe he doesn't get sick. When he is lying in bed, it will be your world and me. Don't look at him having fun now, he won't be able to be strong at that time. Let's curse him for a serious illness! We can straighten our faces and give him a hard beating.我沉浸在想象的报复快乐里，几乎笑出了声，小如还是闷闷不乐的样子。我说，你到底怎么了？

  I was immersed in the imaginary pleasure of revenge, and almost laughed out loud, but Xiaoru still looked sullen. I said, what's wrong with you?小如说，我在想，为什么我们的帽子总爱丢？

  Xiaoru said, I'm thinking, why do we always lose our hats?河莲说，可能山爷爷是个帽子爱好者，头上光秃秃的怕感冒，自己想戴又没人发给它，它的脑袋太大了，只好把我们的帽子收了去救急。

  He Lian said that maybe Grandpa Shan is a hat lover, his head is bare and afraid of catching a cold, he wants to wear it but no one sends it to him, his head is too big, so he has to put our hat away for emergency.我说，不对啊。山爷爷是个老头儿，可我们的帽子是女式的，岂不阴阳倒错？

  I said, no. Grandpa Shan is an old man, but our hats are women's, isn't it yin and yang?小如茅塞顿开说，小毕，你说得太对了！

  Xiao Ru Mao Sai suddenly said, Xiao Bi, you are so right!我大叫，哪儿太对了啊？我怎么一点也听不明白！

  I shouted, where is it so right? I can't understand it at all!小如兴奋地比画着给我解释，男式帽子和女式帽子是有区别的。我们的帽子又浅又大，像一只浅浅的碟子倒扣在头发上，当然不牢靠，所以，很容易被山风卷走……

  Xiaoru excitedly gestured and explained to me that there is a difference between a men's hat and a women's hat. Our hats are shallow and large, like a shallow saucer that is upside down in our hair, of course, it is not secure, so it is easy to be swept away by the mountain wind......我打断她的话说，就算你搞得水落石出真相大白了也丝毫没用。被服厂不会为我们这几个雪线上的女孩子，特制出带胶水的抗风帽子。最好的办法就是以后看到司务长的时候多赔几个笑脸，只求下回训我们的时候嗓门儿小点，就阿弥陀佛了。

  I interrupted her and said that even if you make the truth come to light, it won't be useful. The quilt factory will not make wind-resistant hats with glue for us girls on the snow line. The best way is to pay a few more smiles when you see the secretary in the future, and just ask for a quieter voice when you train us next time, and it will be Amitabha.小如不再理我，埋头翻自己的包袱。战士一般没箱子，连手提袋也没有，所有的家当都储存在一块白布打起的包袱里，可在十五分钟内收拾好所有的东西，出发到地球上的任何一个角落。

  Xiaoru ignored me and buried her head in her own baggage. Soldiers generally have no boxes, not even handbags, and all their belongings are stored in a bag made of white cloth, and they can pack everything up in fifteen minutes and set off to any corner of the earth.我突然看见小如从包袱里掏出一枚黑黑亮亮的物件，细长如针。那时谁的包袱里有什么稀罕东西，大伙都了如指掌，这玩意儿却是我从来没注意到的，不由得好奇。待定睛一看，原来是一根发卡。小如把头发和帽子用发卡别在一起，固定在头上，帽子就像土里长出的蘑菇一般牢靠，再也不怕被山风掠去。

  I suddenly saw Xiaoru take out a black and shiny object from her baggage, slender as a needle. At that time, everyone knew what rare things were in someone's baggage, but I had never noticed this thing, and I couldn't help but be curious. When I looked at it carefully, it turned out to be a hairpin. Xiaoru pinned her hair and hat together with a hairpin and fixed it on her head, and the hat was as firm as a mushroom growing in the soil, and she was no longer afraid of being swept away by the mountain wind.可惜只有小如有发卡，是她从平原来的时候，偶然放在包袱里的。别人就没有这样好的运气了。想去买吧，山上的商店根本料不到女孩子们还会有这种特殊遭遇，从来没备过这货色。于是大家纷纷给内地的亲人写信，让他们十万火急地寄黑发卡到高原。家里的人倒是关怀备至，行动很快，赶紧四处采办。那一段时间，我们格外关心军邮车上高原的日子，接到家信的第一个动作，是先隔着信封摸摸捏捏，看里面掖没掖着火柴梗粗细不折不弯的硬物。有了就高兴，没有就噘嘴，埋怨遥远的亲人太不拿我们的迫切要求当回事了。有一天，果平笑得前仰后合，慷慨地说要分给我们每人一包发卡，足够把头发和帽子钢铁般地焊在一起。因为她家给她寄来了一个包裹，包内有何物一栏里，赫然填写着：发卡。想想吧，整整一包发卡，那是怎样激动人心的事！足足够我们全体用一百年！迫不及待地拆开一看，大家顿时傻了眼，果平简直要哭出来。发卡美丽而脆弱，是塑料制成的。

  Unfortunately, only Xiao Ru had a hairpin, which she accidentally put in her baggage when she came from the plains. Others don't have such good luck. I want to buy it, but the shop on the mountain didn't expect girls to have this special experience, and they had never prepared this goods. So everyone wrote letters to their relatives in the mainland, asking them to send 100,000 black hair cards to the plateau in a hurry. The family was very caring, acted quickly, and quickly procured everywhere. During that period of time, we were particularly concerned about the days when the military mail truck went to the plateau, and the first action we took when we received a letter from home was to first touch and pinch it through the envelope to see if there were any hard objects inside that were not bent or bent with match stalks. If you have it, you will be happy, and if you don't, you will pout, complaining that your distant relatives are too unwilling to take our urgent demands seriously. One day, Guo Ping smiled and generously said that he would give each of us a pack of hairpins, enough to weld our hair and hats together like steel. Because her family sent her a package, the column of what was in the bag was filled in: issuing a card. Think about it, a whole pack of cards, how exciting it is! Enough for all of us to use it for a hundred years! Can't wait to take it apart, everyone is dumbfounded, Guo Ping is about to cry. Hairpins are beautiful and fragile, they are made of plastic.本来黑发卡也不是什么稀罕物，便宜得一毛钱买一板。可那时有一位人物讲话说，妇女用的发卡是钢丝做的，一年要消耗多少吨钢……这句话以后，全国就不造钢丝发卡了，一律用塑料制品代替。也许在平原还可凑合，高原的严寒中，塑料如纸，一碰就碎，哪能担当把帽子和头发紧紧地别在一起的重大使命！

  Originally, black hair cards were not a rarity, and they were cheap enough to buy a board for a dime. But at that time, a person said that the hairpins used by women are made of steel wire, how many tons of steel do they consume a year...... After this sentence, the whole country will no longer make wire hairpins, and they will be replaced with plastic products. Maybe in the plains, you can make do, in the cold of the plateau, plastic is like paper, it breaks when touched, how can it take on the major mission of pinning hats and hair tightly!大家依旧愁眉苦脸，继续沉浸在帽子随时飞上天的恐惧中。只有小鹿的日子稍微好过一些，因为她妈妈把自己以前用过的旧发卡寄了来。拆开信的时候，发卡上还挂着一根头发，可以想见老母亲是多么匆忙地把发卡从自己头上拔了下来，以满足高山上的女儿。因为两代人用的时间太久，钢丝发卡上的黑漆都磨光了，露出银亮的本色。小鹿的帽檐边，远远看去，好像斜插着一根针。

  Everyone still frowned, continuing to be immersed in the fear of hats flying into the sky at any time. Only Xiaolu's life was slightly better, because her mother sent her an old hairpin that she used before. When the letter was opened, there was still a hair hanging on the hairpin, and you can imagine how hastily the old mother pulled the hairpin off her head to satisfy her daughter on the mountain. Because the two generations have used it for too long, the black paint on the wire hairpin has been polished, revealing the true color of silver. The brim of the fawn's hat, from a distance, looks like a needle is inserted diagonally.小如看着小鹿，突然说，我有办法了。她跑到司务长那里，说我要领一包曲别针。司务长对所有要领东西的人都抱有戒心，他警惕地问，干什么用？

  Xiaoru looked at the deer and suddenly said, I have a way. She ran to the secretary and said I wanted a pack of paper clips. The secretary was wary of all those who wanted things, and he asked vigilantly, what is the use?各部门司务长都是些婆婆妈妈的小气鬼，也不知他们是因为格外小气才当上了司务长，还是当上司务长才变得格外小气？反正这个职务有危险的传染性，能让所有坐这把交椅的人，都既吝啬又爱刨根问底。

  The secretaries of various departments are all stingy mothers-in-law, and I don't know if they became secretaries because they were particularly stingy, or because they became superior secretaries? Anyway, this position is dangerously contagious, which can make everyone who sits in this chair both stingy and love to get to the bottom of it.

  小如不肯正面回答他，只是说，明天你就会看到这些曲别针干什么用了。

  Xiaoru refused to answer him directly, just saying that tomorrow you will see what these paper clips are for.司务长嘟囔着，用不完，可记得给我拿回来啊！

  The secretary muttered, I can't use it up, but remember to get it back for me!第二天，在高原的蓝天和白云下，每个女兵的帽子和头发间，都别了一枚崭新的曲别针，它“回”字形的轮廓，大部分别在发丝里，小部分露在帽子外，仿佛一种美丽绝伦的银饰，在雪域的阳光中，闪闪发亮。

  The next day, under the blue sky and white clouds of the plateau, a brand new paper clip was pinned between the hat and hair of each female soldier, and its "back" zigzag-shaped outline was mostly pinned in the hair, and a small part was exposed outside the hat, like a beautiful silver jewelry, shining in the snowy sun.山风依旧肆虐地逞凶，只是它再也无法把我们的帽子掳去，只得打着呼哨，愤愤地把远山的雪雾卷起来，从空中撒向峡谷。

  The mountain wind was still raging, but it could no longer take our hats away, so it had to whistle and indignantly roll up the snow and mist of the distant mountains and scatter them from the air into the canyon.高山的帽子，永远是皑皑的积雪。
The hat of the mountains is always covered with snow.

未知

  09.灵魂飞翔的地方

  09. Where the soul flies

  高原上的卫生员没有正规的课堂，几乎像小木匠学徒一样，由老医生手把手地教。医学这门学问，不太适合自学。你没法在病人身上做试验，基本上不允许反复的失败。你付出的是时间，就算辛苦点不在乎，但病人付出的是血和生命，没法一而再，再而三地让你演习。

  The hygienists on the plateau did not have formal classes, almost like little carpenter apprentices, taught by old doctors. The science of medicine is not suitable for self-study. You can't do experiments on patients, and you can't afford to fail repeatedly. You pay time, even if you don't care about the hard work, but the patient pays blood and life, and you can't let you practice again and again.为病人做臀部肌肉注射时，老医生总是叮嘱：小心啊，千万别把药打到坐骨神经上，万一打错了，病人就会一辈子下肢瘫痪！

  When doing intramuscular injections of the buttocks for patients, the old doctor always advised: Be careful, don't put the medicine on the sciatic nerve, if you hit it wrong, the patient will be paralyzed for the rest of his life!想想吧，多可怕！你随意挥洒，几秒钟的一个动作，就让一个人永远站不起来了，吓不吓人？但这根绞索似的坐骨神经究竟在什么地方，谁知道？你去问老医生，他会说，书上写着呢，自己看去吧！可你翻开书一看，那张人体解剖图上，蛛网似的血管神经，画了几十上百条，好像一张军用地图。坐骨神经只是细细一根，从肌肉中央穿过。臀部——活人身体里这个每天牢牢坐在凳子上的大部位，在书上缩成了乒乓球般的一个简图，埋伏在其中的纤弱神经，头发丝一般，无法想象它的真实模样。更不用说在解剖图谱的下方，还一本正经地注释着：神经走向可有变异，本书仅供参考。

  Think about it, how scary! You wave it at will, and a few seconds of action will make a person never stand up, is it scary? But where exactly is this noose-like sciatic nerve, who knows? You go and ask the old doctor, and he will say, it's written in the book, see it for yourself! But when you open the book, you can see that on the human anatomy map, dozens or hundreds of spider web-like blood vessels and nerves are drawn, like a military map. The sciatic nerve is just a thin one that runs through the center of the muscle. The buttocks, the large part of the living body that sits firmly on the stool every day, shrunk into a simple picture like a ping-pong ball in the book, and the delicate nerves lying in ambush in it, the hair is like a strand, and it is impossible to imagine its true appearance. Not to mention that under the anatomical atlas, there is also a serious annotation: the direction of nerves can be varied, and this book is for reference only.简直让你没法相信它。

  You can't believe it.老医生还形容说，万一把针戳到坐骨神经上，你会有竹扦子扎在粉条上的感觉，这时候悬崖勒马，虽说有损失，还来得及弥补。所以，每次打针的时候，都要高度警惕。

  The old doctor also described that if the needle is poked into the sciatic nerve, you will have the feeling of a bamboo stick stuck in the vermicelli, and at this time you will rein in the cliff, although there is a loss, you will have time to make up for it. Therefore, every time you get an injection, you must be highly vigilant.我们紧追着问，那粉条是粗的还是细的？绿豆粉还是红薯粉？竹扦子是毛衣针那样的，还是穿糖葫芦那种竹棍？

  We chased after each other and asked, are the vermicelli thick or thin? Mung bean flour or sweet potato flour? Is the bamboo skewer like a sweater needle, or is it a bamboo stick like a candied haw?老医生拉下脸来，说你们这帮女孩子怎么这么啰唆，不知道，不知道！医生的嘴、护士的腿，这种事问老护士去！

  The old doctor pulled down his face and said why are you girls so indecisive, I don't know, I don't know! The doctor's mouth, the nurse's legs, ask the old nurse about this kind of thing!老护士的态度倒是不错，可惜只有他一个人碰到过类似的危险情况。他说，注射的时候，碰到病人像弹簧一般跳了起来，结果针头断在肉里面，幸好针只扎进去了一半，根部还像刺一样露在屁股外面。忙过来了几个人，把病人像犯人一样按住，赶快用止血钳揪着针尾，好歹把针拔了出来。他抚着胸口说，那一回，吓得我真魂出窍。

  The old nurse's attitude was good, but unfortunately he was the only one who had encountered a similar dangerous situation. He said that when injecting, the patient jumped up like a spring, and the needle broke in the flesh, but fortunately the needle was only half pierced, and the root was still exposed outside the buttocks like a thorn. A few people were busy, held the patient down like a prisoner, and quickly grabbed the end of the needle with hemostats, and finally pulled out the needle. He stroked his chest and said, that time, I was really out of my mind.我们很感兴趣地问，是扎在坐骨神经上了吗？

  We asked with great interest, is it stuck in the sciatic nerve?老护士说，谁知道？也许是扎在病人的脑神经上了，要不他怎么会大叫一声蹦起来？

  The old nurse said, who knows? Maybe it was stuck in the patient's cranial nerve, otherwise how could he scream and jump up?我们锲而不舍地追问，有竹扦子扎粉条的感觉吗？

  We persistently asked, do you have the feeling of bamboo sticks prickly vermicelli?老护士心有余悸地说，忘啦！忘啦！哪儿有那么复杂精细！不过，从那以后，我看见屁股就害怕，打针的时候，尽量往臀部的上方和外方打，那里似乎离坐骨神经最远。

  The old nurse said with lingering fear, forget it! Forget! Where is it so complex and fine! However, since then, I have been afraid when I see my buttocks, and when I inject them, I try to hit them above and outside my buttocks, which seem to be the farthest from the sciatic nerve.我们趴在图谱上对照，发现老护士说的是一条真理。坐骨神经长得再怎么变异，也不会长到臀部的上外方去。那里像马路上的安全岛，是一个保险地带。

  We lay on the map and found that the old nurse was saying a truth. No matter how much the sciatic nerve grows, it will not grow to the upper and outer side of the buttocks. It is like a safety island on the road, an insurance zone.我们照方办理，而且不断发扬光大。直到有一天，老医生对我们说，我给病人开的医嘱是臀部肌肉注射，可你们把针戳到病人的腰眼上了。

  We handle it as we do and continue to carry it forward. Until one day, the old doctor told us that the doctor's order I gave to the patient was to inject the buttocks intramuscularly, but you poked the needle into the patient's waist and eyes.我们引经据典地说，那儿没有坐骨神经。

  We quote the scriptures that there is no sciatic nerve there.老医生严厉起来，说，那儿有肋间神经！

  The old doctor became stern and said, there is an intercostal nerve there!我们也气起来，说，这神经那神经，谁知道神经是个啥玩意儿？总有一天，大家非要发神经！

  We also got angry and said, this nerve and that nerve, who knows what the nerve is? One day, everyone will have to go crazy!老医生就愣在那儿，自己先发起神经来。

  The old doctor was stunned there, and he started to get nervous first.再比如说学习眼睛，老医生在墙上挂了一张彩色图，说是眼球的横剖面。就是说，用一把又薄又快的刀片，沿着眼球的横轴，向着颅骨方位切下，然后绘出图来。图倒是挺好看的，花花绿绿，最上面是一座弯弯的拱桥，好像苏州园林建筑。据说那就是虹膜。不过，拱桥下面可没有小巧的木船和长长的流水，是一团电线似的黄斑，按照图上的标志，那是视网膜最灵敏的区域。

  Another example is to learn about the eyes, the old doctor hung a color picture on the wall, saying that it was a cross-section of the eyeball. That is to say, use a thin and fast blade to cut along the horizontal axis of the eyeball towards the skull, and then draw a picture. The picture is quite beautiful, with green flowers, and a curved arch bridge at the top, like a Suzhou garden building. It is said that it is the iris. However, there are no small wooden boats and long flowing water under the arch bridge, it is a mass of wire-like macula, which is the most sensitive area of the retina according to the signs on the picture.我隔着眼皮按了按很有弹性的眼珠，对照着这张神秘莫测的图，实在想不通，滴溜溜圆的眼睛，怎么变成了一座五彩的拱桥。

  I pressed my elastic eyeballs through my eyelids, and compared with this mysterious picture, I really couldn't figure out how the dripping round eyes turned into a colorful arch bridge.我同老医生谈了自己的感想，他吹胡子瞪眼地说，你的几何一定不好，没有空间想象力。

  I talked to the old doctor about his feelings, and he blew his beard and glared and said, your geometry must be bad, and you have no space for imagination.我说，那你别让我当卫生员好了，我正不想干这个呢！爬电线杆子不需要空间想象力，本来就在空间里。

  I said, then don't let me be a hygienist, I don't want to do this! Climbing telephone poles does not require spatial imagination, it is originally in space.老医生被我呛得没话说，若有所思。

  The old doctor was choked by me and thoughtful.有一天，老医生对我们说，你们愿不愿意上一堂人世间最真实的解剖课？

  One day, the old doctor said to us, would you like to take the most real anatomy class in the world?我们齐叫，当然愿意。

  We call together, of course we do.老医生说，那就要不怕吃苦，不怕受累，不怕爬山，不怕血……

  The old doctor said that you must not be afraid of hardship, tiredness, climbing, or blood......果平说，那是上课还是打仗？怎么比拉练还艰难？

  Guo Ping said, is it a class or a war? How is it more difficult than training?老医生说，算你猜得对。我们就是要到高高的山上去解剖。说穿了，是一种简易的天葬。

  The old doctor said, you guessed right. We just have to go to the high mountains to dissect. To put it bluntly, it is a simple heavenly burial.天葬是当地兄弟民族的风俗，人死了，请天葬师把尸体背上专门的天葬台，用特制的工具，把肉身分解成无数小块儿，飞翔的兀鹰就把分散的人体，噙向高渺的天空……

  Heavenly burial is the custom of the local brotherly people, when a person dies, ask the heavenly undertaker to carry the body on a special heavenly burial platform, use special tools, and decompose the body into countless small pieces, and the flying vulture will throw the scattered human body into the high sky......我们说，你会天葬吗？

  We said, will you be buried in the sky?

  老医生说，我不会。现在情况特殊，天葬师都找不到了，无法实施正规的天葬，我可以通过解剖，达到和天葬同样的效果。我已经和病人的家属商量好了，由我安葬他们逝去的亲人，尽量达到天葬的效果，他们同意了。

  The old doctor said, I won't. Now the situation is special, the heavenly burial master cannot be found, and it is impossible to carry out a formal heavenly burial, I can achieve the same effect as the heavenly burial through dissection. I have discussed with the patient's family that I will bury their deceased relatives and try to achieve the effect of sky burial, and they agreed.我们战战兢兢地说，什么时间？

  We said tremblingly, what time?老医生一字千钧，说，明天。你们除了可以看到坐骨神经和眼球的构造，还可以看到真正的恶性肿瘤。

  The old doctor said that tomorrow. In addition to the structure of the sciatic nerve and eyeball, you can also see real malignant tumors.那一天晚上，我们都睡得很不安宁，总像有一双铺天盖地的灰色翅膀，毛茸茸地抚摸着我们的头顶。

  That night, we all slept restlessly, always like a pair of overwhelming gray wings, fluffy caressing the top of our heads.早上起来，小如穿上高筒毡靴，戴着口罩，佩着风镜，从头武装到脚。河莲笑她，你这是上解剖课，还是去疫区作战？

  When she got up in the morning, Xiaoru put on high felt boots, a mask, and goggles, armed from head to toe. Ha Lian laughed at her, are you taking an anatomy class, or are you going to fight in an epidemic area?小如说，这样，我的胆子就会大一些。

  Xiaoru said, in this way, I will be bolder.死者是一个牧羊人，得的病是肝癌。病故后，家属本着对解放军的高度信任，把亲人的遗体托付给金珠玛米3，由医生安排。家中活着的人，就赶着羊群向远方走去。老医生拿出一副担架，对我们说，把尸体抬到上面去。

  The deceased was a shepherd who suffered from liver cancer. The old doctor took out a stretcher and said to us, carry the body up to it.我们七手八脚行动起来。逝者是一个五十岁上下的汉子，瘦骨嶙峋。我们把他从太平间请出来，安放在担架上，再把担架抬进解放牌大卡车车厢。

  We took action. The deceased was a man in his fifties, skinny. We took him out of the morgue, placed him on a stretcher, and carried the stretcher into the compartment of the Jiefang brand truck.司机也是第一次执行这种奇特任务，说，开哪儿去？

  The driver is also the first time to perform such a peculiar task, saying, where to drive?老医生说，很简单，开到最高的山上去。

  The driver said, that can't be done. The highest place here is the Himalayas, and the people who climb up are mountaineering heroes, and cars can never get on it.老医生说，我的意思，是把车开到附近公路能够到达的最高海拔。

  The driver said, understood. Anyway, I kept driving forward, and when I got to a place where the car couldn't go, I stopped.担架蒙着白单子，很圣洁的样子。解放车车厢里的地方不算小，但中央摆了一副担架，剩下的地方也就不很宽敞了。我们拼命想离担架远一些，挤到大厢四角。但甭管怎么躲，与死人的距离也超不过两尺。我昨天还给这汉子化验过血，和他说着话，此刻他却静静地躺在那里，再不会呼吸。随着车轮的每一次颠簸，他像一段木头，在白单子底下自由滚动。

The place in the carriage of the liberation car is not small, but a pair of stretchers is placed in the center, and the rest of the space is not very spacious. We desperately tried to stay away from the stretcher and squeeze into the four corners of the compartment. But no matter how you hide, the distance from the dead is no more than two feet. I also had a blood test for this man yesterday and talked to him, but at the moment he was lying there quietly, not breathing again. With every bump of the wheel, he rolled freely under the white sheet like a piece of wood.汽车在蜿蜒的公路上盘旋，离山顶还有很远，路已到尽头。司机把车停下来说，四个轮子没办法了，剩下的路就靠你们的两个轮子了。我在这里等你们。
