  This is really a self-interested idea. Think about it, if you lift the front frame, a dead man's head is less than half a foot behind you, following you silently, does it feel like a chill? The old doctor walked at the forefront, with a major mission, and had to decide which peak would be the last resting place for the soul under this white cloth.在高海拔的地方，徒步行走都很吃力，更甭说抬着担架。幸好病人极瘦，我们攀登时费力稍轻。我们艰难地高擎担架，在交错的山岩上竭力保持平衡。尸体冰凉的脚趾，因为每一次的颠簸，隔着被单颤动不止。坚硬的指甲像啄木鸟的长嘴，不时敲着我和小如的面颊。小如拼命躲闪，连累得担架也歪了，病人的身体发生倾斜，她那个方向被啄得更多。倒是我这边听天由命，比较从容。

The hard nails were like the long beak of a woodpecker, tapping my cheeks and Xiaoru's cheeks from time to time. On the contrary, I resigned myself to fate and was more calm.我们不敢有片刻的大意，紧盯着前面人的步伐。河莲和果平往东我也往东，她们往西我也往西。若是配合不默契，一失手，肝癌牧羊人就会从担架上滑下来，稳稳坐在我和小如的肩膀上。

  We didn't dare to be careless for a moment and stare at the steps of the person in front of us. He Lian and Guo Ping went east and I went east, and they went west and I went west. If there is no tacit understanding, the liver cancer shepherd will slide off the stretcher and sit firmly on the shoulders of me and Xiaoru.山好高啊！河莲仰头望望说，我的天！再这样爬下去，你们干脆把我就地给天葬了算了。

  Himalayan eagles hover angrily above us, their huge wings whizzing by, fanning the warmth of the sun and the coldness of the canyon. Because of the torment of illness, he was as dry as a piece of paper. I remember that when I set the lancet to the thinnest gear, I almost pierced his ear. His blood clotting mechanism has completely collapsed, and thin blood flows endlessly like a red thread......   Looking at the transience and impermanence of life, at this moment, I made up my mind to work hard and cherish life from now on. Everyone looked solemn, and like me, they all felt the chance and preciousness of life in the face of the tragic truth.

  好了，现在，我把坐骨神经解剖出来给你们看。老医生说着，将牧羊人翻转，把一根粗大的白色神经纤维从肉体里剔了出来。

  As if I remember, who among you said that she did not understand the anatomy of the eyeball? I can show you now. The old doctor said, turning the shepherd over again.我大叫道，是我说的。可是我现在已经明白了，非常清楚，我不需要您演示了。我们想回家。

  I shouted, I said it. But I understand now, very clearly, I don't need your demonstration. We want to go home.是的。现在最想干的一件事，就是回家。我们迫不及待地说。

  Yes. One of the things I want to do most now is to go home. We can't wait to say.老医生狐疑地看着我们说，这个机会可是千载难逢。不过，既然你们全都懂了，我就不给你们详细讲了。现在，请你们慢慢往山下走吧。

  The old doctor looked at us suspiciously and said that this opportunity was a once-in-a-lifetime opportunity. However, since you all understand, I won't tell you in detail. Now, please walk slowly down the mountain.我们说，你呢？

  We say, what about you?他说，我要留在这里，把牧羊人分成许多部分，让喜马拉雅鹰把他带到云中去。那是他们信仰的灵魂居住的地方。

  He said, I will stay here and divide the shepherd into many parts and let the Himalayan eagle take him to the clouds. That is where the souls of their faith live.我们说，你害怕吗？

  We say, are you afraid?老医生很沉着地说，为什么要害怕呢？我这是在做善事啊。包括让你们看这些解剖的场面，你们一定觉得很残酷，其实，一个好的医生，必须精确地了解人体的构造，这才是对生命的爱护。不然，你看起来好像很仁慈，因为稀里糊涂一知半解，就会给人看错了病，耽误了病情，那才是最大的残忍呢。

  The old doctor said calmly, why should you be afraid? I'm doing good deeds. In fact, a good doctor must have an accurate understanding of the structure of the human body, which is the love of life. Otherwise, you seem to be very benevolent, because if you are confused and half-understood, you will misjudge the disease and delay the disease, which is the greatest cruelty.我们除了点头，再说不出别的话。

  We can't say anything else but nod.我下决心问道，人的眼睛和别的动物的眼睛，是一样的吗？

  I made up my mind to ask, are human eyes the same as those of other animals?老医生说，从理论上讲，哺乳动物的眼睛结构都是一样的。这话什么意思？

  The old doctor said that theoretically, mammals have the same eye structure. What does this mean?我说，哦，没什么意思。随便问问。

  I said, oh, it's not interesting. Just ask.听了老医生的话，我虽然从道理上明白了，在尸体上学习解剖，是正义、正当的事业，但我还是无法在牧羊人的眼球上，进行学习研究。他曾经那么信任地注视过我，用的就是这双眼睛，我不忍心看到它破碎。还是以后找机会，在一只牛眼上学习吧。

  After listening to the old doctor's words, although I understood from the truth that learning anatomy on corpses was a just and legitimate cause, I still couldn't study and study in the eyes of shepherds. He used to look at me so trustingly, with these eyes, and I couldn't bear to see it broken. It's better to find an opportunity in the future and learn from a bull's eye.我们走了。不敢往身后看。巨大的鹰群从我们头顶俯冲而下，好像巨型轰炸机。

  We are gone. Don't dare to look behind you. A huge flock of eagles swoops down from above us, like giant bombers.小鹿对我说，你知道我今天最大的感想是什么？

  The deer said to me, do you know what my biggest feeling is today?我说，你看着我们累得不行，自己却躲了清闲，一定在暗中偷偷乐吧？

  I said, you look at us so tired, but you hide from leisure, you must be secretly having fun, right?小鹿说，班长，别开玩笑。也许因为你们一直是在负重行军，所以就来不及想更多的事情。我空着手走路，想得就格外多些。

  Xiaolu said, squad leader, don't joke. Maybe because you've been marching with weights, you don't have time to think about more. I walked empty-handed, thinking more about it.小鹿附在我的耳边悄声说，我想的是，生命真好，活着真好，年轻真好。
The deer whispered in my ear, what I thought was that life is good, life is good, and it is good to be young.

未知

  10.八月里穿棉衣

  10. Wear cotton clothes in August

  阿里的军人照相，只有等待一个机会，就是高原服务队上山的日子。山下的人们会在高原最温和的季节，临时组织起一支慰问的队伍。十几个人，文武都有。文的指的是文工团员带来几个小节目，边防哨卡巡回演出。武的是一部浑身散发热气的洗澡车，它呼哧呼哧开到哪儿，汲水烧锅，那里的人就可以洗上一次热水澡。但是，看节目的时候虽然开心，节目看完，也就忘了。洗澡当时舒服，过一段时间，身上又脏起来。最受人欢迎的，还要数服务队的摄影师。

  Ali's soldiers took pictures, and only waited for an opportunity, which was the day when the plateau service team went up the mountain. People at the foot of the mountain will temporarily organize a condolence team during the mildest season of the plateau. There are more than a dozen people, both civil and military. The text refers to the members of the art troupe bringing several small programs and border post card tours. Wu is a bathing car that emits heat all over its body, and it drives wherever it goes, drawing water and boiling pots, and people there can take a hot bath. However, although I was happy when I watched the show, I forgot about it after watching the show. The bath was comfortable at the time, but after a while, the body became dirty again. The most popular is the photographer of the service team.摄影师通常是两个男人，一个老一个年轻。不知人员配备时出于何种考虑，大概是想老摄影师有经验，但是身体可能顶不住，年轻人可以扛机器，多干点力气活儿，有取长补短、前赴后继的意思。

  The photographer is usually two men, one old and one young. I don't know what the consideration is for staffing, probably because the old photographer has experience, but the body may not be able to withstand it, young people can carry the machine, do more hard work, and have the meaning of learning from each other's strengths and moving forward.一天，果平对我说，高原工作队上山来了，里头有一位老资格的摄影师，助手是个机灵的小伙子。

  One day, Guoping told me that the plateau task force had come up the mountain, and there was an old qualified photographer inside, and the assistant was a clever young man.我说，情报这么准，是不是已经偷着去照了一张？

  I said, the intelligence is so accurate, have you already secretly taken a photo?果平大叫冤枉，说摄影师一天只能照五十个人，每人只限两张。大家得排着队来，轮到谁会通知，别的人一律原地待命。

  Guo Ping shouted injustice, saying that photographers can only take 50 photos a day, and each person is limited to two. Everyone has to line up, who will notify when it's their turn, and everyone else will be on standby.我说，那我们这拨儿排在何时？

  I said, then when are we lined up?果平丧气地说，据我所知，大约是三个月后。

  Guo Ping said dejectedly, as far as I know, it was about three months later.我灰心丧气地说，那么久！我都成老太太了。

  I said discouragedly, for so long! I've become an old lady.可是有什么法子呢！等着吧。在那以后的日子里，你要是看到哪个人一边走，一边偷着看什么，不时地捂着嘴乐，一见别人注意他，马上若无其事一本正经起来，飞快地把什么东西藏进兜里……不用猜，他一定是刚从摄影师那里，取回了自己的照片。

  But what can be done! Wait. In the days that followed, if you saw someone walking and secretly looking at something, covering your mouth from time to time, and seeing others paying attention to him, you immediately became serious as if nothing had happened, and quickly hid something in your pocket...... No need to guess, he must have just retrieved his photo from the photographer.我们掐着手指头，计算着轮到我们留影的日子。不料传来的都是坏消息，先是摄影师每天拍摄的人数不断减少，好像一支行动迟缓、作风稀拉的队伍，在玩“增兵减灶”的游戏。摄影师刚开始解释，说是为了保障每人的形象都笑容可掬，照得不好的，比如愁眉苦脸、眨了眼成了瞎子的等等，都要返工，所以耽误了时间。大家刚开始还谅解他们，但后来进度越来越慢，简直像磨洋工，每天只能照十几个人，有些人照得很丑，也并不返工。人们开始愤愤不已，但又敢怒不敢言。生怕谁打了小报告，把说坏话的你告诉了摄影师，他们怀恨在心，轮到你照相的时候，随便一个动作，就把你照成丑八怪。这样的照片，你不要吧，过了这个村，就没这个店。要吧，万里迢迢地寄回家，你妈一看你这么不成嘴脸，准得吓一大跳，心里不好受，多不划算啊。出于这种考虑，人们暗地里埋怨，当面见了摄影师，又是主动打招呼，问寒问暖，再亲切不过了。

  We pinched our fingers and counted the days when it was our turn to take pictures. Unexpectedly, there was bad news, first of all, the number of photographers shooting every day was decreasing, as if a slow-moving and sparse team was playing the game of "increasing troops and reducing stoves". The photographer explained at the beginning, saying that in order to ensure that everyone's image is smiling, and if the photo is not good, such as frowning, blinking and becoming blind, etc., they have to rework, so the time is delayed. At first, everyone forgave them, but then the progress became slower and slower, like grinding foreign workers, they could only take pictures of a dozen people a day, and some of them were ugly and did not rework. People began to be indignant, but they dared not speak out. For fear that someone will make a small report and tell the photographer who said bad things about you, they will hold a grudge and take a picture of you as an ugly person with any action when it is your turn to take pictures. You don't want such a photo, after this village, there is no store. Yes, send it home thousands of miles, your mother will be shocked when she sees you so unattractive, and she feels uncomfortable, how not cost-effective. For this reason, people secretly complained, and when they met the photographer in person, they took the initiative to say hello and ask for warmth, which could not be more cordial.最坏的消息传来了，摄影师无法适应山上的恶劣气候，得了很重的高原病，改变计划，再过两天就要下山返回平原了。

  The worst news came, the photographer could not adapt to the harsh climate in the mountains, suffered from severe altitude sickness, changed his plans, and was going down the mountain to return to the plains in two days.简直是晴天霹雳。（注意啊，这只是一个形容词，因为高原只下雪，不下雨，所以，是没有霹雳这种雷电现象的。）

  It was a bolt from the blue. (Note that this is just an adjective, because the plateau only snows, not rains, so there is no thunder and lightning phenomenon like thunder.) ）怎么办？果平问我。

  What to do? Guo Ping asked me.还能有什么办法？等着服务队明年重来吧。我无可奈何地说。

  What else can be done? Wait for the service team to come back next year. I said helplessly.再想想嘛！果平不屈不挠。

  Think again! Indeed, he was unyielding and unyielding.我说，除非你绑架摄影师，用手枪逼着他给你单独摄影。

  I said, unless you kidnap the photographer and force him to take a photo of you alone with a pistol.你这个办法好极了！果平一蹦老高，然后又赶紧蹲在地上抚着胸口喘气。要知道，高原上任何突如其来的动作，都相当于百米赛跑和徒手格斗。

  Your method is great! Guo Ping jumped high, and then quickly squatted on the ground and stroked his chest and gasped. You know, any sudden action on the plateau is equivalent to a 100-meter race and hand-to-hand combat.我说，什么办法？绑架还是手枪？

  I said, what way? Kidnapping or pistols?果平说，不是绑架，是单独拜访。我们为什么不可以登门找找摄影师，哀求他坚持站好最后一班岗，为我们留个影？

  Guo Ping said that it was not a kidnapping, but a solo visit. Why can't we go to the door to find the photographer and beg him to insist on standing on the last shift and take a picture for us?我说，好啊，不妨一试。咱们快叫上大伙儿，一起去。

  I said, okay, you might as well give it a try. Let's call everyone and go together.果平说，不可。你以为这是打狼啊？一窝蜂，那么多人，还不得把摄影师的心脏病吓成心力衰竭？要是一两个人要照，还能咬咬牙，这么大一群，除了断然拒绝，把你们赶走，谁也没法发慈悲。

  Guo Ping said, "No." Do you think this is a wolf fight? A swarm of bees, so many people, can't scare the photographer's heart disease into heart failure? If one or two people want to take pictures, they can still grit their teeth, such a large group, except for categorically refusing and driving you away, no one can show mercy.我说，咱俩吃独食，总有点于心不忍。

  I said, let's eat alone, and it's always a little unbearable.果平一打我的手说，看你还当了真！谁知成不成事？没准儿白跑一趟，落个话把儿，还不够大伙儿笑话咱的。单独行动，到时见机行事。行则成，不成也不丢人。

  Guo Ping slapped my hand and said, look at you seriously! Who knows if it will succeed or not? Maybe it's a waste of time, and it's not enough for everyone to laugh at us. Act alone and act when the time comes. If you do it, you will succeed, and if you don't do it, you will not be ashamed.于是，我们背着大伙儿，开始了秘密行动。第一步是打听到摄影师的住址。这不难办，他们也不是什么国家首脑，住所不保密。我就把写着招待所门牌号码的字条，交到果平手里。

  So, we carried everyone behind our backs and started a secret operation. The first step is to find out the photographer's address. This is not difficult, they are not heads of state, and their residences are not confidential. I handed the note with the house number of the guest house to Guoping.事不宜迟，咱们明天早上就去。果平说。

  Without further ado, let's go tomorrow morning. Guoping said.为什么一定要早上？晚上不是更从容些？我不解。

  Why must it be in the morning? Isn't it more calm at night? I don't understand.晚上人一般比较累，心情不好。大清早精神饱满，是求人的好时机。你看见过一大早就气哼哼的人吗？果平解释。

  At night, people are generally tired and in a bad mood. Early in the morning is full of energy, which is a good time to ask for help. Have you ever seen someone who is angry early in the morning? Guoping explained.我说，早上心情好？那可不一定。要是正巧做了噩梦呢？

  I said, are you in a good mood in the morning? That's not necessarily. What if you happen to have a nightmare?果平说，不跟你抬杠。记住了，明早行动。

  Guo Ping said, I won't lift the bar with you. Remember, act tomorrow morning.第二天，我们黎明即起，赶往招待所。开门的是个小伙子，想必是那个较为年富力强的摄影师了。原以为我们会看到一张经过睡眠容光焕发的脸，没想到，他的眼圈像扣了两个蓝墨水瓶盖，眼白像一张满布飞机航线的地图，都是红丝。至于有经验的摄影师，根本就没露面，不知躲在哪里。

  The next day, we got up at dawn and rushed to the guest house. The person who opened the door was a young man, presumably the photographer who was in his prime. I thought we would see a radiant face after sleeping, but unexpectedly, his eyes were like two blue ink bottle caps, and the whites of his eyes were like a map full of airplane routes, all red silk. As for the experienced photographer, he didn't show up at all, and he didn't know where to hide.干什么？年富力强堵着门口问。

  Do? Nian Fengqiang blocked the door and asked.我们……想请你……一看他怒气冲冲的模样，我不知该怎样开口。

  We...... I would like to invite you...... When I saw his angry appearance, I didn't know how to speak.我们是卫生员，听说你们身体不好，特地来看望。果平伶牙俐齿地接过话头说。

  We are hygienists, and we heard that you are not in good health, so we came to visit. Guo Ping took over the conversation sharply.年富力强脸色好看些了，说，既是看望，那就感谢了。只是屋里有些不方便，我们还好，放心就是。说完露出送客的模样。

  The young man's face looked better, and he said, since it is a visit, then thank you. It's just that the house is a little inconvenient, we are fine, don't worry. After saying that, he showed off the appearance of seeing off the guests.我想摄影师们一定是刚起床，没叠被子，怕别人看到狼狈样。心想要是不让进屋，其他的话就不好提。就说，我们也不是检查卫生的，请别紧张。

  I think the photographers must have just gotten up and didn't fold the quilt, for fear that others would see the embarrassment. I want to not let you into the house, and it is not easy to mention anything else. Let's just say, we are not checking hygiene, please don't be nervous.年富力强有气无力地说，我要是能紧张起来就好了，现在是疲惫不堪。

  The young man said weakly, if only I could get nervous, but now I am exhausted.果平说，大清早刚起来就疲惫，是不是太娇气了？

  Guo Ping said that he was tired as soon as he got up early in the morning, isn't it too squeamish?年富力强反问，谁刚起来？

  The prime of life asked, who just got up?果平更正道，难道说你们半夜就起来了吗？

  Guo Ping corrected, did you get up in the middle of the night?年富力强说，我还没有睡呢！

  Nian Fengqiang said, I haven't slept yet!我们说，不信。你一夜不睡觉，干什么啦？

  We say, don't believe it. You don't sleep all night, what are you doing?"年富力强说，既然你们不相信人，我就请你们参观参观。说着，侧着身子，请我们进屋。

  Nian Fengqiang said, since you don't believe in people, I invite you to visit. Saying that, he turned sideways and invited us into the house.屋里的混乱程度，超出了我们最大胆的想象。到处都是水盆，里面泡着一张张白色的相纸，纸上不同的风景和人物，在药水里起伏着、重叠着。色泽深浅不同的人脸，好像扁扁的黑蝌蚪，懒洋洋地仰着脸，好像在晒太阳。

  The chaos in the house is beyond our daring imagination. There are basins of water everywhere, soaked in white photo paper, and different landscapes and characters on the paper are undulating and overlapping in the potion. The faces of different shades of color are like flat black tadpoles, lazily raising their faces, as if basking in the sun.我俩齐声问，这是怎么回事？

  We asked in unison, what's going on?年富力强没好气儿地说，这就是在干你们想干的事啊。

  Nian Liqiang said angrily, this is what you want to do.我们说，不懂不懂。我们想干什么事呢？

  We said, don't understand, don't understand. What do we want to do?年富力强说，照相啊。你们以为相片就是那么“咔嗒”一捏，就出来人影了？后面的事麻烦着呢！我们白天照相，晚上冲洗，连轴转，这么干活儿，平原上都受不了，别说是在连鹰都飞不上来的高原。要是不得高原病，那才叫天理不容呢！所以啊，病了是好事，我们就可以名正言顺地下山了。现在我们手里就剩下这点活儿了，抓紧冲洗出来，就能回到山下把氧气吃个饱了！

  The young man said, take pictures. Do you think the photo is so "clicked" and a figure comes out? The rest is troublesome! We take pictures during the day, rinse at night, and rotate the axis, and we can't stand it on the plains, let alone on the plateau where even eagles can't fly up. If you don't get altitude sickness, then it is called heavenly intolerance! So, it's a good thing to be sick, and we can go down the mountain justifiably. Now we have this little work left in our hands, hurry up and rinse it out, and we can go back to the bottom of the mountain to eat enough oxygen!"他这番话虽是气哼哼地说出来，细想想，也有理。看来我们的计策没等实施，就破产了。得，打道回府吧。我向果平使眼色——撤吧。

  Although he said these words angrily, if you think about it carefully, it makes sense. It seems that our plan went bankrupt before it was implemented. Okay, let's go home." I winked at Guo Ping—withdraw.果平假装看不见，对年富力强说，我真同情你们，可惜你这顿牢骚话，应该对他们说。

  Guo Ping pretended not to see and said to Nian Fengliqiang, I really sympathize with you, but it's a pity that you should say this complaint to them.他们是谁？年富力强问。

  Who are they? Nian Feng asked strongly.果平随手一指水盆里的人脸说，罪魁祸首在那儿。是他们害得你们这么辛苦。

  Guo Ping casually pointed to the face in the basin and said, the culprit is there. They are the ones who have made you work so hard.年富力强又不乐意了，说，你这个小姑娘嘴这么损。大家都有父母，家里都惦记着，想看看照片也是人之常情。

  He was in his prime and was unhappy, saying, you little girl has such a broken mouth. Everyone has parents, and it is human nature to want to see photos.

  果平一乐说，接受你的批评。问你一个问题，你说，家里人是更惦记男孩还是女孩？

  Guoping Yile said, accept your criticism. I ask you a question, do you think your family cares more about boys or girls?年富力强不假思索地说，当然是女孩了。女孩麻烦事多，男孩总归要好些。

  In the prime of life, he said without thinking, of course he was a girl. Girls have a lot of trouble, and boys are always better.果平说，对啊，所以，你该优待我们才是。

  Guo Ping said, yes, so, you should treat us preferentially.年富力强明白自己陷入了果平的伏击圈，半晌没作声。正在这时，门开了，一个胡子拉碴的半老头儿走进来，从他一眼扫过药水盆子的犀利目光，我们明白，经验丰富的老摄影师来了。

  Nian Fengliqiang understood that he had fallen into Guoping's ambush circle, and remained silent for a long time. At this moment, the door opened, and a bearded half-old man walked in, and from his sharp gaze that swept over the potion basin, we understood that an experienced old photographer was coming.还顺利吗？老摄影师咳嗽着问。

  Is it still going well? The old photographer coughed and asked.还好。只是去海拔最高的边防站照的相片，因为气候实在太差，风雪来临前抢拍的那些张，曝光量明显不足，底片经过处理，还是不行，人影模糊……年富力强汇报。

  OK. It's just a photo taken at the highest border station, because the climate is too bad, and the exposure is obviously insufficient for those photos taken before the wind and snow...... Report from Nian Fengliqiang.这可如何是好？老摄影师非常不安。

  How can this be good? The old photographer was very upset.把钱退给他们。算我们白辛苦了。年富力强说。

  Return the money to them. It's in vain. Nian Fengqiang said.老摄影师说，是我们失职，太对不起他们了。这样吧，让没照好的人从边防站下来，我们补照。

  The old photographer said that we were derelict in our duties and we were so sorry for them. Let's let those who didn't take good photos come down from the border guards, and we will make up the photos.年富力强说，恐怕不成。照坏了的不是少数，要是都从边防站撤下来，国境线上就没人站岗了。

  If you say it strongly, I'm afraid it won't work. If they all withdraw from the border station, there will be no one standing guard on the border.老摄影师说，既是这样，只有一个办法，我们再上一次最高的哨所。

  The old photographer said that since this is the case, there is only one way, and we will go to the highest post again.年富力强说，您的身体已经这样虚弱了，再上去，危险太大。

  Nian Fengliqiang said, your body is already so weak, and if you go up again, the danger is too great.果平立即插嘴说，我们可以给你们保健，你的心脏要是跳不动，我们给你按摩。呼吸要是困难了，立刻给你吸氧。

  Guo Ping immediately interjected and said, we can give you health care, if your heart can't beat, we will give you a massage. If breathing is difficult, give you oxygen immediately.老摄影师这才发现我们，说，你们是谁？

  The old photographer then found us and said, who are you?果平说，是两个没照上相的女卫生员。

  Guo Ping said that they were two female hygienists who did not take pictures.年富力强说，你是想用这种方式感动我们，好给你照相吧？

  Nian Fengqiang said, you want to move us in this way, so that you can take pictures of you, right?果平说，是你们感动了我们。为了给高原战士照相，自己差点要被照了遗像。

  Guo Ping said, it was you who moved us. In order to take pictures of the plateau soldiers, he almost got a posthumous portrait.我刚想说，果平你这个乌鸦嘴，没想到老摄影师笑起来，牙齿在黑胡子楂儿里闪烁，说，你这姑娘说得不对，摄影师要是以身殉职了，还真没人给他摄遗像，如同理发师不会给自己理发。

  I just wanted to say, Guo Ping, you crow's mouth, I didn't expect the old photographer to laugh, his teeth flashing in the black-bearded hawthorn, and said, you girl is not right, if the photographer dies in the line of duty, no one will really take a portrait of him, just like a barber will not cut himself.果平想想也是，不好意思地笑起来。

  Guo Ping thought about it and laughed embarrassedly.老摄影师对我们说，走吧。又对年富力强说，把摄影包给我。

  The old photographer said to us, let's go. He also said to Nian Fengliqiang, give me the photography package.我们只好走出屋门，老摄影师跟在后面。我说，您身体不好别客气，不必送了。

  We had to walk out of the house, followed by the old photographer. I said, you're not in good health, don't send it.老摄影师说，我不是送你们，是去工作。

  The old photographer said, I'm not sending you off, I'm going to work.我们就一齐默默地往外走。这是高原上一个很晴朗的上午，无遮无拦的紫外线像巨大的光伞，从高远的天际倾泻下来，晒在脸上，感觉不到暖和，但是很刺痛。远处的冰山像正在休息的白骆驼，不规则地趴着，白云在它的脚下浮动，好像脱落下的片片驼绒。

  We walked out silently together. It was a very sunny morning on the plateau, and the unobstructed ultraviolet rays were like huge umbrellas, pouring down from the high sky, shining on the face, not feeling warm, but very stinging. The iceberg in the distance is like a resting white camel, lying irregularly, and the white clouds float at its feet, like pieces of camel fluff falling off.好。停。就这儿。老摄影师命令说。

  Good. Stop. That's it. The old photographer ordered.我和果平继续往前走。跟着老摄影师的年富力强说，你们这两个女兵，怎么不听招呼？

  Guo Ping and I continued to walk forward. Following the old photographer's youth, why don't you two female soldiers listen to greetings?我们愣了，说，谁知说谁呢？

  We were stunned and said, who knows who to say?年富力强说，谁想照相就是说谁呢。

  Nian Liqiang said, whoever wants to take pictures is who it is.我们大喜过望，说，真想不到，摄影师带病坚持工作。

  We were overjoyed and said, I really didn't expect that the photographer insisted on working despite illness.年富力强说，你没看老师傅要亲自给你们照相？他的技术比我高明多了。要是男兵，我就动手。因为你们是女娃子，刚才不说了吗，女孩比男孩重要。

  Nian Fengqiang said, didn't you see that the master wanted to take pictures of you in person? His skills are much smarter than mine. If it's a male soldier, I'll do it. Because you are girls, didn't you just say it, girls are more important than boys.我们很感激，又不知如何表达，只有乖乖地听老摄影师调遣。

  We are very grateful, but we don't know how to express it, so we can only obediently listen to the old photographer's dispatch.果平本想以险峻的雪山为背景，照一张雄赳赳气昂昂的照片。老摄影师说，不可。你们的父母听说孩子到了高山上，一定担心不已。如果看到背景这么荒凉寒冷，心里一定不是滋味。你寄照片回家，原本是想让家长放心，这么着，他们就更不放心了。

  Guo Ping originally wanted to take a majestic photo with the steep snow-capped mountains as the background. The old photographer said, "No." Your parents must be worried when they hear that their children have reached the mountains. If you see the background so desolate and cold, you must not feel good. You sent photos home, originally to reassure parents, but in this way, they are even more uneasy.果平不知所措地说，那以什么为背景呢？在阿里高原，要找一处没有雪山的背景，几乎是不可能的。

  Guo Ping said at a loss, what is the background? In the Ali Plateau, it is almost impossible to find a background without snow-capped mountains.但我们可以让荒凉的感觉尽量淡薄一些啊。老摄影师领着我们往前走。在狮泉河旁像眉毛一般短的道路上找了半天，停在一块标语牌前。这地方怎么样？老摄影师的语气很有点沾沾自喜，好像发现了一个宝石矿。

  But we can make the feeling of desolation as light as possible. The old photographer led us forward. After looking for a long time on the road as short as eyebrows next to the Shiquan River, I stopped in front of a placard. How is this place? The old photographer's tone was a little complacent, as if he had discovered a gem mine.不怎么样。像人民公社的大队部。果平撇撇嘴。

  Not so good. Like the brigade headquarters of the People's Commune. Guo Ping curled his lips.在这穷乡僻壤，能有个像大队部的风景，就很不错了。别的地方照出来，简直像在土星上。年富力强说。

  In this remote area, it would be very good to have a scenery like the brigade headquarters. Shining from other places, it looks like Saturn. Nian Fengqiang said.虽然我也很讨厌毫无情趣的标语牌子，可是想到妈妈假如看到我站在崇山峻岭中的留影，显得那么渺小孤单，一定忧心忡忡，便同意了老摄影师的选择。

  Although I also hate boring slogans, I thought that if my mother saw me standing in the mountains, she would be so small and lonely, she would be worried, so I agreed to the old photographer's choice.摄影师选好角度，支稳机器，指挥着我们摆好姿势。刚要照，果平突然说，慢着，等我一会儿好吗？说完不等别人表态，撒腿就跑。

  The photographer chooses the angle, stabilizes the machine, and directs us to pose. Just about to take a photo, Guo Ping suddenly said, slow down, can you wait for me for a while? After saying that, he didn't wait for others to express his position, and ran away.干什么去？大家问。

  What are you doing? Everyone asked.我得换身衣服。果平回答。

  I have to change my clothes. Guo Ping replied.我用挑剔的目光审查了果平一番，没什么不妥的地方啊，衣服干干净净，脸上也没污点。就说，你像刚消毒完的注射器，清洁极了。

  I reviewed Guo Ping with a critical eye, there was nothing wrong with it, the clothes were clean, and there was no stain on my face. Just say that you are like a freshly sterilized syringe, very clean.果平说，建议你也换换衣服。现在是几月？八月。我们身上穿的是什么？全套的棉袄棉裤，窝囊得像北极熊。这种相片寄回家，我妈掐指一算，什么鬼地方，夏天还会下雪啊？我在信里给我妈描述得这好那好，都会露了馅儿。所以，我得换套单衣，显得精干些。

  Guo Ping said that it is recommended that you also change your clothes. What month is it? August. What do we wear? A full set of padded jackets and pants, nestled like polar bears. This kind of photo is sent home, my mother pinched her fingers, what the hell, will it snow in summer? I described this to my mother in the letter, and it would be stuffy. So, I had to change into a single outfit to look leaner.果平的理由很有说服力，我也想去换衣服了。可老摄影师说，你们是要脸还是要命？这么冷的天，穿着棉衣脚都冻得慌，换单衣，亏你们想得出。只怕照片还没洗出来，你们就躺在床上发烧了。你们并不知道老人的心，以为编一套瞎话，他们就信了？才不是呢！他们会拿着你们的信，反复揣摩，从信瓤看到信封，从邮票看到邮戳，从时间推算路程，心会提到嗓子眼儿。再说，我选景就是再小心，也避不开远处的雪山，总得进到镜头里一星半点，老人是一定会发现的。要是看到你在雪山下面还穿着单衣，认定你不会安顿自己，照顾自己，心就缩成一个硬疙瘩。你寄回照片本来是为了让他安心，结果他更担心。倒还不如穿着棉袄，家里人会想，噢，那里可真冷。不过，孩子知道自己心疼自己……心里反倒安宁些。

  Guo Ping's reasons are very convincing, and I want to change my clothes too. But the old photographer said, do you want face or death? On such a cold day, wearing cotton clothes and feet are frozen and panicked, change into single clothes, you can think of it. I'm afraid that before the photos are developed, you will lie in bed with a fever. You don't know the old man's heart, thinking that if you make up a set of lies, they will believe it? It's not! They will hold your letters, ponder them repeatedly, see the envelope from the letter, see the postmark from the stamp, calculate the distance from time, and their hearts will be in their throats. Besides, no matter how careful I am in choosing the scene, I can't avoid the snow-capped mountains in the distance, I have to enter the camera for a star and a half, and the old man will definitely find out. If you see that you are still wearing a single coat under the snow-capped mountains, and you think that you will not settle down and take care of yourself, your heart will shrink into a hard pimple. You sent back the photos to reassure him, but he was more worried. It's better to wear a padded jacket, and the family will think, oh, it's so cold there. However, the child knows that he feels sorry for himself...... I feel more at ease.我和果平再无话可说，按照部署，各照了一张全身、一张半身的照片。

  Guo Ping and I had nothing more to say, so according to the deployment, we each took a photo of the whole body and a half-body.谢谢。我们向年轻和年老的两位摄影师表示衷心的谢意。

  Thank you. We would like to express our heartfelt gratitude to both photographers, young and old.不必言谢，并不一定成功。万一照坏了，我会通知你们补照的。老摄影师虚弱地说。看来，刚才这一番折腾，耗尽了他的力气。

  Needless to say, it doesn't necessarily work. In case the photo is broken, I will notify you to retake it. The old photographer said weakly. It seems that this tossing just now exhausted his strength.果平说，如果成功了，我们什么时候能看到照片呢？

  Guoping said, if it succeeds, when will we see the photos?那就不一定了。我们还要到边防站去，还有许多照片要洗印。不过，请放心，我们会尽快把相片给你们，让你们的爸爸妈妈看到你们的新样子。年富力强说。

  Not necessarily. We still have to go to the border station, and there are still many photos to be printed. However, don't worry, we'll give you the photos as soon as possible so that your mom and dad can see your new look. Nian Fengqiang said.我和果平，在以后的日子里，怀揣着最美好的想象等待着。我们不敢到招待所去，怕摄影师以为我们催他。他们实在太忙了，我们不忍心再添麻烦。

  Guo Ping and I are waiting with the best imagination in the days to come. We didn't dare to go to the guest house, for fear that the photographer would think we were urging him. They are so busy that we can't bear to add any more trouble.

  有一天，别人带给我们一个纸包，打开一看，正是我和果平的照片。在那块标语牌做背景的照片上，我和果平穿着鼓鼓囊囊的棉衣棉裤，笑得都很开心。

  One day, someone brought us a paper bag, and when we opened it, it was a photo of me and Guoping. In the photo with the placard as the background, Guo Ping and I were wearing bulging cotton clothes and pants, smiling happily.他们呢？我们问。

  What about them? We ask.你们说的是谁？带给我们纸包的人问。

  Who are you talking about? The person who brought us the paper bag asked.就是一老一少的摄影师啊。

  It's a photographer of one old and one young.他们后来又到最高的边防站给战士们照相。加上以前照了没洗出来的活儿，工作量很大。他们连轴转，把所有的照片洗出来，装到袋子里，都写好了名字……后来，他们累得晕倒了，被紧急送回山下。现在，我们按照他们留下的记录，把纸袋里的照片一一分送给大家。来人说。

  They later went to the highest border station to take pictures of the soldiers. In addition, the work that was not washed out before was a lot of work. They turned around, washed out all the photos, put them in bags, and wrote their names...... Later, they fainted from exhaustion and were rushed back down the mountain. Now, according to the records they left, we will give you the photos in the paper bags one by one. The person said.我和果平什么也说不出来，只是朝山下的方向望着。但愿一老一少的摄影师，在充足的氧气里恢复健康。

  Guo Ping and I couldn't say anything, we just looked in the direction down the mountain. I hope that photographers of all ages will recover their health with sufficient oxygen.

未知

  11.胖听

  11. Fat listening

  每月发罐头的日子，是高原的节日。大家聚在司务长的房间里，好像是赶集，七嘴八舌，议论纷纷。人们挑三拣四，乱哄哄的。军用品，质量没得说，主要是选择什么品种水果的问题。

  The day of canning every month is a festival on the plateau. Everyone gathered in the secretary's room, as if they were rushing to the market, gossiping and talking. People are picky and clamoring. Military supplies, there is nothing to say about the quality, the main thing is the question of what variety of fruit to choose.有一个专门要橘子的人，一月是橘子，七月还是橘子。据说他领的罐头从来不吃，都堆在床底下精心保管着。用木板垫起一个架子，罐头像商店陈列的货物，摆得整整齐齐。罐头上还罩着报纸，防着扫地泼水的时候，水珠溅到罐头，铁皮就锈了。大家私下笑话他：这人已经把一棵橘子树的收成，都藏到自己铺板底下啦！后来听说他是准备探家的时候，把橘子罐头都装在麻袋里背回家，让从来没吃过橘子的父母，尝尝南国水果的滋味，人们就不好意思再议论他了。

  There is a person who specializes in oranges, oranges in January, and oranges in July. It is said that he never eats the canned food he receives, and he keeps it carefully under the bed. A shelf is lined with wooden planks, and the cans are neatly arranged like goods displayed in a store. The can is also covered with newspaper, to prevent water droplets from splashing on the can when sweeping the floor and splashing water, and the iron sheet rusts. Everyone laughed at him privately: This person has hidden the harvest of an orange tree under his paving! Later, I heard that when he was preparing to visit his family, he put canned oranges in sacks and carried them home, so that his parents, who had never eaten oranges, could taste the taste of tropical fruits, and people were embarrassed to talk about him anymore.罐头后来有了一市斤和一公斤两种包装，就是一种小筒一种大筒。一般的人都喜欢要大筒的，因为吃起来痛快淋漓，解馋顶饿。再说开罐头的时候方便些，一次解决。要是弄个小筒的，得多费一倍的力气。有个叫小叶的人，偏偏反其道而行之，每次专要小筒。世上的事就是奇怪，大家都不要小筒的时候，司务长巴不得把小筒罐头早点推出去。小叶指名道姓地要小筒，司务长又烦了，说小叶你事真多，大筒小筒还不都是一样吃，到了肚子里一样都化成屎，你不嫌烦我还嫌乱呢！

  Canned food later came in two packages: one pound and one kilogram, which was a small tube and a large tube. Most people like to ask for big tubes, because they taste happy and relieved, satisfying hunger. Besides, it is more convenient when opening cans, and it can be solved at once. If you get a small tube, it will take twice as much effort. There is a man named Xiaoye, but he does the opposite, and every time he wants a small tube. Things in the world are strange, when everyone doesn't want small tubes, the secretary wants to push out the small tubes as soon as possible. Xiao Ye asked for a small tube by name, and the secretary was annoyed again, saying that Xiao Ye has a lot of things, and the big and small tubes are not all eaten the same, and they all turn into in their stomachs.小叶一点也不着急，笑嘻嘻地回敬道，那可不一样。吃豆子拉的是臭的；吃菜拉的是绿的；吃了司务长发的水果罐头，打的嗝儿都又甜又香。

  Xiao Ye was not in a hurry at all, and replied with a smile, that was different. Eating Pacific is stinky; the vegetables are green; After eating the canned fruit from the secretary's hair, the burps were sweet and fragrant.司务长就笑了，说小叶你是属救火队的，叫人发不起火。你可知道，这次来的小筒罐头箱都压在大箱底下，搬动一场，肺里有进的气儿没出的气儿，累得真魂出窍，我不给你当搬运工。真想要小筒，自己动手，丰衣足食吧。

  The secretary laughed and said that Xiaoye, you belong to the fire brigade, so people can't afford to get angry. Do you know that the small canned boxes that came this time were pressed under the big boxes, and they were moved, and there was no air in their lungs, and they were so tired that they were really out of their minds, and I wouldn't be a porter for you. If you really want a small tube, do it yourself, and have plenty of food and clothing.小叶说，我是真想要。可你这儿是“仓库重地，闲人免进”，就不怕我顺手牵羊，多拿了几筒走？

  Xiao Ye said, I really want it. But you are here "a warehouse is an important place, idlers are exempt from entering", so you are not afraid that I will take the sheep and take a few more tubes?司务长说，你不是闲人，是苦力的干活儿。不过，你这么一说，倒是提醒了我，你等大家都领完了罐头，再来忙活你的这点私事吧。一来你可避嫌，二来我也好给你搭把手。

  The secretary said, you are not an idler, you are a coolie. However, when you say this, you remind me that you wait until everyone has finished receiving the canned food before you can get busy with your private affairs. First, you can avoid suspicion, and secondly, I can help you.正好我也在一旁，就说，到时候我来帮忙。

  I happened to be on the side, so I said, I'll help when the time comes.大家就说有人愿意义务劳动，好啊好啊。其实，我心里想的是，库房是个神秘的地方，我倒要看看里头藏着什么宝贝。

  Everyone said that some people were willing to volunteer to work, okay, okay. In fact, what I thought to myself was that the warehouse was a mysterious place, and I wanted to see what treasures were hidden in it.大家领完罐头，已是傍晚时分。吃了晚饭，天就黑透了。小叶叫我去帮忙，库房里黑黢黢的，好像一个阴森的山洞。我嘟囔着说，库房为什么不安个电灯呢？现在我每一个寒毛孔，都充满做贼的感觉。

  After everyone finished receiving the can, it was already evening. After dinner, it was dark. Xiao Ye asked me to help, and the warehouse was dark, like a gloomy cave. I muttered, why is the warehouse uneasy about the light? Now every cold pore of me is full of the feeling of being a thief.也不是打仗需要弹药，谁没事半夜三更时分到库房瞎翻腾？都是小叶这个倒霉鬼，搅得我们不得安宁。司务长手擎一根蜡烛，在一摞罐头箱子后面闪出来，愤愤地说。跳跃的烛光从他的右下颌向左上眉弓闪去，使他那张在白天看来还挺中看的脸庞，顿生凶狠之色。

  It's not that you need ammunition for war, who has nothing to do to go to the warehouse in the middle of the night to toss and turn? It's all Xiaoye, the unlucky ghost, who makes us restless. The chief clerk held a candle in his hand, flashed out from behind a stack of canned boxes, and said indignantly. The jumping candlelight flashed from his right lower jaw to the arch of his upper left eyebrow, making his face, which seemed quite medium during the day, suddenly turn fierce.小叶说，谢谢你啦，我代表家乡的父老乡亲们谢谢你啦。

  Xiao Ye said, thank you, I thank you on behalf of the fathers and villagers in my hometown.司务长说，小叶你别扯得那么远，你老家的人认得我是谁？闲话少说，开始干活儿吧。

  The secretary said, Xiaoye, don't go so far, who do the people in your hometown recognize me? Stop gossiping and get to work.所谓干活儿，就是把顶端的罐头箱子搬下来，垛在一旁，慢慢地寻找不知隐藏在哪里的小筒罐头箱。箱子的外表都是一样的，只是标签不同。我们搬了一箱，用烛光一照，不是，只得把它摞在旁边。又搬了一箱，拿烛光来照，还不是，只好又摆在一边。本来搬几个箱子，对司务长和小叶这样年纪的男子汉来说，不是什么了不起的活儿，但高原这个阴险的魔术师，在人们不知不觉当中，把大家的力气溶解在空气中了。过了没一会儿，他俩就像八十岁的老翁，喘个不停。

  The so-called work is to move down the top canning box, stack it aside, and slowly look for the small canning box that is hidden somewhere. The appearance of the box is the same, but the label is different. We moved a box and lit it with candlelight, no, we had to stack it next to it. I moved another box and took a candlelight to shine, but it wasn't, so I had to put it aside again. Originally, moving a few boxes was not a great job for a man of the age of the secretary and Xiao Ye, but the plateau, an insidious magician, dissolved everyone's strength into the air without people knowing. After a while, they were like eighty-year-old men, panting.司务长鼻孔喷着白汽说，小叶，这是何苦？想体验码头扛大个儿的滋味？

  The chief secretary's nostrils sprayed white steam and said, Xiaoye, why is this bothering? Want to experience what it's like to carry a big man at the dock?小叶说，不好意思，小筒罐头，显得筒子多一些，分的时候好办些。

  Xiao Ye said, I'm sorry, the small tube can, it seems that there are more tubes, and it is easier to do when dividing.我听不懂他的话，说，小叶，你到底什么意思？解释解释。

  I couldn't understand his words, and said, Xiaoye, what do you mean? Explain the explanation.小叶说，我的罐头要带回老家，亲戚朋友多，姑姑舅舅大姨大妈表婶叔伯哥哥……全村人都沾亲。咱从这么远的地方回去，大伙儿都来看我，拿什么招待呢？罐头是个新鲜物，我就每户送上一筒。僧多粥少。也许不该把亲戚叫“僧”，反正就那个意思，嘴多罐头少，要是大筒的就分不过来了，所以……

  Xiao Ye said, I want to take my canned food back to my hometown, I have many relatives and friends, aunts, uncles, uncles, and brothers...... The whole village is close. Let's go back from such a far place, everyone comes to see me, what kind of hospitality should we get? Canned food is a fresh thing, so I will give one tube to each household. There are many monks and little porridge. Maybe you shouldn't call your relatives "monks", anyway, that's what it means, there are more mouths and fewer cans, and if it's a big tube, you can't tell it apart, so ......司务长打断小叶的话说，你就甭“所以”了，我明白啦。休息过来了没有？开始干活儿吧。

  The secretary interrupted Xiao Ye and said, you don't have to say "so", I understand. Have you rested?" Let's get to work.我也开始给他们打下手，终于像挖煤工人一样，在层层叠叠的罐头箱子下面，掘到了一箱标有“每听500克装”的罐头。大家那个高兴啊，就像盗墓贼发现了皇帝的玉玺。司务长用钳子扭开绑箱子的铁丝铁钉，“嘭”地将木板打开，一筒筒雪亮的罐头暴露在烛光下，圆圆的锡铁盖子好像大号勋章，朦胧地反射着一朵朵浅红色的烛火。

  I also began to give them a hand, and finally, like a coal digger, I dug a box of cans marked "500 grams per listen" under the layers of canning boxes. Everyone is happy, just like the tomb robbers found the emperor's jade seal. The secretary twisted the wire and iron nails tied to the box with pliers, opened the board with a "bang", and the snowy cans were exposed to the candlelight, and the round tin and iron lids were like large medals, hazily reflecting light red candles.司务长用调兵遣将的口气说，拿你的吧。

  The chief said in the tone of a troop dispatcher, take yours.小叶欣喜地打量着整箱的罐头，好像那都是他的财产。要知道平常的日子里，你该领几筒，司务长就给你拿出几筒，从未让人如此一饱眼福。现在虽然明知这许多罐头并不是都属于自己，看着也高兴。瞅了半天，小叶指着一筒胖胖的罐头说，我就要那筒了。

  Xiao Ye looked at the whole box of cans happily, as if they were all his property. You must know that on ordinary days, when you should receive a few tubes, the secretary will take out a few tubes for you, which has never been so feast for the eyes. Now although I know that many cans do not belong to me, I am happy to look at them. After looking at it for a long time, Xiao Ye pointed to a chubby can and said, I want that tube.司务长脸上显出很怪异的神情，说，满满一箱罐头任你挑，你为什么偏要那筒？

  The secretary had a very strange look on his face and said, a box full of cans for you to choose, why do you want that tube?小叶费力地把那筒胖罐头从挤得紧紧的箱子里取出，说，满满一箱罐头，我为什么偏偏不能要这筒呢？

  Xiao Ye struggled to take out the fat can from the tightly squeezed box, saying, full of cans, why can't I ask for this tube?我也觉得很纳闷，特别认真地听他们对话。

  I also felt very puzzled, and listened to their conversation very carefully.司务长说，你哪筒都能拿，就是不能拿这筒。

  The secretary said, you can take any tube, but you can't take this tube.小叶的犟脾气上来了，说，我哪筒都不要，就是要拿这筒。

  Xiaoye's stubborn temper came up and said, I don't want any tube, I just want to take this tube.司务长说，我是管发放军用物资的，没有我的批准，你想拿也拿不走。

  The secretary said, I am in charge of distributing military supplies, and without my approval, you can't take it even if you want to.

  小叶一看司务长用职务来压他，正着说是说不过了，反唇相讥道，莫非是司务长看着这筒罐头格外饱满，要利用职权，专门留给自己吃啦？

  Xiao Ye saw that the secretary was using his position to suppress him, and he couldn't say it, and he sarcastically said, could it be that the secretary looked at this tube of canned food that was particularly full, and wanted to use his power to leave it for himself?我想司务长一定会反驳的，没想到，他笑着说，你说得对。我就是要利用一次职权，把这筒罐头留给自己。而且还特地邀请你和我一道品尝这筒罐头。

  I thought the secretary would definitely refute, but I didn't expect him to smile and say, you are right. I just want to use my power to keep this can to myself. And I specially invite you to taste this canned tube with me.我们都不知司务长的确切意思，只见他抽出一把开罐头的专用刀，刚要戳进胖罐头亮闪闪的鼓肚子，突然又停了手，对小叶说，你把它放在自己耳朵边，摇一摇，它就有话对你说。

  We didn't know the exact meaning of the secretary, only to see him pull out a special knife for opening cans, and just about to poke into the shiny bulging belly of the fat can, he suddenly stopped again and said to Xiao Ye, you put it next to your ear, shake it, and it will have something to say to you.罐头会说话？我和小叶吃了一惊。小叶按着司务长说的，把罐头凑在耳边晃了晃。我也赶过去凑热闹，小叶就把罐头递给我。我用力把胖罐头颠来倒去，侧耳细听。它咕嘟着，好像一个不会游泳的胖子，被人开玩笑扔进深潭，套着救生圈，竭力挣扎。

  Can talk? Xiaoye and I were taken aback. Xiao Ye followed what the secretary said, and put the can to his ear and shook it. I also rushed over to join in the fun, and Xiaoye handed me the can. I turned the fat can upside down and listened carefully. It grunts, like a fat man who can't swim, is jokingly thrown into a deep pool, wearing a lifebuoy, and struggling hard.小叶说，这罐头要是一个人，会被你折腾出脑震荡。

  Xiao Ye said, if this can is alone, it will be tossed by you and cause a concussion.司务长问，听到罐头对你说的话了吗？

  The secretary asked, did you hear what the can said to you?我叹了口气说，没听到。

  I sighed and said, I didn't hear it.司务长不急也不恼地说，没听到不要紧，你还可以敲敲它的肚皮，它很诚实，会把刚才对你说过的话，再重复一遍。

  The secretary said without hurry or annoyance, it doesn't matter if you don't hear it, you can still knock on its belly, it is very honest and will repeat what it just said to you.

  这一次，我抢在小叶前面，用手指猛力弹胖罐头。它气愤地发出空空洞洞的回音，好像一个老爷爷被打断了午睡，气得直咳嗽。

  This time, I grabbed Xiaoye in front of him and flicked the fat can with my fingers. It made an empty echo angrily, as if an old man had been interrupted from a nap and coughed angrily.我做了一个鬼脸。小叶不理睬，面带思索之色，好像胖罐头真把什么绝密的情报透露给他了。

  I made a face. Xiao Ye ignored it, with a thoughtful look on his face, as if Fat Can had really revealed some top-secret information to him.司务长对着不开窍的我说，看你这样子，只有让胖罐头自己坦白交代，你才会明白啊。

  The secretary said to me, who was not enlightened, looking at you like this, only if you let the fat can confess himself, you will understand.他说着，果断地用罐头刀扎了下去……只听“噗”的一声响，那动静不像是刺一个没有生命的物体，倒像是宰了一头肥猪。随之一股恶臭从胖罐头的裂口处喷涌而出，蜡烛光都被呛了一个跟头，险些熄灭。那股黑色的气体在仓库内久久盘旋，好像放了一颗催泪弹，我们的眼睛都被熏得眯起来。

  He said, decisively stabbing it with a can knife...... Just listening to the sound of "poof", the movement was not like stabbing an inanimate object, but like slaughtering a fat pig. Then a foul smell gushed out from the crack in the fat can, and the candlelight was choked and almost extinguished. The black gas lingered in the warehouse for a long time, as if a tear gas had been released, and our eyes were squinted with smoke.我说，哎呀，罐头里面藏着一个妖怪？

  I said, oh, there's a monster hidden in the can?司务长说，你过来一看，就明白啦！

  The secretary said, come over and take a look, and you will understand!我躲得远远的，说，不看不看。瞧这味儿，像进了公共厕所。再到跟前看，胃就得来一个反向运动了。

  I hid far away and said, don't look, don't look. Look at this smell, it's like entering a public toilet. Then look at it, and the stomach has to move in reverse.那时候，我们的卫生课刚刚讲到消化器官，正向运动是从口腔到肠胃，反向运动就可想而知了。

  At that time, our hygiene class had just talked about the digestive organs, and the forward movement was from the mouth to the stomach, and the reverse movement could be imagined.小叶比我坚强得多，一声不响地走过去，头俯在那筒胖罐头跟前。正确地讲，那筒罐头现在已经不胖了，垂头丧气地蹲在那里，好像一个饿瘪了的囚徒。我听到小叶喃喃地说……果肉都变成黑色的了……有气体……黄色马口铁的镀膜也脱落了……

  Xiaoye was much stronger than me, and walked over silently, leaning his head in front of the fat can. To be precise, the tube was no longer fat, and he squatted there dejectedly, like a starving prisoner. I heard Xiao Ye murmuring...... The flesh has turned black...... There is gas...... The coating of the yellow tinplate also peeled off......司务长连连夸奖说，小叶你观察得很仔细，对，就是这几个特征。

  The secretary praised again and again, Xiao Ye, you have observed very carefully, yes, these are the characteristics.我还不明白，愣愣地问，我听这些话，好像是在描绘一个病人。

  I still don't understand, I asked in a daze, I heard these words, as if I was describing a patient.司务长说，正是啊！这种胖罐头，按我们军需的行话就叫“胖听”，是罐头中的次品，也就相当于病人了。因为制作或运输中的问题，密封的罐头里面发生了污染，无所不在的细菌大肆繁殖。罐头腐败了，细菌产气，就把罐头的铁皮顶得膨胀起来，变成大胖子。它完全丧失了食用的价值，吃了拉肚子是轻的，碰上肉毒菌，小命就呜呼了。平时我发罐头之前，都要仔细检查，像查特务一般把它们严格挑出来，怕坏了大家的健康。今儿个咱们是到库房里直接取货，你们好眼福啊，看到了平常无缘一见的胖听……

  The secretary said, exactly! This kind of fat canned food, according to the jargon of our military supplies, is called "fat listening", which is a defective product in the can, which is equivalent to a patient. Due to problems in production or transportation, the sealed cans were contaminated and ubiquitous bacteria multiplied. When the canned food rots and bacteria produce gas, the tin sheet of the can is inflated and becomes a big fat man. It completely loses its edible value, and it is light to eat diarrhea, and when it encounters botulinum toxin, it will be dead. Usually, before I send out canned food, I have to check them carefully, and I pick them out strictly like a spiper, for fear of ruining everyone's health. Today, we went to the warehouse to pick up the goods directly, you are so eye-catching, you see the fat listening that you usually don't have the chance to see......小叶不好意思地说，多谢司务长，今天长了见识。本来，我以为胖听比别的罐头更鼓，以为里面装的货色特别多，想让家乡的人多尝尝。司务长，顶撞了，多包涵。

  Xiao Ye said embarrassedly, thank you, Secretary, I gained knowledge today. Originally, I thought that Fat Listening was more bulging than other cans, and I thought that there were a lot of goods in it, and I wanted people in my hometown to try more. The director of the department, confronting me, is more inclusive.司务长说，甭客气啦，快领了你的小罐头走吧。

  The secretary said, don't be polite, take your little can and go.说着，司务长亲自动手，把一堆身材苗条的罐头，推到小叶手里。小叶数了一下，说，司务长，你给我发多了。

  Saying that, the secretary took matters into his own hands and pushed a bunch of slender cans into Xiaoye's hand. Xiao Ye counted and said, Director, you sent me too much.司务长说，这不是骂我吗？我做了多少年的军需，连数都不认？不会多的。快拿着走吧。

  The secretary said, isn't this scolding me? How many years have I been a military quartermaster, and I don't even recognize the number? There won't be much. Take it and go."小叶说，司务长，你再数一遍。我可不愿多吃多占。

  Xiao Ye said, Director, you count again. I don't want to eat too much.司务长说，小叶，我把我这个月的那份也给你了。谁叫你有那么多倒霉亲戚呢！

  The secretary said, Xiao Ye, I also gave you my share of this month. Who told you to have so many unlucky relatives!

未知

  12.拉练

  12. Training

  “拉练”这个词，顾名思义，是“拉到外面去训练”的意思。这个“外面”指的又是哪儿呢？它说的是“屋子外面”。

  The word "pulling", as the name suggests, means "pulling outside to train". Where does this "outside" refer to? It says "outside the house".有人又得说了，屋外有什么了不起的？我们不是常常到屋外活动吗？

  Someone has to say again, what's so great outside the house? Aren't we often outside the house?我说的这个屋外，有几点特殊的地方。第一是时间。它不是春暖花开的三月，也不是赤日炎炎的夏天，还不是金风送爽的秋天……对了，现在只剩下最后一个季节，就是白雪皑皑的冬天了。第二是地点。不是江南，不是塞北，不是平原，是海拔五千米雪线以上的高原永冻地带。

  There are a few special things about this house I mentioned. The first is time. It is not the spring of March, nor the scorching summer, nor the autumn of the golden wind...... By the way, now there is only one last season left, which is a snowy winter. The second is the location. It is not Jiangnan, not the north of the Great Wall, not the plains, but the plateau permafrost above the snow line of 5,000 meters above sea level.什么叫雪线呢？刚听到这个词的人，脑海里会不由自主地出现一条又白又亮的银线，好像是一根由千万根蚕丝拧成的粗绳子，悬挂在险峻的高山半腰。其实，雪线可没那么浪漫，它只是地图上一条假想的线，表示在这个高度以上，积雪和冰川永不融化，寿命与天地同存。在雪线以上的高山行走，随手拣起一块透明的冰块，它的历史都可能超过了一千年，比你爷爷的爷爷还要古老得多。拉练就是让大家到雪线之上露营和自己起火做饭，当然，最主要的节目是行军和真枪实弹的演习。听了动员令后，大家都摩拳擦掌，做着拉练前的诸项准备。

  What is a snow line? When people first hear this word, a white and bright silver thread will involuntarily appear in their minds, like a thick rope twisted from tens of millions of silks, hanging halfway up the steep mountain. In fact, the snow line is not so romantic, it is just an imaginary line on the map, indicating that above this height, the snow and glaciers will never melt, and the life will coexist with heaven and earth. Walking on the mountains above the snow line, picking up a transparent ice cube, it may be more than a thousand years old, much older than your grandfather's grandfather. The training is to let everyone camp on the snow line and start a fire to cook by themselves, of course, the most important program is marching and real gun drills. After listening to the mobilization order, everyone geared up and made various preparations before training.第一要紧的是每人要有一口锅。平常日子都是吃炊事班的大锅饭，自己不用发愁。这回不行了，要野炊，首先得自己备好锅勺。不由得想起一句古话——巧妇难为无米之炊，心想，它说得也不怎么确切，就算有了米，没有锅，巧妇也得抓瞎。

  The first thing is that everyone should have a pot. On ordinary days, I eat a big pot of rice in the cooking class, so I don't have to worry about it. This time it won't work, if you want to have a picnic, you must first prepare your own pots and spoons. I can't help but think of an old saying - it is difficult for a clever woman to cook without rice, thinking that it is not very accurate, even if there is rice, there is no pot, the clever woman has to catch blind.河莲先到炊事班求援。班长说，甭瞎忙活。你们不用备炊具，到时候有我呢。

  Ha Lian first went to the cooking class to ask for help. The squad leader said, don't be busy. You don't need to prepare cooking utensils, I'll be there then."有人自告奋勇帮忙自然好，但不知这忙如何帮法。河莲说，让我先看看你准备的锅。

  It's natural for someone to volunteer to help, but I don't know how to help. Ha Lian said, let me take a look at the pot you prepared first.班长说，我的锅，没什么新鲜的，你天天见，喏，就在那儿。

  The squad leader said, my pot, nothing fresh, you see it every day, oh, it's there.河莲一看，原来炊事班长根本没做特殊准备，打算把每天给大伙烧开水的大铁锅，背出去煮饭就是。

  He Lian saw that the cooking squad leader had not made any special preparations at all, and planned to carry the big iron pot that boiled water for everyone every day and carry it out to cook.河莲说，那怎么行？到时候一安营扎寨，传下号令，就地生火做饭，等你做完了，队伍也该开拔了，我会饿肚子。

  He Lian said, how can that work? When the time comes, as soon as you set up camp, give orders, light a fire and cook on the spot, and when you finish it, it's time for the team to start, and I will be hungry.班长晃着大方脑壳说，我是那样的人吗？要是万一来不及，怎么也得让其他同志先吃，我是享乐在后的。

  The squad leader shook his generous head and said, Am I that kind of person? If it's too late, I have to let other comrades eat first, and I'll have fun later.河莲说，那也不成。你的锅那么大，得多少柴草才能把水烧开？伺候不起。

  He Lian said, that won't work either. Your pot is so big, how much firewood do you need to boil water? He couldn't wait.河莲是我们派出的侦察兵，本以为她会带回好消息，不想无功而返。全班人唉声叹气之时，新情报传回来了，说是经过摸索，有人发明了用罐头盒子做成很漂亮、实用的小行军锅。

  Ha Lian was a scout sent by us, and I thought she would bring back good news, and I didn't want to return in vain. When the whole class sighed, new information came back, saying that after exploration, someone invented a beautiful and practical small marching pot made of a can box.高原海拔高，气压低，饭很不容易做熟。避免夹生的办法，就是尽量提高锅的密闭性，保持住锅里的温度和压力。当然要是有小的高压锅，那是最方便了，可拉练的宗旨就是让大家在冰天雪地里锻炼，哪儿会给大家配锅？不知是谁的创造，用锉刀把罐头盒顶端的焊锡锉掉，使罐头盒盖完整地脱落下来，用的时候再盖上去，一个因陋就简的小锅就成功了。

  The plateau is high at high altitude and low air pressure, making it difficult to cook rice. The way to avoid sandwiching is to try to improve the airtightness of the pot and maintain the temperature and pressure in the pot. Of course, if there is a small pressure cooker, it is the most convenient, but the purpose of the training is to let everyone exercise in the ice and snow, where will everyone be equipped with a pot? I don't know whose creation it was, use a file to file off the solder at the top of the can box, so that the lid of the can box comes off completely, and then cover it when it is used, and a small pot that is simple because of ugliness is successful.我们每人拿出一个水果罐头，开始像手工作坊一般干起来。锉刀吱吱，银屑飘飘。不一会儿，河莲就兴奋地大叫起来，我的小锅出厂啦！

  We each took out a can of fruit and started to do it like a handicraft workshop. The file creaked, and the silver chips fluttered. After a while, He Lian shouted excitedly, my small pot is out of the factory!大家凑过去一看，河莲把罐头盖子平平整整地卸了下来，盖上去的时候严丝合缝，简直像是原装的锅盖。河莲又操起锤子，用小钉在罐头盒——也就是锅的主体部分，钻了两个洞。

  Everyone leaned over to take a look, and He Lian removed the lid of the can flat, and when the lid was put on, it was tightly sealed, just like the original pot lid. He Lian picked up the hammer again, used small nails to pierce the canning box, that is, the main part of the pot, and drilled two holes.我们吃惊地问，这是什么？

  We asked in surprise, what is this?河莲说，这都不明白？拴上铁丝，做个锅耳朵。不然，锅那么烫，谁敢用手提？再说，如何捆到背包上？都是问题。我这是一箭双雕。

  He Lian said, don't understand this? Tie the wire and make a pot ear. Otherwise, the pot is so hot, who dares to carry it with a hand? Besides, How to tie to a backpack? are all questions. I killed two birds with one stone.我们衷心佩服河莲的深谋远虑，锅的制造已进入精加工阶段。低头看看自己手下的活儿，还是粗坯，就赶快提高速度。

  We sincerely admire Ha Lian's foresight, and the manufacturing of the pot has entered the finishing stage. Look down at the work under your hands, it is still rough, so quickly increase your speed.真是见了鬼，我拼命挥舞锉刀，像一个地道的老工人。可我的罐头盒子好像变成一发炮弹，其壳坚硬无比。我累得一脑门儿热汗，它还是岿然不动。

  I really saw a ghost, I desperately wielded a file, like an authentic old worker. But my canned box seemed to have turned into a cannonball, and its shell was extremely hard. I was so tired that I was sweating profusely, but I still remained still.我去找河莲，她成了我们之中的总工程师。真是高人啊，只看了一眼，她就说出了症结所在。你真傻，为什么专门挑了橘子罐头来锉？要知道，它的铁皮质量最好，简直像是不锈钢制成的，难怪你锉不开。像我，选一筒菠萝罐头，又小巧铁皮又软，自然马到成功了。

  I went to find He Lian, and she became the chief engineer among us. What a master, just one look, she said the crux of the matter. You are so stupid, why did you choose canned oranges to file? You know, its iron sheet is of the best quality, it is almost like it is made of stainless steel, no wonder you can't file it. Like me, choose a canned pineapple, small and soft, and naturally succeed.面对先天的失误，除了改换门庭，没别的选择。我立刻加入了“菠萝一族”，其他的操作也和河莲一模一样。经过手忙脚乱的一阵努力，小锅也宣布竣工，同河莲的产品摆在一起，简直是双胞胎。

  In the face of congenital mistakes, there is no other choice but to change the door. I immediately joined the "pineapple clan", and the other operations were exactly the same as Ha Lian. After a period of hard work, the small pot was also announced, and the products of Helian were placed together, which was simply twins.锅的问题解决之后，就是领粮食。规定每个单兵要携带足够三天食用的口粮。按照士兵最低热量标准，共需粮食四斤半。

  After the problem of the pot is solved, it is to receive food. It is stipulated that each individual soldier should carry enough rations for three days. According to the minimum calorie standard for soldiers, a total of four and a half pounds of food is needed.干粮袋是草绿色的，细细长长，瘪的时候好像一段蛇蜕。领导用秤给大家分粮，四斤半大米装进去，粮袋撑得圆圆滚滚，像一条苏醒过来的大蟒。

  The dry food bag is grass green, slender and long, and looks like a snake molt when deflated. The leader used a scale to distribute grain to everyone, and four and a half pounds of rice were loaded into it, and the grain bag was stretched out round, like a big python that had woken up.

  我生平最讨厌吃米饭了，总觉得那些软绵绵的小白粒子，吃多少也填不饱肚子。平日也就罢了，饿了可随时补充零食。可这次是模拟实战，总不能一边坚守阵地，一边嘴巴嚼个不停吧。我对领导说，给我发白面，成吗？

  I hate eating rice the most in my life, and I always feel that those soft little white particles can't fill my stomach no matter how much I eat. It's okay on weekdays, you can replenish snacks at any time when you are hungry. But this time it is a simulated actual combat, and you can't hold your position while chewing your mouth. I said to the leader, give me white noodles, is it okay?不行。领导很干脆地拒绝。

  No way. The leader simply refused.为什么？米面都是碳水化合物，提供的热量卡路里是一样的。我用刚学到的医学知识，为自己做论据。

  Why? Rice and noodles are both carbohydrates and provide the same calorie content. I used the medical knowledge I had just learned to make arguments for myself.在高原上，米可以煮熟。面呢？泡在罐头盒子里，成了糊糊，你怎么吃？领导不理我的卡路里学说，一针见血地指出面的弊病。

  On the plateau, rice can be cooked. Where's the face? Soaked in a can box, it becomes a mush, how do you eat it? The leader ignored my calorie theory and pointed out the shortcomings of the noodles.我宁愿吃那种糨糊样的东西，也不吃米饭。再说红军过雪山草地的时候，吃的也是面粉，不过就是炒熟了而已。我小声反驳。

  I'd rather eat that mushy thing than rice. Besides, when the Red Army crossed the snow-capped mountains and grasslands, they also ate flour, but they were just fried. I retorted in a low voice.领导没想到我会引经据典，一时竟想不出如何批评我，停了一会儿，终于发现了更强大的理由，说，干粮袋就那么长，米能够装进三天的量，面就不行了。

  The leader didn't expect me to quote the classics, and he couldn't think of how to criticize me for a while, and after stopping for a while, he finally found a stronger reason, saying that the dry food bag was so long, and the rice could be filled for three days, and the noodles would not work.我说，不信。

  I said, I don't believe it.领导说，你这个女孩，怎不见棺材不落泪。来，我装给你看！

  The leader said, you girl, why don't you see the coffin and not cry. Come on, I'll pretend to show you!"领导说着，称出四斤半面粉，倒进干粮袋。面比米要难收拾，不少面粉洒在外面，领导就像颗粒归仓的老农，不厌其烦地把每一撮儿面粉都收拾起来，愣往干粮袋里塞。

  The leader said, weighed four and a half pounds of flour and poured it into the dry food bag. Noodles are more difficult to clean up than rice, a lot of flour is spilled outside, and the leader is like an old farmer who returns to the warehouse, tirelessly packing up every pinch of flour and stuffing it into the dry food bag.干粮袋鼓如圆柱，秤里还遗有面粉。在铁的事实面前，我不得不低头服输。同等重量的面，要比米占的地方大。比如说一麻袋可装大米两百斤，装面粉就放不下了。领导告诫道。

  The dry food bag is bulging like a cylinder, and there is flour left in the scale. In the face of ironclad facts, I had to bow my head and admit defeat. The surface of the same weight is larger than the place occupied by the meter. For example, a sack can hold 200 pounds of rice, and it can't hold flour. The leader warned.但我仍不死心，说，具体情况要具体分析。对我的胃来说，三斤面就抵得过四斤半米。

  But I still don't give up, saying that the specific situation needs to be analyzed in detail. For my stomach, three pounds of noodles is worth four and a half pounds.领导说，这不是抵不抵的问题，也不是你的胃说了算的事。你刚才不是说什么卡路里吗？关键是热量，在冰雪高原，你要是没有热量，就得变成白雪公主。

  The leader said that this is not a matter of whether it is worth it, nor is it a matter of your stomach having the final say. Didn't you just say what calories? The key is heat, in the ice and snow plateau, if you don't have heat, you have to become Snow White.我一声不吭地跑出去，过了一会儿，抱着一堆糖进来，对领导说，我不带大米，带水果糖行不行？它提供的卡路里比大米可多多啦。

  I ran out without saying a word, and after a while, I came in with a pile of candy and said to the leader, can I bring fruit candy without rice? It provides more calories than rice.领导这次把脸沉下来，斩钉截铁地说，不成！一个战士不可能在冲锋的时候，往嘴里不停地塞糖！

  The leader sank his face this time and said categorically, no! A warrior can't keep stuffing sugar into his mouth while charging!最后一线希望破灭。虽然他的话也很无理，冲锋的战士不能往嘴里塞糖，难道就可以往嘴里塞米饭团子吗？但人家是领导，咱当小兵的，就只有服从了。

  The last glimmer of hope was dashed. Although his words are also very unreasonable, the charging soldiers can't stuff sugar into their mouths, can they stuff rice balls into their mouths? But they are leaders, and we can only obey as soldiers.衣食住行这句话，我以为很科学。在解决了吃饭问题以后，考虑的就是拉练中的穿了。皮大衣当然是必备的了，要不然，会在酷寒的夜晚冻成冰雕。狼皮褥子也是要带的，在万古不化的寒冰上露宿，没有它，地心的寒气会把我们的五脏六腑凝成一坨。狗毛皮鞋也是要带的，不然会把脚趾冻得指甲脱落。皮帽子当然更得带了，要不，回家的时候会丢了耳朵……我们贴身穿了衬衣衬裤，外面罩了绒衣绒裤，再外面裹着棉衣棉裤，然后披上皮大衣，每个人的体积都比平日增大百分之七十以上，走路的时候像一座毛皮小山在移动。

  I think the phrase "food, clothing, housing and transportation" is very scientific. After solving the problem of eating, the consideration is to wear it during training. A fur coat is of course a must, otherwise, it will freeze into ice sculptures on a cold night. The wolf skin mattress is also to be brought, sleeping on the eternal ice, without it, the cold air in the center of the earth will condense our internal organs into a lump. Dog fur shoes should also be brought, otherwise the toes will freeze and the nails will fall off. Of course, you have to bring a leather hat, otherwise, you will lose your ears when you go home...... We put on shirts and pants, velvet pants on the outside, cotton clothes and pants on the outside, and then put on fur coats.相比之下，住的问题反倒比较简单。每人带一件塑胶雨衣，它的边上有一排纽扣，我以前一直不知是干什么用的，此次经人指教，才知道可以和另外一件雨衣结成一块巨大的篷布，搭一座简易帐篷。每人还要带一把行军锹，到了宿营地，在冰上挖洞，然后把锹把儿埋在里面，就成了帐篷的支柱。

  In contrast, the problem of living is relatively simple. Each person brought a plastic raincoat, which had a row of buttons on the side, I didn't know what it was used for, but this time, after being instructed, I learned that I could tie a huge tarpaulin with another raincoat and set up a simple tent. Each person also had to bring a marching shovel, go to the camp, dig a hole in the ice, and then bury the shovel in it, which became the pillar of the tent.没想到在这个简单的环节上出了问题，因为是两个人合住帐篷，睡觉的时候为了保暖，必须头脚颠倒，打通腿。小鹿是个汗脚，谁都不愿意与她合伙，怕熏着自己。最后还是我高风亮节（谁让我是班长呢），自动表示愿和小鹿同甘苦共患难。大家私下里夸我侠肝义胆，因为小鹿的脚臭让人惨不忍闻。我解释说，其实，我也不是担子拣重的挑，只是想雪地里那么冷，我就不信小鹿的脚还敢出汗？

  I didn't expect something to go wrong in this simple link, because two people shared a tent, and in order to keep warm when sleeping, they had to turn their heads and feet upside down and open their legs. The deer is a sweaty foot, and no one wants to partner with her, for fear of smoking themselves. In the end, it was my high style (who made me the class leader), and I automatically expressed my willingness to share joys and sorrows with Xiaolu. Everyone privately praised me for my chivalrous courage, because the smell of the deer's feet was miserable. I explained that in fact, I don't pick up heavy burdens, I just want to be so cold in the snow, I don't believe that the deer's feet dare to sweat?最后是行。果平穿戴整齐，缓缓地吃力地移出房门，过了一会儿，又像一艘航空母舰似的挪了回来，哭丧着脸道，你们猜，把咱们的全套行头穿起来，负重多少斤？

  Finally, there is the line. Guo Ping was neatly dressed, slowly and laboriously moved out of the door, and after a while, he moved back like an aircraft carrier, crying and saying, "Guess, how many pounds do you carry when you put on our full set of clothes?"河莲说，还不得有三十斤？

  He Lian said, you can't have thirty pounds?果平冷笑道，想得美！改成公斤还差不多！

  Guo Ping sneered, think beautifully! It's almost the same as changing to kilograms!我们花容失色道，你的意思是我们要背着六十斤重的物品，跋涉在冰雪高原？

  We said with a pale face, do you mean we are going to carry sixty pounds of items on our backs and trek on the ice and snow plateau?果平说，那还是少说了，都武装起来，只怕七十斤也打不住。

  Guo Ping said, that's less to say, they are all armed, I'm afraid that they can't stop even seventy pounds.大家半信半疑说，有那么恐怖吗？

  Everyone is dubious, is it that scary?果平说，听我给你们算个细账。

  Guo Ping said, listen to me give you a detailed account.她就掰着手指头，一五一十地算起来。干粮、红十字包、手枪、狼皮褥子、背包、子弹带、行军锹、备用解放鞋、雨衣……我们听到一半，就说别算了，我们信了。

  She broke her fingers and counted them one by one. Dry food, red cross bag, pistol, wolf skin mattress, backpack, bullet belt, marching shovel, spare liberation shoes, raincoat...... When we heard half of it, we said forget it, we believed it.听说行军的平均路程是每日九十华里，个别日子会在一百华里以上，最多的一天将达到一百二十华里。这个数字，对平原来说也许不算什么，但在高原，足以让人胆战心惊。

  I heard that the average distance of the march is 90 miles per day, and some days will be more than 100 miles, and the most day will reach 120 miles. This number may not be much to the plains, but in the plateaus, it is enough to make people tremble.我们能行吗？所有的人心里都在打鼓，可是没有人说出来。谁也不愿被人当作胆小鬼。

  Can we do it? Everyone was beating drums in their hearts, but no one said it. No one wants to be treated as a coward.行军开始了。女兵和男兵一样背负着行囊，像绿色的骆驼在雪原上缓缓移动。为了预防雪盲，临出发时每人又配发了一副墨镜，透过茶色镜片，平日熟悉的风景，变成另外的嘴脸，煞是好玩。冰峰成了咖啡色，远远看去，好像巨大的巧克力冰激凌。白雪成了淡红豆沙色，使人忍不住想舔一口。至于大家的脸色，都成了非洲人的模样，嘴唇成了浓重的黑褐色，好像刚刚吃了炸酱面还没把嘴巴抹干净……

  The march began. Female soldiers carry bags on their backs like male soldiers, moving slowly on the snowy field like green camels. In order to prevent snow blindness, each person was given a pair of sunglasses before departure, and through the brown lenses, the familiar scenery turned into a different face, which was really fun. The ice peaks are coffee-colored, and from a distance, they look like giant chocolate ice cream. The white snow turned into a light red bean paste, making people want to lick it. As for everyone's faces, they all became Africans, and their lips became a thick black brown, as if they had just eaten fried noodles and had not wiped their mouths clean......面对种种奇怪的景色，我们只有自己偷偷地笑，没法彼此交换感想。因为在高原上行军，需要全力以赴，要是你开玩笑的时候，正好一个雪坑没看见，脚下一滑，一个大马趴，大家笑的就不是你的笑话，而是你本人了。笑完了，还得千辛万苦地帮你爬起来。再说那近七十斤重的包袱，稳稳地坐在背上，把肺都压成了薄饼，膨胀不起来，使我们根本没法开怀大笑，只好把笑的念头储存起来，留着晚上空闲的时候再交流吧。

  In the face of all kinds of strange scenery, we can only laugh secretly and cannot exchange feelings with each other. Because marching on the plateau requires all your might, if you are joking, you happen to not see a snow pit, your feet slip, and a big horse lying down, everyone will laugh not at your jokes, but at you. After laughing, I have to work hard to help you get up. Besides, the nearly seventy pounds of baggage sat firmly on his back, pressing his lungs into a pancake and unable to expand, so that we couldn't laugh at all, so we had to store the idea of laughing and save it for the evening when we were free.第一天是适应性行军，有一百华里路程，只翻一座雪山。老兵们说，这简直和玩一样。可女兵们确实没玩过这种严酷的游戏，刚走了不到一半的路程，我们就筋疲力尽。原来为了保护女兵，把我们安排在队伍的中间部分，现在眼看着别人一步步超过我们，越走越远。最后大队人马整体越过疲惫的女兵远去，成了天边的一个黑豆样的斑点。

  The first day was an adaptive march, with a distance of 100 miles and only one snow-capped mountain. Veterans say it's like playing. But the female soldiers really didn't play this harsh game, and we were exhausted after less than half of the journey. It turned out that in order to protect the female soldiers, we were placed in the middle of the team, but now we are watching others surpass us step by step and go further and further. In the end, the brigade as a whole passed the tired female soldiers and became a black bean-like spot on the edge of the sky.我们你看看我，我看看你，明白了以前从书本上看到的一个可怕的词——掉队。那就是你像一粒纽扣，从大衣上掉下来，滚到人所不知的犄角旮旯里。要是没人找到你，你就得在那个黑暗的角落待到海枯石烂。

  We look at me, I look at you, and understand a terrible word I saw in books before - falling behind. That is, you are like a button, falling off your coat and rolling into a corner that no one knows. If no one finds you, you'll have to stay in that dark corner until it's dry.这可怎么办？小鹿几乎要哭起来。

  What can I do? The fawn almost cried.现在最重要的事，是赶上队伍。小如很坚决地说。

  The most important thing now is to catch up with the team. Xiaoru said very resolutely.

  这话当然是不错了。可是，我们赶得上吗？我们为什么会掉队，不就是因为我们追不上大家的脚步吗？赶上队伍谈何容易？不但要赶上部队此刻的行军速度，还要把我们以前落下的补上。恕我悲观，我看是梦想。河莲有根有据地说。因为话太长而且很严肃，说完之后她喘个不停。

  Of course this is good. But, can we catch up? Why do we fall behind, isn't it because we can't keep up with everyone? How easy is it to catch up with the team? Not only to catch up with the marching speed of the troops at the moment, but also to make up for what we left behind before. Forgive me for being pessimistic, I think it's a dream. He Lian said with evidence. Because the words were too long and serious, she gasped after saying them.果平用手揪起背包带子，胸膛能比较自由地吸进更多氧气，说话的时候就可以带出微笑的口吻。她说，你们知道现在最重要的事是什么吗？

  Guo Ping pulled up the strap of his backpack with his hands, and his chest could take in more oxygen more freely, and he could bring out a smile when he spoke. She said, do you know what is the most important thing right now?对于她的重复设问，我们都不理睬。太累了，你打算说什么，快说吧，别啰唆啦！

  We ignored her repeated questions. It's too tiring, what are you going to say, say it quickly, don't be instigated!"果平只好自问自答，说，现在最重要的事，是休息啊。

  Guo Ping had to ask himself and answer, saying, the most important thing now is rest.乌拉！我们立刻用俄语欢呼起来，倒不是对这种语言情有独钟，主要是电影里苏联红军打胜仗的时候，都是这样表达兴奋心情的。

  Ulla! We immediately cheered in Russian, not because we had a soft spot for this language, but mainly because when the Soviet Red Army won the battle in the movie, they expressed their excitement in this way.不管三七二十一，大家立刻倒在雪地上，大口地喘气，先把氧气吸个饱。背上的负重也不敢卸掉，因为再背妥帖很费时间。我们像蜗牛一般，脊梁枕在背包上，头仰得高高，摘下墨镜，看着蔚蓝色的天空。

  Regardless of the three seven twenty-one, everyone immediately fell on the snow, gasped for breath, and took a full breath of oxygen first. I don't dare to remove the weight on my back, because it takes a lot of time to memorize it properly. We were like snails, resting our spines on our backpacks, raising our heads high, taking off our sunglasses, and looking at the azure sky.黄昏已悄然来临，天空急遽地转换着颜色，从海一般清澈的蓝，逐渐加深，好像一缸靛青的染料被打碎了，没有波纹地扩散开来，整个天幕被无声无息地染成蓝宝石的颜色，透明中闪着银光。雪山反射着夕阳的余晖，勾勒出一圈虾红色的轮廓，像是华贵的绸缎织成的剪影。有一只喜马拉雅鹰凝然不动地贴在天际，使你相信在它铁一般的鹰爪下，有一股神秘的高空风，像巨掌一样轻轻托住它的翅膀。

  Dusk had quietly come, and the sky changed color rapidly, from the clear blue of the sea, gradually deepening, as if a jar of indigo dye had been broken and spread out without ripples, and the entire sky was silently dyed the color of sapphire, shining with silver light in the transparency. The snow-capped mountains reflect the afterglow of the setting sun, outlining a circle of shrimp red silhouettes, like a silhouette woven from luxurious silk. A Himalayan eagle stands motionless in the sky, making you believe that under its iron-like claws, there is a mysterious high-altitude wind gently holding its wings like a giant palm.我们要是喜马拉雅鹰就好了。大家齐声说。

  If only we were Himalayan eagles. Everyone said in unison.可惜我们不但不是鹰，连一只最普通的麻雀也不是。我们就这样静静地躺着，感觉万古寒冰的森然阴气，像泉水一般从地心漫上来，渐渐地俘虏了我们的脚，弥漫在我们的关节，浸满了骨髓，笼罩在血液中……一种酷寒而舒适的陌生幻觉，像雾一样包裹了我们的大脑，使它变得像玻璃一般脆而晶莹。我模模糊糊地想到，为什么卖火柴的小女孩，在被冻死以前，会看到那么多美妙的景象，寒冷真是美丽而凄清的神仙世界啊！

  Unfortunately, we are not only not eagles, but even the most ordinary sparrows. We lay quietly like this, feeling the cold yin energy of the eternal ice, spreading from the center of the earth like a spring, gradually capturing our feet, permeating our joints, soaking the bone marrow, and shrouding the blood...... An unfamiliar illusion of cold and comfort that envelops our brain like fog, making it as brittle and crystal clear as glass. I vaguely thought about why the little girl who sold matches, before she was frozen to death, saw so many wonderful scenes, the cold was really a beautiful and desolate fairy world!我们躺着，手拉着手，刚开始很紧很紧，透过皮手套，可以感觉到对方的力量。但是这力量渐渐地涣散下去，骨骼松弛了，血的温度下降了，手套变得像海带一般黏滑，很快就抓不住了，只好彼此松开。我的手刚一接触到雪地，就被它吸了过去，牢牢地粘在冰上。好像手是一块生铁，地是巨大的磁石。我觉得这事有点怪，很想挣脱冰雪的引力。但是没办法，手指根本就不听指挥，它们不再属于我，已经成了绵延万里的冰山的一部分。

  We lay down, holding hands, very tight at first, and through the leather gloves, we could feel each other's strength. But this power gradually dissipated, the bones relaxed, the temperature of the blood dropped, and the gloves became as sticky as kelp, and soon they couldn't hold on, so they had to let go of each other. As soon as my hand touched the snow, it sucked it over and stuck firmly to the ice. It was as if the hand was a piece of pig iron, and the ground was a huge magnet. I think this is a bit strange, and I really want to break free from the gravity of ice and snow. But there is no way, the fingers do not listen to the command at all, they no longer belong to me, they have become part of the iceberg that stretches for thousands of miles.思维变得迟钝而漂浮，苍白无力地混乱运行着，好在一点都不痛苦，也不恐惧，有一种近乎飞翔的感觉……

  The mind became dull and floating, pale and weak and chaotic, but fortunately, there was no pain or fear at all, and there was a feeling of almost flying......你们都给我起来！

  You all give me up!一声断喝，从天而降。我们就是再麻木，也被惊得半坐了起来。只见一彪形大汉，天神般地矗立在面前。

  A broken shout fell from the sky. No matter how numb we were, we were so shocked that we sat up halfway. I saw a big man, standing in front of me like a god.你是谁？我们说不出话，只是用眼光问他。

  Who are you? We couldn't speak, we just asked him with our eyes.我是后勤部收容队的队长。大队人马已经到达宿营地了，到处找不到你们这几位女兵，我们就沿着来路往回找，没想到，你们在这里睡大觉！收容队长怒气冲冲地说。

  I am the leader of the containment team of the logistics department. The brigade has arrived at the camp, and we can't find you female soldiers anywhere, so we will look back along the way we came, but I didn't expect that you would sleep here! The containment captain said angrily.我们懒洋洋地看着他，眼珠也不愿转一下。什么后勤部，什么宿营地，听不懂啦！好像是古代故事里的名词。

  We looked at him lazily, reluctant to roll our eyes. What logistics department, what camp, I don't understand! It seems to be a noun in an ancient story.收容队长很有经验，知道我们已经进入冻伤的意识淡漠期，如果不马上振作起来，就会在这种迟钝的幻觉当中进入昏迷。他指挥带来的收容队员们，把我们拉起来。可是刚把这个从雪地上拉起来，那个又躺下了。把那个扶起来，这个又坐下去。雪地好像一张巨大的软垫子，极力诱惑着我们沉睡在它的怀抱。

  The containment captain was experienced and knew that we had entered a period of frostbite and would fall into a coma in this dull hallucination if we didn't pick ourselves up immediately. The containment team he commanded and brought pulled us up. But as soon as I pulled this one up from the snow, that one lay down again. Lift that one up, and this one sits down. The snow is like a huge soft cushion, trying to tempt us to sleep in its arms.你们还是不是兵了？简直是逃兵！要是指着你们保卫祖国，敌人都得打到家门口！人都说女兵不行，我原来还不信，今天一看，果然不错。应该把你们都开除出去，回家守着父母的热炕头……收容队长怒骂我们，滔滔不绝。

  You are still not soldiers? Simply deserter! If you defend the motherland, the enemy will have to hit your doorstep!" People say that female soldiers can't do it, I didn't believe it at first, but when I looked at it today, it was really good. You should all be expelled and go home to guard your parents' hot kang...... The containment captain scolded us angrily, gushing endlessly.这一骂，把我们骂醒了，自尊心生长起来，神经也变得灵敏了。我们咬着牙，摇摇晃晃地站起来，好像一批女醉鬼。

  This scolding woke us up, our self-esteem grew, and our nerves became sensitive. We gritted our teeth and stood up staggeringly, like a group of female drunkards.快，把她们的背包卸下来！队长命令他的士兵。

  Quick, take off their backpacks! The captain ordered his soldiers.几个男兵把我们的背包放到自己身上。要是平日，我们是一定不会同意的，但在夜色沉沉的雪山上，我们已没有任何反对的力量。

  A few male soldiers put our backpacks on themselves. If it were a weekday, we would definitely not agree, but on the snow-capped mountains in the dark night, we no longer have any opposition.背包一摘走，被压扁的气管立刻膨胀起来，恢复了弹性，我们的精神得了充足气体的灌溉，立刻清醒多了。我们试着走了两步，哎呀，感觉奇妙极了，好像遍地都是弹簧，脚下生风，似乎在飞，无比轻松。

  As soon as the backpack was removed, the flattened trachea immediately expanded, regained its elasticity, and our spirit was irrigated with sufficient gas, and we immediately became much more awake. We tried to take two steps, oh, it felt wonderful, it seemed that there were springs everywhere, and the wind was blowing under our feet, as if we were flying, extremely relaxed.因为我们整天都是在负重七十斤以上的状态中行走，那个附加的重量已经成了身体的组成部分。现在一旦卸下，简直若腾云一般轻盈。巨大的喜悦与轻松，使我们恢复了青春的活力。

  Because we walk with a weight of more than 70 pounds all day long, that additional weight has become an integral part of the body. Now once unloaded, it is as light as a cloud. Great joy and ease restored our youthful vitality.小如说，你们把我们的背包拿走了，多辛苦啊。

  Xiaoru said, how hard it is for you to take our backpacks away.她是一个好心肠的女孩，无论在多么困难的情况下，首先想到别人，总觉得自己对不起别人。

  She is a kind-hearted girl, no matter how difficult the situation, she always thinks of others first and always feels sorry for others.收容队长不耐烦地说，快走吧。我们是男人，比你们的耐力要好多了。再说我们还有马。

  The containment captain said impatiently, let's go. We are men, much better than your endurance. Besides, we still have horses.我这才在黑暗中看到了几匹马。它们美丽的大眼睛闪烁着星星的光芒。

  I saw a few horses in the dark. Their large, beautiful eyes sparkle with the light of the stars.果平说，还是把红十字包和手枪还给我吧。一个是我的工作工具，一个是战士必备的武器。

  Guo Ping said, "Give me back the Red Cross bag and pistol." One is my work tool, and the other is a necessary weapon for a soldier.听果平这么一讲，我们也纷纷要求他们归还这两样卫生兵最基本的标志。好吧，还给你们。可是你们再不许躺下。夜已经越来越深，你们若不能在午夜以前赶到宿营地，就会在雪山上冻死。收容队长严厉地说。

  Listening to Guo Ping's words, we also asked them to return these two most basic symbols of the medical soldiers. Well, back to you. But you are not allowed to lie down again. The night is getting deeper, and if you don't make it to the camp before midnight, you'll freeze to death on the snowy mountains. The containment captain said sternly.我们不再说什么，跟着队长快步向苍茫的远方奔去。也许是长时间的休息，的确让我们恢复了体力；也许是队长的破口大骂，使我们生出雪耻的决心；也许是甩掉背包真的使我们身轻如燕；也许是死亡近在咫尺的威胁，让我们深切地体会到生命的可贵……反正在后面的行军路程中，我们不再说三道四，而是钳闭着嘴唇，机械地迈动双脚，向前向前。

  We didn't say anything more, and followed the captain to run quickly into the vast distance. Maybe it was a long rest that did allow us to regain our strength; Perhaps it was the captain's scolding that made us determined to avenge our shame; Maybe it's because throwing off the backpack really makes us as light as a swallow; Perhaps it is the threat of death that makes us deeply appreciate the preciousness of life...... Anyway, during the march that followed, we no longer talked about it, but closed our lips and mechanically moved our feet forward.赶到宿营地的时候，已经是下半夜了。当我们看到朦胧的灯火时，几乎流出眼泪。好了，总算把你们活着带回来了。收容队长说完，“扑通”一声，差点跪在地上。要知道，为了接应我们，他几乎走了双倍的路啊。

  When I arrived at the camp, it was already the second half of the night. When we saw the dim lights, we almost shed tears. Okay, I finally brought you back alive. After the containment captain finished speaking, he almost knelt on the ground with a "plop". You know, in order to meet us, he has almost doubled the way.

未知

  13.在雪原与星空之间

  13. Between the snowfield and the starry sky

  拉练的夜晚，我们在雪原与星空之间露营。

  On the night of training, we camped between the snowfield and the starry sky.两顶雨布搭的帐篷很窄小，像田野中看秋的农人用玉米秸支的小窝棚。我和小鹿头脚相对，用体温暖和着对方。刚躺下的时候，根本睡不着。平日柔软的被子，此刻变得铁板一样冷硬，被头像锐利的铁锨头，直砍我们的脖子。棉絮好像变成了冰屑，又沉又冷地压在身上。

  The tents set up by two raincloths are very narrow, like small shacks supported by farmers watching autumn in the fields with corn straw. I faced the deer head and toe, warming each other with my body. When I first lay down, I couldn't sleep at all. The usually soft quilt has now become as cold and hard as an iron plate, and our heads are like sharp shovels, cutting our necks. The cotton wool seemed to have turned into ice chips, heavy and cold.这是怎么回事？被子被施了妖法！小鹿在对面瓮声瓮气地说。

  What's going on? The quilt was enchanted! The fawn said angrily on the opposite side.我本想看看她，但沉重的负担使我没法抬起头来。为了保暖，我们把所有的物品，比如十字包、干粮袋、皮大衣，包括毛皮鞋，都堆在被子上面，像一座拱起的绿色坟堆。此刻，要是有一双眼睛从帐篷外窥视我们，一定以为这是军需品仓库。

  I wanted to see her, but the heavy burden prevented me from lifting my head. To keep warm, we piled all our items, such as cross bags, dry food bags, fur coats, including fur shoes, on top of the quilt, like an arched green grave. At this moment, if a pair of eyes peeked at us from outside the tent, they would think that this was a munitions warehouse.我说，被子又不是暖气，自己不会产生热度。它像个水银瓶胆，装进开水它就热，放根冰棍它就凉。我们在零下几十摄氏度的气候里行军，被子的温度当然也是零下了。不能着急，得靠自己身体的暖气，把被子焐热，才会觉得暖和。

  I said, the quilt is not heated, and it will not generate heat. It is like a mercury bottle gall, it is hot when it is filled with boiling water, and it is cold when it is put in a popsicle. We marched in a climate of minus tens of degrees Celsius, and the temperature of the quilt was of course below zero. You can't be in a hurry, you have to rely on the heating of your body and heat the quilt to feel warm.小鹿说，只怕到了明天早上，我们还像两条冻带鱼一样，舒展不开手脚。

  Xiaolu said that I am afraid that by tomorrow morning, we will still be like two frozen hairtails, unable to stretch our hands and feet.我说，反正也睡不着，咱们就说说在高原露营的好处吧。

  I said, I can't sleep anyway, let's talk about the benefits of camping on the plateau.小鹿说，有什么好处？硬要说，第一个好处就是让你不但不困，而且精神抖擞。

  Xiaolu said, what are the benefits? To put it bluntly, the first benefit is that you are not only not sleepy, but also energetic.此话千真万确。不管你行军多么疲劳，在越来越深的午夜中，寒冷的空气好像不是吸入肺里，而是进了胃，化作无数薄荷糖，让你从里往外透出绿色的清醒，神志警觉无比。

  This is absolutely true. No matter how tired you are from marching, in the deeper and deeper midnight, the cold air does not seem to be inhaled into the lungs, but into the stomach, turning into countless mints, making you green sobriety from the inside out, and your mind is extremely alert.我说，可惜这是以第二天的疲倦为代价，要不然，真该推荐所有的科学家都到高原来工作，人类的伟大发明一定会成倍增加。

  I said, unfortunately, this is at the cost of the next day's fatigue, otherwise, I should really recommend all scientists to work on the plateau, and the great inventions of mankind will surely multiply.小鹿说，第二个好处是空气新鲜。城里的空气被人的鼻子滤过千百遍了。这里的空气从没有人呼吸过，就像从没污染过的泉水。你说是不是世界一绝？

  The second benefit is fresh air, Xiaolu said. The air in the city has been filtered by people's noses thousands of times. The air here has never been breathed by anyone, like a spring that has never been polluted. Do you think it's the best in the world?我说，空气倒是很新鲜，只是它里面的氧气含量很少。这就像一种外表很美丽的果子，里面的果仁却又瘦又小。营养太少，中看不中用。
