  I said, the air is very fresh, but there is very little oxygen in it. It's like a fruit that looks beautiful on the outside, but the kernel inside is thin and small. There is too little nutrition, and it is not useful.小鹿说，这话可不对。你敢说这里的空气不中用？那你把头钻进被子里，再捏住鼻子。要是你能支撑三分钟以上，明天我帮你背手枪。

  Xiaolu said, this is not right. Do you dare to say that the air here is useless? Then you get your head into the quilt and pinch your nose. If you can hold on for more than three minutes, I'll help you carry the pistol tomorrow."我说，我当然不敢把头埋进被子，你的脚太臭了。至于手枪，你别卖假人情。你知道规定是人不离枪、枪不离人的。

  I said, of course I don't dare to bury my head in the quilt, your feet stink too much. As for pistols, don't sell fake favors. You know the rule that people do not leave guns and guns.小鹿说，谁的脚要是在这种滴水成冰的时候，还能出汗，一定是赤脚大仙托生的。不信你试试！百见不如一闻。

  Xiaolu said that if anyone's feet can still sweat when the water drips into ice, it must be born of the barefoot immortal. If you don't believe me, try it! It is better to see than to hear once.我不想扫小鹿的兴，就把头缩进被子，但根本不喘气，然后很快地探出头来，说，喔，真的没什么味儿了。

  I didn't want to lose the excitement of the deer, so I retracted my head into the quilt, but I didn't pant at all, and then quickly poked my head out and said, "Oh, it's really tasteless."小鹿很高兴，说露营的第三个好处是，可以增长你的天文学知识。你看，天上的星星亮得像猫眼！

  The deer was happy and said that the third benefit of camping is that it can increase your astronomical knowledge. You see, the stars in the sky are as bright as cat's eyes!我们的雨布虽然薄，但没破洞。只有从两侧的缝隙中，观察星空。铁锹做的帐篷杆和雨布的边缘构成的间隙，很不规则，像是一幅抽象图案。

  Although our raincloth is thin, there are no holes. Only from the gaps on both sides, observe the starry sky. The gap between the shovel tent pole and the edge of the raincloth is very irregular, like an abstract pattern.我说，根本看不到天空的全貌。从我这个角度，北斗七星只能看到一个勺子把儿，牛郎只挑了一个孩子，那个丢了。

  I said, I can't see the whole picture of the sky at all. From my point of view, the Big Dipper can only see a spoon handle, and the cowherd only picked one child, and that one was lost.小鹿说，你以为我这儿完整吗？银河基本断流，蟹状星云变成了对虾的模样。

  Xiaolu said, do you think I'm complete here? The Milky Way is basically cut off, and the crab-shaped nebula has become the appearance of a shrimp.我说，哎哟，真了不起，还知道星云。

  I said, oh, it's amazing, and I know about nebulae.小鹿说，我妈妈最喜欢天文了，从小就教我。

  Xiaolu said, my mother likes astronomy the most and has taught me since I was a child.于是，我们半天都不说话。最后还是小鹿打破了沉默，说我们别说妈妈，那样说一会儿就会流泪的。还是说星星吧。

  So, we didn't speak for a long time. In the end, it was Xiaolu who broke the silence and said that we should not talk about mother, we will cry after saying that for a while. Let's talk about the stars.我赶快拥护，说，就形容自己看到的天和星星的模样吧。

  I quickly supported it, saying, just describe what I saw in the sky and stars.小鹿赶快说，好。

  Xiaolu hurriedly said, okay.想念亲人就像大海中危险的台风眼，我们思维的小船要赶快掉转航向，飞速离开。

  Missing our loved ones is like a dangerous typhoon eye in the sea, and the boat of our thinking must quickly turn around and leave quickly.我摇头晃脑端详了半天说，从我这个角度看天空，它的轮廓像一棵宝蓝色的树冠，树上结着许多银色的榛子。

  I shook my head and looked at it for a long time and said, looking at the sky from my point of view, its outline was like a royal blue crown, and many silver hazelnuts were borne on the tree.小鹿说，从我这边看哪，天空的形状像一件天蓝色的礼服，那几颗最明亮的星星，就是礼服上的银扣子。

  The deer said, from my side, the sky is shaped like a sky blue dress, and the brightest stars are the silver buttons on the dress.我调整了一下姿势，又说，从我的铁锹把儿侧面看过去，天像一扇敞开的钢蓝色大门，星星就是门上凸起的门钉。

  I adjusted my posture and said, looking from the side of my shovel handle, the sky was like an open steel-blue gate, and the star was the raised door nail on the door.小鹿也扭了身子说，我有一个比喻，你可不要笑我。你答应了，我就说。

  Xiaolu also twisted his body and said, I have a metaphor, don't laugh at me. You agreed, I said.我说，只要风和雪不笑你，我才不管呢。

  I said, as long as the wind and snow don't laugh at you, I don't care.小鹿说，从我这儿看上去，天空像极了一头蓝色的奶牛。那些凸起的星星，就像奶牛的乳头，它们离我们这么近，好像一伸手就可以摸着。用嘴吸一吸，就会有蓝色的乳汁流出来。

  The deer said, from my point of view, the sky looks like a blue cow. Those raised stars, like cows' nipples, are so close to us that they seem to be touched with a hand. If you suck it with your mouth, blue milk will flow out.我笑起来，说，小鹿，你是不是饿了或是渴了？

  I laughed and said, Xiaolu, are you hungry or thirsty?小鹿说，你一提醒，我才想起雪原上露营的最大好处，那就是你随时都有冰激凌吃。

  Xiaolu said, as soon as you reminded me, I remembered the biggest benefit of camping on the snowfield, that is, you have ice cream to eat at any time.小鹿说着，伸手到褥子下面去抓，我听到类似野兽爪子搔扒的声音，再以后是积雪被挤压的声音，最后是小鹿咯吱咯吱的嚼雪声和牙帮骨大肆打架的声音。

  The deer said, reaching under the mattress to grab it, I heard a sound similar to the scratching of a beast's claws, then the sound of the snow being squeezed, and finally the sound of the deer creaking and the sound of the teeth and bones fighting.我们的身下，枕着一尺厚的白雪。领导宣布在这里露营以后，我埋头用铁锹拼命挖雪，一会儿就在身边堆起一座小雪山。领导走过来说，你这是干什么？

  Under us, there is a foot of snow on the pillow. After the leader announced that he was camping here, I buried my head in digging snow desperately with a shovel, and after a while, I piled up a small snowy mountain next to me. The leader came over and said, what are you doing?我说，把雪挖走，才能把铁锹埋进土里当支柱，把帐篷支起来。

  I said that only by digging up the snow can the shovel be buried in the soil as a pillar and the tent can be propped up.领导说，你这个傻女子。雪下面是冰，睡在冰地上，明天你的关节就像多年的螺丝钉淋了水，非得锈死不可。

  The leader said, you stupid woman. Under the snow is ice, sleeping on the ice, tomorrow your joints will be like years of screws drenched in water, and they must rust to death.我说，冰和雪还不一样吗？

  I said, isn't ice and snow the same?领导说，当然不一样了。雪是新下的，并不算冷。你没听俗话说过，下雪不冷化雪冷吗？雪底下的永冻冰层，那才是最可怕的。睡在雪地上，就像睡在棉花包里，很暖和的。

  The leader said, of course it's different. The snow is new, not cold. Haven't you heard the saying that snow doesn't get cold when it snows? The permafrost under the snow is the most terrifying. Sleeping on the snow is like sleeping in a cotton bag, it's very warm.

  我半信半疑，但实在没有力气把所有的冰雪都挖走，清理出足够大的面积安营扎寨，只好睡在雪上。这会儿看小鹿吃得很香，不由得也从身下掏一把雪吃。为了预防小鹿汗脚的污染，特地选了我脑袋这侧的积雪。

  I was skeptical, but I really didn't have the strength to dig up all the ice and snow, and cleared up a large enough area to set up camp, so I had to sleep on the snow. At this moment, seeing that the fawn was eating very deliciously, he couldn't help but take out a handful of snow from under him to eat. In order to prevent the contamination of the sweaty feet of the fawn, the snow on this side of my head was specially chosen.海拔绝高地带纯正无瑕的积雪，有一种蜂蜜的味道。刚入口的时候，粗大的颗粒贴在舌头上，冰糖一般坚硬。要过好半天，才一丝丝融化，变成微甜的温水，让人吃了没够。

  The pure and flawless snow at the highest altitude has a honey taste. When you first enter the mouth, the coarse particles stick to the tongue, and the rock candy is as hard as rock sugar. It takes a long time for it to melt and turn into slightly sweet warm water, which makes people eat enough.一时间我们不作声，吭哧哧地吃雪，好像一种南极嗜雪的小野兽。我说，小鹿，你把床腿咽进去半截了。

  We were silent for a while, eating snow like a small snow-loving beast in Antarctica. I said, Xiaolu, you swallowed half of the bed leg.小鹿说，你还说我，你把床头整个装进胃里了。

  Xiaolu said, you still say me, you put the whole bedside into your stomach.我们互相开着玩笑，没想到才一会儿，我和小鹿的身体都像钟摆一样哆嗦起来，好像有一双巨手在疯狂地摇撼着我们，这才感到雪的力量。

  We joked with each other, but we didn't expect that after a while, the bodies of Xiaolu and I trembled like a pendulum, as if a pair of giant hands were shaking us frantically, and then we felt the power of snow.小鹿……我们……不能再……吃下去了，会……冻死。我抖着嘴唇说。

  Fawn...... We...... I can't ...... it anymore If you eat it, you will ...... Freeze to death. I said with a shake of my lips.小鹿回答我，好……我不吃了……我发现，雪是越吃越渴……

  The fawn replied to me, okay...... I don't eat it...... I found that the more I ate snow, the more thirsty I became......我们把自己缩成小小的一团，借以保存最后的热量。许久，许久，才慢慢缓过劲儿来，被雪凝结的内脏有了一点暖气。

  We shrink ourselves into small balls to conserve the last heat. After a long time, a long time, it slowly eased up, and the internal organs condensed by the snow had a little heating.

  我有点困了。小鹿说。

  I was a little sleepy. Xiaolu said.困了就睡呗。我说，觉得自己的睫毛也往一起粘。

  If you are sleepy, go to sleep. I said, feeling my eyelashes sticking together.可是我很害怕。小鹿说。

  But I was scared. Xiaolu said.怕什么？我们的枕头下面有手枪。真要遭到袭击，无论是鬼还是野兽，先给它一枪再说。周围都是帐篷，会有人帮助我们的。我睡意蒙眬地说。

  What are you afraid of? We have a pistol under our pillow. If you really want to be attacked, whether it's a ghost or a beast, give it a shot first. There are tents all around, and someone will help us. I said sleepily.小鹿说，我不是怕那些，是怕明早我们起来，会漂浮在水上。

  Xiaolu said, I'm not afraid of those, but I'm afraid that when we get up tomorrow morning, we will float on the water.我说，怎么会？难道会发山洪？

  I said, how could it be? Will there be flash floods?小鹿说，你是不是感到现在比刚才暖和了？

  Xiaolu said, do you feel warmer now than just now?我说，是啊。刚才我就觉得暖和些了，所以才敢吃雪。吃了雪，就又凉了半天。现在好像又缓过劲儿来了。

  I said, yes. I felt warmer just now, so I dared to eat snow. After eating snow, it was cold for half a day. Now it seems to be slowing down again.小鹿说，这样不停地暖和下去，还不得把我们身下的雪都焐化了？明天我们会在汪洋中醒来。

  Xiaolu said, if the ground keeps warming up like this, it won't melt all the snow under us? Tomorrow we will wake up in the ocean.我说，别管那些了，反正我会游泳。

  I said, forget about that, I'll swim anyway.小鹿说，我不会。

  Xiaolu said, I won't.我说，我会救你的。你知道在水中救人的第一个步骤是什么？

  I said, I'll save you. Do you know what the first step is to save someone in the water?小鹿说，让我浮出水面，先喘一口气。

  The deer said, let me surface and take a breath first.我说，不对。是一拳把你砸晕，叫你软得像面条鱼。你这样的胆小鬼，肯定会把救你的人死死缠住，结果是大家同归于尽。把你打昏后，才可以从容救你。

  I said, no. It was a punch that smashed you unconscious, making you as soft as a noodle fish. A coward like you will definitely entangle the person who saved you, and the result is that everyone will die together. Only after knocking you unconscious can I calmly save you.小鹿说，求求你，高抬贵手，还是不要把我砸晕。我这个人本来脑子就笨，要是你的手劲儿掌握不准，一下过了头，还不得把我打成脑震荡，那岂不是更傻了？我保证在你救我的时候，不会下毒手玉石俱焚。

  The deer said, please, raise your hand, or don't smash me unconscious. I'm stupid in the first place, if you can't control your hand strength accurately, you can't beat me into a concussion, wouldn't that be even stupider? I promise that when you save me, I will not burn the jade and stone."我说，哼，现在说得好听，到时候就保不齐了……

  I said, hum, if you say it nicely now, you won't be able to keep it when the time comes......小鹿说，我们是同吃一床雪的朋友，哪儿会呢……

  Xiaolu said, we are friends who eat the same bed of snow, where will it be......我们各自抱着对方的脚，昏昏睡去。

  We each hugged each other's feet and fell asleep.起床号把我们唤醒的时候，已是高原上另一个风雪弥漫的黎明。我们赶忙跳起，收拾行装。待到我们把被褥收起，把帐篷捆好，才来得及打量一眼昨晚上送我们一夜安眠的雪床。

  When the wake-up call woke us up, it was another snowy dawn on the plateau. We hurriedly jumped up and packed our bags. When we put away the bedding and tied up the tent, we had time to take a look at the snow bed that gave us a good night's sleep last night.咳！伤心极了，我们太高估了人体微薄的热量。雪地上不但没有任何发洪水的迹象，就连我们躺卧的痕迹也非常浅淡，只有一个轻轻的压痕，好像不是两个全副武装的活人曾在此一眠，而是两片大树叶落在这里，又被风卷走了。只是在人形痕迹的两端，有几个不规则的凹陷，好像某种动物遗下的爪痕。

  Cough! It's very sad that we overestimate the meager calories in the human body. Not only did there not show any signs of flooding on the snow, but even the traces of our lying down were very shallow, only a slight indentation, as if it were not two heavily armed living people who had slept here, but two large leaves that fell here and were swept away by the wind. It's just that at both ends of the traces of the human figure, there are a few irregular depressions, like claw marks left by some kind of animal.那是我们半夜吃雪的遗址。
It was the site where we ate snow in the middle of the night.

未知

  14.冰川上有毒蛇咝咝声

  14. The sizzling sound of poisonous snakes on the glacier

  在高原上，爬山是家常便饭。就像你住在六楼，怎么能不爬楼梯呢？在拉练的日子，攀登更是必备的功课，几乎每天都要爬山。

  On the plateau, climbing mountains is commonplace. It's like you live on the sixth floor, how can you not climb the stairs? On the days of training, climbing is a must-have homework, and I have to climb mountains almost every day.爬山的实质，是人和地心引力做不懈的斗争。你用自身的体力，挣脱大地对你的控制，使自己向着太阳升去。如果你背的东西比较多，或者比较胖，那就更倒霉了，你不但得付出和别人一样的努力，还得加倍拼搏。因为那些东西和你多长出来的分量，都像秤砣一般拖着你的腿，逼你后退，你必须像扶老携幼的壮士，带着这些重量一道攀上高峰。

  The essence of climbing mountains is the unremitting struggle between man and gravity. You use your physical strength to break free from the earth's control over you and rise towards the sun. If you carry more things, or are fatter, you are even more unlucky, not only do you have to put in the same effort as others, but you also have to work harder. Because those things and the weight you grow are dragging your legs like scales and forcing you to retreat, you must climb to the peak with these weights like a strong man who helps the old and the young.爬山的时候，喉咙会一阵阵地发出腥甜的味道，好像有一条流着血的小鱼卡在那里。按说，这很没道理，因为爬山时最辛苦的是手和脚。手要紧紧地扒住裸露的山岩，无论多么尖锐的石缝，为了有稳固的支点，你都必须把手指插进去，好像在坚硬的墙壁上钉入十根铁条。脚像螃蟹的爪子，要么尽量向两侧伸展，以扩大身体和山石接触的面积，一旦发生下滑，可以最大限度地增加摩擦力；要么利用脚骨的斜面，把它变成没有知觉的木橛子，深深插入岩缝，就像在巨幅画像下钉两根巨钉，才能保证悬挂着的身体突然坠下时可挽救危局。至于躯干，恨不能生出壁虎似的吸盘，牢牢粘在悬崖上。爬山使人体的各部分紧急动员，所有功能都充分调动起来，肌肉高度紧张，神经分外敏感。此刻的每一瞬间，都执掌着人的生生死死。

  When climbing a mountain, there will be a sweet smell in your throat, as if a small bleeding fish is stuck there. It is said that this is very unreasonable, because the hardest thing when climbing a mountain is the hands and feet. Your hands must grip the exposed rocks tightly, no matter how sharp the stone crevices are, and in order to have a stable fulcrum, you must insert your fingers into them, as if driving ten iron bars into a hard wall. The feet are like crab claws, either stretched to the sides as much as possible to expand the area of contact between the body and the rocks, and once the slide occurs, the friction can be maximized; or use the bevel of the foot bone to turn it into an unconscious wooden stake and insert it deep into the rock crevice, just like nailing two giant nails under a huge portrait, to ensure that the hanging body can be saved when it suddenly falls. As for the torso, I wish I could give birth to a gecko-like sucker, firmly stuck to the cliff. Climbing makes all parts of the human body urgently mobilize, all functions are fully mobilized, muscles are highly tense, and nerves are extremely sensitive. Every moment at this moment is in charge of people's life and death.说起来，喉咙也很要紧，因为它是气道。爬山需要消耗大量的空气，就像前方在打仗，公路上运输的弹药物品就格外多。要是供不上气，手脚必得瘫痪。偏偏高原上稀少的就是空气，喉咙就得拼命工作，那种甜腥的感觉，一定是喉咙的某条微血管崩裂了，沁出鲜血。

  Speaking of which, the throat is also very important because it is the airway. Climbing a mountain requires a lot of air, just like fighting ahead, and there is a lot of ammunition and ammunition transported on the road. If you can't breathe, your hands and feet will be paralyzed. What is scarce on the plateau is air, and the throat has to work hard, and the sweet and fishy feeling must be that a certain microvessel in the throat has broken and blood is pouring out.一天，行军路上遇到一座险峻的高峰。尖兵报告说，曲折的冰崖阻住通路，攀登极为困难。领导给我们每人发了一条登山绳，让死死系在腰上。

  One day, on the march, he encountered a steep peak. The vanguard reported that the winding ice cliffs blocked the passage and the climb was extremely difficult. The leader gave each of us a hiking rope to tie around our waists.干什么用的？这绳子看起来还挺结实。小鹿说。

  What is it for? This rope looks quite strong. Xiaolu said.这是结组绳。你们三个人把它系好，就成了一个结绳组。领导指指小鹿、我和河莲。

  This is a knot rope. The three of you tie it up and it becomes a knot group. The leader pointed to Xiaolu, me and He Lian.什么叫结绳组？小鹿还问。

  What is a knot rope set? The fawn asked again.小鹿，你怎么这么笨？结绳组顾名思义，就是用绳子把咱们三个结成了一组。从今儿后登山时生死与共。要活大家一块儿笑，要死一起成烈士。河莲快人快语。

  Xiaolu, why are you so stupid? As the name suggests, the knot group is to use rope to tie the three of us into a group. From now on, when I climb the mountain, I will share life and death. If you want to live and laugh together, you want to die and become martyrs together. He Lian is quick to speak.领导点头不语，看来河莲解释得不错。

  The leader nodded silently, it seemed that He Lian explained well.那咱们就成了刘关张桃园三结义，恨不同日同时生、但求同日同时死啦！小鹿兴奋得两眼放光。

  Then we will become Liu Guan and Zhang Taoyuan three righteousness, hating that they will be born at the same time on the same day, but they will die at the same time on the same day! The deer's eyes lit up with excitement.领导不爱听，说，这只是万一时的紧急处置措施，不要动不动就说死的事，你们还年轻。

  The leader doesn't like to listen and says, this is just an emergency measure, don't talk about death at every turn, you are still young.河莲思忖着说，要是小鹿掉下去了，还比较好救。她反正分量轻，一把就拽住了。要是小毕嘛，就有点危险，那么重。她要是万一失脚，只怕一个人会把我们两个都拖入深渊，同归于尽。

  He Lian thought and said, if the fawn fell, it would be easier to save. She was light anyway, so she grabbed it with one hand. If it were Xiao Bi, it would be a bit dangerous, so heavy. If she slipped on her feet, she was afraid that one person would drag both of us into the abyss and die together.我说，不就是因为我的吨位比较大，你们就这么害怕吗？好啦，我好汉做事好汉当，要是出现了可怕的事情，一定不会连累你们。我会自动把结组绳解开，和你们脱钩，一个人滑下去好了。

  I said, isn't it because my tonnage is relatively large, are you so afraid? Okay, I'm a good man and a good man, if something terrible happens, it will definitely not affect you. I will automatically untie the knot rope, unhook it from you, and slide down alone.领导说，不许乱讲。真到了那种时候，更要同心协力，两个人的力量怎么也比一个人强。团结就是力量嘛！

  The leader said, don't talk nonsense. When that time comes, it is even more necessary to work together, and the strength of two people is stronger than one person. Unity is strength!河莲说，我和小鹿这就在腰里装些石头，提高自重，救小毕的时候把握大些。

  He Lian said, Xiaolu and I will put some stones in our waists to improve our self-weight, and grasp it more when saving Xiao Bi.我说，不定谁救谁呢！

  I said, maybe whoever will save whom!大家说笑了一会儿，一根绳子让我们格外地亲近起来。

  Everyone laughed and laughed for a while, and a rope made us particularly close.拉练已经进行了许久，我们对爬山也司空见惯。因为第一天行军就出现险情，领导调整了女兵背负的重量，让军马代我们驮一些装备。在后面的行军里，我们基本上可以保证不掉队了。我们自觉已是老兵，对山也有些满不在乎起来。

  Training has been going on for a long time, and we are used to climbing mountains. Because there was a dangerous situation on the first day of the march, the leader adjusted the weight carried by the female soldiers and asked the military horses to carry some equipment for us. In the march that followed, we could basically guarantee that we would not fall behind. We feel that we are veterans and are a little indifferent to the mountains.等到那座陡峭的冰峰矗立眼前，我们才知道，自己又一次低估了山的庄严和伟大。

  When the steep ice peak stood in front of us, we knew that we had once again underestimated the majesty and greatness of the mountain.它横空出世，好像盘古开天辟地时丢下的一根冰棍，高耸入云，经过亿万年冰雪的滋润，长得庞大无比，晶莹剔透。人踏在上面，像一只甲虫爬过，不留一丝痕迹。

  It was born out of thin air, like a popsicle dropped by Pangu when he opened the world, towering into the clouds, and after hundreds of millions of years of ice and snow, it grew huge and crystal clear. People step on it, like a beetle crawling by, leaving no trace.

  队伍拉开距离，开始攀登。小鹿在最前面，我居中，河莲殿后。结组绳松弛地连接着我们，像一根保险索。在通常的时候，它并不影响我们的动作，只是无声地跟随着我们，好像听话的小狗。

  The team distanced themselves and began to climb. The fawn is at the front, I am in the center, behind the River Lotus Hall. The knotted cord is loosely connected to us, like a safety cord. At normal times, it does not affect our movements, but only follows us silently, like an obedient puppy.爬山这件事，在没有出现险情的时候，基本上是你一个人单独挑战大自然。你和大山徒手格斗，每向上前进一尺，都是一个新的回合。你一步一步升高，山就一步一步退却。但山可不是好惹的，嫌你惊扰了它绵延千万年的安静，抽冷子就会给你一点颜色，让你措手不及。要是处置不力，也许就会在瞬息间，以生命作为疏忽的代价。

  When there is no danger, you basically challenge nature alone. You fight the mountain hand-to-hand, and every foot you advance is a new round. You rise step by step, and the mountain retreats step by step. But the mountain is not easy to mess with, if you disturb its quietness that has lasted for thousands of years, it will give you a little color and catch you off guard. If you don't deal with it well, you may pay the price of negligence with your life in an instant.我仰望山顶，上面有松软的冰雪，看起来离我们很近。我想，顶峰上的雪和别处的雪，一定有很大不同。要不然，它们为什么会落在山顶，而不是在山腰呢？就像深海和浅海的鱼是不一样的，高山上的雪更神秘。我一定要尝尝山顶上的雪。

  I looked up at the top of the mountain, with soft snow and ice on it, which seemed very close to us. I think the snow at the top must be very different from the snow elsewhere. Otherwise, why would they land on the top of the mountain, and not on the mountainside? Just like fish in the deep sea and shallow sea are different, the snow in the mountains is more mysterious. I must taste the snow on the top of the mountain.我们爬啊爬，谁也不说话。不是不想说，是不能说。因为一说话，分散注意力，容易发生意外。还有一个原因，雪像音乐厅里特制的墙壁一样，有很好的吸音效果，让你的声音像蒙在棉絮里呻吟一样，传不远，说起来很吃力。但是冰多的地方，又当别论。平滑的冰是音响良好的反射体，相当于大理石板，会使你的声音发出清澈的回音。我们此刻能发出的最大声音，是不停的喘息声。

  We crawled and crawled, and no one spoke. It's not that I don't want to say it, it's that I can't say it. Because when you speak, you are distracted and prone to accidents. Another reason is that snow, like a special wall in a concert hall, has a good sound absorption effect, making your voice like moaning in cotton wool, not far away, and it is very difficult to say. But where there is a lot of ice, it should be a different matter. Smooth ice is a good acoustic reflector, equivalent to a marble slab, that will make your voice echo clear. The loudest sound we can make at the moment is a constant gasp.爬啊爬，距离山顶好像只有五十米的距离了。我们费尽千辛万苦爬过这段距离，发现山顶还骄傲地耸立在五十米之外，漠然地俯视着我们。高原上稀薄的空气发生折射，使距离感变得虚无缥缈，引人错觉。我们并不懊丧，只是坚忍地向前，向上……爬山很能锻炼人的耐力，在攀登的队伍中，你像一支射出的箭，只能一往无前地努力挺进，绝无后退的可能。

  Climbing and climbing, it seems that it is only fifty meters away from the top of the mountain. We struggled to climb this distance and found that the top of the mountain still towered proudly fifty meters away, looking down on us indifferently. The thin air on the plateau is refracted, making the sense of distance ethereal and illusory. We are not frustrated, we just move forward and upward with perseverance...... Climbing a mountain is very good for exercising people's endurance, and in the climbing team, you are like an arrow shot, you can only work hard to advance, and there is no possibility of retreating.我看见有一些鲜红色的小珠子，从我的嘴边滚落。我知道那是我把嘴唇咬破了，鲜血流了出来，马上又被严寒冻成固体。我一直不由自主地咬着嘴唇，好像那样就可以使自己积聚力量，保持高度的警觉，提高对付突然危险的能力。

  I saw some small bright red beads rolling down from my mouth. I knew it was me who bit my lip, blood flowed out, and I was immediately frozen into a solid by the cold. I have been biting my lip involuntarily, as if that would allow me to accumulate strength, maintain a high level of alertness, and improve my ability to deal with sudden danger.在攀登中，人的思想变得很单一，就是抓牢山岩，不要被山甩下来。这样爬得久了，容易想别的事情。我想，祖先创造“爬”这个字，真是英明。它原本一定是预备形容野兽用的，爪和巴，表示所有的爪子，都紧紧地巴在地上，才能完成这个动作。我想，我的二十根脚趾和手指，都是大功臣。假如没有它们劳苦功高地揪住山的毫毛，我一定像块圆圆的鹅卵石，叽里咕噜地滚到山涧里去了……

  In climbing, people's thoughts become very simple, that is, to hold on to the mountains and rocks and not be thrown off by the mountains. After climbing like this for a long time, it is easy to think about other things. I think it is really wise for our ancestors to create the word "climb". It must have been used to describe the beast, the claws and the bark, indicating that all the claws were firmly pressed to the ground to complete this action. I think my twenty toes and fingers are all great heroes. If it weren't for their hard work to grab the hairs of the mountain, I would have rolled into the mountain stream like a round pebble......在我们就要到达山顶之前，我突然听到一种奇怪已极的“咝咝”声，好像毒蛇的舌头在搅拌空气。当然，这是绝不可能的，阿里高原因为酷寒，是没有蛇的。就算有蛇，也绝不可能在冰天雪地里生存。恐怖的声音到底来自何方？没容我思索，腰间仿佛挨了致命的一击，猛地抽紧，勒得我喘不过气，一股螺旋般的下坠力量，像龙卷风一样吸住了我，裹着我迅猛地向山底滑去。

  Before we reached the top of the mountain, I suddenly heard a strange "sizzle" sound, like the tongue of a poisonous snake stirring the air. Of course, this is absolutely impossible, Ali Gao is cold and there are no snakes. Even if there are snakes, it is absolutely impossible to survive in the ice and snow. Where does the sound of horror come from? Without allowing me to think, my waist seemed to have been hit with a fatal blow, and it tightened violently, strangling me to the point of breathlessness, and a spiral of falling force sucked me like a tornado, wrapping me around me and sliding rapidly towards the bottom of the mountain.我在极端的恐惧中明白了——那毒蛇般的声音，是结组绳快速收紧、摩擦冰面的响声。河莲遇到了巨大的危险，正在滑向深渊。随即我看到小鹿在我的上方，也被绳揪动，开始了危险的下滑。

  I understood in extreme fear - the sound of a poisonous snake was the sound of a knot tightening quickly and rubbing against the ice. Ha Lian is in great danger and is sliding into the abyss. Then I saw the fawn above me, also pulled by the rope, and began a dangerous descent.这就是结组绳的力量。它把我们三个联成一个统一的生死与共的集体。要么共赴深渊，要么同挽狂澜。

  This is the power of knotted ropes. It unites the three of us into a unified collective of life and death. Either go to the abyss together, or turn the tide together.稳住！一定要稳住！我听见河莲在喊，小鹿在喊，我也在喊……其实，那一瞬什么声音也没有，只是我们生命的本能在发出共鸣。我们被惯性拖着向下滑，就像坐滑梯，越到后面力量越大。当务之急是拦住我们的身体，阻止致命的下滑。

  Steady! Be sure to hold on! I heard He Lian shouting, Xiaolu shouting, and I was shouting...... In fact, there was no sound at that moment, just the instincts of our lives were resonating. We are dragged down by inertia, like sitting on a slide, and the further we go, the more powerful we become. The priority is to stop our bodies and stop the fatal slide.我们每个人都像八脚章鱼一般，拼命扩大自己与山体接触的面积，以增加摩擦力。见到任何一条岩缝，都毫不犹豫地把手脚插进去，鲜血直流却毫无知觉。脚蹬掉一块又一块石头和冰块，听它们发出震耳欲聋的轰鸣声。七手八脚飞快地做着霹雳舞中类似擦窗户的动作，由于极度奋力，动作扭曲得可怕。我们甚至把脸也紧紧地贴在冰面上，利用凸起的鼻子和眉毛，使身体滑动的速度减慢……

  Each of us, like an eight-legged octopus, desperately expands the area of contact with the mountain in order to increase friction. When they saw any rock crevice, they did not hesitate to insert their hands and feet in, and the blood flowed without any consciousness. Kick off rocks and ice blocks and listen to their deafening roars. The seven hands and eight feet quickly did the movement similar to cleaning the window in breakdancing, and due to the extreme effort, the movements were terribly distorted. We even pressed our faces tightly to the ice, using our raised noses and eyebrows to slow down the sliding of our bodies......终于，恐怖悲惨的下滑停止了。河莲被一块冰凌阻挡在半山，我们从死神手里赢回了关键的一局。

  Finally, the terrifying and tragic decline stopped. He Lian was blocked halfway up the mountain by a piece of ice, and we won a crucial game from the god of death.

  我们彼此看了看，脸色都像铁一般，冰冷坚硬。擦破的地方并没有鲜血流出，它们被冻住了，成了淡红色的冰。哈！我们还活着！这是多么值得庆贺的事情啊！我们揉揉脸上冻僵的肌肉，彼此做个鬼脸。我抖了一下结组绳，沾满冰凌的绳子发出嘣嘣的声响，好像一根巨大的琴弦，也在为我们高兴地叹息。

  We looked at each other, our faces were like iron, cold and hard. No blood flowed out of the scratched places, they were frozen and turned into reddish ice. Ha! We are alive! What a celebration it is! We rubbed the frozen muscles on our faces and grimaced at each other. I shook the knotted rope, and the ice-covered rope made a banging sound, like a huge string, sighing happily for us.剩下的事，就是继续攀登。经历了一次生与死的模拟演习，我们更小心地珍惜生的权利。

  The rest is to continue climbing. After a life and death simulation, we cherish the right to life more carefully.爬啊爬……我几乎已经不去想顶峰的事了，只是机械地爬……突然，眼前一亮。整整几个小时，我的眼帘里除了冰雪还是冰雪，我们已经忘记了世界上还有其他的颜色。一片极大的蔚蓝色，像大鸟的羽毛，无声地将我覆盖。阳光温暖地抚摸着我的额头，把一种让人流泪的关怀，从九天之上无边无际地倾倒下来。

  Climb, climb...... I hardly think about the summit anymore, just climbing mechanically...... Suddenly, his eyes lit up. For a few whole hours, I had forgotten that there were other colors in the world besides ice and snow. A great azure blue, like the feathers of a big bird, silently covered me. The sun warmly caressed my forehead, pouring a tear-jerking care down from the top of the nine days.啊，顶峰到了！

  Ah, the peak has arrived!顶峰是很小的一块地方，眼前一片凄凉的空寂，什么也没有。不，不对，这里有太阳和风。太阳在比你更高的地方，孤单地悬挂着，等着你来作伴。风几乎是和你一般高矮，掠着你的肩膀和头发飞过，好像要把你征服山的消息带到远方。我捏了一小撮儿雪，没敢取太多。我想山顶上的雪，必有一种神圣的魔力，我应该给其他登上山顶的人留一些。伸出舌头舔了一下，遗憾得很，山顶的雪和别的地方的雪，味道是一样的。如果一定要找出它有什么不同，那就是有一点咸、有一点甜，那是我咽喉的血混到里面了。

  The summit is a very small place, and there is a desolate emptiness in front of it, with nothing. No, no, there is sun and wind here. The sun hangs alone higher than you, waiting for you to come as a companion. The wind is almost as tall as you, flying over your shoulders and hair, as if to bring the news of your conquest of the mountain into the distance. I pinched a small handful of snow and didn't dare to take too much. I think the snow on the top of the mountain must have a divine magic, and I should save some for others who climb to the top of the mountain. I stretched out my tongue and licked it, regretting that the snow on the top of the mountain tasted the same as the snow elsewhere. If I had to find out what was different about it, it was a little salty and a little sweet, and it was the blood in my throat mixed in.我站在山顶的时候，小鹿在下山的路上，河莲在上山的路上，结组绳像金字塔的两条边长，山顶暂时成为它的制高点。我轻轻抽了抽绳子，她们都感觉到了，给了我一个回应。

  When I stood on the top of the mountain, the deer was on the way down, and the river lotus was on the way up, and the rope was like the two sides of a pyramid, and the top of the mountain temporarily became its commanding heights. I gently pulled the rope, and they all felt it and gave me a response.我感觉到这是我们的生命之绳。山是不能征服的，我们爬上了山，我们又迅速地离开了山。我们只是山的匆匆过客。当我们还不曾来到这个世界的时候，山就存在了。在我们已经不存在的将来，山依然存在。和山相比，我们是那样渺小，可人也是很伟大的，以我们渺小的身躯，由于努力和团结，我们终于也有一瞬，站得比山更高，群山匍匐在我们脚下。

  I feel that this is our lifeline. The mountain cannot be conquered, we climbed the mountain, and we quickly left the mountain. We are just passers-by in a hurry in the mountains. When we have not yet come to this world, mountains exist. In a future where we no longer exist, the mountains still exist. Compared with the mountains, we are so small, but people are also very great, with our small bodies, because of hard work and unity, we finally have a moment, standing higher than the mountains, and the mountains are crawling at our feet.我又向四周张望了一下，然后下山。不知为什么，登上山以后，人很容易感到心里空荡荡的，好像把一种很宝贵的东西安放在雪山之巅了。

  I looked around again and went down the mountain. For some reason, after climbing the mountain, it is easy for people to feel empty in their hearts, as if they have placed a very precious thing on the top of the snow-capped mountain.

  我们默默地下着山，不断地对付着险情。俗话说，上山容易下山难。上山的时候，容易避开危险。下山则不然，脚心也没长眼睛，一不小心就出问题，有几次我失足下滑，要不是结组绳帮助，也许就会像在幼儿园滑滑梯一样，一直滑到雪山的肚子里，再也不见天日。

  We went down the mountain silently, constantly dealing with danger. As the saying goes, it's easy to go up the mountain, it's hard to go down. When going up the mountain, it is easy to avoid danger. If it weren't for the help of the knot rope, maybe it would be like a slide in kindergarten, all the way to the belly of the snow-capped mountain, and never see the light of day again.下了山，重新回到坚实的土地上，我们把结组绳解开，回头仰望高山，几乎不相信我们用自己的双脚，把它一尺尺量过。但结组绳上的冰雪可以作证，我们以集体的力量，曾经到达过怎样的高度。

  Coming down the mountain and returning to the solid ground, we untied the knot rope and looked back at the mountain, almost believing that we had measured it with our feet. But the ice and snow on the knot rope can testify to what heights we have reached with collective strength.

未知

  15.信使

  15. Messenger

  我十七岁的生日，是在藏北高原过的。那天，正好是军邮车上山的日子，这个生日便像美丽的项圈，久久地悬挂在我胸前。

  My seventeenth birthday was on the northern Tibetan plateau. That day happened to be the day when the military mail truck went up the mountain, and this birthday was like a beautiful collar, hanging on my chest for a long time.喜马拉雅山、冈底斯山、喀喇昆仑山，像三柄巨大的棱锥，将我所在的部队，托举到了离海平面五千多米的高度。我的生日在十月，这正是平原上麦秸垛金黄而干燥的时光，昆仑山却已万里雪飘。就要封山了，封山是冰雪发出的禁令，我们将与世隔绝到春天。

  The Himalayas, Gangdis, and Karakoram Mountains, like three huge pyramids, lifted my troops to a height of more than 5,000 meters above sea level. My birthday is in October, which is the golden and dry time when the wheat straw stacks on the plain are golden and dry, but the Kunlun Mountains are already snowing for thousands of miles. The mountain is about to be closed, and the mountain closure is a ban issued by ice and snow, and we will be isolated from the world until spring.战友们把水果罐头汁倾倒在茶褐色的刷牙缸里，彼此碰得山响，向我祝贺。对于每月只有一筒半罐头的我们来说，这是一场盛大的庆典。

  The comrades-in-arms poured canned fruit juice into the tea-brown toothbrushing jar, bumped into each other, and congratulated me. It was a grand celebration for those of us who only had one and a half cans per month.但心中总有淡淡的悲愁——我想家。

  But there is always a faint sadness in my heart - I miss home.一位白发苍苍的老医生对我说，也许军邮车今天会来的。

  A gray-haired old doctor said to me, maybe the military mail truck will come today.你骗人！我大叫。有时候猛烈地指责别人说谎，其实是太渴望那消息真实。

  You liar! I screamed. Sometimes I violently accuse others of lying, but I am actually too eager for the truth of the news.军邮车大约每月从新疆喀什开上昆仑山一次，日子并不准，仿佛一只来去无踪的青鸟。老医生戍边多年，他的话有时像符咒一样灵验。“每年封山前上山的最后一辆车，总是军邮车。山下的人都知道我们的心。他晃着满头的白发，像一丛银针。”

  The military mail truck drives up the Kunlun Mountains from Kashgar, Xinjiang about once a month, and the days are not accurate, like a blue bird coming and going without a trace. The old doctor has been stationed at the border for many years, and his words are sometimes as effective as charms. "The last car that goes up the mountain before the mountain is closed every year is always a military mail car. People below the mountain know our hearts. He shook his head full of white hair, like a cluster of silver needles. ”那天夜里，军邮车像破冰船一样，跋涉五天，英勇地到了，整个军营为之沸腾。我们真想欢呼，但军人只有打了胜仗才允许欢呼，于是我们屏住气盯着一处房舍。房舍门口站着两个威武的士兵。因为曾有一次，迫不及待的边防军人们跑去抢信，从此在军邮车到来的日子，分拣信件的房间便加站双岗。

  That night, the military mail car traveled like an icebreaker for five days and arrived heroically, and the entire barracks were boiling. We really wanted to cheer, but the soldiers were only allowed to cheer if they won the battle, so we stared at a house with bated breath. There were two mighty soldiers standing at the door of the house. Because once, the border guards who couldn't wait ran to grab the letter, and since then, on the day the military mail car arrived, the room for sorting letters has been double-posted.各单位取信的人站在房外，一取到信就像古代的驿马接到加急文书，拔腿就跑，去把信件送给望眼欲穿的人们。

  The people who took the letter from each unit stood outside the room, and as soon as they received the letter, it was like an ancient stagecoach who received an urgent document and ran away to deliver the letter to the people who were looking forward to it.在高原上奔跑，不是一件轻松的事。这活儿一般都分给腰细腿长的年轻人，但白发苍苍的老医生执拗地要做这件事。知情的人私下里说，他家中有很老的双亲、很弱的妻子、很小的孩儿，想信比别人更甚。

  Running on the plateau is not an easy task. This work is usually given to young people with thin waists and long legs, but the gray-haired old doctor stubbornly wants to do it. People in the know privately said that he had very old parents, a very weak wife, and a very young child in his family, and he wanted to believe more than others.老医生说，有一年封山的时间格外长。半年后军邮车首次上山，信件一直摞到分拣人的胸前。他们在信海中游走，呼吸都很困难。

  The old doctor said that there was a year when the mountain was closed for an exceptionally long time. Half a year later, the military mail truck went up the mountain for the first time, and the letters were stacked all the way to the chest of the sorter. They swim in the sea of letters and have difficulty breathing.

  老医生抱着一大摞信，我们扑上去抢。那时候干部去干校，知青接受再教育，妻离子散的多，信件也格外多。每个人都像蜘蛛一样，吐出思念思索的长丝，织一张自己的情感信息之网。

  The old doctor was holding a large stack of letters, and we rushed to grab them. At that time, cadres went to cadre schools, educated youths received re-education, and their wives and children were separated, and there were many letters. Everyone is like a spider, spitting out the filaments of longing and thinking, weaving their own web of emotional information.霎时老医生手中就空了，接下来是唰唰地撕信，信皮的断屑萧萧而下。

  In an instant, the old doctor's hand was empty, and then he tore up the letter, and the crumbs of the letter skin fell down.我最先看的是父母的信。仿佛有一只温暖而柔软的手，从洁白的笺纸中探出来，抚摸着我额前飘动的乌发，心便不再凄然。

  The first thing I read was a letter from my parents. It was as if a warm and soft hand came out of the white paper and stroked the black hair fluttering on my forehead, and my heart was no longer sad.再看同学和朋友的信。我的同桌此刻在遥远的西双版纳，信中夹了一朵花的标本。她说这是景洪最美丽的花，有沁人肺腑的香气。夹花的那页信纸留有大片紫色的痕液，想象得出花盛开时的娇嫩。我低头嗅那被花汁浸泡过的地方，哪儿有什么香气，有的只是纯正而凛冽的冰雪气息缭绕其中。

  Look at the letters from classmates and friends. My deskmate is in Xishuangbanna in the distance at the moment, and the letter contains a specimen of a flower. She said that this is the most beautiful flower in Jinghong, with a refreshing aroma. The page of letter paper with flowers left a large purple trace, which can be imagined as delicate when the flowers bloom. I looked down and smelled the place soaked in flower juice, where there was any aroma, and some were just pure and awe-inspiring ice and snow scents lingering in it.我连夜回信。平常日子，营区是柴油发电机供电，每晚只亮两个小时，然后就像木偶人似的眨几下眼睛，熄灭了。军邮车一来，首长便传令延长发电时间，以利于拣信和回信。首长其实也很盼信到来。

  I replied overnight. On normal days, the camp is powered by diesel generators, which only light up for two hours a night, and then blinks a few times like a puppet and goes out. As soon as the military mail truck arrived, the chief ordered the power generation time to be extended to facilitate the selection and reply to letters. The chief is actually looking forward to the arrival of the letter.同屋的女兵嘤嘤地哭了起来。她的小侄子病了。我们都放下笔去劝她。然而，女孩子常常是这样：越劝哭得越欢畅。

  The female soldier in the same room cried. Her little nephew is sick. We all put down our pens to persuade her. However, girls often do this: the more they persuade them to cry, the happier they become.老医生悠长地叹了一口气，告诉离得这么远的一个小姑娘，孩子的病就能好了吗？我家里人是从不这样的。

  The old doctor sighed long and told a little girl who was so far away that the child's illness would be cured. My family has never been like this.不一会儿，女兵停止了哭泣，因为从老医生送来的第二批信中，她得知小侄子的病已经好了。

  After a while, the female soldier stopped crying, because from the second batch of letters sent by the old doctor, she learned that her little nephew's illness had been cured.要有经验，老医生说，把信全拆开，码饼干似的排好，从最后面的看起，前面的只能做参考。

  To have experience, the old doctor said, open all the letters, arrange them like biscuits, and look at the back from the back, and the front can only be used as a reference.这自然是至理名言。这么办，时间长了，我们也发现了弱点。好比一本荡气回肠的小说，快刀斩乱麻先看了结尾，再回过头去细细咀嚼，便少了许多悬念和曲折。

  This is naturally a wise saying. After a long time, we also found weaknesses. It's like a heart-wrenching novel, if you read the end of the mess quickly, and then go back and chew it carefully, there will be a lot less suspense and twists.那一次军邮车上山，老医生没有收到一封信。按照他们家的逻辑，没有信来也许就是出事了。他的忧郁持续了整个冬天。

  That time the military mail truck went up the mountain, and the old doctor did not receive a letter. According to their family's logic, if there is no letter, something may have happened. His melancholy lasted all winter.在这海拔五千米的高原营地，每逢有人下山，就会挨门挨户地问，我要走了，要不要带信？哪怕是平日最自私的人，在这件事上也绝对平和而周到，这是高原的风俗。

  In this plateau camp at an altitude of 5,000 meters, whenever someone goes down the mountain, they will go door to door and ask, I am leaving, do you want to bring a letter? Even the most selfish people on weekdays are absolutely peaceful and thoughtful in this matter, which is the custom of the plateau.有时候突然写好一封信，又不知谁能带走，就在吃饭人多时喊，谁能下山，告我一声。一次，一个素不相识的人对我说，我知道你父亲的名字。你看过我的档案？我问。不是。几年前我为你代发过家信。我已经完全记不得是托什么人又转到他手中的，于是赶忙表示迟到的谢意。

  Sometimes I suddenly write a letter, and I don't know who can take it away, so I shout when there are many people eating, who can go down the mountain and tell me. Once, a stranger said to me, I know your father's name. Have you seen my profile? I asked. No. A few years ago, I sent you a letter from home. I couldn't remember who I asked to pass it on to him, so I hurriedly expressed my gratitude for being late.在我十七岁生日过去半年的时候，收到了西双版纳同学的回信，那朵花怎么是紫色的呢？它是雪白的呀！而且，绝不可能没有香气！

  Half a year after my seventeenth birthday, I received a reply from a classmate in Xishuangbanna, why is that flower purple? It's snow-white! Moreover, it is impossible to be without aroma!信是老医生送来的。这是开山后的第一次通邮，他也很快乐，他的家里寄来了平安信。有时候他又突然疑惑，说他家会不会有什么事瞒了不肯告诉他。我们都说不会不会，你是家里的顶梁柱，他们离了你，根本就办不了事，怎么会瞒你！他也觉得很有道理，心宽许多。

  The letter was sent by the old doctor. This was the first mail call since the opening of the mountain, and he was also very happy, and a letter of peace was sent from his home. Sometimes he suddenly wondered, saying that his family would hide something and refused to tell him. We all said no, no, you are the pillar of the family, they can't do anything without you, how can they hide it from you! He also felt that it made sense and was much more relaxed.终于，轮到他探家了。很早就告诉我们：他下山时专门预备一个提包，为大家装信。我便对着昆仑山皑皑的冰雪，咬着笔杆，从从容容地写了大约三十封信，每一封都竭尽我的才能。

  Finally, it was his turn to visit the house. He told us very early that he had prepared a bag when he went down the mountain to carry letters for everyone. I faced the ice and snow of Kunlun Mountain, bit the pen barrel, and calmly wrote about thirty letters, each of which exhausted my talents.我双手捧着这摞信，郑重地交给老医生。他的白发在雪峰的映衬下，晃动得像一盆水中的粉丝，你放心好了！我到了山下第一件事就是为大家发信。假如回信快的话，下次军邮车上来，你们也许就能收到回信了。

  I held the stack of letters in both hands and solemnly handed them to the old doctor. His white hair swayed like a fan in a basin of water against the backdrop of the snowy peaks, don't worry! The first thing I did when I arrived at the foot of the mountain was to send letters to everyone. If the reply is fast, you may be able to receive a reply next time the military mail truck comes up.他走了。军邮车像候鸟，飞来一次又一次，但那三十封信一封也不见回音。原来他下山乘坐的车翻了，这在高原是很平常的事。熊熊烈火吞噬了他银发苍苍的头颅，那个装满信件的旅行包，顷刻间化为青烟。

  He's gone. The military mail truck flew in again and again like migratory birds, but there was no reply to the thirty letters. It turned out that the car he was riding down the mountain overturned, which is a common thing on the plateau. The raging fire engulfed his silver-haired head, and the travel bag full of letters turned into green smoke in an instant.那三十封信，只有给父母的那封，我重写了托人发出。给其他人的，便再也提不起兴致重写。只要抓起笔，老医生的白发就在眼前灼目地闪动，眼珠便发酸。大团大团的冰雪，在我胸中凝结。

  Of those thirty letters, only the one for my parents, I rewrote and sent it to someone. If you give it to others, you can no longer be interested in rewriting it. As long as he grabbed the pen, the old doctor's gray hair flashed in front of his eyes, and his eyes became sour. A large mass of ice and snow condensed in my chest.后来，在老医生的追悼会上，我才知道他的生辰，远没有我想象的那样老。满头灿然的白发，是昆仑山馈赠他的不能拒绝的礼物。

  Later, at the memorial service for the old doctor, I learned that his birthday was far from as old as I thought. The brilliant white hair is a gift that Kunlun Mountain cannot refuse.他死了以后，军邮车还带来过他的家信。我第一次注意了一下地址：是广西一个很偏远的小城。又在地图上仔细寻找，那地方在北回归线以南，属于热带，该是非常炎热的。老医生的家乡，距离昆仑山大约有一万五千里。

  After his death, the military mail truck also brought his family letter. I paid attention to the address for the first time: it is a very remote town in Guangxi. I searched carefully on the map, and the place was south of the Tropic of Cancer, which was tropical, and it should be very hot. The old doctor's hometown is about 15,000 miles away from Kunlun Mountain.

  那封迟到的信，边缘已经磨损，好像烙熟又蒸了几遭的馅饼，几处裂口的地方，被薄而坚韧的透明纸粘贴过，上面打着蓝色的印章：“邮件已破，军邮代封”。

  The late letter, the edges were worn, like a pie that had been baked and steamed several times, and several cracks were pasted with thin and tough transparent paper, with a blue seal on it: "The mail is broken, and the military mail is sealed."不知这是否是封报平安的家信？
I wonder if this is a family letter that reports peace?

未知

  16.葵花之最

  16. The best of sunflowers

  二十年前的那个春天，我是在昆仑山上度过的。

  Twenty years ago, that spring, I spent it on Kunlun Mountain.昆仑山其实只有一个季节——冬天，春节过后那段漫长而寒冷的日子被称为春天，这是我们这帮小女兵从平原家中带来的习惯。

  Kunlun Mountain actually has only one season - winter, and the long and cold days after the Spring Festival are called spring, which is a habit brought by our little female soldiers from their homes in the plains.快到“五一”了，冰封的道路渐渐开通，春节慰问品运到了。五颜六色来自五湖四海的慰问袋最受欢迎。小伙子们希望从绣着花的漂亮布袋里，摸出一双精致的鞋垫，做一个浪漫的梦。姑娘们没有这份心思，只想找点稀罕的吃食，打打牙祭。整整一个冬天，除了脱水菜和军用罐头，没有见过绿色。可惜，关山重重，山路迢迢，花生走了油，瓜子变哈喇，沙枣颠成粉末，面粉烙的小馃子像出土文物……

  It is almost "May Day", the icy road is gradually opened, and the Spring Festival condolences have arrived. Colorful condolence bags from all over the world are the most popular. The guys hope to find a pair of exquisite insoles from a beautiful cloth bag embroidered with flowers and have a romantic dream. The girls didn't have this mind, they just wanted to find some rare food and make a tooth sacrifice. For a whole winter, except for dehydrated vegetables and military cans, I did not see green. Unfortunately, there are many mountains and mountains, mountain roads are long, peanuts are oily, melon seeds become hara, sand dates are turned into powder, and small steamed buns baked with flour are like unearthed cultural relics......突然闻到一股奇异的清香。

  Suddenly smelled a strange fragrance.那是一个绣着黄色“八一”和红色五星的小白口袋。针脚毛茸茸的，绣活手艺不高，想必出自一个笨手笨脚的胖姑娘。

  It was a small white pocket embroidered with yellow "Bayi" and red five stars. The stitches are hairy, and the embroidery skills are not high, presumably from a clumsy fat girl.打开一看，是一袋葵花子。颗颗像小炮弹一样结实，饱满得可爱。我们每人抢了一把，一尝，竟是生的。葵花子中埋着一封信。

  When I opened it, it was a bag of sunflower seeds. Each one is as strong as a small cannonball, full and cute. We each grabbed a handful and tasted it, and it was raw. There is a letter buried in the sunflower seeds.“敬爱的解放军叔叔们……”

  "Dear uncles of the People's Liberation Army......信是从广东省湛江市第二小学发出的。

  The letter was sent from the No. 2 Primary School in Zhanjiang City, Guangdong Province.我们趴在地图上找。唔，湛江，好远！那里是亚热带，一个很热的地方。

  We look for it on the map. Hmm, Zhanjiang, it's a long way! It was subtropical, a very hot place.孩子们请求解放军叔叔们，把他们精心挑选出的葵花种子，种在祖国的边防线上。

  The children asked the uncles of the People's Liberation Army to plant the sunflower seeds they carefully selected on the border defense line of the motherland.我们把手中的葵花子放回布袋。那清香，是阳光、土地和绿色植物的芬芳。

  We put the sunflower seeds in our hands back into the cloth bag. The fragrance is the fragrance of sunshine, land and green plants.昆仑山咆哮的暴风雪，伴随我们进行讨论。

  The roaring blizzard of Kunlun Mountain accompanied us in our discussion.为什么只写给解放军叔叔？边防线上也有解放军阿姨呀。

  Why only write to the uncle of the People's Liberation Army? There are also aunts of the People's Liberation Army on the border line.在国境线上种葵花，多美妙的想法！每当葵花开放的时候，我们将有一条金色的国境线。

  What a wonderful idea to plant sunflowers on the border! Whenever the sunflowers bloom, we will have a golden border.这根本不可能！昆仑山是世界第三极，雪线上连草都不长，还能开葵花？！

  It's simply impossible! Kunlun Mountain is the third pole in the world, and even grass does not grow on the snow line, and sunflowers can still bloom?!我们都默不作声了，只听见屋外风在嘶鸣。

  We were all silent, only to hear the wind hissing outside the house.大家决定由我给孩子们回一封信，就说葵花子是解放军阿姨们收到的，只是这里很冷很冷……

  Everyone decided that I would reply to the children, saying that the sunflower seeds were received by the aunts of the People's Liberation Army, but it was very cold here......昆仑山的“夏天”到了。

  The "summer" of Kunlun Mountain has arrived.信早已写好，却始终没有发出。我们大着胆子，把葵花子种在院子里。

  The letter had already been written, but it was never sent. We had the courage to plant sunflower seeds in the yard.人们都说活不了，却天天跑来看，松土施肥。

  People say they can't live, but they run every day to loosen the soil and fertilize.葵花发芽了。先探出两片嫩黄的叶子，像试探风向的小手掌，肥厚而天真。然后舒展腰肢，前仰后合生机盎然地长大起来。

  The sunflowers have sprouted. First, two tender yellow leaves were poked out, like small palms that tested the direction of the wind, fat and innocent. Then stretch your waist and grow up vigorously.昆仑山默默地认可了这些来自亚热带的绿色幼苗，就像它认可了我们一样。

  Kunlun Mountain silently recognizes these green seedlings from the subtropics, just as it recognizes us.然而，我们高兴得太早了。不知道该算是上个冬天最迟还是下个冬天最早的一股冷风，冻死了绝大部分葵花。

  However, we rejoiced too soon. I don't know if it was the latest cold wind last winter or the earliest next winter, which froze most of the sunflowers to death.奇迹般地保存下一棵幼苗。它并不是最强壮的，也许因为近旁有一块大石头。受到启发，我们用石头为葵花围起一圈不透风的篱笆。

  Miraculously saved the next seedling. It is not the strongest, perhaps because there is a large stone nearby. Inspired by this, we used stones to enclose the sunflowers with an impermeable fence.现在，我们每天趴在石头围墙上看葵花，不知道的人，会以为里面养着活蹦乱跳的小生灵。

  Now, we lie on the stone wall every day to watch the sunflowers, and those who don't know will think that there are living creatures in it.这棵幸运的葵花，一往情深地看着太阳，勇敢地展开桃形的枝叶，茎上纤巧的绒毛，像蜜蜂翅膀一样，在寒风中抖个不停。也许它感到了昆仑山喜怒无常的威严，急匆匆地压缩自己生命的历程，才长到一尺高，就萌出了纽扣大的花蕾，压得最高处的茎叶微微下垂，好像惭愧自己为什么不长得更高一些。

  This lucky sunflower looks at the sun affectionately, bravely spreads its peach-shaped branches and leaves, and the delicate fluff on the stem is like bee wings, shaking in the cold wind. Perhaps it felt the majesty of Kunlun Mountain's moodiness, and hurriedly compressed the course of its life, and only when it grew to a foot high, it sprouted button-sized buds, and the stems and leaves at the highest point drooped slightly, as if it was ashamed of why it didn't grow taller.那一年没有秋天。寒凝一切的风雪，毫无先兆地骤然降临。早上起来，天地一片苍茫，我们几乎是跌跌撞撞地扑向葵花。

  There was no autumn that year. The wind and snow that condensed all the cold suddenly fell without warning. In the morning, the sky and earth were vast, and we almost stumbled to the sunflowers.石围墙也被飓风吹得四散飘去，向日葵却凝然不动地站立在那里，在冰雕玉琢的莹白之中，保持着凄清的翠绿。叶片傲然舒展，像一面面玻璃做的旗，发出环佩般的叮当之声。最不可思议的是，在它生命的最后一刻，居然绽开了一朵明艳的花。那花盘只有五分硬币那么大，薄而平整，冰雪凝冻其上，像一块光滑的表蒙子，刚分裂出的葵花子还未成熟，像丝丝柳絮一样优雅地弯曲着，沁出极轻淡的紫色。最令人警醒的是花盘四周弹射出密集的黄色花瓣，箭头一般怒放着，像一颗永不泯灭的星。

  The stone wall was also blown away by the hurricane, but the sunflowers stood there still, maintaining a desolate emerald green in the white of ice carvings and jade. The leaves stretch proudly, like flags made of glass, making a jingling sound like a ring. The most incredible thing is that at the last moment of its life, a bright flower actually bloomed. The flower plate was only the size of a five-cent coin, thin and flat, frozen on it with ice and snow, like a smooth surface mask, and the newly split sunflower seeds were not yet ripe, bending gracefully like a silk willow catkins, exuding a very light purple. The most sobering thing is that dense yellow petals eject around the flower plate, and they bloom like arrows, like a star that never disappears.

  向日葵身上的冰花越结越厚，最后凝固成一方柱形的冰晶。

  The ice flowers on the sunflower became thicker and thicker, and finally solidified into a square columnar ice crystal.广东省湛江市第二小学当年的孩子们，但愿不要看到我这篇小文。愿他们心中永存一条盛开葵花的金色国境线。

  The children of Zhanjiang No. 2 Primary School in Guangdong Province hope they don't see my article. May their hearts always have a golden border of blooming sunflowers.假如有一天，我能重回昆仑山。在两座最高的山峰中间，有一块只有我们才知道的地方。在深深的永冻土层之下，有一方冰清玉洁的水晶，水晶中有一朵美丽绝伦的花，宛若雏菊半仰着脸，灿然微笑着……

  If one day, I can return to Kunlun Mountain. In the middle of the two highest peaks, there is a place that only we know. Under the deep permafrost, there is a crystal with a beautiful flower, like a daisy with its face half-tilted, smiling brightly......我不知道它是不是世界上最小的葵花，但我知道它是世界上最高的葵花。
I don't know if it's the smallest sunflower in the world, but I know it's the tallest sunflower in the world.

未知

  17.西藏猪

  17. Tibetan pig

  高原上的生物很少。像平原常见的飞鸟，比如麻雀、喜鹊，一种也没有。只有像乌云一般的秃鹫偶然飞过。大概鸟儿也因缺氧憋得喘不过气来吧？

  There are few creatures on the plateau. Like the common birds in the plains, such as sparrows and magpies, there is none. Only vultures like dark clouds flew by by chance. Probably the birds are also out of breath due to lack of oxygen, right?人有一种爱养小动物的天性，我们就从山底下抱上来一只公鸡。一路上，随着海拔的不断升高，鸡冠子越来越紫，最后简直变成黑的了。好不容易熬到了目的地，我们赶紧把公鸡放在雪地上，心想让它换点新鲜空气，也许它会舒服一些。没想到，它的爪子刚一着地，立即就飞跑起来。跑了没多远，就一个跟头栽在地上，扑棱着翅膀死了。大家非常伤心。初到高原的生灵，是不能做剧烈运动的，要给身体一个慢慢适应的过程，可惜公鸡不懂得这个道理，就丧了命。

  People have a nature to raise small animals, so we pick up a rooster from the bottom of the mountain. Along the way, as the altitude continued to rise, the cockscomb became more and more purple, and finally turned black. Finally made it to our destination, we quickly put the rooster on the snow, thinking that maybe it would be more comfortable if it got some fresh air. Unexpectedly, as soon as its claws hit the ground, it immediately flew away. Not far away, one fell to the ground and died with his wings. Everyone is very sad. Creatures who have just arrived at the plateau cannot do strenuous exercise, and they have to give their bodies a slow adaptation process, but unfortunately the rooster does not understand this truth and dies.以后又从山下带上来一头小猪。这回大家有经验了，刚开始半个月，人们紧紧抱着小猪，不叫它活动，可小猪后来还是死了。医生说，小猪得了一种叫作高原肺水肿的重病。

  Later, a piglet was brought up from the bottom of the mountain. This time everyone has experience, in the first half month, people hugged the piglet tightly and did not call it to move, but the piglet died later. The doctor said that the piglet had a serious illness called plateau pulmonary edema.过了些日子，有人从国界那边的印度进口了一只小黑猪。听说它老家的地势也很高，这样就不存在水土不服的问题了。

  A few days later, someone imported a little black pig from India on the other side of the border. I heard that the terrain of its hometown is also very high, so there is no problem of acclimatization.果然，这只来自异国的小黑猪平安地活下来了。大伙给它起名叫黑黑。

  Sure enough, the little black pig from a foreign country survived safely. Everyone named it Heihei.黑黑每天在我们的住处悠闲地漫步，把它的小尾巴得意地卷成一个“8”字。一到开饭的时间，它就从野外赶回来，等在饭厅门口，用长着双眼皮的大眼睛，眼巴巴地瞅着大家，嘴角还会滴下一串口水。

  Heihei strolled leisurely in our residence every day, proudly curling its little tail into a "figure 8". As soon as it was time to eat, it rushed back from the wilderness, waiting at the door of the dining room, staring at everyone with big eyelids, and a string of saliva dripping from the corner of its mouth.我们宁可自己先不吃饭，也要喂黑黑。这个给它撕一块馒头，那个给它舀一勺米饭。黑黑也很聪明，吃完了这个人的一口饭，就会走开，绝不会老围着你。人们抢着喂黑黑，有时就把黑黑搞得很狼狈，鼻梁上贴了一块豆腐，耳朵上挂着一缕粉丝。它很绅士，一点也不着急。等人们散开了，就自己跑到大石头旁边，把头上挂的食物蹭下来，再慢慢吃掉。

  We would rather not eat ourselves or feed Heihei. This one tears a piece of steamed bun for it, and the other scoops a spoonful of rice for it. Hei Hei is also very smart, after eating a bite of this person's meal, he will walk away, and he will never surround you all the time. People rushed to feed Heihei, and sometimes they made Heihei very embarrassed, with a piece of tofu stuck on the bridge of his nose and a wisp of vermicelli hanging from his ears. It is gentlemanly and not in a hurry at all. When the people dispersed, they ran to the big rocks, rubbed off the food hanging on their heads, and then ate them slowly.黑黑最爱喝甜牛奶了。刚开始是因为许多人是从农村来的，喝不惯牛奶。轮到喝牛奶的日子（不是鲜牛奶，高原上哪儿有奶牛啊，是用奶粉冲开的），剩的就格外多。炊事班就准备了一个大木槽盛剩牛奶。黑黑跑过来，把嘴巴拱进槽里，只剩两只眼睛在外面，咻咻地喘着气，埋下头谁也不理。你看不见它狼吞虎咽，只见它的脖子均匀地颤动，但槽里白色奶液的水平面迅速下降，一会儿就露出槽底的木纹了。好像槽子在我们找不到的地方裂了一个大洞，牛奶都渗到地下去了。黑黑抬起头，也很遗憾很吃惊地注视着木槽，好像自己也不明白：刚才还那么多牛奶，怎么一眨眼的工夫就不见了？

  Heihei loves to drink sweet milk. At first, it was because many people came from the countryside and were not used to drinking milk. When it was the turn to drink milk (not fresh milk, where are the cows on the plateau, they were washed away with milk powder), there was a lot left. The cooking class prepared a large wooden trough to hold the leftover milk. Heihei ran over, arched his mouth into the groove, leaving only two eyes outside, panting, burying his head and ignoring anyone. You can't see it gobbling up, only its neck trembling evenly, but the level of the white milk in the trough drops rapidly, and the wood grain at the bottom of the trough is revealed in a while. It was as if the trough had cracked a big hole in a place where we couldn't find it, and the milk seeped into the ground. Hei Hei raised his head, and stared at the wooden trough with regret and surprise, as if he didn't understand: There was so much milk just now, why did the time disappear in the blink of an eye?知道黑黑爱喝牛奶以后，我们就有意多给它剩下一些。

  After knowing that Heihei loves to drink milk, we deliberately gave it more leftovers.在这样丰富的营养下，黑黑迅速长大，不久就成了一只威武的大黑猪。甩着大肚皮走动的时候，好像一张黑丝绒壁毯在旷野移动。

  Under such rich nutrition, Heihei grew up quickly and soon became a mighty big black pig. When I walked around with my big belly, it was like a black velvet carpet moving in the wilderness.高原上的尖石把黑黑的肚皮磨破了。开饭的时候，黑黑再也不能像原来那样飞快地跑过来，只能慢慢往家里挪。炊事班长看了心痛，就领黑黑到卫生科，对正在给人包扎伤口的护士说，给我们的黑黑看看病。

  The sharp stones on the plateau wore out the black belly. When it was time to eat, Hei Hei could no longer run over as quickly as before, so he could only slowly move home. The cooking squad leader saw the heartache, so he took Heihei to the health department and said to the nurse who was bandaging the wound, "Give our Heihei a doctor."

  护士吓了一跳，说，我又不是兽医。

  The nurse was startled and said, I'm not a veterinarian.班长说，这病不用兽医，我就能看。把伤口消消毒，抹点药膏包起来就行。

  The squad leader said, I can see this disease without a veterinarian. Disinfect the wound and wrap it with some ointment.护士说，谁敢钻到猪肚子底下去上药？它不咬人才怪呢！

  The nurse said, who dares to get under the pig's belly and apply medicine? It's no wonder it doesn't bite!班长对护士说，黑黑绝对不会咬你的。然后又对黑黑说，这是给你看病呢，千万不要乱动啊！好了，趴下吧。

  The squad leader said to the nurse, Heihei will never bite you. Then he said to Heihei, this is for you, don't move around! Okay, lie down.黑黑就乖乖地躺在卫生科门外的地上，像平日吃饱了饭晒太阳的样子。

  Hei Hei obediently lay on the ground outside the health department, like he was eating enough to bask in the sun on weekdays.护士双手托着治疗盘，战战兢兢地走过去，消毒、上药……涂酒精的时候，黑黑可能感到有点痛，浑身抖了一下，但真的是没有动。

  The nurse held the treatment tray in both hands and walked over tremblingly, disinfecting and applying medicine...... When applying alcohol, Heihe may feel a little pain, and his whole body trembles, but he really doesn't move.上完了药，黑黑站起来。它的肚子上多了一块雪白的纱布，好像一枚巨大的邮票。

  After applying the medicine, Hei Hei stood up. It has a snow-white gauze on its belly, like a huge stamp.第二天，护士偶然走出治疗室，看见黑黑正在屋外绕来绕去。见到护士，它哼了两声，然后自动躺在地上。原来它肚子上的纱布掉了，伤口又露了出来。护士就又给它上了药。

  The next day, the nurse accidentally walked out of the treatment room and saw Hei Hei walking around outside the house. Seeing the nurse, it snorted twice and then automatically lay on the ground. It turned out that the gauze on its stomach fell off and the wound was exposed again. The nurse gave it medicine again.后来，黑黑的肚子好了，又可以很有风度地在房前屋后散步了。我们眯起眼看看它，想起平原的家。有人说，在我们村子里，有一头和这一模一样的黑猪呢！
Later, my dark stomach was healed, and I could walk in front of and behind the house in a very personable way. We squint at it and think of our home on the plains. Some people say that in our village, there is a black pig exactly like this!

未知

  18.制花圈

  18. Wreath making

  我是特意用“制”花圈这个词，而不用通常的“做”花圈。因为“制”的规模大，有流水作业大生产的味道。

  I deliberately used the word "make" the wreath instead of the usual "make" wreath. Because of the large scale of "production", it has the taste of large-scale production of flow operations.二十多年前，我在藏北高原当兵。高寒、缺氧、病痛……一把把利刃悬挂在半空，时不时地抚摸一下我们年轻的头颅。一般是用冷飕飕的刀背，偶尔也试试刀锋。

  More than 20 years ago, I served as a soldier on the northern Tibetan plateau. High cold, hypoxia, and pain...... A handful of sharp blades hang in mid-air, stroking our young heads from time to time. Generally, a cold blade is used, and occasionally the blade is tried.于是就常有生命骤然折断，滚烫的血沁入冰雪，高原的温度因此有微弱的升高。

  As a result, life is often broken suddenly, hot blood seeps into the ice and snow, and the temperature of the plateau rises slightly.凡有部队的地方就有陵园。每逢清明和突然牺牲将士的时候，我们就要赶制花圈。因为我们是女兵，花圈就要扎得格外美丽。当我们最初扎花圈的时候，觉得像做手工一样有趣。

  Wherever there are troops, there is a cemetery. Whenever there is a Qingming Festival and when soldiers are suddenly sacrificed, we have to rush to make wreaths. Because we are female soldiers, the wreath must be tied particularly beautifully. When we first tied the wreath, we thought it was as fun as making a handicraft.做花圈先要有架子。若在平原，竹子、藤条、木棍……都是上好的材料。但对于高原，这些平常物都是奢侈品。男兵用钢筋焊出一人多高的巨环，中间用钢丝攀出蛛网似的细格。花圈的骨骼就挺立起来了。

  To make a wreath, you need to have a rack. If in the plains, bamboo, rattan, and sticks...... They are all good materials. But for the plateau, these ordinary things are luxuries. The male soldier welded a giant ring more than one person high with steel bars, and climbed out of a thin grid like a spider web with steel wire in the middle. The bones of the wreath stand upright.我们在乒乓球案子上做花。五颜六色的花纸堆积如山，刚开始的时候，似乎有些节日的气氛。女孩们分成几组，有的把纸裁成大小不等的方块儿，有的剪出形状各异的花瓣，有的用糨糊粘绿叶……有条不紊，各显神通。

  We make flowers on table tennis cases. Colorful flower papers are piled up like mountains, and at the beginning, there seems to be a bit of a festive atmosphere. The girls were divided into groups, some cut the paper into squares of different sizes, some cut out petals of different shapes, and some glued green leaves with glue...... Methodical, each shows its magical powers.忙了一阵子之后，所需的花朵基本上备齐了。屋里花红柳绿的，对我们习惯了莹白冰雪颜色的眼睛来说，真是享受。

  After being busy for a while, the flowers you need are basically ready. The house is red and green, which is really enjoyable for our eyes that are accustomed to the color of white ice and snow.该往黝黑的钢环上绑花了。一圈红的，一圈蓝的……白花最多，像高原上万古不化的寒冰。

  It's time to tie flowers to the dark steel ring. A circle of red, a circle of blue...... The most white flowers, like the eternal ice on the plateau.花圈渐渐成形，女孩子们的嬉笑声渐渐沉寂。一朵朵的花是艳丽的，一圈圈的花就有了某种庄严。当一个个硕大的花环肃穆而凝重地矗立在我们面前时，一种被悲哀压榨的痛苦，像鸟一样降临在我们心头。

  The wreath gradually took shape, and the laughter of the girls gradually fell silent. The flowers are gorgeous, and the circles of flowers have a certain solemnity. When huge wreaths stand solemnly and solemnly in front of us, a kind of pain squeezed by sorrow comes to our hearts like birds.

  这是献给一个或一组年轻生命的祭品。

  This is an offering to one or a group of young lives.每次做花圈，都要整整干上一天。先给司令部做，再给政治部做，然后还有后勤部……人们认为女孩天生与花有缘，殊不知这凄冷的花卉，令人黯然神伤。

  Every time you make a wreath, you have to do it for a whole day. First for the headquarters, then for the political department, and then for the logistics department...... People think that girls are born with flowers, but they don't know that this cold flower is sad.有一天下午，我们为一位牺牲在边境线上的战友赶制花圈。因为第二天就要下葬，一直干到凌晨三点。倦意袭来，绑花时钢丝不停地扎手，有鲜血像红豆似的渗出。马上就要完工时，桌上的电话铃猛然响了。我揉着眼睛问，什么事啊？

  One afternoon, we rushed to make a wreath for a comrade who died on the border. Because he was going to be buried the next day, he worked until three o'clock in the morning. Fatigue hit, and the wire kept pricking his hands when tying flowers, and blood oozed out like red beans. Just as it was about to be completed, the phone on the table rang suddenly. I rubbed my eyes and asked, what's the matter?对方低沉着嗓音说，刚才夜间紧急集合时，一个战士翻身跃起，突然倒在地上死去了。请你们再赶制一副花圈。

  The other party said in a low voice that when they were urgently gathering at night, a soldier turned over and jumped up, suddenly fell to the ground and died. Please hurry up and make another wreath.那一瞬，我痛彻骨髓。那个不认识的男孩啊！当我们开始制那副花圈的时候，你还活着。当我们制完那副花圈的时候，就要为你制花圈了。

  At that moment, I was in pain. That boy I don't know! When we started making that wreath, you were alive. When we finish making that wreath, it's time to make a wreath for you.

  那一夜，女兵们彻夜无眠。当雪山上的朝阳莅临军营，大卡车把我们的产品运至墓地。

  That night, the female soldiers stayed awake all night. When the sun on the snowy mountains came to the barracks, big trucks transported our products to the cemetery.摄影干事们很忙。他们用最好的角度把墓前的花圈照下来，寄往内地的某处小村。那些牺牲了的士兵的父母，永远无法到达高原。他们会在无数个月夜，看着相片上的一丘黄土和伟岸辉煌的花圈，潸然泪下。
The camera officers are busy. They took the wreath in front of the tomb from the best angle and sent it to a small village in the mainland. The parents of those soldiers who died will never reach the plateau. They will cry on countless moon nights, looking at the mounds of loess and magnificent wreaths in the photo.

未知

  19.三块糖

  19. Three pieces of sugar

  远处的半山坡上，有一排独立的小房子。平日总是锁着大门，大锁锈迹斑斑，叫人怀疑能否打得开。人们走过的时候，总是绕得远远的，仿佛那里潜伏着瘟疫或猛兽。

  In the distance, halfway up the hillside, there is a row of independent small houses. The door is always locked on weekdays, and the big lock is rusty, making people wonder if it can be opened. When people walk by, they always go far away, as if there is a plague or a beast lurking there.那是医院的太平间。

  It was the morgue of the hospital.真想不通，汉语里为什么把和死亡有关的事，都叫作“太平”。比如，轮船上救生的太平斧，剧场里供大家逃难的太平门……好像一叫太平，再危急的事也可以化险为夷。

  I really can't figure out why everything related to death is called "peace" in Chinese. For example, the peace axe for life-saving on the ship, and the peace gate for everyone to escape in the theater...... It seems that when it is called peace, no matter how critical it is, it can turn danger into safety.但人一死，的的确确是太平了。不太平的，是活着的人。

  But when a person dies, it is indeed peaceful. What is not peaceful is the living people.太平间躺着病死的人，基本上是独往独来。高原地广人稀，死亡的事虽然经常发生，因为总的基数小，出现的频率就不很高。一般死了人，都由值班的医生、护士负责给死人更衣。要是轮到女兵上班，男卫生员们就会说，还是我们来吧，省得你们做噩梦。

  People lying sick and dying in morgues are basically alone. The plateau is vast and sparsely populated, and although deaths occur frequently, because the total base is small, the frequency of occurrence is not very high. Generally, when a person dies, the doctor and nurse on duty are responsible for changing the clothes of the dead. If it is the turn of the female soldiers to go to work, the male hygienists will say, let's do it, so that you don't have nightmares.一天，边境线上发生了激烈的战事，伤亡很大。医生们都在抢救伤员，活着的毕竟比牺牲了的更重要。但尸体从前线拉回，卧在太平间，久久地不处理，也于情理不容。

  One day, fierce fighting broke out on the border, with heavy casualties. Doctors are rescuing the wounded, and being alive is more important than sacrificing after all. But the body was pulled back from the front line and lay in the morgue, and it was unreasonable not to deal with it for a long time.领导找到我说，给女兵一个艰巨的任务。

  The leader approached me and said that he would give the female soldiers a difficult task.我说，您说吧。

  I say, you say.领导说，有一个年轻的班长，战死疆场。人手实在不够，要由你们给他更换尸衣，明晨下葬。

  The leader said that there was a young squad leader who died on the battlefield. There are really not enough manpower, so you will change his body clothes and bury him tomorrow morning.我说，还有谁参加？

  I said, who else is participating?领导说，还有政治部的一名干事，负责登记烈士的遗物等事宜。他以前处理过阵亡将士的事，有经验，你们听他的。但他身体不好，动嘴不动手，你们要多请示，多照顾他。

  The leader said that there is also an officer of the Political Department who is responsible for registering the relics of the martyrs. He has dealt with fallen soldiers before, and he has experience, so listen to him. But he is not in good health, and he doesn't move his mouth, so you have to ask for more instructions and take care of him.我咬着乱颤的牙关，说，是。心想，一个大男子汉，居然要女孩们在死人当前的时候照料他，真不知是他的耻辱还是我们的光荣。

  I gritted my trembling teeth and said, yes. I thought to myself, a manly man actually wants girls to take care of him when he is dead, I really don't know if it is his shame or our honor.我说，人在哪里？

  I said, where are the people?领导说，干事吗？

  The leader said, officer?我说，班长。

  I said, squad leader.领导说，在三号。

  The leader said, on the third.就是说，尸体在太平间的第三间屋子。我回到宿舍，向大家传达了这个前所未有的任务，全场先是静寂了三分钟。炉子里有一块烧得正热的煤，啪地裂开了小缝，火苗从一大朵分裂成两小朵，发出丝绸抖动的声音。

  That is, the body is in the third room of the morgue. I returned to the dormitory and conveyed this unprecedented task to everyone, and the audience was silent for three minutes. There was a piece of hot coal in the stove, which cracked open a small crack, and the flame split from one large flower to two small flowers, making the sound of silk shaking.我说，说话啊，现在又不是为烈士默哀的时间。

  I said, speak, now is not the time to observe a moment of silence for the martyrs.小鹿说，烈士是一位男的啦？

  Xiaolu said, is the martyr a man?我说，阿里高原上的女兵都在这间屋里了，你说他是男的还是女的？

  I said, all the female soldiers on the Ali Plateau are in this room, do you think he is a man or a woman?小鹿说，这个我知道。只是要给一个男青年从里到外换衣服，心里总有点那个，是不是连内裤都要换？

  Xiaolu said, I know this. Just to change clothes for a young man from the inside out, there is always a little bit of that in my heart, do I even have to change my underwear?我说，是。他是我们的兄弟……

  I said, yes. He is our brother......小鹿摆摆手说，大道理你就甭讲了，我都懂。我就权当他是一截木头好了。

  Xiaolu waved his hand and said, you don't have to talk about the big truth, I understand. I'll just treat him as a piece of wood.果平说，比木头还是可怕多了。要知道，他死了。

  Guo Ping said that it is still much more terrifying than wood. You know, he died.小如细声说，咱们平常也不是没有在临床上接触过死人，没什么不一样的。反正都是个死，大着胆子收殓就是了。

  Xiaoru said softly, it's not that we haven't been in contact with dead people in clinical practice, it's no different. Anyway, it's all a death, just have the courage to bury it.河莲说，我看，还是有原则上的不同。病死的人，浑身是囫囵的，就算瘦得只剩下几根大筋，用医学的话讲是恶液质，毕竟五官完整。战死的人，你知道致命伤在哪里？若是在脑袋上，跟关公大老爷似的，头都没有了，或者说头虽然有，但身首异处，需要我们用丝线把脖子和脑袋缝到一起，那咱们可就有得活儿干了。

  He Lian said, I think there are still differences in principle. People who die of illness are all over their bodies, even if they are so thin that only a few large tendons remain, in medical terms, they are cachexia, after all, their facial features are complete. Those who died in battle, do you know where the fatal wounds are? If it is on the head, like the eldest master of Guan Gong, the head is gone, or although the head is there, but the body and head are different, we need to use silk thread to sew the neck and head together, then we have to do the work.我本来胆子还大些，听河莲这样一说，毛骨悚然。可我是班长，三军不可夺帅，就狠狠地对河莲说，不得蛊惑军心！现在也不是冷兵器时代，不会出现一把大刀把头剁飞了的情况。就是战伤在头部，也不过是颅脑粉碎性骨折或大动脉断裂，头骨肯定还是在的。

  I was still bolder, but when I heard He Lian say this, I was creepy. But I am the squad leader, and the three armies cannot take the commander, so I said to He Lian fiercely, do not bewitch the morale of the army! Now is not the era of cold weapons, and there will be no situation where a big knife chops off the head. Even if the battle wound is in the head, it is just a comminuted fracture of the head or a rupture of the aorta, and the skull must still be there.果平说，哎呀我的妈呀，班长你就别讲了。血肉模糊脑浆迸裂，这比一个头叽里咕噜地滚到一边去了，还可怕。

  Guo Ping said, oh my mother, don't talk about it, squad leader. The flesh and blood were blurred and the brain was bursting, which was more terrifying than rolling to the side with a head grunting.我说，不管可怕不可怕，我们必须完成任务。最简单的一个道理就是，要是你阵亡在这荒无人烟远离亲人的地方，浑身上下沾满血和泥巴，到处是和敌人搏斗的痕迹，你愿意就这模样埋进烈士陵园吗？

  I said, whether it's scary or not, we have to get the job done. The simplest truth is that if you die in this deserted place far from your relatives, covered in blood and mud, and there are traces of fighting the enemy everywhere, would you be willing to be buried in the martyrs' cemetery like this?小鹿最先说，我不乐意。听我奶奶说，人死的时候穿着什么衣服，到阎王老子那儿就是什么打扮。所以，人的老衣都得是最好的。我们这么小岁数就不在阳间了，更得穿得像点样子，最好仪表堂堂。

  The deer was the first to say, "I'm not happy." I heard from my grandmother that what clothes people wear when they die is what they dress up when they go to Yama Laozi. Therefore, people's old clothes must be the best. We are not in the human world at such a young age, and we have to dress like that, and it is best to be dignified.果平说，你那是迷信啊。不过，活着的人会常常梦见死去的人。要是我们穿得太破烂，家里人在梦中相见的时候，心里会难过的。

  Guo Ping said, you are superstitious. However, living people often dream of dead people. If we are dressed too tattered, our family will feel sad when we meet in a dream.小如长叹一口气说，真到了为国捐躯的时候，别的我也顾不了，但我希望给我穿一套干净衣服，不一定是新的，但一定要有香皂味儿。

  Xiaoru sighed and said, when it is really time to sacrifice my life for the country, I can't take care of anything else, but I hope to wear a clean set of clothes, not necessarily new, but must have the smell of soap.河莲冷笑道，人都死了，还管那些。要是我啊，生是什么样，死也是什么样，无所谓，生死如一。也省得让别人心里起腻，在这里讨论来讨论去的。一把黄土埋了，大家清静。

  He Lian sneered, people are dead, and they don't care about those. If it were me, what life is like, what death is like, it doesn't matter, life and death are the same. It also saves others from getting tired of discussing and discussing it here. A handful of loess is buried, and everyone is quiet.

  你很难说河莲这番话是正说还是反说，但她刺激了我们，使大家脸上滚烫起来。是啊，都是为了保卫祖国，我们从各地聚集，来到这苍茫的世界第三极。现在有一个兄弟远行了，我们不能在他生前帮他击败敌人，难道在他死后，还不能伸出手去，为他的遗体做点什么，把他打扮得漂亮些吗？

  It's hard for you to tell whether Ha Lian's words are right or wrong, but she stimulated us and made everyone's faces hot. Yes, it is all to defend the motherland, we gathered from all over the world and came to the third pole of this vast world. Now that a brother has traveled far away, can't we help him defeat the enemy while he is alive, can't we reach out and do something for his body and dress him up more beautifully after his death?我们排着队，缓缓地向三号太平间走去。一位瘦得像竹子的干事蹲在太平间门口，低着头，好像在看蚂蚁爬。当然了，地上肯定没蚂蚁，这里高寒缺氧，蚂蚁都不肯做窝。

  We lined up and slowly walked towards Morgue No. 3. A bamboo-thin officer squatted at the door of the morgue, bowing his head as if watching ants crawl. Of course, there are definitely no ants on the ground, and the ants refuse to make nests in the cold and lack of oxygen here.你是小毕班长吧？我姓朱。他伸出手说。

  You are the squad leader of Xiao Bi, right? My surname is Zhu. He stretched out his hand and said.和朱干事握手的时候，有一种被根雕捏住的感觉。我把他左右一打量，决定称他竹干事。竹干事拿出一把钥匙，边缘粗糙锐利，几乎没人用过，递到我手里说，你把太平间的门打开。

  When I shook hands with Zhu Officer, I felt like I was pinched by the root carving. I looked him left and right and decided to call him a bamboo officer. The bamboo officer took out a key, the edges were rough and sharp, almost no one used it, handed it to me and said, you open the morgue door.我说，你怎么不开？

  I said, why don't you open it?他说，我胆小。

  He said, I am timid.一个男人当着一帮女孩子的面，公开承认他胆子小，你还有什么可说的？我原来只以为他是个病秧子，没想到脸皮还挺厚。我心里也吓得够呛，但当着一班人，只有挺身而出，奋勇向前。

  A man openly admits that he is timid in front of a group of girls, what else do you have to say? I just thought he was a sick seedling, but I didn't expect him to have a thick skin. I was scared enough, but in front of a group of people, I had no choice but to stand up and move forward bravely.门开了。太平间的屋子并不很大，但给人阴森森的空旷感觉。地中央水泥制成的停尸台上，直挺挺地仰卧着一堆白色物体，依稀看出人的轮廓。上覆一匹宽长的白布，四角垂地，笼罩地面。我们依次走进去，围着尸床站定，默不作声，好像在瞻仰一座雪丘。

  The door opened. The morgue room is not very large, but it gives an eerie feeling of emptiness. On the morgue made of cement in the center of the ground, there was a pile of white objects lying upright, and the outline of the person was vaguely visible. It is covered with a wide and long white cloth, and the corners hang down to the ground, covering the ground. We walked in in turn, stood still around the corpse bed, silent, as if looking at a snowy hill.竹干事贴墙站着，保持着和尸体最大的距离，对我说，你去把蒙尸布揭开。

  The bamboo officer stood against the wall, keeping the maximum distance from the corpse, and said to me, you go and uncover the corpse cloth.其实，从一进了太平间的门，我们已经没有退缩的余地了。无论如何都得把任务完成，这是铁的戒律。但是我讨厌一个男人临阵脱逃的胆怯，更甭提他还是我们之中，唯一处理过阵亡事宜的老手呢。

  In fact, from the moment we entered the morgue door, we had no room to retreat. No matter what, you have to complete the task, this is an iron commandment. But I hate the timidity of a man fleeing in battle, not to mention that he is the only veteran among us who has dealt with the death of a soldier.我反问，你干吗不去揭布？

  I asked rhetorically, why don't you expose it?竹干事很惊讶地说，你们领导没和你说过吗？

  Officer Zhu was surprised and said, didn't your leader tell you?我说，说了。说你有经验。

  I said, I said. Say you have experience.他说，除了这个，就没说别的了？

  He said, nothing else but this?我只好说，还说你动口不动手。

  I had to say, and said that you don't do anything.竹干事说，这就对了。那我现在动了口，你为什么还不动手？我说，你是老兵，应该给新兵做个榜样。你有经验嘛！

  The bamboo officer said, this is right. Then I've moved my mouth now, why haven't you done it yet? I said, you are a veteran and should be an example for new recruits. You have experience!竹干事苦笑着说，我有什么经验？不过就是处理过一次敌方死尸。那是一个三十多岁的大胡子，两条腿炸断了。原本想就那么连着衣服埋了。后来上级指示，出于革命的人道主义，还是收拾得体面些。第一步要把身上的血污洗了，开始我们用刷子刷，没想到血是刷掉了，但肉也跟着掉。不知是谁想出的法子，在尸体的脖子上套了一根绳子……

  The bamboo officer smiled bitterly and said, what experience do I have? However, it was only once disposed of the enemy's corpse. It was a bearded man in his thirties with two broken legs. I originally wanted to bury it with my clothes like that. Later, the superiors instructed that it was better to clean up decently for the sake of revolutionary humanitarianism. The first step is to wash off the blood stains on the body, and at first we brushed with a brush, but the blood was brushed off, but the flesh also fell off. I don't know who came up with the method, put a rope around the neck of the corpse......我们又怕听又想听，恐惧地盯着竹干事苍白的薄嘴唇。小鹿忍不住哆嗦着下巴问，你们是打算，把他，再吊死，一回吗？

  We were afraid to listen and wanted to hear, staring at the pale thin lips of the bamboo officer in fear. Xiaolu couldn't help but tremble and asked, are you planning to hang him again?竹干事不理这茬儿，接着说，我们在尸体的腰当间也拴了一道绳子……

  The bamboo officer ignored the stubble, and then said, we also tied a rope between the waist of the corpse......河莲说，我的天，该不是要五马分尸吧？

  He Lian said, my God, shouldn't I want to split my body into five horses?小如掩着半边嘴说，有革命的人道主义管着呢，别瞎猜，太吓人了。

  Xiaoru covered half of his mouth and said, there is a revolutionary humanitarianism in charge, don't guess, it's too scary.竹干事有个本事，就是你说破大天，他沉着镇定，一派大将风度，按自己的顺序走，一板一眼说下去。

  Zhu Officer has a ability, that is, you say the sky, he is calm and calm, with the demeanor of a general, in his own order, and he goes on one by one.我们把大胡子上下拴好，就把他沉到河里，拽着两道绳子在河岸上慢慢走。他躺在水里，被太阳晒热的水，从他身上缓缓流过，头发飘着，很悠闲的样子。我们累得够呛，像伏尔加河上苦难的纤夫。大胡子刚开始下水的时候，水是清的。过了一会儿，下游的水流渐渐地变脏了，那是大胡子身上的硝烟和火药末脱落下来。又过了一会儿，水流变红了，那是凝结的血块溶解了……

  We tied the beard up and down, sank him into the river, and walked slowly on the riverbank with two ropes. He was lying in the water, the water heated by the sun slowly flowed over him, his hair fluttering, and he looked very leisurely. We are tired enough to choke, like a thin man in suffering on the Volga. When the bearded man first started to enter the water, the water was clear. After a while, the water downstream gradually became dirty, and it was the smoke and powder powder from the bearded man's body falling off. After a while, the water turned red, and it was a clotted blood clot that dissolved......小如捂着耳朵说，竹干事，求求你，别讲了。我直恶心。

  Xiaoru covered his ears and said, Officer Bamboo, please, don't talk about it. I was disgusted.河莲兴致勃勃地说，讲，讲！真是新鲜事，从来没听过！

  He Lian said with great interest, speak, speak! It's really new, I've never heard of it before!我从骨子里是一点也不想听这种可怕的经历的。可我知道，当一个女兵，必要的时候要有铁石心肠。竹干事看起来瘦弱，意志却很顽强，才不在乎你是不是恶心欲吐，坚持按自己的想法行事。

  I don't want to hear this horrible experience at all. But I know that when a female soldier is necessary, she must have a heart of stone. The bamboo officer looks thin, but his will is very tenacious, so he doesn't care if you are nauseous or not, and insists on acting according to your own ideas.……等到河水再次变清的时候，我们就把大胡子拉到岸上，平放在岩石上……竹干事依旧平静地叙述着。

  …… When the river became clear again, we pulled the beard to the shore and laid it flat on the rocks...... The bamboo officer still narrated calmly.大胡子的肚子是不是胀得像个鼓？河莲嘟起自己的腮帮，好像自己也被人按到水里，淹了个半死。

  Is the bearded man's belly swollen like a drum? He Lian pouted her cheeks, as if she had been pressed into the water and drowned half-dead.没有。溺水的人腹胀如鼓，那是因为在水中挣扎，把太多的水灌入胃里。或死后尸身腐败，产生气体所致。大胡子是死后入水，牙关紧闭，肚子里没进水。再说，我们很快把他从水中拖出来，他也来不及腐败。竹干事很科学地解释。

  No. People who drown have bloated stomachs because they struggle in the water and pour too much water into their stomachs. Or it is caused by the decay of the body after death, which produces gas. The bearded man entered the water after death, his teeth were closed, and there was no water in his stomach. Besides, we quickly dragged him out of the water, and he didn't have time to corrupt. The bamboo officer explained very scientifically.可他总会有一点变化的。就像我们在水里洗衣服，时间长了，手指肚也会泡得发白。果平很有点打破砂锅问到底的英雄气概。女孩子好像有个通病，越可怕的东西越好奇。

  But he will always change a little. Just like when we wash clothes in water, after a long time, our fingers will turn white. Guo Ping is a bit heroic in breaking the casserole and asking to the end. Girls seem to have a common problem, and the more terrible things are, the more curious they are.竹干事有些惊异地说，你有经验，猜得很对。大胡子被流动的河水洗得很干净，皮肤稍微有一点肿，这使他看起来比我们刚认识他的时候，胖了一点。我和我的战友们坐在河滩的巨石上，谁也不说话，抽着烟，静静地等着呼啸的山风和西斜的太阳，把大胡子吹干。突然，我的战友站起来，走到大胡子身边，把一支点燃的香烟塞到他手里。我说，这是干什么？战友说，我刚才拖他的时候，看到他右手的食指和中指肤色很黄，说明他是一个老烟鬼。他躺着看着咱俩吸烟，一定眼红得不行。给他解解馋吧。

  Officer Bamboo said with some surprise, you have experience and guessed correctly. The beard was washed clean by the flowing river water, and his skin was slightly swollen, which made him look a little fatter than when we first met him. My comrades and I sat on the boulders on the river beach, no one spoke, smoked a cigarette, and quietly waited for the howling mountain wind and the westward sun to blow dry our beards. Suddenly, my comrade stood up, walked up to the bearded man and stuffed a lit cigarette into his hand. I said, what is this for? My comrades said that when I dragged him just now, I saw that the index and middle fingers of his right hand were very yellow, indicating that he was an old smoker. He was lying down and watching us smoke, he must be so jealous. Let's satisfy his cravings.我看着袅袅的烟气，像风车一样，在大胡子胸前绕啊绕……

  I looked at the smoke, like a windmill, circling around the bearded man's chest......后来呢？我们几乎异口同声地问。

  What happened later? We asked almost in unison.没有什么后来。竹干事说。后来大胡子被风吹干了，衣服和脸都很干净，只要不看他的膝盖以下，像一个旅游时睡着了的异国人。我们给他的遗体照了相，按照他们的风俗，用白布裹起来妥善地安葬了。每一步处理都照了相。听说这些相片都在外交部的铁匣子里放着，作为曾经发生的历史，保存着。

  Nothing came later. Zhu officer said. Later, the beard was dried by the wind, and his clothes and face were clean, as long as he didn't look below his knees, like a foreigner who fell asleep while traveling. We took pictures of his body and, according to their customs, wrapped it in white cloth and buried it properly. Every step of the process was photographed. I heard that these photos are placed in the iron box of the Ministry of Foreign Affairs, and they are preserved as a history of what happened.屋里很安静。好像大家都消失在空气里了。许久后，小如说，我以后再也不喝狮泉河的水了，它洗过死人。
