  The house was quiet. It seems that everyone has disappeared into the air. After a long time, Xiaoru said, I will never drink the water of the Lion Spring River again, it has washed the dead.竹干事说，你尽管喝水就是。洗过死人的狮泉河水，早就流进印度洋，只怕现在都到北冰洋里打漩涡了。

  The bamboo officer said, you just drink water. The water of the Lion Spring River, which washed the dead, has long flowed into the Indian Ocean, and I am afraid that it has now swirled in the Arctic Ocean.河莲最先从故事中苏醒，说，竹干事，你既然这么有实践经验，为什么非要我们班长揭开盖布，何不身先士卒？

  He Lian was the first to wake up from the story and said, Zhu Officer, since you have such practical experience, why do you have to ask our squad leader to uncover the cover, why not take the lead?竹干事说，你以为我不想在女孩子面前表现英雄气概？只是从那次以后，一碰到和死人有关的事，我就骤发心动过速，吃什么药也不管事，真气死人。也不是害怕，我当时不害怕，以后也不害怕。但是我脑子不怕，心却不争气。战友们都知道我这毛病，凡是和后事沾边的活儿，一概不让我参加。这次战事较大，大家都很忙，是我主动要求处理尸首的。这会儿心跳已经像锣鼓点了。我就不亲自动手了，请诸位娘子军原谅。

  The bamboo officer said, do you think I don't want to be heroic in front of girls? It's just that since then, whenever I encountered something related to the dead, I would have a sudden tachycardia, and I didn't care what medicine I take, and I was really angry. I wasn't afraid, I wasn't afraid then, and I wasn't afraid in the future. But my mind is not afraid, but my heart is not competitive. My comrades-in-arms all know that I have a problem, and I will not be allowed to participate in any work related to the aftermath. This war was big, everyone was very busy, and I took the initiative to ask for the disposal of the corpses. At this moment, the heartbeat was already beating like a gong and drum. I won't do it myself, please forgive me.我们表示了充分的理解。只是河莲嘟囔了一句，竹干事，可惜了。你这个样子，恐怕当将军无望了。

  We express our full understanding. It's just that He Lian muttered, it's a pity for the bamboo officer. If you look like this, I'm afraid there is no hope of becoming a general.我义不容辞地走上前去，揭开了尸床上的盖布。我的动作很大，想象中，那布该是冷重如山。不想白布像云一般，飘然飞起，在半空中平平地伸展开，好像被一股神奇之气横托着，久久才悠然而落。一名年轻士兵的脸，像新月一样，洁白光滑地对着天花板，静静地躺在水泥床上，眼皮微睁，蝌蚪般漆黑的瞳仁，稍微倾斜地看着我们。

  I was obliged to step forward and uncover the cover on the corpse bed. My movements were very large, and in my imagination, the cloth should be as cold as a mountain. I don't want the white cloth to fly up like a cloud, stretching out flat in mid-air, as if it is held horizontally by a magical aura, and it takes a long time to fall leisurely. The face of a young soldier, like a crescent moon, white and smooth against the ceiling, lying quietly on the cement bed, with slightly open eyelids and tadpole-like black pupils, looked at us slightly at an angle.

  悚然震惊！

  Shocked!在揭开这块布之前，虽然他明明就在我们身边，我们下意识里以为他未必真的存在。揭开这块布以后，他以极大的威严君临一切，不存在的是我们。

  Before uncovering this cloth, although he was obviously by our side, we subconsciously thought that he might not really exist. After uncovering this cloth, he reigns over everything with great majesty, and we are the ones who do not exist.他穿着很整齐的棉军装，只是腰间有些臃肿，好像揣了几颗手雷。其他部位严谨利落，并无血迹，一时间竟看不出伤处所在。脸如同大理石雕刻，因为失去了热血灌注，就像高大的乔木在冬季落尽叶子，线条刚硬简洁。嘴唇的曲线因为死前的痛苦与坚忍，略有弯曲，好像有一句很重要的话，封闭在紧咬的牙关之后。他的手很规矩地半握着拳，紧贴着裤线安放着，似乎准备随时收起肘关节，取胸前半端位，唰唰摆动起来，应和着口令开始跑步。

  He was wearing a very neat cotton military uniform, but his waist was a little bloated, as if he was carrying a few grenades. The other parts were rigorous and neat, and there was no blood, and the wound could not be seen for a while. The face is like a marble carving, because it has lost its blood infusion, like a tall tree losing its leaves in winter, and the lines are rigid and simple. The curve of the lips was slightly curved because of the pain and perseverance before death, as if there was a very important sentence, closed behind the clenched teeth. His hands were half-clenched into fists in an orderly manner, placed close to the trouser line, as if ready to fold his elbow joints at any time, take the front half of his chest, swing it, and start running with the password.竹干事挤在墙角嘶哑着嗓子说，先找到伤口，然后清洗。然后给他穿上新军装。旧衣服里面的每一件遗物，都要告诉我，我好做登记。如果有钱什么的，更要保存好，以便交给家属。

  The bamboo officer squeezed into the corner and said in a hoarse voice, first find the wound, and then clean it. Then put on his new military uniform. Every relic in the old clothes, tell me, so that I can register it. If you have money or something, you must keep it so that it can be handed over to your family.我们无声地点点头，表示明白了。

  We nodded silently to show understanding.我轻轻地走到班长面前，解开了他棉衣的扣子。那些圆滑的塑料扣子，因为一直在冰冷的太平间里沉浸着，摸在手里，如同机器制造的冰雹。我的手指不一会儿就冻僵了，解得很慢，大家凑过来要给我帮忙。我说，河莲站对面，暂时有我们两人就够了。别的人听我指挥，需要什么东西，你们好去找。

  I gently walked up to the squad leader and unbuttoned his cotton coat. Those sleek plastic buttons, because they have been immersed in the cold morgue, are like machine-made hail in their hands. My fingers froze after a while, and they were very slow to untie, and everyone came over to help me. I said, opposite Ha Lian Station, it is enough for the two of us to have it for the time being. Others listen to my command, what you need, you can find it."我知道给死人脱衣穿衣，比给活人做这套动作麻烦多了。本来只以为他不会配合，操作者多费点力气就是，干起来才明白，生死这道分水岭，把简单的事变成了一道天大的难题。

  I know that undressing the dead is much more troublesome than doing this action for the living. I just thought that he would not cooperate, but the operator took a little more effort, but when he did it, he realized that the watershed of life and death turned a simple thing into a huge problem.上衣扣子解开后，局势开始明朗。腰间的膨出更加明显，暴露出白色的三角巾，那里必是致命的伤口所在。三角巾其实完全不能再称为白色，它被鲜血染成通红之后又凝结为深咖啡色，坚硬干燥，像一块巨大的巧克力板。

  After the shirt was unbuttoned, the situation began to become clear. The bulge on the waist is even more obvious, exposing a white triangular scarf, where the fatal wound must be. The triangular scarf can no longer be called white at all, it is dyed red with blood and then condensed into a dark coffee color, hard and dry, like a huge chocolate board.我企图把它解开，马上发现是痴心妄想。血液凝固再加冷冻，强度赛过钢板。我头也不抬地问，腹部缠着浸满陈血的三角巾，解不开，怎么办？

  I tried to untie it, but immediately found out that it was wishful thinking. Blood coagulation and freezing, the strength is more than the steel plate. I asked without raising my head, I had a triangular scarf soaked in old blood wrapped around my abdomen, and I couldn't untie it, what should I do?我知道竹干事在远处密切注视着事态的进程，以他的经验，随时准备答疑解难。

  I know that Officer Zhu is closely watching the development of the situation from a distance, and with his experience, he is always ready to answer questions and solve problems.先把情况搞清楚。竹干事指示。

  Let's figure out the situation first. Bamboo officer instructed.我观察了一下三角巾，因是战友匆忙包扎，不似专业医务人员规范，有的地方紧，有的地方松。我把手指探到血绷带之下，艰难地暗中摸索。先是在腹部正面触到半个圆滚滚的东西，好像是老式的台灯罩，然后又在它的四周摸到一摊腻滑的东西，好像是盘起来的电缆。经过卫生员训练，我对人的肚子部位大致该有什么，已是心里有数，但对这摊物件，实在想不出是什么，颇感莫名其妙。

  I observed the triangular scarf, because it was hastily bandaged by my comrades-in-arms, unlike the norms of professional medical staff, some places were tight, and some places were loose. I put my finger under the blood bandage and groped secretly with difficulty. First, I touched a half-round thing on the front of my abdomen, which seemed to be an old-fashioned lampshade, and then I felt a slippery thing around it, as if it was a coiled cable. After training as a hygienist, I already know what a person's stomach should have, but I really can't think of what it is for this stall of things, which is quite inexplicable.看我愣着发呆，竹干事说，摸着什么啦？

  Seeing me in a daze, the bamboo officer said, what are you touching?我说，不知道。硬，滑，圆，一缕一缕的……

  I said, I don't know. Hard, slippery, round, strands of ......那是肠子。竹干事说。

  That's the intestines. Zhu officer said.我结巴着说，在……哪儿？肠……子？

  I stammered, in ...... Where? Intestines...... Child?就在你手底下。竹干事把头扭向一侧，不看我，盯着太平间洁白无瑕的墙壁说。

  It's under your hands. The bamboo officer turned his head to the side, didn't look at me, and stared at the white and flawless walls of the morgue and said.我说，你也没见，怎么知道？

  I said, you haven't seen it either, how do you know?竹干事说，这就是老兵和新兵的不同、干部和战士的区别。咱们吃军粮的年头还不一样呢。子弹击中了这小伙子的肚子，肠子流了出来……就这样。很简单。

  Officer Zhu said that this is the difference between veterans and recruits, and between cadres and soldiers. The years when we eat military rations are different. The bullet hit the young man's stomach and his intestines flowed out...... That's it. Very simple.既然确定是腹部外伤，伤处就是清洁处理的主要部位。再像挖巷道那样，把手探进去作业肯定不成，需要把三角巾取下来。

  Since it is determined that it is an abdominal trauma, the wound is the main part of the cleaning treatment. Like digging a roadway, you will definitely not be able to do the work by sticking your hand in, and you need to take off the triangle towel.拿剪子。我吩咐道。

  Take scissors. I ordered.小鹿说，拿哪种剪子呢？

  The deer said, which kind of scissors to take?我们每个人只有巴掌大的旅行剪刀，平常剪个补丁什么的，还可凑合。对付这种血染的绷带，简直是头发丝系轮船，力不从心。炊事班还有几把抠鱼鳃破鱼肚的大铁剪刀，用于烈士身体显然不敬。我略一思索，转而对果平说，去，把手术室的剪刀拿来。

  Each of us only has a palm-sized travel scissors, and we usually cut a patch or something, and we can make do. Dealing with this blood-stained bandage is simply like a hair tied to a ship, and it is powerless. The cooking class also had several large iron scissors that cut the gills of fish and broke the belly of the fish, which were obviously disrespectful to the martyrs' bodies. I thought for a moment, then turned to Guo Ping and said, go, bring the scissors in the operating room.按说我一个小兵，没权私自把手术室的装备带到太平间。但县官不如现管，果平是手术室的护士，我是她的班长，调把剪刀出来，还不手到擒来？

  It is said that I am a small soldier and have no right to bring the equipment of the operating room to the morgue without permission. But the county official is not as good as the current management, Guo Ping is the nurse in the operating room, I am her squad leader, transfer the scissors out, and don't you get it?果平跑出又跑进，把锋利的手术剪刀递我说，给。

  Guo Ping ran out and ran in, handed me the sharp surgical scissors and said, give.我操刀就剪，原以为必然势如破竹，没想到，不锈钢的剪刀只把血纱布豁开一个小切口，就再也推不动了。好像用刮胡刀片切西瓜，深入不下去。

  I cut it with a knife, I thought it would be like a bamboo shoot, but I didn't expect that the stainless steel scissors only opened a small incision in the blood gauze, and I couldn't push it anymore. It seems to be cutting watermelon with a razor blade, and it can't go deep.我埋怨果平，你这剪刀也太钝了。

  I complained to Guo Ping, your scissors are too dull.果平委屈地说，我特地挑了把新的呀！

  Guo Ping said aggrievedly, I specially picked a new one!我说，那就换大号的手术刀。

  I said, then change to a large scalpel.果平刚要再跑，竹干事说，刀也不一定行。手术器械都是给活人准备的，自然以小巧精确为上。对付死人，又是血又是泥的，搅到一块儿，比混凝土还结实，好比是秀才遇见兵，没用。人已经死了，就不必考虑那么多了，用锯吧。

  Guo Ping was about to run again, but the bamboo officer said that the knife might not work. Surgical instruments are all prepared for living people, so naturally small and precise is the best. Dealing with the dead is both blood and mud, stirred together, stronger than concrete, like a scholar meeting a soldier, useless. People are already dead, so you don't have to think so much, just use a saw.我对小如说，你到木工房去一趟，借把锯来。

  I said to Xiaoru, you go to the carpentry room and borrow a saw.小如说，他们那儿正赶做棺材哪，不一定借得出来。

  Xiaoru said that they are rushing to make coffins there, and they may not be able to borrow them.我说，就一会儿，跟他们说点好话。再说了，咱们这儿要是不给烈士穿好衣服，他们的棺材里躺谁啊！

  I said, just say something nice to them in a while. Besides, if we don't dress the martyrs here, who will lie in their coffins!小如拔腿走，竹干事说，顺便再借个木匠来。

  Xiao Ru pulled out his legs and left, and the bamboo officer said, by the way, borrow another carpenter.小如说，干什么啊？

  Xiaoru said, what are you doing?竹干事说，谁能使锯子？你们还是我？我是会，可这会儿我的心跳已经一百八十下了，没法干活儿。也许我官僚，调查研究不够，你们这里还有女木匠？

  Bamboo officials say, who can use a saw? Are you or me? I will, but at this time my heart is already beating 180 beats, and I can't work. Maybe I'm a bureaucrat, I don't investigate enough, you still have female carpenters here?河莲鼓了鼓嘴巴。我知她老爹是将军，指挥打仗可能有遗传，但木匠肯定没练过，把嘴鼓成蛤蟆也没用。

  He Lian puffed out her mouth. I know that her father is a general, and commanding wars may have heredity, but the carpenter must not have practiced, and it is useless to puff out his mouth into a toad.小如说，借借试试。但锯子有百分之八十的准头，木匠只有百分之二十的把握。

  If you say it, please give it a try. But the saw has an eighty percent accuracy, and the carpenter is only twenty percent sure.竹干事说，你先去。木匠如果不来，我就带着枪去请。

  Officer Zhu said, you go first. If the carpenter doesn't come, I'll take a gun and invite him.这事就算商量妥了，没想到河莲说，用人工多慢啊，用电锯多好啊。

  Even if this matter was discussed, I didn't expect He Lian to say, how slow it is to use labor, how good it is to use a chainsaw.我没好气地说，到哪儿找电锯？

  I said angrily, where can I find a chainsaw?河莲胸有成竹，说手术室就有电动骨科锯。

  He Lian is confident and says that there is an electric orthopedic saw in the operating room.果平说，哎呀，我倒忘了，真是有的。只是平时极少用，只有截肢的时候才拿出来。河莲，你眼里真有东西，连我这个手术室护士都没想到。

  Guo Ping said, oh, I forgot, there really is. It's just that it's rarely used, and it's only taken out when amputated. He Lian, there is really something in your eyes, even I, the operating room nurse, didn't expect it.河莲说，你忘了我曾在手术室代过几天班？你的家当都印在我的脑瓜里了。随时留心地形地物和一切地面设施的分布与功能，是一个优秀军人必不可少的素养……

  He Lian said, have you forgotten how many days I worked in the operating room? Your belongings are all printed in my mind. Paying attention to the distribution and function of terrain and all ground facilities at any time is an essential quality for an excellent soldier......我打断她说，河莲，那你会用电锯吗？

  I interrupted her and said, Ha Lian, then you will use a chainsaw?河莲做出不好意思的模样说，真叫你猜着了，我偷着练过，还真能凑合着用。

  He Lian made an embarrassed look and said, I really guessed it, I practiced secretly, and I can really make do with it.果平惊道，你本事可真大，就差没偷着给自己开刀了吧？

  Guo Ping said in surprise, you are really capable, you almost didn't secretly open a knife on yourself, right?河莲惭愧地说，我用锯没有师傅指点，按照书上写的自己摸索，操作不一定正规，也算是自学成才。

  He Lian said ashamedly, I used the saw without the master's guidance, and I groped according to what was written in the book, and the operation was not necessarily formal, and it could be regarded as self-taught.果平取回骨科电锯，寒光闪闪，令人生畏。河莲接过来，对着烈士说了一句，大哥，我自知手艺不精，可事到临头，只有我为您做这件事了。您就多担待着点吧。我呢，手下也悠着点劲儿。好在您那么重的伤都忍了，这会儿感觉也不灵敏，熬一熬，马上就过去了。您要没什么意见，咱这就开始了。

  Guo Ping retrieved the orthopedic chainsaw, which was cold and frightening. As for me, my subordinates are also a little more relaxed. Fortunately, you have endured such a serious injury, and you don't feel sensitive at this time. If you don't have any objections, let's get started."我们扭过头看看尸床上的班长。千真万确，我们都看见他眨了一下眼睛。

  We turned our heads to look at the squad leader on the corpse bed. It's true, we all saw him blink.河莲说完，操着电锯，接上电源，跃马横刀，就在血板上操练起来。电锯发出喑哑的噪音，像一头沉闷的野兽在呜咽。布三角巾的纤维应声断裂，沿着锯口的边缘卷曲起来，每根布毛的外周都是暗褐色的，但血未能浸透的内芯，还保持着布的本色，好像一种外红内白的奇异羊毛，被一根根扯断了。

  After He Lian finished speaking, she wielded a chainsaw, connected to the power supply, and practiced on the platelet. The chainsaw made a hoarse noise, like a dull beast whimpering. The fibers of the cloth triangular scarf broke and curled along the edge of the saw edge, and the outer periphery of each cloth hair was dark brown, but the inner core that was not soaked with blood still maintained the true color of the cloth, like a strange wool that was red on the outside and white on the inside, torn off one by one.机械化就是比手工快得多，片刻工夫，血板像断裂的盔甲，碎为两瓣。河莲放下电锯，用力一掰，血板就像散了桶箍的木板，向两侧打开。班长神秘的腹部，暴露在众人眼前。

  Mechanization is much faster than manual work, and in a moment, the platelets are like broken armor, shattered into two lobes. He Lian put down the chainsaw and broke it hard, and the blood plate opened to both sides like a wooden board with a loose bucket hoop. The squad leader's mysterious abdomen was exposed to everyone.真相大白。

  Truth.他的下腹部是一个触目惊心的大弹孔，肠子汹涌地流出来。急救时，战友们用一个大号军用饭碗扣在肠管上面。碗口罩不住，长长的肠子就盘在碗的四周，好像水泥管子上头盖了一顶小草帽。

  His lower abdomen was a shocking large bullet hole, and his intestines flowed out violently. During first aid, the comrades-in-arms used a large military rice bowl to buckle the intestinal tube. The bowl mask can't be held, and the long intestines are coiled around the bowl, like a small straw hat covered with a cement pipe.竹干事远远地看了一眼，闭着眼睛说，把碗取下来，把肠子塞回去。

  Zhu Officer glanced at it from a distance, closed his eyes and said, take off the bowl and stuff the intestines back.这无疑是正确的。但人的肠子流出来容易，塞回去可不那么简单。首先是碗取不下来。它和肠子紧密粘成牢不可破的一坨，好像埋藏了千万年的化石。

  This is undoubtedly true. But it is easy for people's intestines to flow out, and it is not so simple to stuff it back. The first is that the bowl cannot be removed. It is tightly glued to the intestines into an unbreakable lump, as if it has been buried for millions of years.

  当然，可以再用刀锯之类，强行把碗取下。但无论怎样小心，都会伤了班长的肠子。哪里能忍心让战友再受伤害！我们盯着竹干事，等他拿主意。

  Of course, you can use a knife or saw to forcibly remove the bowl. But no matter how careful you are, it will hurt the squad leader's intestines. How can you bear to let your comrades be hurt again! We stared at the bamboo officer and waited for him to make up his mind.竹干事眯缝着眼，似看非看地朝着这边，想必也在发愁。

  The bamboo officer squinted his eyes and looked this way, presumably worried as well.点火！竹干事说。

  Ignition! Zhu officer said.烧哪儿？我们齐声问。

  Where to burn? We asked in unison.当然是烧炉子！莫非你们还想把房给烧了？竹干事火了。

  Of course, burn the stove! Could it be that you still want to burn down the house? The bamboo officer is on fire.太平间里是没有炉子的。当初盖屋的时候，设计者一定想死人不需要保暖。今天为了让凝固的肠子和饭碗分开，必须加热太平间。

  There is no stove in the morgue. When building a house, the designer must have thought that the dead did not need to be warm. Today, in order to separate the solidified intestines from the rice bowl, the morgue must be heated.搬炉子架烟囱来不及，我们分头从别处找来几个炭盆，把燃烧的红柳根放进去，围着尸床摆了一圈。旗帜般的火苗在盆里欢快地跳跃着，由于冷热空气的剧烈对流，火舌会突然冲出盆子的上空，互相勾引着，在一个极短的瞬间，在空气中融成不规则的火环。然后又气急败坏地分开，独自很有弹性地跳动着，给屋里带来春天的气息。静卧着的班长的头发被气流吹开，惨白的脸庞反射着金粉色的光辉。

  It was too late to move the stove chimney, so we found a few charcoal basins from elsewhere, put the burning red willow roots in it, and placed them in a circle around the corpse bed. The flag-like flames jumped happily in the basin, and due to the violent convection of hot and cold air, the tongues of fire would suddenly rush out of the air above the basin, seducing each other, and in a very short moment, they would melt into an irregular ring of fire in the air. Then they separated angrily, beating alone and elasticly, bringing the breath of spring to the house. The hair of the squad leader who was lying still was blown away by the airflow, and his pale face reflected a golden pink brilliance.等待。等待铁和血的分离。许久，许久。我们默不作声，在死去的人周围架起火焰，让人有一种宗教般的感悟，说不出话来。竹干事似乎受不了压抑的气氛，到屋外换气。

  Await. Wait for the separation of iron and blood. For a long time, for a long time. We are silent and set up flames around the dead, giving people a religious sense that they are speechless. The bamboo officer seemed to be unable to stand the oppressive atmosphere and went outside to ventilate.有滴答的血水从尸床上流下。河莲用手轻轻一拔，碗就取掉了。

  There was dripping blood flowing down from the corpse bed. He Lian gently pulled it out with her hand, and the bowl was removed.我们都倒抽了一口冷气。没了饭碗的掩饰，致命的伤口更加狰狞可怖。血肉横飞不说，透过肠子的缝隙，依稀看得到尸床的水泥板。

  More terrifying is yet to come. The squad leader's wound on the front was scary, but the gun hole on his back was very small. The enemy frantically used the internationally banned Tom bomb, causing huge wounds.河莲严峻地说，班长，你知道这说明了什么？

  How is this possible? For a while, we were stunned, and quickly searched for the bamboo officer with our eyes, and he led a round-faced soldier just into the door.这是和班长烈士一起参加战斗的战士，让他给你们讲讲经过吧。竹干事看着地面说。

  Of course we know that He Lian is not a spy, but the mood of the round face is also understandable. After listening to him, we knew why the bullet hit the young squad leader from behind.在边界上活动的叛匪，极端剽悍骁勇。他们奉行一种打得赢就抢、打不赢就跑的策略，经常从国境的那一端武装回窜，见了老百姓的牛羊就抢，然后一声呼哨，流窜回那边，围着篝火烤着抢来的羊腿，吃个一醉方休。待到羊腿吃光，舔舔嘴唇，他们又开始策划下一轮的抢劫了。

  The rebels operating on the border are extremely fierce and brave. They pursued a strategy of grabbing if they won and running away if they couldn't win, often fleeing back armed from the other end of the border, robbing the cattle and sheep of the common people when they saw them, and then whistling and wandering back there, roasting the stolen lamb legs around the bonfire, and eating drunk before resting. When the lamb leg was eaten and licked their lips, they began to plan the next round of robbery.老百姓遇难，首先想到的是找边防军。这一天，有人报告，叛匪又来了，抢了牛羊，正在向格乐山口逃窜。边防军兵分几路，向格乐方向飞驰，力争在国境线的这一面，把敌人堵截住，把老百姓的牛羊救下。

  When ordinary people are killed, the first thing they think of is to find border guards. On this day, someone reported that the rebels had come again, robbed cattle and sheep, and were fleeing towards Gele Pass. The border guards divided into several routes and galloped in the direction of Gele, striving to block the enemy on this side of the border and save the cattle and sheep of the common people.我和班长一路，我们跑得最快，班长做梦都想立功。圆圆脸说。

  The squad leader and I ran the fastest all the way, and the squad leader dreamed of making meritorious contributions. The round face said.前面是一座高山，有一个山口。我们骑着马，旋风一般向前冲去。马上就要到山顶了，按照常规，应该下马，匍匐前进，侦察好前面的情况，再继续追击。可是班长求胜心切，怕敌人赶在我们前面撤回国境那边，就大叫了一声，同志们，跟我冲啊！第一个飞上了山顶。叛匪多么老奸巨猾，他们算定了边防军一定会拼命堵截，就事先在路上埋伏好了，把枪口的准星和山顶对成了一条线，只待我们的人马一出现，就开枪阻击。在平常的电影和小说里，都是我们打鬼子的埋伏，其实，敌人也会这一套，也能给我们布个口袋阵。班长骑着马，冲上顶峰的那一瞬，我正好在班长旁边，稍靠后一点。班长英武极了，背后是雪原，像是天兵天将。没想到，就在这一秒钟，敌人的枪声响了……他们都是惯匪，加上又有准备，枪法很好，第一枪就击中了班长的马眼。那马眼珠迸裂，一声嘶鸣，痛得腾空跳了起来，疯狂地掉转了身子……正在这时，敌人的第二枪赶到了，他瞄的是班长的胸膛，由于战马飞腾而起，转了一百八十度的圈，这发子弹就从班长的背后射入，把肚子炸开了。

  In front is a high mountain with a mountain pass. We rode our horses and rushed forward like a whirlwind. We are about to reach the top of the mountain, and according to the usual practice, we should dismount, crawl forward, scout the situation ahead, and then continue the pursuit. But the squad leader was eager to win, afraid that the enemy would rush to withdraw to the border in front of us, so he shouted, comrades, rush with me! The first flew to the top of the mountain. How cunning the rebels were, they calculated that the border guards would desperately intercept them, so they ambushed them on the road in advance, aligned the crosshair of the muzzle of the gun with the top of the mountain, and only waited for our men and horses to appear, and then shot to block them. In ordinary movies and novels, it is our ambush to fight the devils, in fact, the enemy will also do this, and he can also set up a pocket formation for us. The moment the squad leader rode his horse and rushed to the top, I happened to be next to the squad leader, a little behind. The squad leader is extremely heroic, with a snowfield behind him, like a heavenly soldier and a general. Unexpectedly, at this second, the enemy's gunfire rang out...... They were all habitual bandits, and they were prepared, and their marksmanship was very good, and the first shot hit the squad leader's horse's eye. The horse's eyes burst, he let out a hiss, jumped up in pain and turned around frantically...... At this moment, the enemy's second shot arrived, he aimed at the squad leader's chest, and as the war horse soared, it turned 180 degrees, and the bullet shot in from the back of the squad leader, blowing his stomach open.我们慌了，眼见得班长的肠子像绳子一样地掉出来。我们喊，班长班长……班长说，喊什么，没见过人肠子，还没见过猪肠子吗！他一边把掉出来的肠子往伤口里送，一边说，别管我！快打敌人！我们立刻开始了还击，把子弹像泼凉水一般地洒过去。叛匪看势头不好，就甩下被打死的同伙和抢来的牛羊，缩回到国境那边。

  We panicked and saw the squad leader's intestines fall out like a rope. We shouted, squad leader...... The squad leader said, what are you shouting, I haven't seen human intestines, haven't I seen pig intestines yet! He sent the fallen intestine to the wound and said, leave me alone! Fight the enemy! We immediately began to fight back, spilling bullets like cold water. Seeing that the momentum was not good, the rebels threw away their killed accomplices and stolen cattle and sheep, and retreated to the other side of the border.我们围着班长，他的肠子送回去一部分，还剩一些塞不进去。人的肚子也像箱子似的，有的时候，你要是把东西都翻出来，再放就盛不下了。不知是谁想起，战地救护手册上写过，碰到肠子流出来，要用一个干净的碗扣在上面。我就把饭碗拿出来，那个碗就是我的……圆圆脸指指炭盆旁的大号军用饭碗。

  We surrounded the squad leader, and some of his intestines were sent back, and some of them couldn't be stuffed. A person's stomach is also like a box, sometimes, if you turn things out, you can't hold them again. I don't know who remembered that the field ambulance manual wrote that when the intestines flow out, you have to use a clean bowl to buckle it. I took out the rice bowl, and that bowl was mine...... The round face points to the large military rice bowl next to the charcoal basin.……一个战友撕开了急救包，把班长的肚子包扎起来。班长说，战斗很漂亮啊，除了我，你们都可以立功。我们说，班长，头功是你的。班长说，我口渴……到处都是雪，因为追击紧张，我们都没带水壶，这时就用嘴巴含了雪，化成水，喂给班长……班长的血流个不止，地下成了一片红雪。班长刚开始还能咽下我们的水，但过了一会儿，牙关就越来越紧，雪水也喂不进了。我们吓得不行，有几个人就掉眼泪。班长说，别哭，战士可以流血，不能流泪……我好想家里的人啊……话没完，人就不行了……

  …… A comrade-in-arms tore open the first aid kit and bandaged the squad leader's stomach. The squad leader said, the battle is beautiful, except for me, you can all make meritorious contributions. We said, squad leader, the first credit is yours. The monitor said, I'm thirsty...... There was snow everywhere, because the pursuit was tense, we didn't bring a water bottle, so we took the snow in our mouths, turned it into water, and fed it to the squad leader...... The squad leader's blood flowed continuously, and the ground turned into a piece of red snow. The squad leader was able to swallow our water at first, but after a while, his teeth became tighter and tighter, and he couldn't feed the snow water. We were so scared that a few people shed tears. The squad leader said, don't cry, soldiers can shed blood, not tears...... I miss my family so much...... If the words are not finished, people will not be able to do it......圆圆脸说到这儿，泪流满面。

  The round face burst into tears when he said this.河莲说，合着你们班长连一个敌人也没打死，整个是壮志未酬。没点军事头脑，死得没价值，冤枉啊。

  He Lian said, together with your squad leader, did not even kill an enemy, the whole ambition was unrewarded. Without military acumen, death is worthless, unjust.圆圆脸说，不许你这么说我们班长。他只比我大一岁，也没上过军事院校，看过唯一讲兵法的书，就是《水浒》。他用命告诉我们，让我们都记住了，打仗会流血。

  The round face said, you are not allowed to say that about our squad leader. He is only one year older than me, has never been to a military academy, and the only book that talks about the art of war is "Water Margin". He told us with his life that we all remember that there will be blood in war.河莲说，干什么都会流血。

  He Lian said that everything you do will bleed.圆圆脸愤愤地说，你们躲在后方，流什么血！

  Yuanyuan said indignantly, you are hiding in the rear, what kind of blood are you shedding!一句话把大家噎得哑口无言。竹干事有气无力地说，分工不同。你去让后勤部把新衣服送来，记着要比你们班长平日穿的大一号，帽子要大两号，鞋要大三号。

  One sentence left everyone speechless. Zhu Officer said weakly that the division of labor was different. You go and ask the logistics department to send you new clothes, remember to be one size larger than your squad leader usually wears, two sizes larger for hats, and three sizes larger for shoes.圆圆脸走了。大家说，下一步干什么？

  The round face left. Everyone said, what is the next step?我说，把班长全身的旧衣服都换下来。

  I said, change all the old clothes on the squad leader's body.竹干事说，对。可以用电锯，但记着别把衣服的兜锯破，一会儿还得清点遗物。

  The bamboo officer said, yes. You can use a chainsaw, but remember not to saw the belt of clothes, and you will have to count the relics later.河莲很乐意干这活儿，电锯忙碌不停，好像在锯一棵古树。棉衣锯开了，棉裤锯开了，绒衣锯开了，绒裤锯开了……卸下的衣服堆在墙角，支离破碎。

  He Lian was happy to do this job, and the chainsaw was busy non-stop, as if sawing an ancient tree. The cotton clothes were sawn open, the cotton pants were sawn open, the velvet clothes were sawn open, and the velvet pants were sawn open...... The unloaded clothes were piled up in the corner and shattered.班长现在像个婴儿一样无牵无挂地躺着，我们开始为他洗澡。我们用新的毛巾，泡在温水里，轻轻绞干，很仔细地给他洗脸擦身。

  The squad leader is now lying carefree like a baby, and we start bathing him. We used a new towel, soaked it in warm water, gently wrung it dry, and carefully washed his face and body.把班长像件瓷器一般洗干净，新衣服也送来了。穿衣的时候，我们遇到了今天以来最大的困难。新衣服不像旧衣服，可以一毁了事，必得整整齐齐、妥妥帖帖套在死人身上。人又不是木板，你说怎么穿？

  The squad leader was washed like a piece of porcelain, and new clothes were delivered. When it came time to get dressed, we encountered the biggest difficulty since today. New clothes are not like old clothes, they can ruin things, and they must be neatly and properly put on the dead. People are not wooden boards, how do you say to wear them?裤子还好说，我们搬起他的腿，托着他的腰，费了九牛二虎之力，总算穿上了。那一堆肠子不好处理，塞不进去又不能耷拉着。大家就把地上的瓷碗又捡了起来，盖在肠子上，用绷带绑好。除了小伙子的肚子看起来有些大腹便便，基本上说得过去。

  The pants are okay, we lifted his legs, supported his waist, and finally put them on. That pile of intestines is not easy to handle, and it can't be stuffed in or drooped. Everyone picked up the porcelain bowl on the ground again, covered it with intestines, and tied it with a bandage. Except that the young man's belly looks a little pot-bellied, which is basically reasonable.关键在上衣。好不容易穿上一只袖子，那一只无论如何都穿不上。班长的胳膊硬如铁棒，完全不会打弯。

  The key is in the top. Finally put on one sleeve, and that one couldn't be worn anyway. The squad leader's arm is as hard as an iron rod and will not bend at all.给死人穿衣服，是不能一只袖子单穿的，必须扶他坐起来，把他的两只胳膊一齐向后伸展，就像我们平日上双杠做预备动作似的，同时往后悠，两人齐努力，衣服才能穿上。竹干事萎靡不振，声音小得像马蜂嗡嗡，幸好还清楚。

  Dressing a dead person cannot be worn alone with one sleeve, and he must be helped to sit up, stretching his two arms back, just like we usually do preparatory movements on the parallel bars, and at the same time, walk backwards, and the two of them work together before the clothes can be put on. The bamboo officer was sluggish, and his voice was as small as a wasp buzzing, but fortunately it was clear.

  虽说我们和烈士班长相处已经有一段时间了，但一想到要扶他坐起，还是让人不寒而栗。小鹿说，我还是在前面压着他的腿吧，省得他一下坐不稳了，摔到床下。

  Although we have been with the martyr squad leader for a while, the thought of helping him sit up is still chilling. Xiaolu said, I'd better press his leg in front of him, so that he wouldn't be able to sit steadily and fall under the bed.大家都觉得她有点担子拣轻的挑的意思，可一想她最小，就拉倒了。

  Everyone thought she was a bit of a burden picking lightly, but when they thought that she was the smallest, they pulled it down.河莲主动说，我在后面扶着。你们给他穿衣服，动作要快点，时间长了，我可坚持不了。

  He Lian took the initiative to say, I was holding on from behind. You dress him, move faster, I can't hold on after a long time.竹干事有气无力地说，他怎么也是个小伙子，你是小姑娘。他的分量有你两个沉，要是撑不住了，我帮你。

  Zhu Officer said weakly, he is also a young man, you are a little girl. His weight is as heavy as yours, if you can't hold on, I'll help you."河莲说，没事。万一顶不住，我就坐到水泥台子上，和他背靠背。小时候玩翻饼烙饼的游戏，都这么来着。

  He Lian said, it's okay. In case I couldn't stand it, I sat on the cement platform and stood back to back with him. When I was a child, I played the game of flipping pancakes, and it was like this.竹干事叹道，好样的。你这丫头有勇有谋，以后能当团长。

  The bamboo officer sighed, good. You girl is brave and resourceful, and you can be the head of the regiment in the future."河莲说，团长算什么？官太小了，我起码要当到军长。

  He Lian said, what is the regiment commander? The official is too small, at least I want to be a military commander.大家说着，颤颤巍巍地把班长扶坐起来。那张原本已经看熟的脸，一旦从躺着变成立着，又使人震惊一次。班长的身后，由于积血形成大片尸斑，全是怪异的深蓝色。他的手向后伸的时候，胳膊也是半只白半只青，煞是恐怖。

  Everyone said, trembling and helping the squad leader to sit up. That face that was already familiar, once it changed from lying down, was shocking again. Behind the squad leader, a large area of corpse spots formed due to blood accumulation, all of which were strange dark blue. When his hand stretched back, his arm was also half white and half green, which was terrifying.我们给他穿上本白色的士兵衬衣，把不祥的蓝色遮盖住，然后是绒衣和棉衣。待到一切收拾完毕，我们已累得汗流浃背。

  We put on a white soldier's shirt that covered the ominous blue, followed by a velvet coat and a cotton coat. By the time everything was tidied up, we were sweating from exhaustion.班长重新睡下时，身着崭新的军装，除了腰带处有点窝囊，其余精干无比。但是我们在给他穿鞋子戴帽子的时候，困难重重。虽然竹干事未雨绸缪加大了尺码，但班长的头和脚都肿胀了，帽子戴不下，鞋子穿不上。

  When the squad leader went back to sleep, he was dressed in a brand new military uniform, except for a little sloppy belt, the rest were extremely capable. But we had a lot of difficulties when we put on his shoes and hats. Although the bamboo officer took precautions and increased the size, the squad leader's head and feet were swollen, and he couldn't wear his hat or shoes.怎么办？我们只有再次请示竹干事。

  What to do? We can only ask the bamboo officer again.用剪子。竹干事说。

  Use scissors. Zhu officer said.剪哪儿？我们不知底细。

  Where to cut? We don't know the details.剪帽子的后面和鞋的两侧，但要伪装好，让人从正面看不出来。竹干事捂着胸口，支撑着说。

  Cut the back of the hat and the sides of the shoe, but camouflage it so that it is not visible from the front. The bamboo officer covered his chest and said.我们照章办理，总算收拾就绪。现在，一个军容整齐的小伙子，微闭着眼，英俊潇洒地躺在我们面前，好似胜仗之后在树下小憩。

  We followed the rules and finally got ready. Now, a young man with a neat military appearance, with his eyes slightly closed, lies handsomely in front of us, as if he is taking a nap under a tree after winning a battle.啊啊，总算干完啦！我们小声欢呼起来。当然，当着烈士遗体欢呼，很不礼貌，但死亡既已无可挽回，年轻的士兵，此刻必然也满心希望以最整洁优雅的形象告别人间，大概也会原谅我们。

  Ahhhh, it's finally done! We cheered softly. Of course, it is very rude to cheer in front of the bodies of the martyrs, but since death is irreparable, young soldiers must also be full of hope to say goodbye to the world with the neat and elegant image at this moment, and they will probably forgive us.竹干事用眼光命令我们把白布蒙上。他认为只有和烈士隔开，我们才有权大声喧哗。我对他说，你要是不舒服，就去休息。剩下的事，我们能干。我冲着破碎的旧衣服努了努嘴，心想，不就是抱出去烧掉吗？

  The bamboo officer ordered us to cover the white cloth with our eyes. He believes that only by being separated from the martyrs can we have the right to make loud noises. I told him, if you are not feeling well, go and rest. We can do the rest. I pouted at the broken old clothes, thinking, isn't it just to take it out and burn it?竹干事说，剩下的事，你可干不了，那是我的正经项目。说完，他掏出一个文件夹，摊开后说，你们谁给我找个凳子来？

  Officer Zhu said, you can't do the rest, that's my serious project. After saying that, he took out a folder, spread it out and said, which of you will find me a stool?烈士躺着，竹干事坐着，我们开始清点并记录军衣兜里的遗物。

  The martyrs were lying down, the bamboo officers were sitting, and we began to count and record the relics in the pockets of our military uniforms.钢笔一支。英雄牌，黑色老式。河莲像饭馆里跑堂的小伙计，拉长嗓门儿报着。

  A pen. Hero card, black old-fashioned. He Lian was like a young man running a hall in a restaurant, stretching her voice to report.伤湿止痛膏两贴，啊，不对。是一贴半。有一面已经揭掉用了。小如轻声说，刚才我给他擦身的时候，在左膝盖看到那半贴了。想不到年纪轻轻的，就得了关节炎。

  Two patches of pain relief cream, ah, no. It's a sticker and a half. One side has been removed. Xiaoru said softly, when I wiped his body just now, I saw the half sticker on my left knee. Unexpectedly, at a young age, he got arthritis.竹干事不喜欢婆婆妈妈，说，关节炎是高原病，和年纪没关系。谁都能得，比如你，比如我。接着干活儿吧。

  Zhu Officer didn't like his mother-in-law, saying that arthritis is altitude sickness and has nothing to do with age. Anyone can get it, like you, like me. Let's get to work.小鹿高声叫起来，说，哈！你们猜，我在他兜里翻出了啥？

  The fawn shouted loudly and said, Ha! Guess what, what did I dig out of his pocket?竹干事说，大惊小怪什么？一个当兵的，能有啥？肯定没存折。

  Officer Zhu said, what are you fuss about? What can a soldier have? There is definitely no passbook.小鹿不理他，继续兴致勃勃地说，是糖啊。三块真正的水果糖，和发给我们的一模一样的水果糖。

  The deer ignored him and continued to say with great interest, it's sugar. Three pieces of real fruit candy, exactly the same fruit candy that was sent to us.小鹿的手心里，托着几块包着草绿色糖纸的水果糖。摩擦久了，翘起的糖纸几乎掉光，椭圆形的糖块沾着斑斑点点的绿色，好像池塘里的小乌龟。

  In the palm of the deer's hand, he held a few pieces of fruit candy wrapped in grass green sugar paper. After rubbing for a long time, the upturned sugar paper almost fell off, and the oval sugar cubes were stained with speckled green, like a little turtle in a pond.竹干事放下笔说，这就不必记了。都是军需发的大路货，没什么特别的价值。家属也不一定需要。

  The bamboo officer put down his pen and said, "I don't have to remember this." They are all big road goods issued by military supplies, and they have no special value. Dependents don't necessarily need it.看着那三块糖，我突然热泪盈眶。在这之前，我一直无法把死去的班长当成一个曾经活过的人，尽管他在我身边，我仍觉得他是幻影，一切都不真实。但这一瞬，我明白他曾是一个活生生的人，像我一样爱吃糖。我被刻骨的悲伤击中。

  Looking at the three pieces of candy, I suddenly burst into tears. Before this, I had never been able to regard the dead squad leader as a person who had lived, and even though he was by my side, I still felt that he was a phantom and that everything was not real. But at this moment, I understood that he was a living person who loved sugar like me. I was struck by bitter sadness.在高原上，凡是外出，可能遭遇种种意外。飓风、雪崩、饥饿、酷寒……要想生存下去，你必须要有热量。糖就是最好的热能，所以，每逢有人走进风雪，叮嘱的最后一句话定是——你带上几块糖了吗？

  On the plateau, whenever you go out, you may encounter all kinds of accidents. Hurricanes, avalanches, hunger, extreme cold...... To survive, you have to have calories. Sugar is the best heat energy, so whenever someone walks into the wind and snow, the last word of advice must be - have you brought a few pieces of sugar?糖，在某些时候，就是生命啊。

  Sugar, at some point, is life.这几块糖，是班长临出发的时候，装入口袋的。哦，也许不是这一次，从糖的磨损和任务的紧急程度看，估计是早已放在身边的陈物。糖，是高原的护身符，班长放入这糖的时候，一定是满怀生的渴望。此刻，糖仍在，生命已悄然远去。这几块糖，寄托了班长对生命的眷恋，怎能说没有特别的价值！

  These pieces of candy were put in the pocket of the squad leader when he was about to leave. Oh, maybe not this time, judging from the wear and tear of the sugar and the urgency of the task, it is estimated that it is an old thing that has been kept by my side for a long time. Sugar is the talisman of the plateau, and when the squad leader puts this sugar, he must be full of desire for life. At this moment, the sugar is still there, and life has quietly gone away. These pieces of candy pin on the squad leader's attachment to life, how can it be said that there is no special value!我对竹干事说，留着这几块糖吧。送给他的爸爸妈妈，这上面有烈士最后的手印。

  I said to the bamboo officer, keep these pieces of sugar. Given to his parents, it has the last handprint of the martyr on it.竹干事说，女孩子就是事多，多愁善感。

  Zhu Officer said that girls are more dominant and sentimental.但他还是很给我面子，在登记簿上歪歪扭扭地记下：军用水果糖三颗。

  But he still gave me face and wrote down crookedly in the register: three military fruit candies.还有吗？竹干事问。

  Is there anything else? The bamboo officer asked.没有了。我们齐声回答。

  Without. We answered in unison.没钱吗？竹干事追问。

  No money? The bamboo officer asked.没有。我们万分肯定地回答。

  No. We answer in the absolute affirmative.一分也没有吗？竹干事继续问。他倒不是不相信我们，因为事关烈士的遗产，必得一清二楚。

  Not a single cent? The bamboo officer continued to ask. It's not that he doesn't believe us, because it's about the legacy of the martyrs, and it must be clear.一分钱也没有。我们斩钉截铁地回答。河莲小声嘀咕，山上一千公里内没有人烟，哪儿有商店？倒是想用钱买氧气，可谁卖给你啊。

  Not a penny. We answer categorically. He Lian muttered softly, there is no one within a thousand kilometers of the mountain, where is the shop? I want to buy oxygen with money, but who will sell it to you?"竹干事假装什么都没听见，走到破烂的碎军衣堆前，说，我还得亲自检查一遍，这是规矩。他一块块碎布细细捏着，好像哨兵在搜查敌军的情报。最后拿起一件衬衣的残骸，说这里面有个小兜，你们看了没有？

  The bamboo officer pretended not to hear anything, walked to the pile of tattered military uniforms, and said, I have to check it myself, this is the rule. He squeezed the rags finely, as if a sentinel was searching for enemy intelligence. Finally, he picked up the remains of a shirt and said that there was a small pocket in it, have you seen it?果平说，没看。那个兜有什么用？装了东西，磨得胸前痛。

  Guo Ping said, I didn't see it. What is the use of that pocket? Loaded with things, grinding my chest painfully.竹干事冷冷地说，那是女人。男人总是把最心爱的东西藏在这里。说着，他从衬衣的布条里，抽出一个牛皮纸信封。

  The bamboo officer said coldly, it was a woman. Men always hide their most beloved things here. As he spoke, he pulled out a kraft paper envelope from the strip of his shirt.我们惊骇莫名，看着竹干事打开信封，他突然扑哧一声笑了。我们这才敢围拢过去，端详信封中的东西。

  We were shocked and inexplicable, watching the bamboo officer open the envelope, he suddenly burst into laughter. Only then did we dare to gather around and examine the contents of the envelope.一张四寸大小的彩色照片，花红柳绿一个乡下妞，露着不整齐的白牙，很忸怩地看着我们。

  A four-inch color photo, a country girl with red flowers and willows, showing untidy white teeth, looked at us very shyly.这是班长他姐吧？要不是他妹？可是怎么长得不大像？河莲自语着，顺手还掀开白布单，朝烈士脸上瞄了两眼。

  This is the squad leader's sister, right? If it weren't for his sister? But why don't they look alike? He Lian muttered to herself, lifted the white cloth sheet, and glanced at the martyr's face.竹干事说，你这个姑娘，一阵聪明一阵傻。有把姐妹的照片这么贴心摆着的吗？依我的经验，肯定是未婚妻。

  Officer Zhu said, you girl, you are smart and stupid. Is there a photo of the sisters so intimately? In my experience, it must be a fiancée.未婚妻？我们惊叫着，又像铁桶一般围过去，火眼金睛地将那女子看了个彻底。小鹿捂着嘴说，嘻嘻，长得可真难看！

  Fiancee? We screamed, surrounded like an iron bucket, and looked at the woman thoroughly with fiery eyes. The fawn covered his mouth and said, hehe, it's really ugly!不知是乡下的摄影师水平太差，还是这女子貌不上相，反正从照片上看：眉毛粗重，鼻梁塌扁，嘴唇阔大，牙列不齐。全脸唯一可夸奖的是眼睛，大而圆，有一种猫一般的灵光。

  I don't know if the level of the photographer in the countryside is too poor, or if this woman is not good-looking, anyway, from the photo: thick eyebrows, flattened nose bridge, wide lips, and uneven dentition. The only thing that can be praised on the whole face is the eyes, which are large and round, with a cat-like aura.我们之中相貌最好的小如，倒还比较宽容，说，她笑得挺开心啊。

  Xiao Ru, who is the best looking among us, is more tolerant, saying, she smiles very happily.果平说，这照相馆的手艺也太次了，把人脸涂得像猴脸。

  Guo Ping said that the craftsmanship of this photo studio is too secondary, painting the human face like a monkey face.照片原是黑白的，为了好看，那女子特地上了颜色。乡下的摄影师用水彩颜料乱涂一气，脸色赤若夕阳，红色还描到脸的轮廓以外，像打碎了红墨水瓶，洇得到处都是。

  The photo was originally black and white, but in order to look good, the woman specially colored it. The photographer in the countryside painted with watercolor paint in a breath, his face was as red as the sunset, and the red color was traced beyond the outline of the face, like breaking a red inkwell, and it was everywhere.小鹿说，我看班长挺漂亮的小伙儿，怎么找这么一个困难户啊？还把她当宝贝，揣在离心脏最近的地方。真是眼神不济啊！

  Xiaolu said, I think the squad leader is a very beautiful young man, how can he find such a difficult household? He also treated her as a treasure and carried her closest to his heart. What a bad look!放肆！竹干事火了，说，她是谁？你们以为是普通的乡下姑娘啊？她是烈士的心上人，是烈士的遗属。现在她还不知道班长的死讯。要是知道了，还不得哭得天昏地暗！你们拿她开心，对得起良心吗？

  Licentious! The bamboo officer was on fire and said, who is she? Do you think it's an ordinary country girl? She is the sweetheart of the martyrs and the bereaved family of the martyrs. Now she doesn't know the death of the squad leader. If you know, you can't cry in the dark!" Are you happy with her and worthy of your conscience?我们原也没想那么多，只是看着一张可笑的照片，就笑起来。女孩子总是这样的，一件并不可笑的事，只要有一个人开始笑，大家就跟着凑热闹，笑上半天。经竹干事这么一说，问题有些严重。想象那照片上的长着猫眼的姑娘，过不了多久就会悲痛欲绝，我们顿时抱愧无比，大家都低下了头。竹干事看我们蔫了，又安慰我们说，好了，总的说来，你们今天的表现还是不错的。班长虽说没轮上和自己的未婚妻告别，有你们这么多姑娘给他送行，心里也该知足了。

  We didn't think so much, we just looked at a ridiculous photo and laughed. Girls are always like this, a thing that is not funny, as long as one person starts laughing, everyone will join in the fun and laugh for a long time. When Jingzhu said this, the problem was a bit serious. Imagine the girl with cat's eyes in the photo, and it won't be long before she will be grief-stricken, and we are all so ashamed that everyone bows their heads. Officer Zhu saw that we were wilting, and comforted us and said, okay, in general, your performance today is still good. Although the squad leader didn't take his turn to say goodbye to his fiancée, he should be content with so many of you girls seeing him off.竹干事说着，在遗物登记簿上规规矩矩地写下：亲人照片一张。他又把堆在地上的碎衣物，像捡破烂的老汉一样，根根梢梢翻了个遍，每个衣角都用大拇指和食指对着捻一回，看藏没藏着东西，直到万无一失。

  The bamboo officer said, and wrote down in an orderly manner on the relic register: a photo of a relative. He turned over the broken clothes piled on the ground like an old man picking up rags, twisting each corner of his clothes with his thumb and index finger to see if there was anything hidden until it was foolproof.

  好了，我们可以撤了。竹干事合上登记簿，疲惫已极地说。他把钢笔和伤湿止痛膏细致地包好，照片也用白纸夹起来。只是把军用水果糖丢在墙角，说，这个就算了吧。转送家属，吃又吃不得，留着还挺伤心，不如眼不见为净。

  Okay, we can withdraw. The bamboo officer closed the registration book, exhausted and said. He carefully wrapped the pen and the pain relief ointment, and the photo was also clipped with white paper. Just throw the military fruit candy in the corner and say, forget it. Forwarding it to the family, it is not possible to eat it, and it is quite sad to keep it, it is better to be out of sight.糖块叽里咕噜地滚着，刚开始声音很脆，好像玻璃弹球在找坑，渐渐地就不怎么响了，太平间地上积满尘土，它们保证已脏得发不出动静了。

  The sugar cubes were rolling in the middle, and the sound was very crisp at first, as if a glass pinball was looking for a pit, and gradually it became less loud, and the morgue floor was covered with dust, and they were guaranteed to be too dirty to move.我们缓缓地往外走，小如突然停了脚，说，竹干事，有一句话，我不知当说不当说。

  We walked out slowly, and Xiaoru suddenly stopped and said, "Officer Bamboo, there is a sentence that I don't know whether to say or not."快走到门前的竹干事，简短地回答，说。

  The bamboo officer who walked to the door replied briefly and said.小如说，竹干事，把相片还给班长吧。

  Xiaoru said, bamboo officer, return the photo to the squad leader.我们一时没明白，但是我们马上就明白了。小如接着说，照片带回去，还给谁呢？给那个姑娘，她会难过死的。他的父母也会难过的，她本来会是他们的儿媳妇，可是以后永远不会是了。最难过的还是班长，他那么心爱的东西被拿走了，永不还他。照片被不认识的人传着看，代为保管，他会不乐意的……

  We didn't understand it for a while, but we understood it right away. Xiaoru continued, who will take the photo back? Give it to that girl, and she will die sadly. His parents will also be sad, she would have been their daughter-in-law, but she will never be. The saddest thing is the squad leader, what he loves so much has been taken away and will never be returned to him. If the photo is passed on to someone you don't know and keeps it on your behalf, he will not be happy......

  我们被小如的话感动，双脚牢牢地站在地上，用这个姿势告诉竹干事，要是他不答应小如的请求，我们就不离开太平间了。

  We were moved by Xiaoru's words, stood firmly on the ground with our feet, and told the bamboo officer in this posture that if he did not agree to Xiaoru's request, we would not leave the morgue.竹干事什么也没说，从纸夹里抽出红脸姑娘的照片，递到小如手里。我们一道走到白如雪峰的尸床前，小如轻轻地揭开白布。班长向上扬起的眉毛是微笑模样，好像在睡梦中赞同我们的主张。我们轻轻地把他的衣扣解开，把照片平平整整地插进他左胸前的衬衣口袋。我看到那张照片有节奏地起伏着，班长年轻的心在托着它跳动。

  Officer Zhu didn't say anything, took out a photo of the red-faced girl from the paper clip and handed it to Xiao Ru. We walked to Bai Ruxuefeng's corpse bed together, and Xiaoru gently lifted the white cloth. The squad leader raised his eyebrows with a smile, as if he agreed with our proposition in his sleep. We gently unbuttoned his clothes and inserted the photo flat into the shirt pocket on his left chest. I saw the photo rising and falling rhythmically, and the young heart of the squad leader was beating with it.我们走出太平间，好像在里面待了一百年，山川河流都有了很大的改变。天变低了，云变重了，太阳是多角形的，雪山也变黑了。竹干事冲我们扬扬细瘦的胳膊，说，再见了，女兵们。但愿有一天我阵亡的时候，还能由你们来为我换衣。

  When we walked out of the morgue, it seemed that after a hundred years in it, the mountains and rivers had changed a lot. The sky is getting lower, the clouds are getting heavier, the sun is polygonal, and the snow-capped mountains are getting darker. The bamboo officer raised his thin arm at us and said, goodbye, female soldiers. I hope that one day when I die, you will change my clothes.我们说，我们不给你换衣服，你还是好好活着，自己给自己换衣服吧。

  We said, we don't change your clothes, you should live well and change your own clothes.

  回到宿舍，我们都拼命讲其他的事情，再也不提一个“死”字。

  Back in the dormitory, we all desperately talked about other things, never mentioning the word "death" again.我趴在地上，从床底下翻自己的细软。找了半天，才从长筒靴后面找到我的宝贝盒。它是我求老兵用三个罐头盒子的铁皮，剪开打制而成。我专挑菠萝罐头盒，因为它的皮不仅结实耐用，而且都是金黄色的，精心砸制出来，好像纯金制成的万宝箱。我抱着它走到背人的角落，打开，里面是满满一盒军用水果糖。它们穿着草绿色的衣服，好像是饱满的小水雷。我一直想不通，高原部队发的糖，为什么是绿色的，难道糖纸也要伪装吗？如果战争打响了，你往嘴里塞进一块红糖纸的糖，就会被敌人发现，而绿糖纸就可安然无恙吗？

  I lay on the ground and turned my soft body under the bed. After searching for a long time, I found my baby box from behind the boots. It was made by me begging the veterans to cut and beat the iron sheets of three can boxes. I specifically chose pineapple canned boxes because their skin is not only strong and durable, but also golden yellow, carefully smashed out, like a treasure chest made of pure gold. I carried it to the corner of the person on my back, opened it, and found a box full of military fruit candy. They are dressed in grass green, like small mines full of mines. I have never been able to figure out why the sugar issued by the plateau troops is green, and does the sugar paper also have to be disguised? If the war starts, you put a piece of brown sugar paper in your mouth, and the enemy will find out, and will the green sugar paper be safe and sound?好了，不想这种节外生枝的问题了，正事要紧。我开始挑选水果糖。平日吃糖的时候，随便抓一块就是。但这一次，我苛刻已极。糖纸稍微有些残破的，颜色不鲜艳的，包括虽然外形完整，但由于被揉搓过，显出一副无精打采样子的水果糖，都毫不留情地淘汰。最后入选的种子选手，都像刚从生产流水线上跳下来的产品，容光焕发。糖块像石子一样坚硬，两端拧起的糖纸，好像小姑娘的刷子辫，舒展又漂亮。

  Okay, I don't want to have this kind of extraneous problem, it's important to get down to business. I started picking fruit candies. When you eat sugar on weekdays, just grab a piece. But this time, I was extremely harsh. The sugar paper is slightly broken and not brightly colored, including the fruit candy that is intact in appearance but shows a listless appearance due to being rubbed, all of which are mercilessly eliminated. The final selected seed contestants are all radiant like products that have just jumped off the production line. The sugar cubes are as hard as stones, and the sugar paper twisted at both ends is like a little girl's brush braid, stretched and beautiful.我揣着糖果，用那把锐利的钥匙开了门，再一次走进太平间。屋子里有一种新衣服浓重的桉叶味儿，混合着炭盆燃烧后的袅袅烟气，好像是一片被雷电击过的热带雨林。班长安详地睡着，我附在他的耳边，轻轻说，对不起啊，再打搅一次……

  I carried the candy, opened the door with the sharp key, and walked into the morgue again. There was a strong smell of eucalyptus leaves in the room, mixed with the smoke from the burning of the charcoal basin, like a tropical rainforest struck by thunder and lightning. The squad leader fell asleep peacefully, and I whispered in his ear, sorry, disturbing me again......我把三块水果糖小心翼翼地放在他的右裤兜里，我记得很清楚，我们正是从那个兜里取出了他的旧水果糖。我把班长的衣服重新抚平，让他睡得更舒适些，然后缓缓退出。

  I carefully placed three pieces of fruit candy in his right trouser pocket, and I remember very well that we took out his old fruit candy from that pocket. I smoothed out the squad leader's clothes again to make him sleep more comfortably, and then slowly exited.我感觉背后有凉风袭来。

  I felt a cool breeze blowing behind me.回头一看，是竹干事。

  Looking back, it was a bamboo officer.你又来干什么？竹干事问。

  What are you doing again? The bamboo officer asked.我……来看看……我支吾着说。我知道像竹干事这样的老兵，将生死看得淡如烟云。把糖的事如实说出，他会笑我的。

  I...... Let's take a look...... I hesitated. I know veterans like Zhu Officer who take life and death lightly. Tell me the truth about sugar, and he will laugh at me.生和死的区别，其实没有我们想象的那样大。不过是蚕蜕了一层皮。竹干事缓缓地说。

  The difference between life and death is actually not as big as we think. It's just a silkworm shedding a layer of skin. Zhu Officer said slowly.我转移话题说，那你来干什么？

  I changed the subject and said, what are you doing here?竹干事说，我领着木工来装棺。

  The bamboo officer said, I led the carpenter to load the coffin.经他一说，我才看到，在不远处，一座朱红色的棺木，在几个人的肩头，宫殿一般雄伟地矗立着。

  After he said this, I saw that not far away, a vermilion coffin stood majestically like a palace on the shoulders of several people.工人们开始装殓班长，棺里铺了松软的棉被。班长从水泥的台子上搬到木制的小屋，一定会感觉暖和些的。

  The workers began to bury the squad leader, and the coffin was covered with a soft quilt. The squad leader moved from the cement platform to the wooden hut, and he would definitely feel warmer.竹干事对我说，不必遮遮掩掩，我都看到了。他以后没有机会吃糖的。

  The bamboo officer said to me, no need to hide it, I saw it all. He won't have a chance to eat sugar in the future.我说，才不对呢。我相信在一个春天的晚上，天上有着圆圆的月亮，班长定会和他相片上的未婚妻，在烈士陵园的台阶上相会，每人嘴里含着一块糖。
I said, it's not right. I believe that on a spring night, when there is a full moon in the sky, the squad leader will meet his fiancée in the photo on the steps of the martyrs' cemetery, each with a piece of candy in their mouths.

未知

  20.最高的花生糖作坊

  20. The tallest peanut candy workshop

  有一天，我们之中年龄最大的河莲说，你们谁吃过花生糖？

  One day, Ha Lian, the oldest among us, said, Which of you has ever eaten peanut candy?大家一齐嚷起来，我吃过！

  Everyone shouted together, I ate it!是啊，哪个女孩子小时候没吃过香喷喷、甜蜜蜜的花生糖呢？只要一想起那滋味，舌头下面就储存了一包口水要流出来。

  Yes, what girl hasn't eaten fragrant and sweet peanut candy when she was a child? As soon as I thought of that taste, a packet of saliva was stored under my tongue to flow out.河莲说，那我们自己做花生糖来吃，开一间世界上最高的花生糖作坊，好不好？

  He Lian said, then let's make our own peanut candy to eat and open the world's tallest peanut candy workshop, okay?在我们这些女孩子里，果平是以吃肉闻名的，我们都说她的祖先一定不是从猴子变来的，而是一只老虎变的，所以，见了肉就没命；而河莲是以巧出名的，她说要办什么事，一定能办到。

  Among us girls, Guo Ping is famous for eating meat, and we all say that her ancestors must not have changed from monkeys, but from a tiger, so they died when they saw meat; And He Lian is famous for her skill, she said that if she wants to do something, she will definitely be able to do it.我们立刻大叫，开花生糖作坊，好哇！好哇！

  We immediately shouted, open a peanut sugar workshop, so wow! Wow!我们都吃过花生糖，可是，我们都没有做过花生糖，连脑子最聪明的河莲也没有做过。不过这难不倒我们，大家回忆起小时候吃过的花生糖，不就是一些炒熟了的花生米裹在琥珀色的糖稀里，放凉了就成了吗，没什么了不起的。

  We have all eaten peanut candy, but none of us have ever made peanut candy, not even He Lian, who has the smartest brain. But this is not difficult for us, everyone recalls the peanut candy they ate when they were children, isn't it just some fried peanuts wrapped in amber sugar thin, and it becomes when it cools down, nothing remarkable.我们开始筹措原料。

  We started raising raw materials.因为我不吃羊肉，炊事班长对我比较优待。在大家吃羊肉的日子里，允许我自己挑别的食品。这一回，我放弃了最爱吃的大红枣，要了满满一大碗生花生米。

  Because I don't eat mutton, the cooking squad leader treats me more favorably. On the days when everyone eats mutton, I am allowed to choose other foods by myself. This time, I gave up my favorite red dates and asked for a large bowl full of raw peanuts.还有必不可少的糖，这也很好办。为了给大家补充营养，每人每月可分到一茶缸白糖。现在大伙儿争着贡献出来，河莲忙说，够了够了，花生只有一碗，小马不能配大鞍子，要不就比例失调了。

  There is also essential sugar, which is also very good. In order to supplement nutrition for everyone, each person can get a teapot of sugar every month. Now everyone is scrambling to contribute, He Lian hurriedly said, enough, enough, there is only one bowl of peanuts, and the pony cannot be matched with a big saddle, or the proportion is out of balance.原料备好以后，发现没有锅；没有锅，就没法熬糖和炒花生。我们的花生糖作坊还没开张，就面临倒闭的危险。

  After the raw materials are prepared, it is found that there is no pot; Without a pot, you can't boil sugar and fry peanuts. Our peanut candy workshop was in danger of going out of business before it even opened.就在我的刷牙缸里熬糖吧，虽说它小了一点，多熬几缸子也就够了。果平挺身而出，解决了一半的难题。

  Just boil sugar in my toothbrushing tank, although it is a little smaller, a few more jars are enough. Guo Ping stepped forward and solved half of the problem.但总不能用刷牙缸炒花生米呀，它的底面积太小了，最下面的花生煳透了，表层的还没有热乎呢。

  But you can't fry peanuts with a toothbrush jar, its bottom area is too small, the bottom peanuts are cooked through, and the surface is not hot yet.于是，有人提议吃罐头，然后……

  So, someone suggested eating canned food, and then ......大家听了都说这个主意好，七手八脚地打开了一筒一公斤装的菠萝罐头，你一勺我一口地迅速吃光，接着操起剪子，把罐头盒剪开，真是好大一张洋铁皮。我们把洋铁皮的周边卷起来，一个简易的铁锅就做好了。摆在炉台上，还蛮像样的。

  Everyone said that this idea was good, and they opened a canned pineapple in a kilogram of each one, and I quickly ate it all in one bite, and then picked up the scissors and cut the canning box, which was really a big foreign iron sheet. We roll up the periphery of the iron sheet, and a simple iron pot is ready. Placed on the stove, it is quite decent.我们把花生米倒进自制的铁锅里，炉火在下面熊熊地燃烧着，花生米因为受热噼啪作响，有轻微的香气飘散出来。

  We poured the peanuts into a homemade iron pot, and the fire was burning below, and the peanuts crackled due to the heat, and a slight aroma wafted out.我们正想为自己的发明鼓掌叫好，可怕的事情发生了：那个马口铁做的锅子，受不了高温的熏烤，中央突然软塌塌地陷落，熔化出一个红色的裂口。半熟的花生米像滑雪运动员一样，沿着烧红了的锅壁，飞快地掉进炉膛里去了……

  We were about to applaud our invention when something terrible happened: the tinplate pot, unable to withstand the high temperature, suddenly collapsed in the center, and melted into a red crack. The half-cooked peanuts quickly fell into the hearth along the red-hot pot walls like skiers......一股焦煳味儿弥漫在空中，我们垂头丧气，作坊失败了。

  A smell of burnt air filled the air, and we were dejected, and the workshop failed.

  不要灰心，我们再想想办法。河莲一点不气馁，明亮的大眼睛四处搜寻，一眼落在门后铲煤的铁锨头上，说，就用这个当锅吧。说着，端起铁锨，洗净了煤灰，架在炉台上，比个真锅还神气。

  Don't be discouraged, let's think of a way. He Lian was not discouraged at all, her big bright eyes searched around, and her glance landed on the shovel head of the coal shovel behind the door, saying, just use this as a pot. Saying that, he picked up the shovel, washed off the coal ash, and put it on the stove, which was more energetic than a real pot.铁锨很厚，再也不会熔化掉。

  The shovel is thick and will never melt again.我们把花生米倒进去，用筷子不停地拨拉。当筷子头变得焦黑的时候，花生米也熟了，散发出扑鼻的香味。真想先吃几粒，但为了我们作坊的声誉，大家都耐心地忍着馋虫的煎熬。

  We pour the peanuts into it and pull it with chopsticks without stopping. When the chopsticks become charred, the peanuts are also cooked, emitting a pungent fragrance. I really wanted to eat a few first, but for the reputation of our workshop, everyone patiently endured the torment of gluttonous insects.花生凉了以后，我们小心地把花生衣搓掉，把白白胖胖的花生放在一个碟子里。

  After the peanuts were cold, we carefully rubbed off the peanut coat and put the white and fat peanuts in a plate.下一个步骤就是熬糖了。这是比较简单的活儿，把糖放进茶缸，用筷子搅啊搅，不一会儿白糖就融化成淡黄色的糖稀，冒出透明的气泡。当糖稀的颜色变成褐红色并闪出油漆一样的亮光时，河莲果断地喊了一声，好了！她飞快地把糖稀浇到碟子里的花生米上，并用筷子不停地搅拌，使它们混合得更均匀。一种属于真正的花生糖的甜香气，刺激得我们一个劲儿地咽唾沫。几次想尝尝正在冷却过程中的花生糖，都叫河莲给拦住了。她说，一定要等到花生糖完全做好了，用小刀割成一小条一小条的，像街上卖的一样，才分给我们吃。

  The next step is to boil the sugar. This is a relatively simple job, put the sugar into the teapot, stir and stir it with chopsticks, and after a while, the sugar will melt into a pale yellow sugar thin, and transparent bubbles will appear. When the color of the sugar thin turned brownish and flashed like paint, Ha Lian shouted decisively, okay! She quickly poured the sugar thin onto the peanuts in the saucer and stirred them with chopsticks to make them mix more evenly. A sweet aroma of real peanut candy that stimulates us to swallow saliva. Several times I wanted to taste the peanut candy that was in the process of cooling, but I was stopped by He Lian. She said that we must wait until the peanut candy is completely ready, cut it into small strips with a knife, like those sold on the street, before distributing it to us.为了那神圣的一刻，我们眼巴巴地盯着那个碟子，祈祷它快快变凉。

  For that sacred moment, we stared at the saucer eagerly, praying that it would cool down soon.等啊等，碟子终于冷却了。当河莲郑重地拿起小刀，分割花生糖的时候，我们听到了极清脆的响声。

  Wait, wait, the dish finally cooled down. When He Lian solemnly picked up the knife and divided the peanut candy, we heard a very crisp sound.花生糖已经凝固得像石头一样坚硬，无论怎么使劲儿，都不能使它和碟子分离，更无法把它变成一小条一小条的糖块。

  The peanut candy had solidified as hard as a stone, and no matter how hard it tried, it could not be separated from the plate, let alone turned into small pieces of sugar.河莲难过地说，我犯了一个大错误，应该在碟子里抹上油，这样花生糖就可以磕下来了。现在，我们的作坊出了废品。

  He Lian said sadly, I made a big mistake, I should have greased the plate so that the peanut candy could be knocked down. Now, our workshop is scrapped.我们都劝她放宽心，不要紧的。这不是废品，只不过吃起来稍微麻烦一点罢了。

  We all advised her to relax, it didn't matter. This is not a waste product, it's just a little more troublesome to eat.我们这座世界上最高的花生糖作坊，出产的第一批产品，吃的时候需用这种姿势——双手捧着碟子，像花猫洗脸一样，用舌头舔碟子。

  The first products produced by our world's tallest peanut candy workshop need to be in this posture when eating - holding the plate with both hands, licking the plate with its tongue like a cat washing its face.不过，说到味道，那可真是好极了！

  However, when it comes to taste, it's really good!

未知

  21.碗里的小太阳

  21. The little sun in a bowl

  我不吃羊肉，总觉得那肉里有一股青草味儿。小的时候，跟父母到北京的东来顺馆子里吃过一顿涮羊肉，回来后全身起了风疹。医生说是过敏，让我终生忌食羊肉。

  I don't eat mutton, I always feel that there is a grassy smell in the meat. When I was a child, I went to Donglaishun Restaurant in Beijing with my parents to eat a meal of lamb-shabu, and when I came back, I had rubella all over my body. The doctor said it was an allergy, which made me avoid eating mutton for the rest of my life.到了西藏，羊肉就成了主要菜肴。做法很粗犷，用斧子将整头羊劈成碗口大的坨子，连骨头带肉丢进高压锅，再塞入一块酱油膏，撒点作料，拧上锅盖急火猛攻。一个小时后，一道名为“大块羊肉”的高原菜就算烧得了。大家就拎着饭碗来打菜。

  In Tibet, mutton became the main dish. The method is very rough, use an axe to split the whole sheep into a lump with a bowl mouth, throw the bones and meat into the pressure cooker, then stuff a piece of soy sauce paste, sprinkle some ingredients, screw on the lid of the pot and attack rapidly. An hour later, a plateau dish called "big mutton" was cooked. Everyone came to cook with their rice bowls.我对同屋的果平说，你把我的那份儿菜打走好了。

  I said to Guo Ping in the same room, you can beat my share of the dishes away.果平说，那你吃什么呀？

  Guo Ping said, then what do you eat?我说，吃咸菜呀，我是宁肯吃咸菜也不吃羊肉的。

  I said, eat pickles, I would rather eat pickles than mutton.果平说，你好傻啊，会写美丽的‘美’字吗？

  Guo Ping said, you are so stupid, can you write the beautiful word 'beauty'?我说，会写呀！说完，就用勺子把儿在手心上写了一个大大的“美”字给她看。

  I said, I can write! After saying that, he used a spoon to write a big word "beauty" on the palm of his hand to show her.果平说，原来你还挺聪明的呀！那你为什么不吃羊肉呢？什么叫‘美’？‘大’‘羊’两个字摞起来就是‘美’啊，西藏的羊多大啊！

  Guo Ping said, it turns out that you are quite smart! So why don't you eat lamb? What is 'beauty'? The words 'big' and 'sheep' are stacked together to mean 'beautiful', how big are the sheep in Tibet!我便如实相告，吃羊肉过敏。

  I will tell you the truth, I am allergic to mutton.于是，在吃羊肉的日子里，只有我一个人孤零零地吃咸菜。时间长了，被炊事班长发现，他说，老吃咸菜怎么行？长久下去会得病的。

  So, on the days of eating mutton, I was the only one eating pickles. After a long time, he was discovered by the cooking squad leader, and he said, how can you eat pickles all the time? You will get sick in the long run.我说，那好啊，你给我做猪肉。可那些猪肉都是从平原运来的，数量不多，都让我吃了，就太对不起大家了。几次小灶以后，我对炊事班长说，我还是吃咸菜吧，这样心安。

  I said, okay, you make me pork. But those porks were all transported from the plains, and there were not many of them, so I was so sorry for everyone. After a few small stoves, I said to the cooking squad leader, I should eat pickles, so that I can rest assured.炊事班长见我很坚决，就说，要不这样吧，你跟我到食堂的库房里挑一挑，看你喜欢吃什么，就拿点什么；反正每个人都有一份儿伙食费，你不吃羊肉就吃别的好了。

  The cooking squad leader saw that I was very resolute, so he said, why don't you go to the warehouse of the canteen with me to pick and see what you like to eat, just take something; Anyway, everyone has a share of food expenses, so if you don't eat mutton, you can eat something else.

  我第一次走进库房。哇，好丰富！一箱箱的奶粉，成麻袋的红糖白糖，还有花生米、葡萄干、脱水菜、压缩饼干……真够琳琅满目的。可惜都是干菜坚果类，根本引不起人的食欲。

  I walked into the warehouse for the first time. Wow, so rich! Boxes of milk powder, brown sugar and white sugar in sacks, as well as peanuts, raisins, dehydrated vegetables, and compressed biscuits...... It's really dazzling. Unfortunately, they are all dried vegetables and nuts, which cannot arouse people's appetite at all.就没有蔬菜吗？比如红红的萝卜、绿绿的黄瓜？我实在太渴望吃青菜了，明知没有多少希望，还是试探着问。

  Is there no vegetables? For example, red radishes, green cucumbers? I was so eager to eat vegetables that I knew there was not much hope, so I still asked tentatively.有啊。炊事班长很肯定地说，随手拈出一筒罐头。三下五除二，打开来，倒真是有红红的萝卜、绿绿的黄瓜，只是它们强烈地冒出一股酸气。原来这是酸菜罐头。

  Yes. The cooking squad leader said with certainty that he casually took out a tube of cans. Three times five divided by two, when opened, there are really red radishes and green cucumbers, but they strongly emit a sour smell. It turns out that this is canned sauerkraut.吃了几次酸菜罐头，我就腻了。我跟在炊事班长的屁股后面转，突然发现一只神秘的小麻袋，袋口的线绳扎得紧紧的，灰头灰脑地缩在墙角。

  After eating canned sauerkraut a few times, I got tired of it. I followed behind the buttocks of the cooking squad leader, and suddenly found a mysterious little sack, the rope at the mouth of the bag was tied tightly, and it was huddled in the corner in disgrace.那是什么？可不可以吃？我问。

  What is that? Can you eat it? I asked.吃不得。那是一种虫子干儿，有怪味道。炊事班长说。

  I can't eat. It was a kind of bug dried with a strange smell. The cooking squad leader said.我好奇地解开绳子，出现在眼前的是满满的一麻袋红橙鼓胀的——大海米！

  I untied the rope curiously, and what appeared in front of me was a sack full of red and orange bulges - sea rice!噢！我今天就吃这种虫子干儿了！我快活地大叫着，要知道我们自打到了西藏，还没尝过海味儿呢！我顺手抓了一把海米填进嘴里，嚼得咯咯响，鲜香满口。

  Oh! I'm going to eat this kind of bug today!" I shouted happily, you know, we haven't tasted the sea since we arrived in Tibet! I grabbed a handful of sea rice and stuffed it into my mouth, chewing it until it was full of fresh flavor.

  炊事班长吃惊地瞪着我，因为，他自小生活在西北的山区，从没见过海里的生物。

  The cooking squad leader glared at me in surprise, because he had lived in the mountains of the northwest since he was a child and had never seen a creature in the sea.但连续吃了几次海米之后，我又腻了。这一回，我长了经验，不让炊事班长当向导，自己在库房里转呀转，想再发掘出点不同凡响的食品。

  But after eating sea rice several times in a row, I got tired of it again. This time, I gained experience, and I didn't let the cooking squad leader be a guide, and I walked around the warehouse by myself, wanting to discover some extraordinary food.果然，我又找到一只奇怪的麻袋。看起来鼓鼓囊囊，拎一下却很轻。打开一看，原来是又大又圆的山西红枣。

  Sure enough, I found another strange sack. It looks bulging, but it is very light when you pick it up. When I opened it, it turned out to be a big and round Shanxi red date.我立刻用随身带的饭盆舀了半盆，连蹦带跳地跑出库房，对等在外面的炊事班长说，我今天就吃这个喽！

  I immediately scooped half a basin with the rice bowl I brought with me, ran out of the warehouse with a bounce, and said to the cooking squad leader waiting outside, I'll eat this today!炊事班长说，这个当零食吃可以，当正经菜可不行。

  The cooking squad leader said that this can be eaten as a snack, but not as a serious dish.我说，能行能行，又能当菜又能当饭。说着就跑远了。

  I said, I can do it, I can do it, I can be a dish and a meal. As he spoke, he ran away.以后，我和我的朋友们就热切地盼着吃羊肉的日子。我进库房用来盛红枣的器皿越来越大，最后，简直变成了一只小脸盆。炊事班长吃惊地说，你一个女孩子，一顿吃得了这么多的红枣吗？小心别闹肚子。

  From then on, my friends and I eagerly looked forward to the day of eating mutton. The vessel I used to hold dates in the warehouse became bigger and bigger, and in the end, it turned into a small basin. The cooking squad leader said in surprise, are you a girl who can eat so many red dates in one meal? Be careful not to make a stomach upset.我说，当然吃得了，你就放心吧。

  I said, of course you can eat it, don't worry.他不知道，每次都是我们全屋的女孩子一块儿吃红枣。在那些最严寒的日子里，我们团团地围坐在火炉旁，把红枣洗净，撒上白糖，放在小锅里，慢慢地煮。

  He didn't know that every time the girls in our house ate red dates together. On those coldest days, we sat around the stove, washed the dates, sprinkled with sugar, put them in a small pot, and cooked them slowly.在呼啸的风雪声里，红枣渐渐地膨胀起来，好像一轮轮暖洋洋的小太阳，把我们的脸都映得红艳艳的。

  In the sound of the whistling wind and snow, the red dates gradually expanded, like warm little suns, making our faces bright red.女孩子吃红枣，是很补身体的。
Girls eat red dates, which is very nourishing for the body.

未知

  22.雪线上的蛋花汤

  22. Egg drop soup on the snow line

  鸡蛋在昆仑山上是很稀罕的东西。

  Eggs are a rare thing in Kunlun Mountain.你想啊，海拔五千多米，多么品种优良的母鸡也活不了。从平原到高原几千公里的路程，汽车一路上“跳迪斯科舞”，鸡蛋就是铁皮的，也会被颠出缝。

  You think, at an altitude of more than 5,000 meters, no matter how good a hen is, it can't survive. Thousands of kilometers from the plains to the plateau, cars "dance disco dances" along the way, and eggs are made of iron sheets, and they will also be stitched out.于是，军需部门就给我们运鸡蛋的代用品。其一是蛋黄粉，色泽像金皇后玉米面一样灿烂。掺上水，用油一煎，就成了金闪闪的蛋黄饼。可惜好看不好吃，根本没有鸡蛋味儿，曾噎得人直翻白眼儿。

  So, the quartermaster department gave us substitutes for transporting eggs. One is egg yolk powder, which is as brilliant as the golden queen cornmeal. Mix it with water and fry it in oil to make a golden egg yolk cake. It's a pity that it's not good-looking or delicious, it doesn't smell like eggs at all, and it made people roll their eyes at once.用鸡蛋黄养鱼都养不活，人要一天吃这个，能得黄疸病！有人说。

  Raising fish with egg yolk can't be raised, and people have to eat this a day to get jaundice! Some people say.食堂若吃蛋黄粉，准得剩一大盆，像漫天的迎春花。

  If you eat egg yolk powder in the canteen, you will have to leave a large pot, like spring flowers in the sky.其二是一种有清有黄的冻蛋，是把整个鸡蛋打进铁桶，速冻而成。说起来倒是全须全尾的原装，吃到嘴里，却比鲜蛋差得远。好像鸡蛋的魅力是一种很温暖的东西，一冻就丢了。

  The second is a kind of frozen-egg with clear and yellow, which is made by beating the whole egg into an iron bucket and freezing it quickly. Speaking of which, it is the original with all the beard and the tail, but when you eat it in your mouth, it is far worse than fresh eggs. It seems that the charm of eggs is a very warm thing, and it is lost as soon as it is frozen.其三就是鸡蛋罐头了。圆圆滚滚的球体卧在玻璃罐里，随浑黄的液体浮动。除了形状上还保持着基本轮廓，很难使人想到它是母鸡的产品。

  The third is canned eggs. The round sphere lies in a glass jar, floating with the yellowish liquid. In addition to maintaining the basic outline in the shape, it is difficult to imagine that it is a product of a hen.于是，我们这些远离家乡的年轻军人，就像思念绿色一样，思念白色的温暖的有着粗糙外壳的真正的鸡蛋。

  So, we, the young soldiers far from home, miss the green and the white warm real eggs with rough shells.有一年过节，炊事班长很神秘地叫我，喂，你是女娃，有个事要问你。

  One year during the holidays, the cooking squad leader called me mysteriously, hey, you are a girl, I have something to ask you.炊事班长很能吃苦，做饭的手艺可不敢恭维。

  The cooking squad leader is very good at enduring hardships, and his cooking skills are not complimentary.什么事？你说好了。我心不在焉地应道。

  What's up? You said it. I replied absentmindedly.喏，你看。他伸出蜷得像个鸟窝似的手掌——我看到在他皲裂的手指圈起的半圆形凹体中，有一个粉红色的鸡蛋。

  Look. He stretched out his palm curled up like a bird's nest—I saw a pink egg in the semicircular concave body circled by his cracked fingers.是真的吗？我惊喜地问。

  Is it true? I asked in surprise.当然是真的！要是有只老母鸡，也许能孵出鸡娃来！炊事班长得意地说。

  Of course it's true! If there is an old hen, it may be able to hatch a chicken baby! The cooking squad leader said proudly.这肯定不行。就算它原来是一颗有生命的种子，跋涉冰峰雪岭时也早冻死了。我顾不上反驳炊事班长，只一个劲儿地问，它为什么没被颠破呢？

  This certainly won't work. Even if it turned out to be a living seed, it would have frozen to death when trekking through the ice and snow mountains. I didn't care about refuting the cooking squad leader, so I kept asking, why hasn't it been broken?炊事班长不乐意了，说，瞧你这个样，好像巴不得它破了！这是我老乡特地从家乡带来的，一路上抱着纸盒，连个盹都没舍得打。

  The cooking squad leader was not happy and said, look at you like this, it seems that it is broken! This was specially brought by my fellow villagers from my hometown, holding the paper box along the way, and I was not even willing to take a nap.我说，这真是一个经历了长途拉练的鸡蛋。

  I said, this is really an egg that has gone through a long journey.炊事班长说，别废话，知道叫你来干什么吗？

  The cooking squad leader said, don't talk nonsense, do you know what you are asked to do?我说，把这个鸡蛋送给我。

  I said, give me this egg.吓！想得美！炊事班长晃着他的方脑袋说，老乡一共送我三个鸡蛋，三个鸡蛋够谁吃的？今天过节，我想用这三个鸡蛋给大伙儿做一锅真正的鸡蛋汤。你是城里人，你喝过那种片片缕缕像米汤似的鸡蛋汤吧？咱就做那样的。

  Scare! Think beautifully! The cooking squad leader shook his square head and said, the fellow gave me a total of three eggs, who can eat three eggs? Today is the holiday, and I want to use these three eggs to make a real pot of egg soup for everyone. You are from the city, have you ever drunk that kind of egg soup like rice soup? Let's do that.喝过。我说。

  drunk. I say.那好，你就给咱做。炊事班长说着，把我推到锅前。

  Okay, you can do it for us. The cooking squad leader said, pushing me to the pot.在呼呼的热水面前，我可傻了眼。不错，我是喝过那漂浮如丝带的甩袖汤，但我根本就不知道它是怎么做出来的！可我又不好意思对向我寄予了无限期望的炊事班长说“我不会”。在炊事班长的方头颅里，既是城里人，又是女人，就该天生会做鸡蛋汤。

  In front of the whirring hot water, I was dumbfounded. Yes, I have drunk the floating sleeve soup like a ribbon, but I have no idea how it is made! But I was embarrassed to say "I won't" to the cooking squad leader who had infinite expectations for me. In the square head of the cooking squad leader, he is both a city man and a woman, so he should be born to make egg soup.嘿！有什么了不起的！鸡蛋汤鸡蛋汤，顾名思义，把鸡蛋倒进水里就成汤！我痛下决心。

  Hey! What's so great! Egg soup Egg soup, as the name suggests, pours eggs into water and it becomes soup! I made up my mind.打蛋！我命令道。

  Beat the eggs! I ordered.炊事班长乖乖地拿出个大铝盆（可以当行军锅的那种，比一般脸盆要大和深），把三个鸡蛋打进去，用手指把蛋壳内的每一滴黏液都刮净。

  The cooking squad leader obediently took out a large aluminum basin (the kind that can be used as a marching pot, larger and deeper than an ordinary basin), beat three eggs into it, and scraped every drop of mucus in the eggshell with his fingers.三个鸡蛋像三颗金蚕豆，在空旷的盆底滚来滚去；没有了外壳的鸡蛋，更小更少。

  three eggs are like three golden broad beans, rolling around at the bottom of an empty basin; Eggs without a shell, smaller and less.一大锅水开了，冒着汹涌的白汽。我端起盆，正想把搅匀的蛋液倒进去，突然觉得它们太单薄了。

  A large pot of water boiled, steaming white steam. I picked up the basin and was about to pour the mixed egg liquid into it, but suddenly I felt that they were too thin.加水。我说。

  Add water. I say.往哪里加水？炊事班长谦虚地问。

  Where to add water? The cooking squad leader asked humbly.当然是往……鸡蛋里加水了。我胸有成竹地说。

  Of course...... Water is added to the egg. I said confidently.加多少？炊事班长小心翼翼地请教。

  How much to add? The cooking squad leader carefully asked for advice.就加……一大勺吧！我指挥若定。

  Add ...... A big spoonful! My command seems to be determined.现在盆里的景象好看多了，黄澄澄的半盆，再没有捉襟见肘的窘迫。好了，现在就把鸡蛋液倒进锅里，并且一个劲儿地用筷子搅拌。一会儿，我们就会有香喷喷的真正的鸡蛋汤喝了。我有条不紊地吩咐着。

  Now the scene in the basin is much better, and Huang Chengcheng's half basin is no longer stretched. Okay, now pour the egg mixture into the pot and stir it with chopsticks. In a moment, we will have fragrant real egg soup to drink. I ordered in an orderly manner.人高马大的炊事班长乖乖地听着指挥，三个珍贵的鸡蛋和一大勺凉水倾倒进沸锅……一时间，锅里锅外都很安静。

  The tall cooking squad leader obediently listened to the command, and three precious eggs and a large spoonful of cold water were poured into the boiling pot...... For a while, it was quiet inside and outside the pot.一个人只能喝一碗，多了就不够了。今天你辛苦，就给你喝两碗吧。炊事班长思谋着。

  One person can only drink one bowl, and more is not enough. You have worked hard today, so I'll give you two bowls. The cooking squad leader was thinking.鸡蛋是你的，你本该多吃多占点。我说。

  The eggs are yours, you should have eaten more. I say.想象中的鸡蛋汤该有仙女水袖般飘逸的蛋花，该有糯米般甜蜜的蛋丝，该有……

  The imaginary egg soup should have egg drops as flowing as fairy water sleeves, sweet shredded eggs like glutinous rice, and should have ......满满一大锅水再次开了。

  A large pot of water opened again.锅里什么也没有，只是云雾般地混浊。那三个鸡蛋神秘地失踪了，融化在一大锅雪水中。

  There was nothing in the pot, just cloudy clouds. The three eggs mysteriously disappeared and melted in a large pot of snow water.

  我和炊事班长面面相觑，目光在询问，鸡蛋呢？万里迢迢从家乡带来的鸡蛋哪儿去了？！

  The cooking squad leader and I looked at each other, our eyes asking, where are the eggs? Where did the eggs brought from my hometown go? !喝汤的时候，我对大家说，今天这汤是鸡蛋汤，真正的鸡蛋汤！

  When I was drinking the soup, I said to everyone, today this soup is egg soup, real egg soup!同伴们莞尔一笑，说，是吗？做梦吧！

  The companions smiled and said, yes? Dream!是真的！我亲眼看见三个鸡蛋的，它们就在这汤里，我不骗你们！我急得都要哭了。

  It's true! I saw three eggs with my own eyes, they are in this soup, I won't lie to you! I was so anxious that I was about to cry.大家还是半信半疑，因为，汤里实在是看不到鸡蛋的影子。

  Everyone is still skeptical, because there is really no shadow of eggs in the soup.不信，你们问炊事班长。我使出最后的撒手锏。

  If you don't believe me, ask the cooking squad leader. I made the last move.大家把脸转向炊事班长。炊事班长扶着他的大方脑袋，什么话也没说。

  Everyone turned their faces to the cooking squad leader. The cooking squad leader held his generous head and said nothing.于是，大家一哄而散，没有人相信我关于鸡蛋汤的神话。

  So, everyone dispersed, and no one believed my myth about egg soup.炊事班长，你为什么不说？为什么不说？我气愤地质问他。

  Cooking squad leader, why don't you say it? Why not say? I asked him angrily.大家没看见鸡蛋，你叫我说什么？炊事班长心平气和地说。

  Everyone didn't see the egg, what did you tell me to say? The cooking squad leader said calmly.那一天，我喝了好多鸡蛋汤，一边喝一边想，鸡蛋藏到哪儿去了呢？

  That day, I drank a lot of egg soup, and as I drank it, I thought, where did the eggs go?这个问题我一直想了好多年。我想，假如我不在鸡蛋里掺水，事情也许会好得多。当然，如果锅不是那么大，如果我们有许多鸡蛋，我们就一定会喝上美味的鸡蛋汤了。
I have been thinking about this question for many years. I thought that if I hadn't mixed water into the egg, things might have been much better. Of course, if the pot is not that big, if we have many eggs, we will surely have a delicious egg soup.
