  23.乘降落伞的西瓜

  23. Watermelon by parachute

  从平原到西藏高原，要坐六天的汽车。蔬菜水果都是很娇气的，哪里顶得住这样的颠簸？更不消说一路上雪花飘飘，气温在零摄氏度以下，再好的叶绿素也成冰激凌了。

  From the plains to the Tibetan plateau, it takes six days by car. Vegetables and fruits are very squeamish, how can they withstand such bumps? Not to mention the snowflakes along the way, the temperature is below zero degrees Celsius, and no matter how good the chlorophyll is, it becomes ice cream.但是，平原上的人还是挺关心高原上的人的，每年八九月份山下最热的时候，总要装上几卡车蔬菜，每车配备两个司机，昼夜兼程，把六天的旅程压缩成三天，赶上山来，想让吃了一年干菜和罐头的高原人享个口福。

  However, the people on the plains are still very concerned about the people on the plateau, and when it is the hottest time at the foot of the mountain in August and September every year, several trucks of vegetables must be loaded, each car is equipped with two drivers, day and night, compressing the six-day journey into three days, catching up with the mountain, hoping to let the plateau people who have eaten dried vegetables and canned food for a year enjoy a blessing.但再新鲜的蔬菜，经过几千公里的折磨，也面目全非了。茄子皱得像核桃，蒜苗黄得像京剧里奸臣的胡须，只有青椒还绿着，但绿得十分可疑，用手指轻轻一弹，皮就噗的一声破了，流出一包绿汪汪的清水，原来它早已冻烂了。

  But no matter how fresh the vegetables are, after thousands of kilometers of torture, they are unrecognizable. The eggplant is wrinkled like a walnut, the garlic sprouts are as yellow as the beard of a traitor in Peking Opera, only the green pepper is still green, but it is very suspicious, and with a flick of his finger, the skin will burst with a pop, and a bag of green water will flow out, and it turns out that it has long been frozen.有一次，运菜的车遇上了暴风雪。昆仑山是喜怒无常的，就是在最温暖的季节也会骤然翻脸，降下鸡蛋大的冰雹。菜车像破冰船似的抵达高原，通知大家去卸车。

  Once, the truck transporting vegetables encountered a snowstorm. Kunlun Mountain is moody, and even in the warmest season, it will suddenly turn its face and rain hail the size of eggs. The vegetable cart arrived at the plateau like an icebreaker and informed everyone to unload the truck.到了车跟前，吓了我们一大跳：这哪里是车，简直就是一座移动的小雪山。

  When we arrived at the car, we were shocked: where is this car, it is simply a moving snowy mountain.扒开篷布上厚厚的积雪，露出一个个装菜的纸箱。押车的人抱起一个箱子，砰地丢下车，咚的一声巨响，好像摔下来一箱炮弹。

  Peeling open the thick layer of snow on the tarpaulin, revealing cardboard boxes containing vegetables. The escort picked up a box and threw it off the car with a bang, as if a box of shells had fallen.你轻一点好不好？我们一齐冲他嚷。要知道，在高原上，蔬菜像黄金一样贵重，哪里容得他这般粗暴蹂躏！

  Can you be lighter? We shouted at him together. You know, on the plateau, vegetables are as precious as gold, where can he be so rudely ravaged!砸得再重些也不碍事。押车员大大咧咧地说。

  No matter how hard it is, it won't get in the way. The escort said carelessly.我们愤愤不平地打开箱子一看，才发现他说的是实情。这一箱里面装的是黄瓜，每一根都翠绿挺拔，像警棍一般笔直，用手一碰，发出清脆的玻璃器皿之声，好像翡翠雕成的工艺品。

  We opened the box indignantly and found that he was telling the truth. This box contains cucumbers, each of which is green and straight, straight like a baton, and when touched with your hand, it makes a crisp glassware sound, like a handicraft carved from jadeite.又打开一箱，是西红柿。每一颗果实都红润闪光，好像红玛瑙。手指稍不留意碰破了西红柿的皮，流出的不是红汁，而是橙色的冰晶。

  Another box was opened, and it was tomatoes. Each fruit is ruddy and shiny, like red agate. My fingers didn't pay attention to breaking the skin of the tomatoes, and instead of red juice, orange ice crystals came out.

  再打开一箱，是豆角。平日熟识的豆角显出一副陌生的模样，居然塑料似的半透明。透过朦胧的豆荚，依稀看到乳白色薄而软的豆粒，好像一只只惊讶的眼睛。

  Open another box and it is beans. The beans I am familiar with on weekdays show an unfamiliar appearance, and they are translucent like plastic. Through the hazy pods, I could vaguely see the milky white thin and soft beans, like surprised eyes.严寒使所有的蔬菜都改变了风味，吃到嘴里，都是雪花的味道。

  The severe cold has changed the flavor of all vegetables, and when you eat them, they taste like snowflakes.这种运输的艰难情况，几年后得到了一点改善。有一年快过春节的时候，接到通知，飞机将给我们空投报纸和蔬菜；还有一年降落伞运载的是西瓜。

  This difficult situation of transportation improved a little after a few years. One year, when the Spring Festival was approaching, we received a notice that the plane would airdrop newspapers and vegetables to us; Another year, the parachute carried watermelons.空投的日子到了，我们都眼巴巴地望着天空。冬天吃西瓜，就是在平原，也是很奢侈的事情。我们已经快忘了西瓜的滋味了，这是多么快活开心的节日！西瓜一落地就得马上收藏起来，千万不能在雪地里裸露时间太长了。要知道，当时的气温是零下几十摄氏度，要是把西瓜冻僵就糟了。

  The day of the airdrop has arrived, and we are all looking at the sky eagerly. Eating watermelon in winter, even in the plains, is also a luxury. We have almost forgotten the taste of watermelon, what a happy and happy festival it is! Watermelons must be collected as soon as they land, and they must not be exposed in the snow for too long. You know, the temperature at that time was minus tens of degrees Celsius, and it would be bad if the watermelon was frozen.

  飞机来了，因为周围都是狰狞的山峰，飞机不敢低飞就开始空投了。一朵朵洁白的降落伞像鸽群一般在高天浮动。

  The plane came, because it was surrounded by hideous mountain peaks, and the plane did not dare to fly low and began to drop. White parachutes float high in the sky like a flock of pigeons.天气很晴朗，但仍有看不见的气流在天穹穿行。突然有一只降落伞脱离了队伍，向远处的山谷翩翩飞去。

  The weather is clear, but there are still invisible air currents running through the sky. Suddenly a parachute broke away from the ranks and flew towards the valley in the distance.其他的降落伞都乖乖地落了地，久候的人们扑过去，迫不及待地打开伞下坠着的麻袋。打开一袋是报纸，打开另一袋是蔬菜，再打开一袋又是报纸……就是不见西瓜。

  The other parachutes fell to the ground obediently, and the people who had been waiting for a long time rushed over and couldn't wait to open the sacks that fell under the umbrellas. Open one bag is newspaper, open another bag is vegetables, and open another bag is newspaper...... I just don't see watermelons.赶快同飞机上联系，问是不是忘投西瓜了？

  Hurry up and contact the plane and ask if you forgot to throw a watermelon?飞机上回答，乘降落伞的西瓜，千真万确地空投下来了。

  The plane replied that the watermelon by parachute was really airdropped.完了！人们仰天长叹：那个飘往雪原深处的降落伞，装载的就是高原人望眼欲穿的西瓜啊！
Finished! People looked up to the sky and sighed: The parachute that floated into the depths of the snowfield was loaded with watermelons that plateau people wanted to wear!

  24.第一次打针

  24. First injection

  打针是医务人员的基本功，每个医生护士都有给别人打第一针的经历。那滋味虽说比不上打第一枪惊心动魄，但也令人终生难忘。

  Injections are the basic skills of medical staff, and every doctor and nurse has the experience of giving the first injection to others. Although the taste is not as thrilling as the first shot, it is also unforgettable.在正式打针以前，我们先经历了短暂的画面学习。比如注射部位、神经的走向、针头与皮肤的角度等，都像背口诀似的谨记在心。

  Before the official injection, we first experienced a short period of picture learning. For example, the injection site, the direction of the nerve, the angle of the needle and the skin, etc., are all kept in mind like memorizing a mantra.终于有一天，我们要真刀真枪地在病人身上实习了。

  Finally, one day, we are going to practice on patients.我的老师是一位男护士，姓胡（我们是第一批分到藏北的女护士，在我们之前的护士，自然都是男的了）。胡护士让我复述了一遍肌肉注射的操作程序以后，就说，行，你出师了。推上治疗车，到病房打针去吧。

  My teacher is a male nurse surnamed Hu (we were the first female nurses assigned to northern Tibet, and the nurses before us were naturally all male). After Nurse Hu asked me to repeat the operation procedure of intramuscular injection, she said, okay, you are a teacher. Push the treatment car and go to the ward for injections.我听了很高兴，赶紧把打针的家伙准备好。推着车要走的时候，见胡护士揣着两只手，一副无动于衷的模样。

  I was very happy to hear this, and quickly prepared the guy who gave the injection. When I was about to leave with the cart, I saw Nurse Hu holding two hands, looking indifferent.我奇怪地说，咦，你怎么不同我一道走？

  I said strangely, hey, why don't you go with me?他说，这次你一个人去。打针又不是拔河，要那么多人干什么？

  He said, this time you go alone. Injections are not tug-of-war, why do so many people do it?我吓了一跳，乞求他说，你跟我一起去好吗？不用你动手，站在一边给我壮个胆就成。

  I was startled and begged him, can you go with me? You don't need to do it, just stand aside and give me courage.胡护士毫不通融，你错了，有人在旁袖手旁观，你才容易心慌。真到你独自面对病人，胆量自然就来了。

  Nurse Hu is not accommodating, you are wrong, someone is watching from the sidelines, you are easy to panic. When you face the patient alone, courage will naturally come.我还是不死心，就说，你要是不去，我打针有什么毛病，自己也发现不了，不是对病人不负责任吗？

  I still don't give up, just say, if you don't go, I can't find out what is wrong with my injection, isn't it irresponsible to the patient?胡护士想了想说，这样吧，你打完第一针就找个借口走回来，我去检查一下，问问病人的感觉，就能知道你的技术如何了。

  Nurse Hu thought for a while and said, let's do this, you will find an excuse to walk back after the first injection, I will go check it out, ask the patient how it feels, and you can know how your technique is.谁让胡护士是我师傅呢，只有照他的主意办。我一个人推着小治疗车，向幽深的病房走廊走去。那一瞬间，我好孤独，有一种独闯虎穴的忐忑。

  Who let Nurse Hu be my master, I can only do it according to his idea. I pushed the small treatment cart alone towards the deep corridor of the ward. At that moment, I was so lonely, and I felt a sense of apprehension about breaking into the tiger's den alone.进了病房，病人像往常一样微笑着迎接我，我的心略微安定了一点。我翻开了治疗簿，第一个接受我“治疗”的是一个名叫“黄金”的人，很高大威武的样子。

  When I entered the ward, the patient greeted me with a smile as usual, and my heart calmed down a little. I opened the treatment book, and the first person to receive my "treatment" was a man named "Gold", who looked very tall and powerful.我鼓足勇气，轻声地说了一句，黄金，打针。

  I mustered up the courage and said softly, gold, injection.我以为他一定会不放心地问我，怎么就你一个人来了？老护士呢？但实际上他什么也没说，乖乖地趴在床上，很自觉地做出了挨针扎的姿势。

  I thought he would ask me uneasily, why are you here alone? Where's the old nurse? But in fact, he didn't say anything, just lay on the bed obediently, and consciously made a stitch posture.

  我松了一口气，口中念念有词，都是注射的诀窍，左手绷紧了他的皮肤，右手笔直地竖起针管，一咬牙一闭眼，正要不管不顾地往下戳，心里突然打了一个哆嗦。我想平日里不小心手上扎了一根刺，都会疼得直吸冷气；金属针头可比竹刺粗多了，那还不得疼死？真不忍心下此毒手啊！要是我一针攮下去，病人痛得熬不住，一个跟头跳起来，会不会把我的针尖折断在肉里？那麻烦就大了！这样一想，手变得酥软，老捏着针管比画，针头刺了几下都没捅进肉里。

  I breathed a sigh of relief, there were words in my mouth, all the tips of injection, my left hand tightened his skin, my right hand straightened the needle, gritted my teeth and closed my eyes, and was about to poke down regardless, but my heart suddenly shivered. I think if I accidentally get a thorn in my hand on weekdays, it will hurt so much that I will inhale the cold air; The metal needle is much thicker than the bamboo thorn, so it can't hurt to death? I really can't bear to do this! If I put a needle on it, the patient can't stand the pain, and if he jumps up, will he break the tip of my needle in the flesh? That's a big problem! Thinking about it like this, his hands became soft, and he kept pinching the needle and drawing, and the needle was stabbed a few times without stabbing into the flesh.黄金动了动身子说，护士，你咋还不扎？我都冻得起鸡皮疙瘩了。

  Huang Jin moved his body and said, Nurse, why don't you prick yet? I can even afford to get goosebumps.再不能拖下去了，要不病人旧病没好，又添一个重感冒，索性豁出去了，长痛不如短痛。我说了一句，黄金，你可千万别动！说时迟那时快，手一抡，就把注射器像菜刀一样砍了下去……

  I can't drag it on any longer, or the patient will not get better from the old illness, and add another severe cold, so I will simply open up, and the long pain is not as good as the short pain. I said, gold, don't move! Saying that it was too late, he picked up his hand and cut the syringe like a kitchen knife......在此之前，我在萝卜和棉花团上练过打针，真的一试，才发现差别大了。人的皮肤比萝卜软得多，比棉花要瓷实得多，有一种很怪异的感觉。也许是我的劲儿用得太大了，几乎没有遇到任何抵挡，针头就顺畅地插进了黄金的身体。

  Before that, I had practiced injections on radish and cotton balls, and when I really tried it, I found that there was a big difference. Human skin is much softer than radishes and much more porcelain than cotton, which has a very strange feeling. Maybe I used too much energy, and without encountering any resistance, the needle was smoothly inserted into the golden body.俗话说，万事开头难。我进针的这个头儿开得不错，后面就容易得多了。我很均匀地推动着药液，拔针的动作也快捷麻利。黄金惊奇地说，我还没什么感觉，你的针就打完了。真是青出于蓝而胜于蓝啊！

  As the saying goes, everything is difficult at the beginning. The head of my needle opened well, and the back was much easier. I pushed the medicine evenly, and the needle pulling out was quick and quick. Gold said in surprise, I don't feel anything yet, your injection is finished. It's really better than blue!我很得意地回到护士值班室，对焦急地等在那里的胡护士说，你去验收好了。

  I proudly returned to the nurse's duty room and said to Nurse Hu, who was waiting there anxiously, that you go to the acceptance.胡护士从病房回来的时候，不像我想象的那么满面春风。他皱着眉对我说，病人对你打针的技术反映还是不错的，说你打针的时候一点也不疼……”

  When Nurse Hu came back from the ward, her face was not as full of spring breeze as I imagined. He frowned and said to me, the patient's response to your injection technique is still good, saying that it doesn't hurt at all when you inject......我不好意思地微笑着，很想说几句表示谦虚的话。可是，还没等我想出词句，就听胡护士话锋一转说，但是，我发现了一个很严重的问题……”

  I smiled embarrassedly, wanting to say a few words of humility. However, before I could come up with a word, I heard Nurse Hu change his words, but I found a very serious problem......"我赶紧检讨，我准备的时间太长了，把病人给冻坏了……”

  I hurriedly reviewed, I prepared for too long and froze the patient......"胡护士说，这还是小事，你的过失比这个可大多了。我在黄金的屁股上看了一下，根本就没有你消毒皮肤的痕迹……”

  Nurse Hu said, this is still a small matter, and your fault is much greater than this. I looked at the golden ass, and there was no trace of your disinfecting skin at all......"我一下子如同五雷轰顶。天哪，我忘了这件最重要的准备工作，没用碘酒、酒精消毒，就把针头捅到病人的身体里了。

  I was like five thunderbolts all of a sudden. Oh my God, I forgot about this most important preparation, and instead of sterilizing with iodine or alcohol, I stabbed the needle into the patient's body.

  我吓得几乎哭出来，说，病人不会得败血症吧？

  I was so scared that I almost cried and said, "The patient won't get sepsis, right?"胡护士说，我得赶快向医生报告，让他给病人吃点消炎药，但愿一切平安无事。

  Nurse Hu said, I have to report to the doctor quickly and ask him to give the patient some anti-inflammatory medicine, I hope everything is safe.从那天以后，好多日子我都抬不起头来，尤其是害怕见到黄金。幸好他的身体很健康，没留下什么后遗症。

  Since that day, I couldn't lift my head for many days, especially for fear of seeing gold. Fortunately, he was in good health and did not leave any sequelae.第一次打针的教训真是刻骨铭心，我以后再也不敢这样粗心大意了。
The lesson of the first injection was really unforgettable, and I will never dare to be so careless again.

  25.女孩的纸

  25. Paper for girls

  女孩们用的纸比别人多。干净的柔软的洁白的纸，是伴随她们整个青春的朋友。

  Girls use more paper than others. Clean, soft and white paper is a friend who accompanies them throughout their youth.我们到了西藏，才发现这里的“毛伴”4，根本就没有卫生纸卖，更不要说卫生巾之类的东西了。大家开始并不着慌，因为刚从家里来不久，提包里都还有存货呢。

  When we arrived in Tibet, we found that the "Mao Ban" 4 here did not sell toilet paper at all, let alone sanitary napkins and the like. Everyone didn't panic at first, because they had just come from home, and there was still stock in their bags.高原的日子在寒冷中一天天过去。终于有一天，女孩们发现已无纸可用。

  The days of the plateau passed day by day in the cold. Finally one day, the girls find out that there is no paper left.这可怎么办？尤其是果平，已是等米下锅的局面。

  What can I do? Especially Guoping, it is already a situation where the rice is in the pot.这是一个绝对要回避男性的问题，我们缩在屋里苦思冥想。

  This is a question that we absolutely have to avoid men, and we huddle in the house and think hard.有人说，干脆给山下的商店发个电报，叫他们速运一大卡车卫生纸来。

  Some people said that they simply sent a telegram to the shop at the foot of the mountain and asked them to quickly transport a large truckload of toilet paper.河莲说，这是不可能的。山上只有我们这几个女孩，别人又不需用这东西。要是拉上一卡车，什么时候才能卖得完？毛伴才不会做这种赔本的生意呢。

  Ha Lian said that this is impossible. There are only a few girls like us on the mountain, and others don't need this thing. If you pull up a truck, when will it sell out? Mao Ban will not do this kind of loss-making business.大家愁眉苦脸地你看着我、我看着你。除了从毛伴那里买，想不出还有什么其他的途径搞到纸。

  Everyone frowned, you looked at me, I looked at you. I can't think of any other way to get paper except to buy it from my furry companion.我有办法了。果平突然胸有成竹。

  I have a way. Guo Ping suddenly felt confident.大家忙问她有何高招，她笑而不答，一副高深莫测的模样。大家见她不肯说，也就作罢。反正她的形势最紧急，她都不急了，别人乐得逍遥。

  Everyone hurriedly asked her what her tricks were, but she smiled and didn't answer, looking inscrutable. Everyone saw that she refused to speak, so they gave up. Anyway, her situation is the most urgent, she is not in a hurry, and others are happy to be free.过了几天，我的纸也用完了。我悄悄找到果平，说，把你的纸分我一点用。

  After a few days, I ran out of paper. I quietly found Guo Ping and said, give me a little bit of your paper.果平说，我哪里有纸？谁说我有纸了？

  Guo Ping said, where do I have paper? Who says I have paper?我说，你好坏呀！没纸的时候，要我们大家帮你想办法。你有纸了，就独自享用。真自私。

  I said, you are so bad! When you don't have paper, we all want to help you figure a way. You have paper, and enjoy it alone. Truly selfish.果平笑起来，说，我真的没有纸。不过你说我自私倒是对的。我要把我的办法告诉你，你也会自私起来。

  Guo Ping laughed and said, I really don't have paper. But you're right to say I'm selfish. I'm going to tell you my way, and you'll be selfish too.我说，不管是什么法子，我得先得到纸。我这里急等着用，你速速从实招来。

  I said, no matter what the method, I have to get the paper first. I'm waiting to use it here, you can quickly recruit it."果平附在我的耳朵上说，我用的不是纸，是包扎外科伤口用的止血绷带。

  Guo Ping attached to my ear and said, I am not using paper, but a hemostatic bandage to dress surgical wounds.我一听，这真是一个好办法。后来大家就你传我、我传你，都用止血绷带代替卫生纸。

  When I heard it, it was really a good idea. Later, everyone passed on me and I passed on you, and they all used hemostatic bandages instead of toilet paper.有一天，河莲对我们说，领导找她谈话了，说最近没有外伤病人，可止血物品消耗得太快。看来我们得想另外的法子。

  One day, He Lian told us that the leader had talked to her and said that there were no trauma patients recently, but the hemostatic items were consumed too quickly. It seems that we have to think of another way.我说，只有寄望于毛伴。毕竟它是我们和山下繁华地区之间唯一的通道。

  I said, I can only hope for my furry companion. After all, it is the only passage between us and the bustling area below the mountain.我和河莲就到毛伴，同卖货的藏族小伙子说，我们需要纸。

  He Lian and I went to Mao Ban and told the Tibetan boy who sold the goods that we needed paper.热情的小伙子为我们找出一箱信纸。

  The enthusiastic lad found a box of letters for us.不！不！不是这个纸！我和河莲一个劲儿摇头。

  No! No! Not this paper! He Lian and I shook our heads vigorously.小伙子又搬出了成捆的蜡光纸，五颜六色，煞是好看。

  The young man moved out the bundles of wax paper, which were colorful and very beautiful.不！更不对了！我们俩摆手跺脚加比画，总算让他明白了我们的意思：需要一种洁白柔软的大张纸。到底有没有？

  No! It's even more wrong! The two of us waved our hands and stomped our feet to paint, and finally let him understand what we meant: we needed a large piece of white and soft paper. Is there any?小伙子笑眯眯茅塞顿开的样子，连连说，那样的纸有！多得是！说着就到后面库房去找。

  The young man smiled and said again and again, there is such a piece of paper! How many! As he spoke, he went to the back warehouse to look for it.我和河莲相视而笑：真是踏破铁鞋无觅处，得来全不费工夫。

  He Lian and I looked at each other and laughed: It's really nowhere to find a place to step on iron shoes, and it takes no effort to get it.过了一会儿，小伙子满面尘灰地抱着一大卷纸，气喘吁吁而来。高原缺氧，任何动作都像剧烈运动一样费力。

  After a while, the young man came panting with a large roll of paper covered in dust. The plateau is hypoxic, and any movement is as strenuous as strenuous exercise.我和河莲赶紧迎过去，刚想谢他，细一看，不禁傻了眼。那不是什么细软的卫生纸，而是画国画的宣纸。

  He Lian and I hurriedly greeted him, just wanted to thank him, but when we looked closely, we couldn't help but be dumbfounded. It was not soft toilet paper, but rice paper for painting Chinese paintings.这个，是不是很好？像你们说的那样——白——软——大？小伙子的神情透着为别人做了好事之后的得意。

  Isn't this good? Like you said - white - soft - big? The young man's expression was full of pride after doing good deeds for others.那当然……是了……只是，这个……太可惜了……”我和河莲结结巴巴，不知如何答对他的好心。

  Of course...... Yes...... It's just that this ...... It's a pity...... "I stuttered with Ha Lian, not knowing how to answer his kindness.

  这个不可惜。已经运到这里好多年了，从来没有人要。你们买了吧，价钱很便宜……”藏族小伙子恳切地说。

  This is not a pity. It has been shipped here for many years and no one has ever asked for it. You buy it, the price is very cheap......" the Tibetan young man said earnestly.河莲和我商量，没有现成的卫生纸，止血绷带又不能再用了，我们就先买了这宣纸，回去救个急吧。

  He Lian discussed with me that there was no ready-made toilet paper, and the hemostatic bandage could no longer be used, so we bought this rice paper first and went back to save the emergency.我们把宣纸带回去，滴上水做了个试验。洁白的宣纸又柔韧又吸水。我们刚想欢呼，突然发现一个严重的问题：宣纸经过长途跋涉，纸缝里夹满尘沙。

  We took the rice paper back and dripped it with water to do an experiment. White rice paper is flexible and absorbent. We were just about to cheer when we suddenly discovered a serious problem: rice paper had traveled a long distance, and the gaps in the paper were full of dust and sand.这可怎么办？谁都知道，女孩子用的纸要很清洁的。

  What can I do? Everyone knows that the paper used by girls should be very clean.河莲说，我们把土抖干净，然后用高压锅消毒。这样有什么病菌也不怕了。

  Ha Lian said, we shake the soil clean and then disinfect it with a pressure cooker. In this way, there are no germs to be afraid of.大家就高高兴兴地把纸送去蒸，从此再也不用为纸着急了。

  Everyone happily sent the paper to be steamed, and they no longer had to worry about the paper.但我有时候想起来，真是为那些宣纸可惜啊。
But sometimes I think about it, it's really a pity for those rice papers.

  26.藏族的花围裙

  26. Tibetan flower apron

  我小的时候在幼儿园表演藏族舞蹈，每个小姑娘都要扎一条花围裙，那是藏族女装最显著的标志，我们都喜欢得不得了。可那么多的小朋友，到哪里去找许多真正的藏族小围裙呢？幼儿园的阿姨很会想办法，买来白毛巾，贴上彩色蜡光纸的窄条，一条五光十色的藏族小围裙就做好了。

  When I was a child, I performed Tibetan dance in kindergarten, and every little girl had to wear a flower apron, which was the most obvious symbol of Tibetan women's clothing, and we all liked it very much. But so many children, where can they find many real Tibetan aprons? The kindergarten aunt is very good at finding a way, buying a white towel, pasting a narrow strip of colored wax paper, and a colorful Tibetan apron is ready.我把这条毛巾和纸做的围裙扎在腰间，对着落地的穿衣镜一照，哈！美丽极了。雪山上的仙女就是这个样子啊！

  I tied this apron made of towel and paper around my waist and looked at the dressing mirror on the floor, ha! It's beautiful. The fairy on the snowy mountain is like this!来到西藏，看到藏族女人果真围着同样的围裙。也许是扎在腰间的时间太久了，高原的紫外线把颜色晒褪了，它们没有我想象中的漂亮。

  When I came to Tibet, I saw Tibetan women wearing the same apron. Maybe it's because I've been wearing it around my waist for too long, and the ultraviolet rays of the plateau have faded the color, and they are not as beautiful as I imagined.离我们住的地方不远有一条小街，藏语称它为“毛伴”。一天，我在毛伴的小店里闲逛，突然在柜台里发现一条极鲜艳的藏族围裙，缝缀着七彩的绸条，好像把天上的彩虹剪来一段贴在上面了。

  Not far from where we live, there is a small street called "Mao Ban" in Tibetan. One day, I was wandering around Mao Ban's small shop, and suddenly found a very bright Tibetan apron in the counter, sewn with colorful silk strips, as if a piece of rainbow in the sky was cut and pasted on it.这条围裙多少钱？我迫不及待地问售货的藏族小姑娘。

  How much does this apron cost? I couldn't wait to ask the little Tibetan girl who was selling.她微笑着用不很熟练的汉语报出一个价钱，并不很贵，我一算，自己身上带的钱足够了，就一边忙着掏钱，一边连声说，我就要这条围裙了，请赶快给我包起来。

  She smiled and quoted a price in Chinese that she was not very proficient in, which was not very expensive, and when I calculated, I had enough money on me, so I was busy paying for it, and said repeatedly, I want this apron, please wrap it up for me quickly.藏族小姑娘数完了钱，却一动也不动，充满歉意地对我说，单有钱是买不了围裙的。

  The little Tibetan girl finished counting the money, but she didn't move, and said to me apologetically that money alone could not buy an apron.我吃了一惊说，买个围裙还需要什么证明吗？

  I was taken aback and said, do you need any proof to buy an apron?她说，还需要两尺布票。

  She said that two feet of cloth tickets are also needed.那个时候，每年都发一种布票，凭票才可以买布制品，我们的衣服因为都是统一发的，就没有布票。我一时抓了瞎。

  At that time, a kind of cloth ticket was issued every year, and cloth products could be bought with the ticket, and our clothes were issued uniformly, so there was no cloth ticket. I was blinded for a while.我不死心地说，这个围裙都是绸缎做的，为什么要布票呢？是不是有些没道理？

  I don't give up and say, this apron is made of satin, why do you need to cloth the ticket? Isn't it a bit unreasonable?小姑娘红着脸把围裙拿过来，翻过绸缎的背面让我看，那是一层淡紫色的布。她小声说，没有办法，这是规定。

  The little girl blushed and brought the apron over, turned over the back of the satin to show me, it was a layer of lilac cloth. She whispered, there is no way, this is the rule.我再不好说什么了，垂头丧气回到宿舍，把缘由一讲，大家七嘴八舌地帮着我想办法。

  I couldn't say anything anymore, so I returned to the dormitory dejectedly, told the reason, and everyone helped me figure out a way.果平说，让你妈妈给你寄几尺布票来吧。

  Guo Ping said, let your mother send you a few feet of cloth tickets.我撇着嘴说，我还以为你有什么好主意呢！就这个办法啊，我早想过了，不行的。我们家在北京，寄来的是北京布票，在西藏是不能用的。必须要有西藏布票才行。

  I curled my lips and said, I thought you had a good idea! I've thought about this way, it's not possible. Our family is in Beijing, and we sent Beijing cloth tickets, which cannot be used in Tibet. There must be a Tibetan cloth ticket.河莲说，我们同你一起再去找卖围裙的藏族小姑娘，大家一块儿为你说话，人多力量大，没准儿就把她的心说动了。

  He Lian said, let's go with you to find the Tibetan girl who sells aprons, everyone will speak for you together, there are many people and great power, maybe she will move her heart.我连说，不成不成。我看得出她是一个好心的小姑娘，我们要是不给布票就拿走了她的围裙，她会伤心的。要是那样，我情愿不要围裙了。

  I keep saying, no, no. I can see that she is a kind little girl, and she will be sad if we take away her apron without giving a cloth ticket. If that's the case, I'd rather not have an apron.正在大家一筹莫展的时候，一直没吭声的小如附在我的耳边说，我倒有一个办法，你可如此这般……不过要你一个人去，千万不可一大帮人凑热闹。这事能成最好，不成就算了，千万不要再为难小姑娘……”

  Just when everyone was at a loss, Xiaoru, who had been silent, whispered in my ear and said, I have a way, you can be like this...... But if you want to go alone, don't join in the fun with a large group of people. This can be the best, if it doesn't work, don't embarrass the little girl anymore......"我连连答应着，再次进了毛伴。

  I agreed again and again and entered Mao Ban again.

  藏族女孩依旧笑眯眯地看着我，不待我说话，就把那条精美的围裙拿了过来，用略带生硬的汉语说，布票，有了？你的？

  The Tibetan girl still looked at me with a smile, and before I could speak, she took the exquisite apron and said in slightly blunt Chinese, cloth ticket, do you have it? Yours?我记着小如的指示，不慌不忙地说，我没有布票。

  I remembered Xiaoru's instructions and said calmly, I don't have a cloth ticket.听了我的话，她脸上的笑容还在，但拿围裙的手就想往回缩了。

  After listening to my words, the smile on her face was still there, but the hand holding the apron wanted to retract back.我忙说，可是我有一张背心票啊。

  I hurriedly said, but I have a vest ticket.那时候，我们虽然不发布票，但每人每年有一张背心票，可以买一件背心。

  At that time, although we did not issue tickets, each person had a vest ticket every year, and they could buy a vest.她垂着睫毛说，可是，围裙和背心是不一样的。

  She said with her eyelashes down, but an apron and a vest are different.我说，是啊，是不一样。但是，如果我没有背心票，要买一件背心，就要给你两尺布票。对不对？

  I said, yes, it's different. However, if I don't have a vest ticket, if I want to buy a vest, I will give you a two-foot cloth ticket. Right?她又笑起来说，是这样规定的。

  She laughed again and said that it was stipulated.

  我说，那现在我用背心票换你的两尺布票，也说得过去啊。所以，我就可以用这个背心票买藏族围裙了。你说是不是啊？

  I said, now I will exchange the vest ticket for your two-foot cloth ticket, which makes sense. So, I can use this vest ticket to buy a Tibetan apron. Don't you think so?她开心地笑了，露出珍珠一样的牙齿说，这样的买卖，我以前从来没有做过。不过，你说得也有道理。就按你说的办吧，谁让你这样喜欢我们藏族的花围裙呢。

  She smiled happily, showed her pearl-like teeth and said, I have never done such a deal before. However, you have a point. Just do as you say, who made you like our Tibetan flower apron so much.我高高兴兴地抱着围裙回了家。伙伴们都开心极了，每人扎着围裙照了一张相。

  I happily happily hugged my apron and went home. The partners were so happy that each of them took a photo with an apron.只可惜那时的相片都是黑白的，不能充分显示出我的藏族围裙是多么光彩夺目。

  It's a pity that the photos at that time were all black and white, which did not fully show how dazzling my Tibetan apron was.

  01.一个人就是一支骑兵

  01. A person is a cavalryman

  我曾行进在漫天皆白的冰雪中，在一支骑兵的中段靠后部分，那时还不到17岁的年龄，在藏北边防线上。无比艰难的跋涉中，我往前看，是英勇攀缘的战友；向后看，也是英勇攀缘的战友。我明白自己是队列中的一员，只能做一件事，攀缘。那时的我很懦弱，高寒与缺氧像两把冰锥，揳入我的前胸后背。极端的苦乏，让我想到唯一解脱的方法就是自杀。我用仅存的气力做告别人世的准备，可是因为我在连绵不绝的队列中，队列的节奏感和完整性，让我找不到机会对自己下手，就这样拽着马尾翻过雪山，被迫保全了性命。

  I once marched in the white ice and snow, in the middle of a cavalry, at the back of the middle section, when I was not yet 17 years old, on the northern Tibetan border defense. In the extremely difficult trek, I looked forward and saw a heroic comrade-in-arms; Looking back, he is also a comrade-in-arms who climbed heroically. I understand that I am a member of the queue and can only do one thing, climb. At that time, I was very cowardly, and the cold and lack of oxygen were like two ice picks, digging into my chest and back. The extreme suffering made me think that the only way to be relieved was to commit suicide. I used my remaining strength to prepare to say goodbye to the world, but because I was in the endless queue, the rhythm and integrity of the queue made me unable to find a chance to attack myself, so I dragged my ponytail over the snowy mountains and was forced to save my life.之后，我对军队生出一种敬畏和崇拜。

  After that, I developed a sense of awe and admiration for the military.军队是有头有尾的，也有心脏。司令部就是军队的指挥中枢，而司令员就是至高无上的王。无论情况怎样危急，无论条件怎样恶劣，无论事态多么复杂，无论困难怎样重峦叠嶂，指挥机关总是镇定和胸有成竹的。它冷静而清醒，不出昏着儿，不忘乎所以。胜不骄败不馁，紧张地运筹帷幄。我私下里曾想，司令员永远是不可战胜的吗？他可有孤单无能的时刻？一次，司令员病了，卫生科长派我去给他输液。司令员虚弱地躺在白色被子里，须发杂乱，同寻常庄户老汉并无太大的区别。他的萎靡让在一旁看护他的我，有了发问的勇气。

  The army has a beginning and an end, and it also has a heart. The headquarters is the command center of the army, and the commander is the supreme king. No matter how critical the situation is, no matter how harsh the conditions, no matter how complex the situation is, no matter how difficult it is, the command organ is always calm and confident. It is calm and sober, not fainting, and not forgetting what is happening. Victory is not arrogant and defeated, and tense strategizing. I privately thought, is the commander always invincible? Does he have moments of loneliness and incompetence? Once, the commander fell ill, and the chief of the health section sent me to give him an infusion. The commander lay weakly in a white quilt, with messy hair and beard, not much different from an ordinary old man. His malaise gave me, who was watching over him, the courage to ask questions.趁他神志稍清，我说，司令员，你可有胆小的时候？

  While he was a little clearer, I said, Commander, are you timid when you are timid?他看着输液瓶里眼泪般溅落的药水说，有。

  He looked at the tear-like splashing of the potion in the infusion bottle and said, yes.我说，什么时候？

  I said, when?他说，就是现在。我不知道我还要躺多久，才能站起来指挥我的队伍。

  He said, now. I don't know how long I have to lie down before I can get up and command my team.在那时的我看来，这个回答同没回答差不多。

  At that time, it seemed to me that this answer was almost the same as not answering.一支军队是有政委有政治部的，它看起来有些儒雅气，虽说也佩着枪，杀气却不浓重。但你不可小觑，它坚硬如铁又心细如发。它的勇气是深藏不露的，永远知道最初的方向和最后的目的地。知道我们何时软弱，它会给予激励；知道我们何时轻敌，它会给予警示；知道我们何时灰心丧气举步不前，它会给予鞭策……政委经验丰富，处事老到，表面上不动声色，内里洞若观火。说起来我对于政委的好感，还来源于一份血缘。我的父亲曾是一位师政委，这使得我近水楼台先得月地敢于探询政委的内心世界。

  An army has a political commissar and a political department, and it looks a little elegant, although it also wears a gun, but it is not murderous. But you must not underestimate it, it is as hard as iron and as delicate as hair. Its courage is deeply hidden, always knowing the initial direction and the final destination. Knowing when we are weak will motivate us; knowing when we underestimate the enemy, it will give warning; Knowing when we are discouraged and staggering gives us a spur...... The political commissar is experienced and experienced in handling affairs, calm on the surface, and like watching a fire on the inside. Speaking of which, my good impression of the political commissar also comes from a blood relationship. My father was a political commissar of the division, which made me dare to explore the inner world of the political commissar first.您是什么时候变得像一个政委的？我问父亲。

  When did you become like a political commissar? I asked my father.这句话有很大的语病，如果问别的政委，可能会被批评。好在他是我的父亲，原谅我的好奇和冒犯。

  This sentence has a big language problem, and if you ask other political commissars, you may be criticized. Fortunately, he is my father and forgives my curiosity and offense.他说，嗯，政委是慢慢变成的。

  He said, well, the political commissar slowly became.我说，具体是什么时候呢？比如是您30多岁？40多岁？还是更老的时候？

  I said, when exactly is it? For example, are you in your 30s? 40s? Or when you are older?他说，很难找到一个具体的时间，总之变化是逐渐发生的。你先要做自己的政委，然后才能做大家的政委。

  He said that it is difficult to find a specific time, in short, changes occur gradually. You have to be your own political commissar first, and then you can be everyone's political commissar.

  这句话，我也不大懂。当时认为主要区别在于——当自己的政委不用任命，而成为一支军队的政委，是需要更高机构任命的。直到很久以后我才醒悟，一个当不好自己政委的人，不配给更多将士当政委。

  I don't understand this sentence either. At that time, it was believed that the main difference was that when one's own political commissar did not need to be appointed, but became a political commissar of an army, it was necessary to be appointed by a higher body. It wasn't until much later that I realized that a person who can't be his own political commissar is not worthy of being a political commissar for more soldiers.说完了一支军队的司令员和政委，就要说后勤部长了。通常我们想起后勤部长，总伴着食堂的烟火气。后勤部掌管的就是粮草之事，虽说有“兵马未动粮草先行”的古话罩着，但比较起来，后勤部的重要性还是稍逊一筹。比如破釜沉舟，砸坏的都是属于后勤部的设备，可见对于打胜仗来说，后勤部是可以暂时割舍的。起码离开几小时或是一天，没有太大的风险。

  After talking about the commander and political commissar of an army, let's talk about the Minister of Logistics. Usually we think of the Minister of Logistics, always accompanied by the fireworks of the canteen. Although there is an old saying that "soldiers and horses do not move grain and grass first", in comparison, the importance of the logistics department is still slightly inferior. For example, the equipment belonging to the logistics department is all destroyed, which shows that for winning the battle, the logistics department can be temporarily abandoned. At least leave for a few hours or a day, there is not much risk.我一回忆起当年阿里军分区的后勤部长就想笑，他有点邋里邋遢，单帽的檐总是捋不直，好像被特意发了一个伪劣品。我们是新兵，但帽檐笔直。后来才明白，只有极老的兵，才敢藐视军纪。发夏装的时候，他说，这几个女娃娃怎么能在雪山上穿单衣呢？快给基地打报告，把她们的夏装换成冬装，才不会落下病。

  As soon as I recalled that the logistics minister of the Ali Military Sub-district wanted to laugh, he was a little sloppy, and the brim of his hat was always not straightened, as if he had been deliberately sent a counterfeit product. We are recruits, but with a straight brim. Later, I realized that only extremely old soldiers dare to despise military discipline. When distributing summer clothes, he said, how can these female dolls wear single clothes on the snowy mountains? Quickly report to the base and change their summer clothes to winter clothes, so that they will not fall ill.那时候的藏北高原比现在要冷。在一个风和日丽的冬日，我随手拿了温度计到室外去测，得到的数据是零下38度。我有365天都没有脱下过棉裤的记录，膝关节还是如小虫噬咬。男兵的夏装和冬装式样相同，只是一个瘦些一个宽松些。男兵领夏装的时候故意放大一号，就可以把夏装罩在棉衣棉裤上。女式军服夏装有掐腰和小翻领，想要把它套在棉袄棉裤之上简直痴心妄想。

  At that time, the northern Tibetan plateau was colder than it is now. On a sunny winter day, I took a thermometer outdoors to measure it, and the data I got was minus 38 degrees. I haven't taken off my cotton pants for 365 days, and my knee joints are still bitten by insects. The male soldiers' summer clothes are the same as those of winter clothes, but one is thinner and the other is looser. When a male soldier wears a summer dress, he deliberately enlarges one size, so that he can cover the summer coat over the cotton clothes and pants. The women's military uniform summer coat has a pinched waist and small lapels, and it is simply wishful thinking to put it on top of a padded jacket and pants.那时年轻的我们，其实很想在严寒的风雪中，穿半开领的夏装窈窕过市，让众多的男性士兵侧目。至于久远的损害，我们完全顾及不到。后勤部长铁嘴断金，一句话毁了少女们扮俏的梦想。那时我们是愤愤的，便私下里骂他军阀作风。后勤部长似乎能掐会算，他说，你们现在骂我，将来会感激我，傻娃娃啊。女式夏装在严寒的高原，的确是没有用武之地的鸡肋。勉强穿戴，关节炎、气管炎一定会缠上我们。现在我已年逾花甲，还未曾骨折且没有大关节的红肿热痛，这和后勤部长的“军阀”作风密切相关。

  At that time, when we were young, we actually wanted to wear half-open collared summer clothes to pass through the city in the cold wind and snow, which made many male soldiers look sideways. As for the long-term damage, we can't take it into account at all. The Minister of Logistics broke the gold with an iron mouth, and one sentence ruined the girls' dream of dressing up. At that time, we were indignant, so we privately scolded him for his warlord style. The logistics minister seemed to be able to pinch and calculate, he said, you scold me now, and you will be grateful to me in the future, silly doll. Women's summer clothes are indeed useless on the cold plateau. Reluctantly wearing it, arthritis and tracheitis will definitely haunt us. Now that I am over sixty years old, I have not had a fracture and no redness, swelling, heat and pain in my large joints, which is closely related to the "warlord" style of the logistics minister.以上是我对一支作战军队基本配置的了解。

  The above is my understanding of the basic configuration of a combat army.也许你要说，哦，你忘了，还有武器。

  Maybe you're going to say, oh, you forgot, and weapons.是的，军队不能没有武器，骑兵不能没有马。但这对骁勇的军队来讲，并不是最重要的。

  Yes, the army cannot be without weapons, and the cavalry cannot do without horses. But this is not the most important thing for the brave army.那时我有一支步枪，我练到闭着眼睛能在几分钟之内迅速拆解组装，也用它打出了优秀的成绩。我一直以为配给我这种小兵的枪，一定是无名鼠辈。直到我退役很多年之后，才知道它是大名鼎鼎的AK-47的一个版本。枪械是不断改进的，武器是不断发展的，然而如果没有人来操控，枪就是钢铁的生冷拼装，无人机也不过是求婚时的玩具。虽然由于级别的不同，枪和无人机的性能也会有天壤之别，但骨子里，它们是没有生命的。马是骑兵的伴侣，但马服从人。

  I had a rifle at that time, and I practiced it to the point where I could quickly disassemble and assemble it in a matter of minutes with my eyes closed, and I also used it to achieve excellent results. I always thought that the gun given to me as a soldier must be a nameless rat. It wasn't until many years after I retired that I learned it was a version of the famous AK-47. Firearms are constantly improving, weapons are constantly evolving, but if no one controls them, guns are raw and cold assemblies of steel, and drones are just toys when proposing. Although the performance of guns and drones will be very different due to different levels, at the core, they are lifeless. The horse is the companion of the cavalry, but the horse obeys the man.一个人就是一支骑兵，你要有勇气。你能掌握什么技术，这并不是最关键的。普天下的本领千千万，归根结底，都是枪和无人机的远房亲戚。

  A person is a cavalryman, you have to be courageous. What technology you can master is not the most critical. There are thousands of skills in the world, and in the final analysis, they are all distant relatives of guns and drones.你之所以成为你，是因为你有你的司令员和政委，你有你的后勤部长，你是你自己的小兵，又是你自己的统帅。

  You become you because you have your commander and political commissar, you have your logistics minister, you are your own soldier, and you are your own commander.你要知道这支军队向何处去。你要在这支军队沮丧的时候给它打气。你要在这支军队迷路的时候，做它永不失灵的GPS。你要在这支军队忘乎所以的时候，适时地给它兜头一盆冷水。你要在这支军队重伤的时候，给它输血，给它包扎。你要在这支军队畏葸不前的时候，擂响战鼓。你要在它恬然酣睡的时候吹起冲锋号。你要给它以休养生息的机会，你要让它安然和健康，你要知道什么对它是真正的好，并要不懈地坚持。你要知道什么是可能伤害它的陷阱，早早地发出警报远离可能的危险。你要冷静要镇定要充满激情又能适可而止，你要令行禁止健步如飞，你要能帮他找到最相宜的伴侣……

  You need to know where this army is going. You have to cheer up this army when it is frustrated. You have to do the GPS that never fails when this army gets lost. You have to give this army a basin of cold water at the right time when it gets carried away. When this army is seriously injured, you have to give it blood transfusions and bandages it. You have to beat the war drums when this army is afraid of advancing. You have to blow the charge horn while it is sleeping. You have to give it a chance to recuperate, you have to make it safe and healthy, you have to know what is really good for it, and you have to persist unremittingly. You need to know what traps can harm it and alert early to stay away from possible dangers. You have to be calm, calm, passionate and moderate, you have to be able to walk like flying, and you have to be able to help him find the most suitable partner......想到虽是独自一人，但身后有一支军队。车辚辚马萧萧，罡风浩荡，旌旗猎猎，号角长鸣，司政后一应俱全指挥若定，真是令人豪情万丈的事情。

  I thought that although I was alone, there was an army behind me. The chariots and horses are rustling, the wind is mighty, the flags are hunting, the horn is sounding, and the chief minister is all commanded, which is really a proud thing.

  02.你站在金字塔的第几层

  02. You stand on the first floor of the pyramid美国心理学家马斯洛有一段名言：“如果你有意地避重就轻，去做比你尽力所能做到的更小的事情，那么我警告你，在你今后的日子里，你将是很不幸的。因为你总是要逃避那些和你的能力相联系的各种机会和可能性。”每逢读到，我总是心怀战栗地感动。

  American psychologist Maslow famously said: "If you deliberately avoid the heavy and do less than you can do, then I warn you that you will be very unfortunate in your days to come." Because you always have to run away from the opportunities and possibilities that are connected to your abilities. "Whenever I read it, I am always moved with trembling.一个人就像是一粒种子，天生就有发芽的欲望。只要是一颗健康的种子，哪怕是在地下埋藏千年，哪怕是到太空遨游过一圈，哪怕被冰雪封盖，哪怕经过了鸟禽消化液的浸泡，哪怕被风刀霜剑连续斩杀……只要那宝贵的胚芽还在，一到时机成熟，它就会在阳光下探出头来，绽开勃勃的生机。

  A person is like a seed, born with the desire to germinate. As long as it is a healthy seed, even if it has been buried underground for thousands of years, even if it has traveled around space, even if it is covered by ice and snow, even if it has been soaked in the digestive juices of birds and birds, even if it is continuously killed by wind knives and frost swords...... As long as the precious germ is still there, when the time comes, it will poke its head out in the sun and bloom with vitality.现代心理学有很多精彩的论证，这些论证不能像实证的物理、化学，拿出若干铁一般的证据，心理学的很多假说建立在对人的行为的推断和研究之上，被千千万万的人所证实。

  Many hypotheses in psychology are based on the inference and study of human behavior, which have been confirmed by thousands of people.马斯洛先生所创建的人的基本需要的“金字塔”理论，就是这样一个伟大的学说。他研究了很多人的行为和动机，特别是那些自我实现程度很高的人，之后得出了一个结论。简言之，就是在我们人类的精神内核中存在着一个内在需要的金字塔，分成了五个台阶。

  The "pyramid" theory of the basic needs of man created by Mr. Maslow is such a great doctrine. He studied the behavior and motivations of many people, especially those with a high degree of self-actualization, and came to a conclusion. In short, there is a pyramid of intrinsic needs in the spiritual core of our human beings, divided into five steps.在第一个台阶上，是我们的温饱需要——最基本的生存之道。饥肠辘辘，你今晚吃什么饭？是人的第一考虑。寒冬腊月，你今夜睡在哪里？是火车站的长凳还是马路上的水泥管？这都是头等大事。

  On the first step, it is our food and clothing needs - the most basic way to survive. Hungry, what are you eating tonight? It is the first consideration of people. In the cold winter moon, where are you sleeping tonight? Is it a bench at a train station or a cement pipe on the road? This is all top priority.当这个需要满足之后，紧接着就是安全的需要了。你有了吃、有了住，你今天的生命有了保障，可是如果你被其他的人或动物或自然界的恶劣条件所侵犯，你远期的生命就陷在水深火热之中了。因此，一旦温饱不成问题，人马上就考虑安全系数。这一点，如果你不相信，尽可以放眼看去，马上能看到富人区森严的安保设施和世上风行的形形色色的自卫器械。当你从一个熟识的环境换到一个新环境，那种不安和紧张，与陌生人交谈时的畏葸和不自在，如此等等，都从另一个方面证实了安全对人的重要性。

  When this need is satisfied, the need for security is immediately followed. You have food and shelter, and your life today is guaranteed, but if you are violated by other people, animals, or the harsh conditions of nature, your long-term life will be in dire straits. Therefore, once food and clothing are not a problem, people immediately consider the safety factor. If you don't believe this, you can look around and immediately see the strict security facilities in the rich areas and all kinds of self-defense equipment that are popular in the world. When you change from a familiar environment to a new environment, the uneasiness and nervousness, the fear and discomfort when talking to strangers, and so on, all confirm the importance of safety for people from another aspect.现在我们已经到了金字塔的第三台阶。在这个台阶上大大地写着“爱”。这不仅是男女之爱、亲子之爱、手足之爱……这些源于血缘和繁衍的爱意，还有同伴之爱、集体之爱、祖国之爱、民族之爱、文化之爱……总之，这里所提到的“爱”，有着宽泛的含义，但它是那样不可或缺，是人类精神活动的高级需要。我们常常说，一个不懂得爱的人是灰暗和孤独的。也就是说，人的精神需要如果不能完成这种超越和提升，就是饱含瑕疵的半成品。

  Now we have reached the third step of the pyramid. On this step is written "love" in large letters. This is not only the love between men and women, parent-child love, and siblings...... These love stems from blood and reproduction, as well as companion love, collective love, motherland love, national love, and cultural love...... In short, the "love" mentioned here has a broad meaning, but it is so indispensable and a high-level need for human spiritual activities. We often say that a person who does not know how to love is gray and lonely. In other words, if human spiritual needs cannot complete this transcendence and improvement, it is a semi-finished product full of flaws.爱之高处，就是尊严感了。人是一种特殊的动物，人是有尊严感的。一只虫子可以没有尊严，一株树木可以没有尊严，但是一个人不是这样。如果丧失了尊严感，那就不是一个完整的人了。中国的古话里有“不吃嗟来之食”，有“士可杀不可辱”，有“君子一言，驷马难追”，等等，讲的都是尊严的问题。

  The height of love is the sense of dignity. Man is a special animal, and man has a sense of dignity. A bug can have no dignity, a tree can have no dignity, but a person is not like that. If you lose your sense of dignity, you are not a complete person. In the old Chinese saying, there are "don't eat the food that comes from sighing", there are "scholars can be killed but not humiliated", there are "a gentleman's words, it is difficult to chase a horse", etc., all of which talk about the issue of dignity.在金字塔的最高点，屹立着自我价值的体现和追求。什么是自我价值的最高体现——那就是充满了创造性的劳动。我以为劳动是有高下之分的，不是指在价值层面上，而是指在带给人的由衷喜悦程度上。你可以想象并同意，一个科学家在得不到任何报酬的情形下，不倦地研究某一个与现实相隔十万八千里的学术问题，比如“哥德巴赫猜想”，为自己换不到一块窝窝头，但毫无疑问陈景润乐在其中；你基本上不能同意一位老农在得知三年没有人收购麦子的情况下，除了自己够吃之外还会不辞劳苦地广撒麦种。在前者，创造性的劳动里面蕴含着极大的挑战和快乐；在后者，则充斥着重复性劳动的艰辛和疲惫。

  At the highest point of the pyramid, stands the embodiment and pursuit of self-worth. What is the highest manifestation of self-worth - that is, full of creative labor. I think that labor is different, not in terms of value, but in the degree of sincere joy it brings to people. You can imagine and agree that a scientist tirelessly studies an academic problem that is 108,000 miles away from reality without getting paid, such as the "Goldbach conjecture", and cannot exchange a piece of nest for himself, but there is no doubt that Chen Jingrun enjoys it; You basically can't agree with an old farmer who knows that no one has bought wheat for three years, and in addition to having enough to eat, he will tirelessly sow wheat seeds. In the former, creative labor contains great challenges and joys; in the latter, it is full of hardships and exhaustion of repetitive labor.

  人类精神需要的金字塔，在某种意义上讲，是一种铁律，几乎是不可逃避的。当然，我们不能想象一个人在自己的温饱都得不到保障的时候，能够像斯蒂芬·霍金那样去研究宇宙大爆炸这样的问题。这也就是鲁迅先生所说的：年轻人，一是要生存，二是要发展。有一个顺序，有孰先孰后的问题。在解决了温饱和安全这些最基本的生存需要之后，你必定要不满足，你必定要有新的追求。人类精神发育的法则，你是绕不过去的。你吃得饱了，你睡得暖了，你有大房子了，你安居乐业了，你很有安全的保障了……可是，我敢说，在心底最深邃的地方，你有火焰一样的躁动，你如果无法满足它，你就没有恒久的快乐。

  The pyramid of human spiritual needs is, in a sense, an iron law, almost inescapable. Of course, we cannot imagine a person being able to study such a problem as the Big Bang like Stephen Hawking when he is not guaranteed food and clothing. This is what Mr. Lu Xun said: young people, one is to survive, and the other is to develop. There is an order, and there is a question of who comes first. After solving the most basic survival needs such as food, clothing and security, you must be dissatisfied, you must have new pursuits. You can't get around the laws of human spiritual development. You have eaten enough, you have slept warmly, you have a big house, you live and work in peace and contentment, and you are very safe...... However, I dare to say that in the deepest part of your heart, you have a restlessness like a flame, and if you cannot satisfy it, you will not have permanent happiness.

  让我们回到本文开端所引用的马斯洛的那段话。你以为你逃避了风险，你以为你躲避了责任，你以为你成功地掩饰了自己的才华，你以为你心甘情愿地收敛包裹自己，你就可以在人们的艳羡之中安安稳稳地过一生了吗？我相信，你可以用奢华的装备和风流倜傥的举止成功地欺骗几乎所有的人，包括和你至亲至爱之人，但是，每每月朗星稀之时，你永远欺骗不了的一个人，就会在你独处的时候顽强地站在你的面前，拷问你、鞭挞你、谴责你、纠正你……这个人不是别人，正是你自己！由于每一个人都是那样与众不同，由于你所具有的内在生命力一直在熊熊燃烧，所以，当你完成了自己人生的台阶之后，你就要向上攀登。你只有在这种不倦的探索中才能丰富自己的人生，才能得到生命的欢愉，才能感觉到自己内在的充实和价值。

  Let's go back to the passage of Maslow quoted at the beginning of this article. Do you think you have avoided risks, you think you have avoided responsibility, you think you have successfully concealed your talent, you think you are willing to restrain and wrap yourself, and you can live your life peacefully in the envy of people? I believe that you can successfully deceive almost everyone, including your loved ones, with luxurious equipment and dashing demeanor, but every month when the stars are rare, a person you can never deceive will stand tenaciously in front of you when you are alone, torture you, whip you, condemn you, and correct you...... This person is none other than yourself! Because everyone is so different, because your inner vitality is always burning, you have to climb up when you have completed the steps of your life. Only in this tireless exploration can you enrich your life, get the joy of life, and feel your inner fulfillment and value.人是追求创造性快乐的动物，如同飞越大洋的候鸟脑内的罗盘，掌控着我们的一系列选择和决定。你一生将成为怎样的人？在你的价值体系里是怎样的顺序？这些看起来很浩大很空茫的标准，实际上很细致地决定着我们工作、学习、生活的各个层面。

  Humans are animals that pursue creative pleasure, like a compass in the brain of a migratory bird flying across the ocean, controlling a series of choices and decisions in our hearts. What kind of person will you be in your life? What is the order in your value system? These seemingly vast and empty standards actually determine all aspects of our work, study and life in great detail.记得我在北大讲演的时候，有同学递上来一张字条，上面写着：“我智商很高，从小到大一直是班干部，考上北大更证明了我的实力。只要我愿意，继续读硕士和博士都不成问题。你说，我选择金钱作为我一生奋斗的目标，你看怎样？”我把这张字条念了。我说，我很感谢这位同学对我的信任，人生的价值是多元的，以金钱为自己终生的奋斗目标，也大有人在。但我以为，金钱只是手段，在它之后，还有更为深远的目标在导引着你。如果你唯钱是图，那么，你的周围将没有真正的朋友。因为古往今来，已经无数次地证明了，在金钱的旗帜下会聚拢来很多无耻小人。同时，你很可能得不到真正的爱情。因为爱情可以被金钱出卖，却不可被金钱所购买。那个爱上你的人，有可能不是爱你本人，而是爱上了你的信用卡。如果你把金钱当成了证明你的自我价值的工具，我要说，除了单一和狭隘，还有一种盲从，你用世俗的标准代替了内在的准星。

  I remember when I was giving a speech at Peking University, a classmate handed me a note that read: "I have a high IQ and have been a class cadre since I was a child, and being admitted to Peking University proves my strength." As long as I want, it is not a problem to continue with my master's and doctorate. You say, I chose money as my life's goal, what do you think? I read this note. I said, I am very grateful to this classmate for his trust in me, the value of life is diverse, and there are many people who take money as their lifelong goal. But I think that money is just a means, and after it, there are more far-reaching goals guiding you. If you only want money, then you will have no real friends around you. Because throughout the ages, it has been proved countless times that many shameless villains will gather under the banner of money. At the same time, you will most likely not get true love. Because love can be sold by money, but it cannot be bought by money. The person who falls in love with you may not love you personally, but your credit card. If you use money as a tool to prove your self-worth, I would say that in addition to singleness and narrow-mindedness, there is also a kind of blind obedience in which you replace the inner crosshair with worldly standards.我翻阅了几期《华融之声》，看到华融人的志气和理想。谈到从工商银行调到华融来的理由，最主要的是期望自己的能力得到更好的发展。我觉得这是很好的理由，是内心和外在的统一，是朝着自我实现路上的迈进。当然了，自我实现的路，绝不会是一帆风顺的。我们常常会遭遇到挫折和失败，但人生的价值并不在于永远是胜利和成功，而在于这个过程当中我们得到了独一无二的属于自己的体验。在生存之道解决之后，在工作中得到乐趣，就是一个极好的选择。要知道，我们每个人，一生用于工作的时间大于七万小时。可不要小瞧了这七万小时，如果你是在快乐和创造中，你是在寻找自我价值的挑战中，你的人生就会过得很充实。如果你只是为了更多的钱、更宽敞的房子、更多的应酬和名声上的虚荣，你将在七万小时甚至更多的时间里委屈着自己，扼杀着自己，毁灭着自己的自由。

  I flipped through several issues of "The Voice of Huarong" and saw the ambition and ideals of Huarong people. When it comes to the reasons for transferring from ICBC to Huarong, the most important thing is to expect better development of their abilities. I think this is a good reason, it is the unity of the heart and the outside, and it is a step towards self-realization. Of course, the road to self-realization will never be smooth sailing. We often encounter setbacks and failures, but the value of life is not that it is always victory and success, but that we get a unique experience in the process. After the way of survival is solved, having fun at work is an excellent choice. You know, each of us spends more than 70,000 hours working in our lives. Don't underestimate these 70,000 hours, if you are in the joy and creation, you are in the challenge of finding self-worth, your life will be very fulfilling. If you just want more money, a more spacious house, more socializing, and vanity in fame, you will wronged yourself, strangled yourself, and destroyed your freedom for 70,000 hours or more.我在美国印第安人的保留地遇到一位印第安族的心理学家。她说，在我们古老的印第安人那里，有一个风俗，即使自己的温饱没有解决，我们也会用自己的食物拯救他人。因为，对我们来说，帮助别人是精神的传统。我并不是要挑战马斯洛，我只是说，精神有时比肉体更重要。

  I met an Indian psychologist on an American Indian reservation. She said that among our ancient Indians, there was a custom that even if our own food and clothing were not solved, we would use our own food to save others. Because, for us, helping others is a spiritual tradition. I'm not trying to challenge Maslow, I'm just saying that sometimes the spirit is more important than the body.这是那位印第安族心理学家最后留给我的话。

  These are the last words that the Indian psychologist left me.

未知

  03.人可以最大限度地逼近真实

  03. People can get as close to the truth as possible

  朋友给我讲过这样一个故事。

  A friend told me such a story.他祖父小的时候很聪明，也很有毅力，学业有成，正欲大展宏图之际，曾祖将他叫了去，拿出一个古匣，对他说，孩子，我有一件心事，终生未了。因为我得到它们的时候，一生的日子已经过了一半，剩下的时间不够我把它做完了。做学问，就要从年轻的时候着手，我要是交给你一件半成品，不如让你从头开始。

  When his grandfather was very smart, persevering, and academically successful, when he was about to make a big move, his great-grandfather called him there, took out an ancient box, and said to him, "Child, I have something on my mind that will not end for the rest of my life." Because by the time I got them, half of my life had passed, and there wasn't enough time left for me to finish it. To do learning, you must start from a young age, if I give you a semi-finished product, it is better to let you start from scratch.原委是这样的。早年间，江南有一家富豪，酷爱藏书。他家有两册古时传下的医书，集无数医家心血之大成，为杏林一绝。富豪视若珍宝，秘不传人，藏在书楼里，难得一见。后来，富豪出门遇险，一位壮士从强盗手里救了他的性命。富豪感恩不尽，欲以斗载的金银相谢。壮士说，财宝再多、再贵重，也是有价的。我救了你，你的命无价。富豪说，莫非壮士还要取了我的命去？壮士大笑说，我不是要你的命，是想用你的医书救普天下人的性命。富豪想了半天，说，我可以将医书借给你三天，但是三日后的正午，你必得完璧归赵。说罢，命人从嵯峨的木制书楼里，将饱含檀香气味的医书捧了出来。

  The whole story is like this. In the early years, there was a rich family in Jiangnan who loved to collect books. His family has two volumes of medical books passed down in ancient times, which are the culmination of countless doctors' hard work and are a unique in Xinglin. The rich regard it as a treasure, secret and hidden in the library, which is rare to see. Later, when the rich man went out in danger, a strong man saved his life from robbers. The rich man was grateful and wanted to thank him with a bucket of gold and silver. The strong man said that no matter how much treasure and how valuable it is, it is also valuable. I saved you, your life is priceless. The rich man said, could it be that the strong man is going to take my life? The strong man laughed and said, I don't want your life, I want to use your medical book to save the lives of people all over the world. The rich man thought for a long time and said, I can lend you the medical book for three days, but at noon three days later, you will have to return to Zhao completely. After saying that, he ordered someone to hold out the medical book full of sandalwood from the towering wooden library.壮士得了书后，快马加鞭急如星火地赶回家，请来乡下的诸位学子，连夜赶抄医书。书是孤本，时间又那样紧迫，荧荧灯火下，抄书人目眦尽裂，总算在规定时间之内依样画葫芦地描了下来。壮士把医书还了富豪，长出一口气，心想，从此以后便可以用这深锁在豪门的医学宝典造福于天下黎民了。

  After the strong man got the book, he hurried home like a spark, invited students from the countryside to copy medical books overnight. The book is a solitary book, and the time is so tight, under the flickering lights, the scribe's eyes are cracked, and he finally drew it down within the specified time. The strong man returned the medical book to the rich man, let out a sigh, and thought that from now on, he could use this medical treasure book locked in the wealthy family to benefit the people of the world.谁知，抄好的医书拿给医家一看，才知竟是不能用的。医家以人的性命为本，须严谨稳当。这种在匆忙之中由外行人抄下的医方，讹脱衍倒之处甚多，且错得离奇，漏得古怪，寻不出规律，谁敢用它在病人身上做试验呢？

  Who knows, when I took the copied medical book to the doctor, I found out that it was useless. Doctors are based on human life and must be rigorous and stable. This kind of medical prescription copied by laymen in a hurry has many errors, and it is bizarre, leaky, and cannot be found to be regular, who dares to use it to test on patients?壮士造福百姓之心不死，急急赶回富豪家，想晓以大义，再请富豪将医书出借一回，这一次，请行家高手来抄，定可以精当了。当他的马冷汗涔涔到达目的地时，迎接他的是冲天火光。富豪家因遭雷击燃起天火，藏书楼内所有的典籍已化为灰烬。

  The strong man's heart to benefit the people is not dead, and he hurried back to the rich man's house, wanting to know the righteousness, and then ask the rich man to lend the medical book once, this time, please ask the expert master to copy it, and you will be able to do it well. When his horse arrived at his destination in a cold sweat, he was greeted by sky-high fire. The rich man's house was struck by lightning and ignited a fire, and all the books in the library have been reduced to ashes.从此这两册抄录的医书，就像鸡肋，一代代流传了下来。没有人敢用上面的方剂，也没有人舍得丢弃它。书的纸张黄脆了，布面断裂了，后人就又精心地誊抄一遍。因为字句的文理不通，每一个抄写的人都依照自己的理解，将它订正改动一番，闹得愈加面目全非，几成天书。

  Since then, these two copied medical books have been passed down from generation to generation. No one dared to use the above prescription, and no one was willing to throw it away. The paper of the book was yellow and brittle, and the cloth surface was broken, so later generations carefully copied it again. Because the words and sentences are unreasonable, everyone who transcribes them corrects and changes them according to their own understanding, making it more and more unrecognizable, almost a book.曾祖的话说到这里，目光炯炯地看着祖父。

  When my great-grandfather said this, he looked at his grandfather with bright eyes.祖父说，您手里拿的就是这两册书吗？

  Grandfather said, are you holding these two books in your hand?曾祖说，正是。

  The great-grandfather said, exactly.祖父说，您是要我把它们校勘出来？

  Grandfather said, do you want me to proofread them?曾祖说，我希望你能穷毕生的精力让它死而复生。但你只说对了一半，不是它们，是它。工程浩大，你这一辈子，是无法同时改正两本书的。现在，你就从中挑一本吧。留下的那本，只有留待我们的后代子孙再来辨析正误了。

  The great-grandfather said, I hope you can spend your whole life trying to bring it back to life. But you're only half right, it's not them, it's it. The project is huge, and you can't correct two books at the same time in your life. Now, you can choose one of them. The book left behind can only be left for our descendants to distinguish right from wrong.祖父看着两本一模一样的宝蓝色布面古籍，费了斟酌，就像在两个陌生的美女之中挑选自己的终身伴侣，一时不知所措。

  My grandfather looked at two identical royal blue ancient books on canvas, and after much deliberation, it was like choosing his lifelong partner among two strange beauties, and he was at a loss for a while.随意吧。它们难度相同，济世救人的功用也是一样的。曾祖父催促。

  Feel free. They are the same in difficulty, and the function of saving the world is the same. Great-grandfather urged.祖父随手点了上面的那一部书。他知道从这一刻起，这一个动作，就把自己的一生同一方未知的领域，同一个事业，同一种缘分，牢牢地粘在一起。

  My grandfather casually ordered the book above. He knew that from this moment on, this action would firmly stick his life together in the same unknown field, the same career, and the same fate.好吧。曾祖把祖父选定的甲册交到他手里，把乙册收了起来，不让祖父再翻，怕祖父三心二意，最终一事无成。

  All right. The great-grandfather handed over the book A selected by his grandfather to him, put away the book B, and did not let his grandfather turn it over again, for fear that his grandfather would be half-hearted and eventually achieve nothing.祖父没有辜负曾祖的期望，皓首穷经，用了整整半个世纪的时间，将甲书所有的错漏之处更正一新。册页上临摹不清的药材图谱，他亲自到深山老林一一核查。无法判定成分正误的方剂，他采集百草熬药炼成汤，以身试药，几次昏厥在地。为了一句不知出处的引言，他查阅无数典籍……那册医书就像是一盘古老石磨的轴心，天文地理，古今中外，凡是书中涉及的知识，祖父都用全部心血一一验证，直至确凿无疑。祖父的一生围绕着这册古医书旋转，从翩翩少年一直变作鬓发如雪。

  My grandfather did not live up to his great-grandfather's expectations, and spent half a century correcting all the mistakes and omissions in the book. The unclear atlas of medicinal materials on the album page was personally checked by the deep mountains and old forests. Unable to determine the correct or wrong ingredients, he collected herbs to boil the medicine into soup, tested the medicine with his body, and fainted several times. For a quote of unknown origin, he consulted countless classics...... That medical book is like an axis of an ancient stone mill, astronomy and geography, ancient and modern, Chinese and foreign, all the knowledge involved in the book, my grandfather used all his efforts to verify it one by one until it was conclusive. My grandfather's life revolved around this ancient medical book, from a graceful boy to a snowy sideburn.按说祖父读了这许多医书，该能成为一代良医。但是，不。祖父的博学只为那一册医书服务，凡是验证正确的方剂，祖父就不再对它们有丝毫留恋，弃而转向新的领域探索。他只对未知事物和纠正谬误有兴趣，一生穷困艰窘，竟不曾用他验证过的神方医治过病人，获得过收益。

  It is said that my grandfather read so many medical books and should be able to become a good doctor of a generation. But, no. My grandfather's erudition only served that medical book, and whenever the correct prescriptions were verified, my grandfather no longer had the slightest nostalgia for them, and abandoned them to explore new fields. He was only interested in the unknown and correcting fallacies, and he was poor and difficult all his life, but he never used the divine formula he had verified to heal patients and obtain benefits.到了祖父垂垂老矣的时候，他终于将那册古书中的几百处谬误全部订正完了。祖父把眼睛从书上移开，目光苍茫，好像第一次发现自己已走到生命的尽头。

  When my grandfather was dying, he finally corrected all the hundreds of fallacies in the ancient book. My grandfather took his eyes off the book, his eyes blank, as if he had found himself at the end of his life for the first time.人们欢呼雀跃，毕竟从此这本伟大的济世良方可以造福无数百姓了。

  People cheered, after all, this great recipe for the world could benefit countless people.但敬佩之情只持续了极短的一段时间。远方发掘了一座古墓，里面埋藏了许多保存完好的古简，其中正有甲书的原件。人们迫不及待地将祖父校勘过的甲书和原件相比较，结果是那样令人震惊。

  But the admiration lasted only for a very short time. An ancient tomb was excavated in the distance, which contained many well-preserved ancient slips, among which there was the original of the Jia book. People can't wait to compare the book A proofread by their grandfather with the original, and the result is so shocking.祖父校勘过的甲书，同古简完全吻合。

  The book proofread by my grandfather is completely consistent with the ancient slips.也就是说，祖父凭借自己惊人的智慧和毅力，以广博的学识和缜密的思维，加之异乎寻常的直觉，像盲人摸象一般在黑暗中摸索，将甲书在漫长流传过程中产生的所有错误全改正过来了。

  In other words, with his amazing wisdom and perseverance, with his extensive knowledge and meticulous thinking, coupled with his extraordinary intuition, he groped in the dark like a blind man touching an elephant, and corrected all the mistakes made in the long circulation of Jia Shu.

  祖父用毕生的精力，创造了一个奇迹。

  My grandfather used his life's energy to create a miracle.但这个奇迹，又在瞬忽之间烟消灰灭，毫无价值。古书已经出土，正本清源，祖父的一切努力都化为劳而无功的泡沫。人们只记得古书，没有人再忆起祖父和他苦苦寻觅的一生。

  But this miracle disappeared in an instant and was worthless. The ancient books have been unearthed, the source has been cleared, and all my grandfather's efforts have turned into a bubble of futile efforts. People only remember ancient books, and no one remembers his grandfather and his hard search.讲到这里，朋友久久地沉默着。

  At this point, my friend was silent for a long time.古墓里出土了乙医书的真书吗？我问。

  Was the real book of Yiyshu unearthed in the ancient tomb? I asked.没有。朋友答。

  No. Friend answered.我深深地叹息说，如果你的祖父在当初选择的那一瞬间挑选了乙书，结果就完全不一样了啊。

  I sighed deeply and said that if your grandfather chose Book B at the moment of his original choice, the result would have been completely different.朋友说，我在祖父最后的时光也问过他这个问题。祖父说，对我来讲，甲书乙书是一样的。我用一生的时间说明了一个道理，人只要全力以赴地钻研某个问题，就有可能最大限度地逼近它的真实。

  My friend said that I had asked my grandfather this question in his final days. My grandfather said, for me, book A and book B are the same. I have spent my life illustrating a truth, as long as people study a problem with all their might, it is possible to get as close to its truth as possible.祖父在上天给予的两个谜语之中随手挑选了一个。他证明了人的努力可以将千古之谜猜透。

  My grandfather casually chose one of the two riddles given by God. He proved that human efforts can guess the mysteries of the ages.这已经足够。
That's enough.

未知

  04.盲人看

  04. Blind people see

  每逢下学的时候，附近的那所小学就有稠密的人群糊在铁门前，好似风暴前的蚁穴。那是家长等着接各自的孩童回家。

  Whenever school is over, there is a dense crowd in front of the iron gate at the nearby primary school, like an ant nest before a storm. It was parents waiting to pick up their children home.在远离人群的地方，有个人倚着毛白杨悄无声息地站着，从不张望校门口。直到有一个孩子飞快地跑过来，拉着他说，爸，咱们回家。他把左手交给孩子，右手拄起盲杖，一同横穿马路。

  Away from the crowd, there was a person standing quietly leaning on Mao Baiyang, never looking at the school gate. Until a child ran over quickly, pulled him and said, Dad, let's go home. He handed his left hand to the child, and with his right hand he picked up a blind cane, and crossed the road together.多年前，这个盲人常蹲在路边，用二胡拉很哀伤的曲调。他技艺不好，琴也质劣，音符断断续续地抽噎，叫人听了只想快快远离。他面前的盛着零钱的破罐头盒，永远看得到锈蚀的罐底。我偶尔放一点钱进去，也是堵着耳朵到近前。

  Many years ago, this blind man often squatted on the side of the road, playing a very sad tune with an erhu. His skills are not good, the piano is also of poor quality, and the notes are choking intermittently, making people just want to stay away when they hear it. The broken can box containing change in front of him can always see the rusted bottom of the can. I occasionally put a little money in it, but I also plug my ears to get closer.后来，他摆了一个小摊子，卖点手绢、袜子什么的，生意很淡。一天晚上，我回家，一下公共汽车，黑寂就包抄过来。原来这一片突然停电，连路灯都灭了，只有电线杆旁一束光柱如食指捅破星天。靠拢才见是那个盲人打了手电，在卖蜡烛、火柴，价钱很便宜。我赶紧买了一份，喜滋滋地觉着带回光明给亲人。

  Later, he set up a small stall selling handkerchiefs, socks, etc., and the business was very weak. One night, I went home, got off the bus, and Hei Ji outflanked me. It turned out that the power suddenly went out in this area, and even the street lights were extinguished, only a beam of light next to the telephone pole pierced the stars like an index finger. When I got closer, I saw that it was the blind man who had turned on a flashlight, selling candles and matches, and the price was very cheap. I quickly bought one and happily felt that I was bringing back the light to my relatives.之后的某个白日，我又在路旁看到盲人，就气哼哼地走过去，说，你也不能趁着停电发这种不义之财啊！那天你卖的蜡烛算什么货色啊？蜡烛油四下流，烫了我的手。烛捻儿一点也不亮，小得像个萤火虫尾巴。

  One day later, I saw a blind man on the side of the road, so I walked over angrily and said, you can't take advantage of the power outage to make such ill-gotten gains! What kind of candle did you sell that day? The candle oil flowed down and burned my hands. The candle twister is not bright at all, as small as a firefly tail.他愣愣地把塌陷的眼窝对着我，半天才说，对不住，我……不知道……蜡烛的光……该有多大，萤火虫的尾巴……是多亮。那天听说停电，就赶紧批了蜡烛来卖。我只知道……黑了，难受。

  He pointed his collapsed eye sockets at me in a daze, and said for a long time, I'm sorry, I...... I don't know...... The light of the candle...... How big should it be, the tail of a firefly...... How bright it is. When I heard about the power outage that day, I quickly approved candles to sell. I only know...... It's dark, it's uncomfortable.

  我呆住了。那个漆黑的夜晚，即便烛火如豆，还是比完全的黑暗好了不知几多。一个盲人在为明眼人操劳，我还不分青红皂白地指责他，我好悔。

  I was stunned. That dark night, even if the candlelight was like a bean, was still much better than complete darkness. A blind man is working for a sighted person, and I still accuse him indiscriminately, and I regret it.后来，我很长时间没到他的摊子买东西。确信他把我的声音忘掉之后，有一天，我买了一堆杂物，然后放下了五十块钱，对盲人说，不必找了。

  Later, I didn't go to his stall to buy anything for a long time. After being convinced that he had forgotten my voice, one day I bought a bunch of miscellaneous items, put down fifty dollars, and said to the blind man, no need to look for it.我抱着那些东西，走了没几步，被他叫住了。大姐，你给我的是多少钱啊？

  I held those things and walked a few steps when he stopped me. Sister, how much money did you give me?我说，是五十元。

  I said, it's fifty yuan.他说，我从来没拿过这么大的票子。

  He said, "I've never had such a big ticket."见他先是平着指肚，后是立起掌根，反复摩挲钞票的正反面。

  Seeing that he first flattened his fingers, then stood up the base of his palm, and repeatedly rubbed the front and back of the banknote.我说，这钱是真的。您放心。

  I said, the money is real. You can rest assured.他笑笑说，我从来没收到过假钱。谁要是欺负一个瞎子，他的心就先瞎了。我只是不能收您这么多钱，我是在做买卖啊。

  He smiled and said, "I've never received fake money." Whoever bullies a blind man will be blind first. I just can't charge you so much money, I'm doing business.我知道自己又一次错了。

  I know I was wrong again.不知他在哪里学了按摩，经济上渐渐有了起色，从乡下找了一个盲目的姑娘，成了亲。一天，我到公园去，忽然看到他们夫妻相跟着，沿着花径在走。四周湖光山色美若仙境，我想，这对他们来讲，真是一种残酷。

  I don't know where he learned massage, but his finances gradually improved, and he found a blind girl from the countryside and became a relative. One day, I went to the park and suddenly saw them following each other, walking along the flower path. The surrounding lakes and mountains are as beautiful as a fairyland, and I think this is really cruel to them.闪过他们身旁的时候，听到盲夫有些炫耀地问，怎么样？我领你来这儿，景色不错吧？好好看看吧。

  When I flashed past them, I heard the blind man asking a little ostentatiously, how is it? I brought you here, the scenery is good, right?" Take a good look.盲妻不服气地说，好像你看过似的。

  The blind wife said unconvincedly, as if you had seen it.盲夫很肯定地说，我看过，常来看的。

  The blind man said with certainty that I had seen it and often came to see it.听一个盲人连连响亮地说出“看”这个字，叫人顿生悲凉，也觉出一些滑稽。

  Hearing a blind man say the word "look" loudly again and again makes people feel sad and funny.盲妻反唇相讥道，介绍人不是说你胎里瞎吗？啥时看到这里好景色的呢？

  The blind wife retorted, didn't the introducer say that you are blind in the womb? When did you see the good scenery here?盲夫说，别人用眼看，咱可以用心看，用耳朵看，用手看，用鼻子看……加起来一点不比别人少啊。

  The blind man said, others see with their eyes, we can see with our hearts, with our ears, with our hands, and with our noses...... It adds up to nothing less than others.他说着，用手捉了妻子的指，沿着粗糙的树皮攀上去，停在一片极小的叶子上，说，你看到了吗？多老的树，芽子也是嫩的。

  He said, grabbed his wife's finger with his hand, climbed up the rough bark, stopped on a very small leaf, and said, "Do you see it?" The older the tree, the more tender the buds are.那一瞬，我凛然一惊。世上有很多东西，看了如同未看，我们眼在神不在。记住并真正懂得的东西，必得被心房茧住啊。

  At that moment, I was shocked. There are many things in the world, and seeing them is like not seeing them, and our eyes are not there. What you remember and really understand must be cocooned in the heart.后来盲夫妇有了果实，一个瞳仁亮如秋水的男孩。他渐渐长大，上了小学，盲人便天天接送。

  Later, the blind couple had fruit, a boy with eyes as bright as autumn water. He grew up and went to elementary school, and the blind picked him up every day.

  初起那个孩童躲在盲人背后，跟着杖子走。慢慢胆子壮了，绿灯一亮，他就跳着要越过去。父亲总是死死拽住他，用盲杖戳着柏油路说，让我再听听，近处没有车轮声，我们才可动……

  At first, the child hid behind the blind man and followed the cane. Slowly he became bolder, and as soon as the green light came on, he jumped to cross over. My father always dragged him tightly, poked the asphalt with his blind cane and said, "Let me listen again, there is no sound of wheels nearby, we can move......终有一天，孩子对父亲讲，爸，我给你带路吧。他拉起父亲，东张西望，然后一蹦一跳地越过地上的斑马线。于是盲人第一次提起他的盲杖，跟着目光如炬的孩子，无所顾忌地前行，脚步抬得高高，轻捷如飞。

  One day, the child said to his father, Dad, let me show you the way. He pulled his father up, looked around, and jumped over the zebra crossing on the ground. So the blind man lifted his blind staff for the first time, followed the child with torch-like eyes, and moved forward without scruples, his steps raised high and light.孩子越来越大了。当明眼人都不再接送这么高的孩子时，盲人依旧每天倚在校旁的杨树下，等待着。
The child is getting bigger. When the sighted people no longer pick up and drop off such tall children, the blind people still lean under the poplar trees next to the school every day, waiting.

  05.柱子的弹性

  05. Elasticity of columns

  有一个故事，说的是一根柱子，一根三百年前的柱子。那根柱子很坚固，支撑着一座宏伟的大厅。那座大厅很大，大到修建的时候没有人相信一根柱子就能支撑起沉重的穹顶。年轻的建筑师用了种种科学方程式来证实他的这根柱子是何等牢靠和坚固，足够应用。人们虽然不能反对他的公式，却可以反对由他来担当这座市政大厅的总设计师。

  There is a story about a pillar, a pillar from three hundred years ago. The pillar was strong and supported a magnificent hall. The hall was so large that no one believed that a single pillar could support a heavy dome when it was built. The young architect used scientific equations to prove how solid and strong his pillar was to be applied. Although one cannot object to his formula, one can object to him as the chief designer of the municipal hall.年轻的设计师面临一个选择。如果他坚持他的设计，他的设计就永远停留在纸上了。如果他变更他的设计，人们就看不到这根独撑穹顶的柱子了。设计师沉吟再三，修改了他的图纸，又添加了四根柱子。人们对这个更加稳妥的设计拍手叫好，据此建起了壮丽的大厦。

  Young designers are faced with a choice. If he sticks to his design, his design stays on paper forever. If he had changed his design, people would not have seen the column that supported the dome alone. After much thought, the designer revised his drawings and added four more columns. People applauded this more secure design, and magnificent edifices were built accordingly.很多年过去了。年轻的设计师变成了墓碑，大地震袭击了城市。很多建筑都倒塌了。唯有具有五根柱子的市政大厅依然巍峨耸立。人们说，幸亏有五根柱子啊！

  Many years have passed. The young designer turned into a tombstone, and a big earthquake hit the city. Many buildings collapsed. Only the city hall with five columns still stands tall. People say, fortunately, there are five pillars!终于到了维修的时刻。人们惊讶地发现，除了最早设计的那根独撑天下的柱子，其余的四根柱子距离穹顶都有一道窄窄的间隙。也就是说，它们并不承接穹顶的重量，只是美丽的摆设。

  Finally, it's time for repairs. People were surprised to find that except for the first pillar designed to support the world alone, the other four pillars had a narrow gap from the dome. That is, they do not carry the weight of the dome, but are just beautiful decorations.于是人们惊叹这匪夷所思的设计，给予设计者以排山倒海的赞美。回答他们的只是墓草的摇曳。

  So people were amazed by this incredible design and gave the designer overwhelming praise. All they answered was the swaying of the grave grass.

  设计师没有收获生前的称誉，但他收获了一根柱子。设计师是可以怒发冲冠一走了之的，但为了他的柱子的诞生，他妥协和避让了。设计师是可以在事成之后即刻就公布他的计谋的，但为了他的柱子无可辩驳的质地，他保持了宁静的缄默。设计师是可以在一份遗嘱或一部著作中表达他的先见和果敢的，但为了他的柱子的荣誉，他不再贪恋丝毫的浮华。设计师为了他的柱子，隐没在历史的尘埃中。

  The designer did not reap the accolades of his lifetime, but he reaped a pillar. The designer could have been angry and left, but for the birth of his pillar, he compromised and avoided it. The designer could announce his plan as soon as it was done, but for the sake of the irrefutable texture of his columns, he maintained a quiet silence. The designer could express his foresight and courage in a will or a book, but for the honor of his pillars, he no longer coveted the slightest glitz. The designer hid in the dust of history for his columns.这是一根有弹性的柱子。它的设计者把自己的性格赋予了它，于是柱子比设计师活得更长久。

  This is a resilient post. Its designer gave it its own character, so the pillar lived longer than the designer.
