  06.失却四肢的泳者

  06. A swimmer who has lost his limbs

  一位外国女孩给我讲了这样一个故事。

  A foreign girl told me such a story.举办残障人运动会，报名的时候，来了一个失却双腿的人，说，我要参加游泳比赛。登记小姐很小心地询问，您在水里将怎样游呢？失却双腿的人说，我会用双手游泳。

  When I signed up for the Paralympic Games, a person who lost his legs came and said, "I want to participate in a swimming competition." The registration lady carefully asked, how will you swim in the water? The person who lost his legs said, I can swim with my hands.又来了一个失却双臂的人，也要报名参加游泳比赛。小姐问，您将如何游呢？失却双臂的人说，我会用双脚游泳。

  Another person who lost his arm also had to sign up for a swimming competition. The lady asked, how will you swim? The person who lost his arms said, "I can swim with my feet."小姐刚给他们登记完，又来了一个既没有双腿也没有双臂，也就是说，整个失却四肢的人，也要报名参加游泳比赛。小姐竭力保持镇静，小声问，您将怎样游泳？那人笑嘻嘻地答道：我将用耳朵游泳。

  As soon as the young lady finished registering them, another person came who had neither legs nor arms, that is, the entire person who had lost his limbs also had to sign up for the swimming competition. The young lady tried her best to stay calm and whispered, "How will you swim?" The man replied with a smile: I will swim with my ears.他失却四肢的躯体好似圆滚滚的梭。由于长久的努力，他的耳朵硕大而强健，能十分灵活地扑动向前。下水试游，如同一枚鱼雷出舱，速度比常人还快。于是，知道底细的人们暗暗传说，一个伟大的世界纪录即将诞生。

  His body was like a round shuttle. As a result of long-term efforts, his ears are large and strong, and he can flutter forward very flexibly. Launching into the water for a test swim, it is like a torpedo out of the cabin, faster than ordinary people. As a result, people who know the details secretly legend that a great world record is about to be born.正式比赛那天，人山人海。当失却四肢的人出现在跳台上的时候，简直山呼海啸。发令枪响了，运动员扑通扑通入水。一道道白箭推进，浪花迸溅，竟令人一时看不清英雄的所在。比赛的结果出来了，冠军是失却双臂的人，亚军是失却双腿的人，季军是……

  On the day of the official competition, there were crowds. When the person who lost his limbs appeared on the jumping platform, it was like a tsunami. The starting gun sounded, and the athlete plopped into the water. White arrows advanced one after another, and the waves splashed, making it impossible to see where the hero was for a while. The results of the competition are out, the champion is the one who has lost both arms, the runner-up is the one who has lost his legs, and the third place is ......英雄呢？没有人看到英雄在哪里，起码是在终点线的附近找不着英雄独特的身姿。真奇怪，大家分明看到失却四肢的游泳者跳进水里了啊！

  What about the heroes? No one saw where the hero was, at least not near the finish line. It's really strange, everyone clearly saw the swimmer who lost his limbs jumped into the water!于是更多的人开始寻找，终于在起点附近摸到了英雄。他沉入水底，已经淹死了。在他的头上，戴着一顶鲜艳的游泳帽，遮住了耳朵。那是根据泳场规则，在比赛前由一位美丽的姑娘给他戴上的。

  So more people began to look for it, and finally found the hero near the starting point. He sank to the bottom and was already drowning. On his head, he wears a bright swimming cap that covers his ears. It was put on by a beautiful girl before the competition, according to the rules of the swimming pool.我曾把这故事讲给旁人听。听完之后的反应，形形色色。

  I once told this story to others. The reaction after listening was varied.有人说，那是一个阴谋。可能是哪个想夺冠军的人出的损招，扼杀别人才能保住自己。

  Some people say that it was a conspiracy. It may be a loss move made by someone who wants to win the championship, and only by strangling others can he save himself.有人说，那个来送泳帽的人，如果不是一个漂亮的女孩子就好了，泳者就不会神魂颠倒。就算全世界的人都忘记了他的耳朵的功能，他也会保持清醒，拒绝戴那顶美丽却杀人的帽子。

  Some people say that the person who came to deliver the swimming cap would not have been fascinated if it weren't for a beautiful girl. Even if people all over the world forget the function of his ears, he will remain awake and refuse to wear that beautiful but murderous hat.有人说，既然没了手和脚，就该安守本分，游什么泳呢？要知道水火无情，孤注一掷的时候，风险随时会将你吞没。

  Some people say that since you have no hands and feet, you should keep your duty and swim? You must know that water and fire are ruthless, and when you are desperate, risk will engulf you at any time.有人说，为什么要有这么个混账的规则，游泳帽有什么作用？各行各业都有这种教条的规矩，不知害了多少人才，种种陋习何时才会终结？

  Some people say, why is there such a bastard rule, what is the function of a swimming cap? All walks of life have this dogmatic rule, I don't know how many talents have been harmed, and when will all kinds of bad habits end?我把这些议论告诉女孩。她说，干吗都是负面？这是一个笑话啊，虽然有一点深沉。当我们完整的时候，奋斗比较容易。当我们没有手的时候，我们可以用脚奋斗。当我们没有脚的时候，我们可以用手奋斗。当我们手和脚都没有的时候，我们可以用耳朵奋斗。

  I told the girl about these discussions. She said, why are they all negative? It's a joke, albeit a little deep. When we are whole, it is easier to struggle. When we don't have hands, we can fight with our feet. When we don't have feet, we can fight with our hands. When we have no hands and feet, we can fight with our ears.但是，即使在这时，我们依然有失败甚至完全毁灭的可能。很多英雄，在战胜了常人难以想象的艰难困苦后并没有得到最后的成功。

  But even then, we still have the possibility of failure or even complete destruction. Many heroes did not achieve final success after overcoming unimaginable hardships and hardships for ordinary people.凶手正是自己的耳朵——你最值得骄傲的本领。

  The murderer is your own ears - your proudest skill.

未知

  07.断臂的姐姐

  07. The sister with a broken arm

  我有一个妹妹，比我年轻（这是废话啦），聪慧机警。她在北大读完计算机专业，到一家工厂当工程师。多年来，她一直是我的作品的忠实读者，经常提出一些很尖锐、很中肯的意见，使我受益匪浅。

  I have a younger sister who is younger than me (this is nonsense) and is intelligent and alert. She graduated from Peking University with a computer major and worked as an engineer in a factory. She has been a loyal reader of my work for many years, often giving some very sharp and pertinent opinions that have benefited me a lot.原以为我俩一文一理，是两股道上跑的车，绝无聚头的日子。不想随着国门打开，洋货涌入，国产计算机的局势日见危急，妹妹所在的工厂濒临倒闭，最后竟到了只发微薄的生活费的境地。

  I thought that the two of us were the same thing, and we were two cars running on the road, and there would never be a day when we got together. I don't want to see the opening of the country, the influx of foreign goods, the situation of domestic computers is becoming more and more critical, and my sister's factory is on the verge of closure, and finally it has reached a situation where only meager living expenses are paid.一日，老母对我说，看你写些小文章，经常有淡绿色的汇款单寄来，虽说仨瓜俩枣的，管不了什么大事，终是可以让你贴补些家用，给孩子买只烧鸡的时候，手不至于哆嗦得太甚。你既有了这个本事，何不教你亲妹妹两招。她反正也闲得无事，试着写写，万一高中了，岂不也宽裕些？

  One day, my mother said to me, watching you write some small articles, there are often pale green remittance slips, although it is three melons and two dates, you can't control any major things, but in the end you can make up for some household expenses, and when you buy a roast chicken for your children, your hands will not tremble too much. Since you have this ability, why don't you teach your sister two tricks. She is idle anyway, try to write, in case she is in high school, wouldn't she be more generous?母亲这样一说，倒让我很不好意思起来，好像长久以来自己私藏了一件祖传的宝贝，只顾独享，怠慢了一奶同胞的妹妹。

  When my mother said this, I was very embarrassed, as if I had hidden an ancestral treasure for a long time, only focusing on it and neglecting my sister.我支吾着说，世界级的大文豪海明威先生说过，写作这种才能，是几百万人当中才能摊上一份的，不是谁想写都能写的。

  I hesitated to say that Mr. Hemingway, a world-class writer, said that the talent of writing can only be shared among millions of people, not anyone who wants to write can write.老母撇撇嘴说，她与你同父同母，我就不信只有你能写，她就写不得！

  My mother curled her lips and said, she is the same father and mother as you, I don't believe that only you can write, she can't write!话说到这份儿上，我只有对妹妹说，你写一篇，拿来给我看看。

  Speaking of this, I can only say to my sister, you write one and show it to me.妹妹很为难地说，写什么呢？我又不像你，到过人迹罕至的西藏。我是生在北京，长在北京，最远的旅行就是到了北大的未名湖畔。这样简单的人生经历，写出的文章，只怕小孩子都不会看的。

  My sister was very embarrassed and said, what should I write? I'm not like you, I've been to Tibet, which is inaccessible. I was born in Beijing, grew up in Beijing, and the farthest trip was to the unnamed lake of Peking University. Such a simple life experience, the articles written, I am afraid that children will not read them.我说，先不要想那么多吧。你就从你最熟悉最喜欢的事情写起，不要有任何顾虑和框框。写的时候也不要回头看，写作就像走夜路，一回头就会看到鬼影，失了写下去的勇气。你只管一门心思地写，一切等你写出来再说。

  I said, don't think so much yet. You start with what you are most familiar with and like, without any worries or frames. Don't look back when writing, writing is like walking at night, and when you look back, you will see ghosts and lose the courage to write. You just write with all your heart, and everything will be told when you write it.

  妹妹听完我的话，就回她自己的家去了。其后的很长一段时间无声无息。当我几乎把这件事忘记的时候，她很腼腆地交给我几张纸，说是小说稿写完了，请我指正。

  After listening to my words, my sister went back to her own home. It was silent for a long time afterwards. When I almost forgot about it, she shyly handed me a few pieces of paper, saying that the novel was finished and asked me to correct it.我拿着那几张纸，翻来覆去地看了好几遍，好像是在研究这纸是什么材料制成的。我知道妹妹很紧张地注视着我，等待着我的裁决。我故意把这段时间拉得很长——不是要折磨她，是在反复推敲自己的结论是否公正。

  I took those pieces of paper and looked at them over and over several times, as if I was studying what material the paper was made of. I know my sister is watching me nervously, waiting for my verdict. I deliberately lengthened this period - not to torture her, but to repeatedly deliberate on whether my conclusions were fair.我慢吞吞地说，你的文章，我看完了。我在这里看到了许多不成熟和粗疏的地方，但是，我要坦率地说，你的文字里面蕴含着一种才能……

  I said slowly, I've finished reading your article. I see a lot of immaturity and sloppy here, but I want to be honest that there is a talent in your words......妹妹吃惊地说，你不是骗我吧？不是故意在鼓励我吧？这是真的吗？我真的可以写一点东西吗？

  My sister was surprised and said, you didn't lie to me, did you? Isn't he trying to encourage me? Is this true? Can I really write a little thing?我说，我有什么必要骗你呢？写作是一件很辛苦的事情，说真的，我真不愿你加入这个行列，它比你做电脑工程师的成功概率要低得多。但是，如果你喜欢，可以一试，李白说过，天生我材必有用。如果你爱好用笔来传达你对人世间的感慨，就沿着这条路走下去好了。

  I said, why should I lie to you? Writing is a hard job, and seriously, I really don't want you to join the ranks, it's much lower than your chances of success as a computer engineer. However, if you like it, you can give it a try, Li Bai said, I am born to be useful. If you like to use a pen to convey your feelings about the world, just go down this path.妹妹的脸红起来，说，姐姐，我愿一试。

  My sister blushed and said, sister, I would like to try.我说，那好吧，回去再写十篇来。

  I said, okay, go back and write ten more articles.用了大约一年时间，妹妹的十篇文章才写好。我一次都没有催过她。我固执地认为，一个人如果真正热爱一个行当，不用人催，他也会努力的。若是不热爱，催也无用。

  It took about a year for my sister's ten articles to be written. I never urged her. I stubbornly believe that if a person really loves a profession and doesn't need to be urged, he will work hard. If you don't love it, it's useless to urge it.当我看到厚厚一沓用计算机打得眉清目秀的稿子时，知道妹妹下了大功夫。读稿的时候，我紧张地控制着表情肌，什么神态也不显露出来。看过之后，把稿子随手递还。

  When I saw a thick stack of manuscripts typed on a computer, I knew that my sister had put in a lot of effort. When I read the manuscript, I nervously controlled my facial muscles and didn't show any expression. After reading it, return the manuscript casually.怎么样呢？她焦灼地问。

  How? She asked anxiously.我淡淡地说，还好，起码比我想象的要好得多。有几篇甚至可以说是很不错的了。

  I said lightly, fortunately, at least it was much better than I thought. There are a few that can even be said to be very good.妹妹很明显地松了一口气，说，这下我就放心了。把稿件又塞给我。

  My sister was obviously relieved and said, now I am relieved. Stuff the manuscript into me again.想干什么？我陡然变色。

  What do you want to do? I suddenly changed color.妹妹说，我写好了，属于我的事就干完了，剩下的活儿就是你的了。你在文学界有那么多的朋友，帮我转一下稿子，该是轻而易举的啊。

  My sister said, I have written it, and my work will be done, and the rest will be yours. You have so many friends in the literary world, it should be easy to help me transfer the manuscript.我说，是啊是啊，举手之劳。但是，我不能给你做这件事。

  I said, yes, yes, thank you for raising your hand. However, I can't do this for you.

  在旁侧耳细听的老母搭了腔，你平常不是经常给素不相识的文学青年转稿子吗，怎么到了自己的亲妹妹头上反倒这样推三阻四？

  The old mother who was listening carefully on the side spoke, don't you usually transfer manuscripts to literary young people you don't know, why do you push it like this when it comes to your own sister?我把手压在妹妹的文稿之上，对她说，转稿子是很容易的事情，只是我想让你经历一个文学青年应该走的全部磨炼过程。正是因为你不仅仅是为了发一篇稿子，你是为了热爱，把写作当作终生喜爱的事业来看待的，所以我更不能帮你这个忙。为你转了稿，其实是害了你。经了我的手，你的稿子发了，你就弄不清到底是自己已到了能发表的水平还是沾了姐姐的光。况且我能帮你发一篇，我不能帮你发所有的篇目。就算我有力量帮你发了所有的作品，那究竟是你的能力还是我的能力呢？一个有志气的人，应该一针一线、一砖一瓦都由自己独立完成。

  I put my hand on my sister's manuscript and told her that it was easy to transfer the manuscript, but I wanted you to go through all the tempering processes that a literary youth should go through. It is precisely because you are not just for publishing a manuscript, you are for love, and you regard writing as a lifelong love, so I can't help you with this. Transferring the manuscript for you is actually harming you. After my hand, your manuscript is published, and you can't figure out whether you have reached the level of publication or if you are stained with your sister's light. Besides, I can help you post one, I can't help you post all the articles. Even if I have the strength to help you post all the works, is it your ability or mine? An ambitious person should complete every stitch and thread, brick by brick by himself.妹妹沉思良久后说，姐姐，这么说，你是不愿帮我的忙了？

  After thinking for a long time, my sister said, sister, if you say so, you don't want to help me?我说，妹妹，姐姐愿意帮你。只是如何帮法，要依我的主意。在这件事上，请你原谅，姐姐只肯出脑，不肯出手。我可以用嘴指出你的作品有何不足，但我不会伸出一根手指接触你的稿子。

  I said, sister, sister is willing to help you. It's just that how to help the law depends on my ideas. In this matter, please forgive me, my sister is only willing to think out and refuse to take action. I can point out what is lacking in your work, but I won't stick out a finger to touch your manuscript.老母在一旁说，是不是因你当初是单枪匹马走上文坛的，今天对自己的妹妹才这般冷面无情？

  My mother said on the side, is it because you entered the literary world single-handedly that you are so cold and ruthless to your sister today?我说，妈妈，我至今感谢你和父亲在文化圈子里没有一个熟人，感谢我写第一篇作品时的举目无亲。它激我努力，逼我向前。我不能因自己干了这一行，就剥夺了妹妹从零开始的努力过程。这对于一个作家是太重要的锻炼，犹如一个婴儿是吃母乳还是喝苞谷糊糊长大，体质绝不相同。

  I said, Mom, I am grateful that you and my father do not have a single acquaintance in the cultural circle, and thank you for not having a relative when I wrote my first work. It motivates me to work hard and pushes me forward. I can't deprive my sister of the process of working hard from scratch just because I did this business myself. This is too important an exercise for a writer, just like whether a baby grows up eating breast milk or drinking bud grains, the physique is by no means the same.妹妹说，姐姐于我，要做西西里岛上出土的维纳斯，不肯伸出双臂。

  My sister said, "My sister wants to be the Venus unearthed in Sicily, and she refuses to stretch out her arms."我说，错。维纳斯的胳膊是别人给她折断的，欲补不能。我是王佐，自断一臂。

  I said, wrong. Venus's arm was broken by someone else, and she couldn't make up for it. I am Wang Zuo, and I cut off an arm.妹妹说，我懂了。

  My sister said, I understand.在其后又是将近一年的时光中，妹妹像没头苍蝇似的，为她的文稿寻找编辑部。我在一旁冷眼旁观，这中间我有无数次机会举荐她的稿子，但我时时同自己想要帮她一把的念头，做着不懈的斗争。我替毫不相干的青年转稿子，殷勤地向编辑询问他们稿子的下落，竭尽全力地为他们的作品说好话……但我信守诺言，没有一个字提及妹妹的作品。

  For nearly a year after that, my sister looked for the editorial office for her manuscript like a headless fly. I watched coldly, and I had countless opportunities to recommend her manuscript, but I always fought unremittingly against my desire to help her. I transferred manuscripts for irrelevant young people, diligently asked the editor about the whereabouts of their manuscripts, and tried my best to say good things about their works...... But I kept my promise and didn't mention my sister's work in a single word.妹妹在图书馆找到各种编辑部的地址，忐忑不安地寄出她的稿子，然后是夜不能寐的、漫长焦灼的等待……终于，她的十篇文稿全部投中，在各种刊物上发表了。

  My sister found the addresses of various editorial offices in the library, sent her manuscript uneasily, and then waited a long and anxious wait that kept her awake at night...... Finally, all ten of her manuscripts were submitted and published in various publications.居然无一退稿！而且这都是我自己奋斗来的啊！妹妹喜极而泣，自信心空前地加强了。

  None of them were rejected! And this is all my own struggle! My sister cried with joy, and her self-confidence was strengthened like never before.老母对我说，想不到你这招儿居然很灵，只是为一服虎狼之药，药性凶猛了些。

  My mother said to me, I didn't expect that your trick was actually very effective, just to take the medicine of tigers and wolves, the medicinal properties were more ferocious.我说，哪里是什么虎狼之药，不过是平常人的正常遭遇罢了。我们现在凡做一事，总是先想到认识什么人，试图依靠他人的力量。其实，这世上最值得信赖的人正是你自己。尤其是那种成功概率比较低的事，更要凭自己的双手去做，以积累经验。过程掺了水分，不如不做。

  I said, where is the medicine of tigers and wolves, but it is just a normal experience for ordinary people. Whenever we do anything now, we always think of who we know and try to rely on the power of others. In fact, the most trustworthy person in this world is yourself. Especially for things with a low probability of success, you have to do it with your own hands to accumulate experience. The process is mixed with water, so it is better not to do it.老母笑吟吟地说，现如今两个女儿的文字都可换回些柴米油盐酱醋茶钱，喜煞人也。

  The mother said with a smile that now the words of the two daughters can be exchanged for some firewood, rice, oil, salt, sauce, vinegar, tea money, which is also very pleasant.我拉着妹妹的手说，革命尚未成功，你我仍须努力啊。

  I took my sister's hand and said, the revolution has not yet succeeded, you and I still have to work hard.

未知

  08.为富人担保的穷人

  08. The poor who guarantee the rich

  南方的一座小城。

  A small city in the south.瘦弱的女人，大约四十岁，朴素到陈旧的装束，使人很难把她和推销信用卡这种新潮行当联系起来。她业绩显赫，在很短的时间内就推销了上千张，每张卡一千元开户，且全部是个人购买。

  The thin woman, about forty years old, was plain to the point of old-fashioned attire, making it difficult to associate her with the trendy business of selling credit cards. She has a remarkable performance, selling thousands of cards in a short period of time, opening an account for 1,000 yuan per card, and all of them are purchased by individuals.你干得很出色啊，我说。

  You did a great job, I said.人到了没办法的时候，就给逼出主意来了……我原在工厂做工，后来不景气……银行给了我一大沓表，每张表相当于一张卡。要是我不能把这些卡推销出去，今后的工作就很难说……她淡淡地讲着，表情也像一张新的信用卡，平和而干净。

  When people have no choice, they force out ideas...... I used to work in a factory, but then it was sluggish...... The bank gave me a large stack of tables, each equivalent to a card. If I can't sell these cards, it's hard to say what I'm going to do in the future...... She spoke lightly, her expression like a new credit card, peaceful and clean.你的第一张卡卖给谁了呢？是不是很难？我小心地问，万事开头难，以她的年纪、长相和性格，第一步肯定布满荆棘。

  Who did you sell your first card to? Isn't it difficult? I asked carefully, everything is difficult at the beginning, with her age, appearance and personality, the first step must be full of thorns.没想到她笑起来，说，第一张是一点也不难的，我一下子就卖出去了。我自己买了我的第一张卡。虽然没那么多闲钱，但我得先学会用卡，要是我都稀里糊涂的，还能指望别人买卡吗？

  Unexpectedly, she laughed and said, the first one is not difficult at all, and I sold it all at once. I bought my first card myself. Although I don't have that much spare money, I have to learn to use the card first, if I am confused, can I still expect others to buy the card?说着，她从衣袋里掏出一个男用钱夹，里头小格子很多，好像盛不了多少钱，是人造革的。她一边说一边很亲切地抚摸着皮夹，好像它是一只有呼吸的小动物。它是我工作的帮手，用个文明词，就是道具。

  As she spoke, she took out a men's wallet from her pocket, which had a lot of small lattices in it, as if it couldn't hold much money, and it was leatherette. As she spoke, she stroked her wallet affectionately, as if it were a breathing little animal. It is a helper in my work, and to use a civilized word, it is a prop.她接着讲述卖第二张卡的经历。

  She went on to talk about her experience selling a second card.那是一户大款。我把来意说了，他连头也不抬地说，你给我出去，我不要什么卡。积我多少年的经验，没有什么比现钱更好使的东西了。

  That's a big deal. I told him what I meant, and he said without even raising his head, you go out for me, I don't want any cards. With so many years of experience, there is nothing better than cash.我说，您的生意越做越大，钱会越聚越多。总有一天，您带的现钱会使您走不动路。

  I said, the bigger your business gets, the more money you will accumulate. One day, the cash you bring will make you unable to walk.他把头抬起来一字一顿地说，那我会雇人给我扛着钱。

  He raised his head and said word by word, then I will hire someone to carry the money for me.我说，积我多少年的经验，钱放在谁的口袋里，也不如放在自己兜里保险。我请您看看这个国外最新式样的钱夹。

  I said, with how many years of experience I have, it is better to put the money in my own pocket than to put it in my own pocket. I invite you to take a look at this latest foreign style wallet.他注意地盯着我说，你是推销钱夹还是推销信用卡的？

  He stared at me attentively and said, are you selling wallets or credit cards?我不理他，把小格子一个个展示给他看。然后说，这些地方都是装信用卡的，真正有钱的人，随身带的是卡而不是钱。当然他们有时也带一点零钱，那是为了付小费和施舍乞丐。用卡是一种文明的方式，真正的大亨是不屑于用大拇指数钱的，他们只用食指把卡推过去。假如您到大饭店请朋友吃饭，当着客人的面数一大沓旧钞票，是一件煞风景的事。别的不说，就说钞票不干净，上面带着数不清的病菌，您跟别人握手，人家也许会嫌您脏……

  I ignored him and showed him the small grids one by one. Then he said that these places are all equipped with credit cards, and people who are really rich carry cards instead of money with them. Of course, they sometimes bring a little change, which is to tip and give alms to beggars. Using cards is a civilized way, and real tycoons disdain to count money with their thumbs, they only push the card with their index finger. If you go to a hotel to invite friends to dinner, it is a bad thing to count a large stack of old banknotes in front of the guests. If nothing else, just say that the banknotes are not clean, with countless germs on them, and if you shake hands with others, people may think you are dirty......他突然打断我的话，说，你还有完没完了？咒我啊？我买你的卡就是了！

  He suddenly interrupted me and said, are you still finished? Are you cursing me? I'll just buy your card!说完这段经历，她又安静地沉默下来。

  After talking about this experience, she fell silent again.我说，你对大款倒真是不卑不亢。但世上的大款终究是少数，对工薪阶层你说什么呢？

  I said, you are really not humble or arrogant about big money. But the big money in the world is a minority after all, what do you say to the working class?女人说，我对那些常常出差的人讲，你们出门在外，最怕的不就是丢钱吗？办事且不说，单是……咱们都这么大岁数了，我也不怕难为情，街上卖的带拉锁的裤头就是干这个使的。可夏天您就不出汗了？出了汗，您就不洗内衣了？那点钱像耗子搬家似的藏来藏去，烦不烦人？要是遇着临时用钱的事，你还得上厕所掏钱，着急不着急？

  The woman said, I tell those who often travel on business, isn't the most feared thing you are most afraid of when you go out? Don't talk about doing things, just ...... We are all so old, and I am not afraid of embarrassment, the trousers with zippers sold on the street are for this. But you don't sweat in summer? If you sweat, you won't wash your underwear? Is it annoying to hide that little money around like a mouse moving? If you encounter a temporary use of money, you have to go to the toilet to pay for it, are you in a hurry?听你这么一说，他们一定乐意买卡了。我说。

  Hearing you say this, they must be happy to buy a card. I say.也不一定。有的人是立马掏钱了。也有的人说，别把你的卡说得那么好，带钱会丢，带卡就不会丢了吗？小偷可不认这个理！

  Not necessarily. Some people paid for it immediately. Some people also say, don't say your card so well, you will lose it with money, but you won't lose it with your card? The thief doesn't admit this!那你怎么回答呢？我有些替她着急。

  So how do you answer? I'm a little anxious for her.我就说，使卡的时候，要和本人的身份证配合着用。您把卡装在左兜里，把身份证装在右兜里。单偷了卡，他也没法用。哪儿那么巧，小偷先掏了您左兜又掏您右兜，您就把卡和身份证一块儿都丢了呢？回来补个卡就是了，避免了损失。听我这么一说，那些出差的人就笑起来，说我们买你的卡了。

  I said that when using the card, it should be used in conjunction with my ID card. You put your card in your left pocket and your ID card in your right pocket. If he stole the card, he couldn't use it. Where is it so coincidental that the thief first took out your left pocket and then your right pocket, and you lost your card and ID card? Just come back and make up for the card to avoid losses. When I said this, those who were on business laughed and said we bought your card.

  我说，这座城市并不大，就算大款和出差的人都买了你的卡，离你要完成的数量也还差着呢。

  I said, the city is not big, even if the big money and business travelers buy your card, it is still far from the amount you want to complete.她叹了一口气说，是啊，我就是只有再想办法了。

  She sighed and said, yes, I just have to think of another way.比如碰到有人结婚，我就找到他的朋友们，说，你们正在想送给新人什么礼物吧？我给你们出个主意，凑钱送他们一张信用卡吧，这比送人家一大堆锅碗瓢盆、床单被面要好。一是小两口可以买自己心爱的东西，你们就尽了心意。二是什么时候人家用起这卡来，都会想起朋友的情意。哪怕卡上的钱用完了，他们自己往卡里续钱，也还记得你们开的这个头……

  For example, when someone gets married, I find his friends and say, what gift are you trying to give to the newlyweds? I'll give you an idea, pool money to give them a credit card, which is better than giving them a lot of pots and pans, sheets and quilts. First, the young couple can buy their favorite things, and you will do your best. Second, when people use this card, they will think of their friends' affection. Even if the money on the card runs out, they renew the money in the card themselves, and they still remember the head you opened......要是哪个单位效益好，预备表彰先进模范，我就去对领导说，给你们的先进人物送一张卡吧，这个礼物新颖……

  If any unit has good benefits and is ready to commend advanced models, I will go to the leaders and say, give a card to your advanced figures, this gift is novel......女人说，不过，一下子能奖励一千块钱的单位并不多。有的领导说，我们的胃口没有那么大。我就说，你只需奖二十块钱就行。轮到领导搞不懂了，我说，每张卡需要二十块钱的开户费，您把这个钱出了，剩下的钱由个人出，也可以啊。现在的二十块钱实在算不了什么，买只烧鸡还缺条腿呢。您要是单奖给谁二十块钱，保证拿不出手，可您奖了他这个开户的手续，礼轻情意重啊！中国人有时候挺怪的，心疼小钱，不心疼大钱。许多人不愿意让这个开户的资格作废，就买了卡……

  The woman said, however, there are not many units that can reward a thousand yuan at once. Some leaders say that our appetite is not so big. I said, you only need to give 20 yuan. When it was the leader's turn to understand, I said, each card needs an account opening fee of 20 yuan, you pay this money, and the rest of the money is paid by the individual, which is also acceptable. Twenty yuan now is really nothing, and I still lack a leg to buy a roast chicken. If you give a single prize of 20 yuan to someone, you guarantee that you won't be able to get it, but you reward him with this account opening procedure, which is light and affectionate! Chinese people are sometimes quite strange, they feel sorry for small money, not big money. Many people are unwilling to invalidate this qualification to open an account, so they buy a card......我说，看来你很顺利啊。

  I said, it seems that you are going well.她停顿了一下说，我光跟您说好的了，也有很为难的时候。

  She paused and said, I just told you that it was okay, but there were times when I was very embarrassed.我说，举一个你最为难的例子好吗？

  I said, can you give me a most difficult example?她低下头，短发遮没了半个脸庞，一种疲倦的沧桑浮上眼帘，眼角罩起银杏叶一般的纹路。

  She lowered her head, her short hair covered half of her face, a tired vicissitudes came to her eyes, and the corners of her eyes were covered with ginkgo biloba-like lines.

  有一次，我向一位总经理推销信用卡……她困难地说。

  Once, I pitched a credit card to a general manager...... She said with difficulty.无论在哪个部门，我都是从最大的头头开始推销。上行下效，中国人信这个。只要领导买了，底下的人就放心了，要不，你说破天，大家也不信你。

  No matter what department I am in, I start selling from the biggest beginning. Up and down, Chinese believe this. As long as the leader buys it, the people below will be relieved, otherwise, if you say it breaks the sky, everyone will not believe you.买卡是需要担保人的。也就是说，如果持卡人恶意透支的话，担保人要承担损失。因为银行是以天为单位结算的，如果有人在一天内快速支出巨额，不到晚上结算的时候，电脑是反映不出来的。假如这个人跑了，银行的损失就得找担保人了。

  A guarantor is required to buy a card. In other words, if the cardholder maliciously overdraws, the guarantor will bear the loss. Because banks settle on a daily basis, if someone spends a huge amount quickly in one day, the computer will not be able to reflect it until the evening settlement. If this person runs away, the bank's losses will have to find a guarantor.我请总经理代理单位为他的职员担保。

  I asked the general manager to act as an agent for his staff.他说，这年头，谁能担保得了谁呢？当父母的担保不了儿女，丈夫担保不了老婆。我不能为他们担保。

  He said, these days, who can guarantee whom? When parents can't guarantee their children, husbands can't guarantee their wives. I can't vouch for them.我说，如果你不能为所有的员工担保，只为您的高级职员担保吧。

  I said, if you can't vouch for all employees, only vouch for your senior staff.他说，那也不行，我信不过他们。

  He said, "That's not good, I can't trust them."我说，那您只为您的副总经理担保如何？

  I said, how about you only guarantee your deputy general manager?总经理说，除了我自己，我不相信这世界上的任何人。

  The general manager said that I don't trust anyone in this world except myself.我定定地看着他，一字一句地说，这个世界上总还是好人多。

  I looked at him steadily and said word by word that there are always many good people in this world.他似笑非笑地对我说，你真是这样认为吗？

  He said to me with a smile, do you really think so?我说，真的，我受过很多次骗，但我还是愿意相信别人。当我搞不清一个人该相信还是不该相信的时候，我只有相信他。

  I said, really, I've been deceived many times, but I'm still willing to trust others. When I can't figure out whether a person should believe or not, I can only believe him.他的脸色很难看，说，你的话算数吗？

  His face was ugly, and he said, do your words count?我说，我虽是一个女人，但比许多男人更守信用。

  I said, although I am a woman, I am more trustworthy than many men.他说，那好吧，我给你一个实践的机会。咱们两个素不相识，现在，你如果肯给我做担保人，我就买你的卡。而且，我为我的员工做担保。

  And he said, well, I'll give you a chance to practice. The two of us have never known each other, and now, if you are willing to be my guarantor, I will buy your card. Moreover, I vouch for my employees.我一下子愣在那里了。他是腰缠万贯的大富翁，我是一个下岗的女工。如果他要恶意透支，我就是砸锅卖铁也还不上他欠的钱啊！但我知道我不能退却，这已经不单是能不能推销出去一张卡的事情了，还关乎某种做人的原则。

  I was stunned there for a moment. He is a millionaire with a lot of money, and I am a laid-off female worker. If he wants to maliciously overdraw, I won't be able to pay back the money he owes even if I smash the pot and sell iron! But I know I can't retreat, it's not just about whether I can sell a card, it's about a certain principle of life.在他富丽堂皇的老板台前，我停顿了片刻。不是我迟疑，而是为了能让他更好地听清我的话。我再次一字一句地对他说——我是一个穷人，但我愿意为您担保。

  In front of his palatial boss, I paused for a moment. It's not that I'm hesitating, but that he can hear me better. I said to him word by word again - I am a poor man, but I am willing to vouch for you.女人说完这句话，久久地沉默了。

  After the woman said this, she was silent for a long time.我说，你以后害怕了吗？

  I said, are you afraid in the future?她说，是啊。不过，我不后悔。只是这事至今没跟我丈夫讲，他是个安分守己的人，若知道了，会吓得睡不着觉的。

  She said, yes. However, I don't regret it. It's just that I haven't told my husband about this so far, he is a person who keeps to himself, and if he finds out, he will be so scared that he can't sleep.

  停了许久，女人说，其实，这还不是最为难的。我最难过的是有一次碰到从前一起做工的姐妹。

  After a long pause, the woman said, in fact, this is not the most difficult. The saddest thing for me was that I once met a sister who used to work together.她们问我现在在做什么。我跟她们说起卡来，并不是要显摆什么，只是天天都和卡打交道，卡已经成了我生活中的一部分，不由自主地说起来了。单是说说卡是怎么回事也就罢了，千不该万不该，我不该在介绍完卡以后说了一句，卡有这么多的方便，你也买张卡吧……

  They asked me what I was doing. When I talk to them about cards, I don't mean to show anything, I just deal with cards every day, and cards have become a part of my life, and I can't help but talk about them. Just talk about what the card is about, it shouldn't be, I shouldn't have said after introducing the card, the card has so many conveniences, you can also buy a card......我真的不是故意要推销卡，只是一种习惯。

  I really didn't mean to sell the card, it was just a habit.以前的姐妹就说，你说的这个卡大概真是很好。我们信你的话。可对我们来说有什么用呢？能用这卡从自由市场买菜吗？能上小卖铺买酱油吗？能到煤店里买煤吗？能在学校里用它交孩子的学费吗……

  The previous sisters said, the card you said is probably really good. We believe your word. But what good is it for us? Can I use this card to buy groceries from the free market? Can I buy soy sauce at a small shop? Can you go to the coal store to buy coal? Can I use it to pay my child's tuition at school......我站在马路当央，一句话也说不出来……

  I stood on the road and couldn't say a word......推销卡的女人垂着眼帘，忽然，她睁开了眼睛，说，您知道什么样的人最不喜欢买卡了吗？

  The woman who was selling the card lowered her eyes, and suddenly, she opened her eyes and said, do you know what kind of people don't like to buy cards the most?我猜测着说，是收入比较窘迫的人吧？

  I guessed that he was a person with a more embarrassing income, right?她说，您错了。收入少的人不买卡，只是觉着卡和自己的关系不大，就像人们不关心火星上发生什么事一样。如果他们挣了钱，还是会买的。

  She said, you are wrong. People with low incomes don't buy cards, they just feel that cards have little to do with them, just like people don't care about what happens on Mars. If they make money, they will still buy it.最不喜欢买卡的人是那些有灰色收入的人，因为卡上的每一笔花费都有案可查，你什么时候在什么地点花了多少钱，电脑这只神眼都会忠实地记录下来。虽说是给用户保密，但心里有鬼的人不愿在世上留下任何痕迹，他们只愿意神不知鬼不觉地在暗处花钱。所以，有些人我是从来不动员他们买卡的。他们是卡的黑洞。

  The people who don't like to buy cards the most are those with gray income, because every expense on the card has a record, and the computer will faithfully record how much you spent when and where you spent. Although it is a secret for users, people with ghosts in their hearts do not want to leave any traces in the world, they are only willing to spend money in the shadows without knowing it. So, some people I never mobilize them to buy cards. They are the black holes of cards.她疲惫地一笑，说，卡是个新事物，是和世界接轨的东西。可有些简单的东西到了咱们这儿，就变得复杂起来。我现在只盼那个我做担保的富人，不要上了恶意透支的黑名单。

  She smiled tiredly and said that cards are a new thing, something that is in line with the world. But some simple things get complicated when they come to us. I only hope that the rich person I guarantee will not be blacklisted for malicious overdrafts.

未知

  09.保安是谁变的

  09. Who changed the security guard

  我跟保安的会面主要在小区的一出一进当中。看着那些年轻的面庞，我常常想，他们以前是做什么的呢？在进入城市之前，在穿上保安的制服之前，他们是什么人呢？蝴蝶是毛虫变的，蚊子是孑孓变的，青蛙是蝌蚪变的，保安是谁变的？

  My meetings with security guards were mainly in and out of the community. Looking at those young faces, I often wondered, what did they do before? Before entering the city, before putting on the uniform of the security guards, who are they? Butterflies are caterpillars, mosquitoes are mutated, frogs are tadpoles, and who is the security guard?和一些保安聊过天，他们都很谨慎，从不多说什么，至多只讲自己家在农村，上过或是初中或是小学，好像上过高中的不是很多，基本上是招工来的。用人单位的代表到了乡里，说有到北京干活儿的机会，需怎样的条件，谁愿意去？于是年轻人纷纷应征。有的是亲戚朋友介绍来的，滚雪球一般。总之，保安基本上来自农村。

  I talked to some security guards, they were very cautious, never said much, at most only said that their home was in the countryside, and they had gone to junior high school or elementary school, as if they had not been to high school much, and they were basically recruited. The representative of the employer went to the township and said that there was an opportunity to work in Beijing, what conditions were required, and who was willing to go? So young people applied one after another. Some were introduced by relatives and friends, and they snowballed. In short, security guards basically come from rural areas.一个农村的小伙子，冷不丁来到了繁华的大都市，他们的心地会发生怎样的变化呢？我没看到过针对保安的相关研究，设想一下，可能会有这样几种可能吧。

  How will a young man from the countryside suddenly come to a bustling metropolis, how will their hearts change? I haven't seen any research on security, but imagine that there may be several possibilities.一是惊讶。高楼大厦车水马龙，不夜的霓虹灯和袒胸露背的华衣……这些和寂静的山村简朴的民俗实在是天壤之别。人在震惊之后，很容易滋生出渺小和自卑的心理。能以平和之心对抗陌生的繁华，是一种再造的定力，而非人的本性轻易可以到达的高度。

  One is surprised. High-rise buildings are busy with neon lights and bare chests and backless clothes...... These are really different from the simple folklore of the silent mountain village. After people are shocked, it is easy to breed a sense of insignificance and inferiority. Being able to fight against the unfamiliar prosperity with a peaceful heart is a kind of reconstruction determination, not a height that human nature can easily reach.一天，我在西客站附近上了公共汽车，一位老者也上了车，因这周围有家医院，他佝偻着腰，几乎可以断定他有病。从他迅速扫视四周的眼神又可以觉出他的病并不是很重。他走到一位看守着行李的小伙子面前，很果断地说，你站起来。那个小伙子不知何故，带着乡下人的服从和退缩马上站了起来。老者很利落地坐在了小伙子的位置上，然后说，给老年人让座是应该的。进了城，以后学着点。那小伙子愣愣地、冷冷地站着，一言不发，让人无法猜测他的心事。

  One day, I got on the bus near the West Railway Station, and an old man also got on the bus, because there was a hospital around, and he was hunched over, and he could almost conclude that he was sick. From the way he quickly scanned his surroundings, he could tell that his illness was not very serious. He walked up to a young man guarding the luggage and said decisively, you stand up. The young man, somehow, immediately stood up with the obedience and retreat of a countryman. The old man sat neatly in the young man's seat, and then said that it was appropriate to give up his seat to the elderly. After entering the city, I will learn something in the future. The young man stood stunned and cold, without saying a word, making it impossible to guess what was on his mind.我看不过，就挤过去，对那位老人家说，他给你让座是应该的，可你也该说声谢谢啊，这也是应该的啊。说完之后，我就直勾勾地盯着他，表示自己的坚持。那位老人很不甘心，见周围也有人用目光支持我，才很不情愿地说了声，谢……

  I couldn't see it, so I squeezed over and said to the old man, he should give up his seat to you, but you should also say thank you, this is also what you should do. After saying that, I stared at him straight and expressed my persistence. The old man was very unwilling, and when he saw that there were people around him supporting me with their eyes, he said reluctantly, thank you......小伙子还是愣愣地站着，毫无表情。我不知道这个小伙子以后会不会变成保安，即使不是真正在册的正规保安，也许会摇身一变成了黑保安，看他那漠然的神情和高大的身板，这可能性还真不小。

  The young man still stood stunned, expressionless. I don't know if this young man will become a security guard in the future, even if he is not a real registered regular security guard, he may turn into a black security guard, looking at his indifferent expression and tall body, this possibility is really not small.曾经传得沸沸扬扬的“凶桥”的故事，说的是在北京健翔桥附近有一座过街天桥发生抢劫案，劫匪穷凶极恶，抢了钱还不说，临走时还在血肉模糊的事主腿上又刺了几刀，防着被害人爬起来追赶或报案。公安辛苦破案，最后查出凶手原来是附近灯具店的黑保安。

  The story of the "murderous bridge" that was once widely circulated tells of a robbery on a flyover near Jianxiang Bridge in Beijing, where the robbers were extremely vicious, not to mention robbing money, and stabbed the victim several more times in the leg of the bloody victim before leaving, preventing the victim from getting up to chase or report the case. The police worked hard to solve the case, and finally found out that the murderer turned out to be a black security guard from a nearby lamp store.又是保安！不管是黑是白，这几年，听到的保安打砸抢的案子，实在是不少了，还有屡屡发生的监守自盗。“保安”那在人们心中原本趋向暗淡的形象，如今干脆抹上了血痕。

  It's security again! Whether it is black or white, in recent years, I have heard a lot of cases of security guards smashing and robbing, as well as repeated self-thefts. The image of "security guard", which originally tended to be dull in people's minds, is now simply smeared with blood.扯远了，回到刚才的话题。于是在想，青年农民进了城，穿上保安的衣服，他们就真的变成了负有庄严使命的保安了吗？谁来帮助他们完成这个巨大的转变？不单是要训练他们走正步、敬礼和纠察、服从，更要教会他们敬业和尊重、忠诚和勇敢。

  Digression, back to the topic just now. So I was thinking, when young farmers entered the city and put on security guard clothes, did they really become security guards with solemn missions? Who will help them through this huge transformation? It is not only to train them to walk upright, salute and picket, and obey, but also to teach them dedication and respect, loyalty and bravery.

  比如那个被迫让座的小伙子，我敢说城市给他的第一课肯定是不愉快的。凭什么我的座位要让给你？凭什么你坐了我的座连个谢谢也不说？凭什么城里人就可以指令乡下人？

  For example, the young man who was forced to give up his seat, I dare say that the first lesson the city gave him must have been unpleasant. Why should I give up my seat to you? Why did you sit in my seat and not even say thank you? Why can city people command country people?我在报道中看到寻求保安杀人越货的动机时，总有一条是说这些年轻人一旦进入城市就会产生不平衡的心理，然后想找一条快速发财出人头地的路子。于是，抢劫偷盗就成了享乐的捷径。

  When I see in the report the motive of seeking security guards to kill people and override goods, there is always a saying that these young people will have an unbalanced psychology once they enter the city, and then want to find a way to get rich and get ahead quickly. As a result, robbery and theft became a shortcut to pleasure.原来那关键是不平衡。这就是变化的第二条。是啊，退一万步讲，同样是人，为什么你降生在城市，我就在农村？为什么你锦衣玉食，我却要风餐露宿？为什么你坐享其成，我却要白手起家？这一连串的问号如乌鸦盘旋在年轻的心的上空，如果没有疏导和讨论，那一时的偏颇就可能酿出滔天的惨祸。

  It turns out that the key is imbalance. This is the second point of change. Yes, taking 10,000 steps back, the same person, why were you born in the city, I am in the countryside? Why do you have fine clothes and food, but I have to sleep in the open? Why do you sit back and enjoy it, but I have to start from scratch? This series of question marks is like a crow hovering over the young heart, and if there is no guidance and discussion, that momentary bias may cause a monstrous disaster.不错，人间是有很多不平，这不平是与生俱来的，几乎是一种命定。我指出这一点，不是取消你的奋斗，而是请你的奋斗站在坚实的基础上。你不可以犯法，你不可以靠伤害他人以达到自己的目的。你不可以将道德和传统只维持在一个狭小的圈子里。比如在你的小村庄，你知情达理，是个好孩子，一旦到了城市的汪洋大海，你觉得什么人都不认识你了，你不必为口碑负责，你就可以空前地放肆起来。

  Yes, there are many injustices in the world, and this injustice is innate, almost a kind of fate. I point out that I do not cancel your struggle, but ask your struggle to stand on a solid foundation. You can't break the law, you can't hurt others to achieve your own goals. You cannot keep morality and tradition in a narrow circle. For example, in your small village, you are knowledgeable and a good boy, once you arrive at the vast sea of the city, you feel that no one knows you, you don't have to be responsible for word of mouth, you can be unpresumptuous.我认识一个乡村的女孩，她品行方正。到了城市不久，她就觉得当保姆挣钱太辛苦、太慢了，她要去当小姐。我说，你知道这小姐不是戏文里知书达理带着丫鬟的小姐，是有很多下流的东西在里面的。女孩说，阿姨，我都知道。可这又怎么样？就是下流了，也没有一个人认识我。我回家去，照样是一流的，起码也是个中上流吧。

  I know a girl from the countryside who has a good character. Soon after arriving in the city, she felt that being a nanny was too hard and too slow to earn money, and she was going to be a young lady. I said, you know that this young lady is not a well-educated young lady with a maid in the play, there are a lot of obscene things in it. The girl said, Auntie, I know everything. But so what? Even if it's obscene, no one knows me. When I go home, it's still first-class, at least it's an upper-middle class.我无言。淳朴的乡村以古老的方式约束之下的道德，一旦脱离了那个环境，就变得如同出土的丝绸，在一个极短暂的时间还能保持着绚烂，然而很快就褪色而灰飞烟灭了。因此，我对那些沙哑着嗓子颂扬乡村的歌唱始终心存疑虑，怕那只是理想中的眷恋，而非真正意义上的向往。

  I am speechless. The morality of the simple countryside under the constraints of the ancient way, once separated from that environment, becomes like unearthed silk, which can remain brilliant in a very short period of time, but soon fades and disappears. Therefore, I have always been suspicious of those songs that praise the countryside in a hoarse voice, fearing that it is just an ideal attachment, not a true yearning.一日，我和朋友约了在街头见面，为了醒目，地点就选在了一家银行大厦的前面。随着年龄渐长，我越来越像个没出过门的老太太，凡是同人约定的事，总要早早地上路，生怕晚了。北京这地方，堵起车来，谁都没有办法，无论你打出多少时间的富余，还是要迟到。若是顺利了，简直就提前到不可思议的地步。那一天，恰好是后面一种情况。我百无聊赖地流连在碧绿色的玻璃幕墙边，像个准备打劫银行的匪徒踱来踱去，这毫无疑问引来了一位年轻保安的询问。我如实禀告。他笑起来说，你和我妈有点像，她要是哪天出门，早早地就上路了，有时会提前好几个钟点就到了。

  One day, my friend and I made an appointment to meet on the street, and in order to stand out, the location was chosen in front of a bank building. As I get older, I become more and more like an old lady who has never been out, and I always have to go on the road early for fear of being late. In Beijing, there is no way to get stuck in traffic, no matter how much time you spend, you still have to be late. If it goes well, it will be unbelievably advanced. That day happened to be the latter situation. I lingered boredly by the turquoise glass curtain wall, pacing around like a bandit preparing to rob a bank, which undoubtedly attracted a young security guard's question. I will tell you the truth. He laughed and said, you are a bit like my mother, if she goes out one day, she will be on the road early, sometimes several hours early.

  我问，你妈妈现在在哪里呢？

  I asked, where is your mother now?聊天就这样开始了。他告诉我，他来自陕西的一个小村子，说他老妈以前是从来不看《新闻联播》的，因为那正是家中刷锅洗碗、喂猪的时间，老妈在灶台边忙得昏天黑地。可自从儿子到北京当了保安，老妈就雷打不动地开始看新闻了。老爹说，你不就着灶膛还是温和的，把猪食熬了，还关心什么国家大事？这都是老爷们儿的事，和你无关。老妈坐在小凳子上一动不动看着屏幕，说，我不是关心国家大事，我是关心我的儿子。他在北京做保安，新闻里播北京的事多，也许我会看到儿子。老爸说，哪儿有那么巧？就是有了，我叫你就是，你该干什么就干什么吧。老妈说，电视上如果有了儿子的影儿，那也是领导坐着车从他站岗的地方一晃就过去了，哪里等得及你叫我？还是我自己守着吧。

  The chat began. He told me that he was from a small village in Shaanxi and said that his mother had never watched "News Network" before, because it was the time at home to wash pots and dishes and feed pigs, and my mother was busy by the stove in the dark. But since her son went to Beijing to become a security guard, my mother began to watch the news without moving. Dad said, don't you just be gentle on the stove, boil the pig food, and care about what national affairs do you care about? This is all about the old men, and it has nothing to do with you. My mother sat motionless on a small stool and looked at the screen, saying, I don't care about national affairs, I care about my son. He works as a security guard in Beijing, and there are many things in Beijing on the news, maybe I will see my son. Dad said, where is such a coincidence? There is, I call you that, you can do whatever you need to do. My mother said that if there is a shadow of her son on TV, it is also the leader who passed by in a car from where he stood guard. I'll keep it myself."小伙子告诉我，他的父母就这样一直守着电视机，等着他在屏幕上以一个保安的姿态出现。他告诉过他们，自己穿上保安的衣服威风凛凛。

  The young man told me that his parents had been guarding the TV, waiting for him to appear on the screen as a security guard. He told them that he was majestic in the clothes of a security guard.其实保安这一行是很无聊的，天天守着一个地方。最初的新鲜劲儿过去之后，再好的风景也会看腻。以后年岁大了，不能老做保安啊，要有一技之长啊。可我的一技之长在哪里？雇你的人是不会想这些的，可你自己会想，几乎每天都在想，但光想又有什么用呢？要有行动。可我的行动目标在哪里呢？不知道。

  In fact, the security guard line is very boring, guarding a place every day. After the initial freshness passes, no matter how good the scenery is, you will get tired of it. When you get older, you can't be a security guard all the time, you have to have a skill. But where is my skill? The person who hires you won't think about this, but you will think about it yourself, almost every day, but what's the use of just thinking about it? Take action. But where is my goal? I don't know.我看到面前的小伙子眼神里露出散淡的光，完全没有焦点。正在这时，我的朋友来了，我就离开了这位年轻的保安，但他的目光让我久久难忘。我想，这就是保安进入城市之后面临的第三个挑战了，那就是——茫然。

  I saw a faint light in the eyes of the young man in front of me, completely out of focus. At this moment, my friend came, and I left the young security guard, but his gaze made me forget it for a long time. I think this is the third challenge that security guards face after entering the city, that is, they are at a loss.惊讶、不平衡和茫然，这三点变化带来的震动，其实也完全能从正面来解读。人为什么会惊奇？是因为我们离开了熟悉的环境，面临未曾有过的机遇和多种崭新的可能性。人为什么会不平衡？因为早先的稳定被打破了，一种变化的种子已经悄然发芽。当然了，不是每一粒种子都能开花，但播下种子就比荒芜的旷野强过百倍。面对不平衡，不怨天尤人，不妄自菲薄，沉下心来，细分短长，以自身的努力来补上命运的差异。至于说到茫然，我甚至以为，适当的茫然不单是一种可以接纳的阶段，而且几乎是年轻人的特权。你有权茫然，但是不可以茫然太久，太久的茫然就是思索的懒惰和行动的放弃。茫然的前提是要有向前一步寻找的动力，你须把茫然化作一种探寻的勇敢。茫然如同糯米，只有开阔的视野和不懈的学习，才能如同适宜的温度，将茫然的酒曲发酵成醇厚的甜酒。

  Surprise, imbalance and confusion, the shock brought about by these three changes can actually be interpreted from the front. Why are people surprised? It is because we are away from the familiar environment and face unprecedented opportunities and a variety of new possibilities. Why are people unbalanced? Because the earlier stability was broken, the seeds of change have quietly sprouted. Of course, not every seed can bloom, but sowing seeds is more than a hundred times better than a barren wilderness. In the face of imbalance, do not complain, do not belittle yourself, calm down, subdivide the short and long, and make up for the difference in fate with your own efforts. As for dazedness, I even think that appropriate dazedness is not only an acceptable stage, but also almost a privilege of young people. You have the right to be dazed, but you can't be at a loss for too long, too long is the laziness of thinking and the abandonment of action. The premise of being at a loss is that you must have the motivation to take a step forward and search, and you must turn the dazedness into a kind of courage to explore. Only a broad vision and unremitting learning can ferment the dazed koji into mellow sweet wine like the right temperature.你很难想象，在当今城市中有一位白发苍苍的保安在执行任务，保安已经从传统的打更老大爷或高尚别墅的女管家，变成了如今朝气蓬勃的年轻人的事业。无数青涩的果子将在这个行业中缓缓成熟，散发出活力的芳香和丰收的光彩。
It's hard to imagine that in today's city, there is a gray-haired security guard on a mission, and the security guard has changed from the traditional night watchman or the housekeeper of a noble villa to the career of today's energetic young people. Countless green fruits will slowly ripen in this industry, exuding a vibrant aroma and a bountiful harvest.

未知

  10.心理学教授的弟子

  10. A disciple of a psychology professor

  一位心理学教授在录取报考她的研究生时，勾掉了得分最高的学生，取了分数略低的第二名。有人问，你是不是徇私舞弊或屈服于什么压力，才舍高就低？

  A psychology professor ticked off the student with the highest score and took the second place with a slightly lower score when admitting her graduate school. Some people ask, are you cheating for personal gain or succumbing to some pressure to give up high and low?她说，否。我在进行一项心理追踪研究，或者说是吸取教训。

  She said, no. I'm doing a psychotracking study, or learning a lesson.她是位德高望重的学者，在专业范畴内颇有建树。别人一定要她讲讲录取标准。她缓缓地说，我已经招了多年的研究生，好像一个古老的匠人。我希望我所热爱的学科在我的学生手里发扬光大。老一辈毕竟要逝去，他们是渐渐暗淡下去的苍蓝。新的一辈一定要兴旺，他们是渐渐苏醒过来的嫩青。但选择什么样的接班人呢？我以前总是挑选那些得分最高、看起来兢兢业业、学习刻苦、埋头苦干，像鸡啄米一样片刻不闲的学生。我想，唯有因为热爱，他们才会如此努力取得优异的成绩，因此，他们应该是最好的。我在私下里称他们为“苦大仇深型”的学生。

  She is a highly respected scholar with considerable achievements in her professional field. Others must tell her about the admission criteria. She said slowly, I have been recruiting graduate students for many years, like an old craftsman. I hope that the subjects I love will flourish in the hands of my students. After all, the older generation is going to die, and they are a gradually fading blue. The new generation must prosper, they are young people who are gradually awakening. But what kind of successor to choose? I used to always select students with the highest scores, who seemed conscientious, studied hard, worked hard, and were like chickens pecking at rice. I think it is only because of love that they work so hard to achieve excellent results, so they should be the best. I privately call them "bitter and hateful" students.许多年过去了，我有从容的时间，以目为尺注视他们的脚步，考察他们的历史，以检验当年决定的命中率。

  Many years have passed, and I have time to calmly watch their footsteps and examine their history to test the hit rate decided that year.我发现自己错了。在未来的发展中最生龙活虎、最富有潜质并且宠辱不惊，成为真正的学科才俊的是那样一种人——他们表面上像狮子一样悠闲，甚至有点漫不经心和懒散；小的成绩并不能鼓励他们，反而让他们藐视般的淡漠；对于导师的指导和批评，往往是矜持而有保留地接受，使得他们看起来不很虚心；多少有些落落寡合，经常得不到众口一词的称赞；失败的时候难得气馁灰心，几乎不需要鼓励；辉煌的时候也显不出异样的高兴，仿佛对成就有天然的免疫力。他们的面部表情总是充满孩子般的好奇，洋溢着一种快乐，我称之为“欢喜型”。

  I realized I was wrong. In the future development, it is the kind of people who are the most alive, the most potential, and the ones who are not frightened, and become real academic talents - they are as leisurely as lions on the surface, and even a little careless and lazy; Small achievements do not encourage them, but make them contemptuous and indifferent; Teachers' guidance and criticism are often accepted with reserved and reserved attitudes, making them look not very humble; is somewhat uncompatible, and often does not get praise from everyone; It is rare to be discouraged and discouraged when you fail, and you hardly need encouragement; When it is brilliant, it does not show any unusual happiness, as if it has a natural immunity to achievement. Their facial expressions are always full of childlike curiosity and a kind of happiness, which I call the "joyful type".苦大仇深型的学习者，主要是为了改善自己的生存状态，追求科学知识给自身带来的优裕与好处。一旦达到目的，对于科学本身的挚爱就渐渐蒸发，代之以新的更敏捷的优化生存状态的努力。作为一种生活方式的选择，自然无可厚非，但作为学业继承者，则不是最好的人选。

  Learners who are bitter and hateful are mainly to improve their living conditions and pursue the benefits and benefits brought by scientific knowledge to themselves. Once the goal is achieved, the love for science itself gradually evaporates and is replaced by new and more agile efforts to optimize the state of existence. As a lifestyle choice, it is understandable, but as an academic successor, it is not the best candidate.欢喜型的学习者，也许一开始他们走得不快，脚力也并不显出格外的矫健，但心中的爱好犹如不断喷发的天然气，始终燃烧着熊熊的火焰，风暴无法将它吹熄。在火光的引导下，欢喜型的人边玩边走，兴趣盎然地不断攀登，绝不会因路边暂时的风景而停下脚步，直到高远的天际。

  Joyful learners, maybe they don't walk fast at first, and their legs are not particularly vigorous, but the hobby in their hearts is like a continuous eruption of natural gas, always burning with a raging flame, and the storm can't blow it out. Guided by the firelight, the joyful people play and walk, climbing with interest, never stopping because of the temporary scenery on the side of the road until the high sky.心理学教授说，几乎世上所有的事，都可以划分成“苦大仇深型”和“欢喜型”。比如读书，若是为了一个急切的目的而读，待事过境迁，就会与书形同路人。如果真是爱好喜欢，就会永远将书安放枕边，梦中与书相会。

  Psychology professors say that almost everything in the world can be divided into "bitter and hateful" and "joyful". For example, if you read a book for an urgent purpose, you will be like a passerby when things change. If you really like it, you will always put the book by your pillow and meet the book in your dreams.

未知

  11.你是百分之三吗

  11. Are you three percent?

  如果有一天，你说，这份工作给予我高峰体验，让我得到了很大的乐趣，更不可思议的是，还让我得到了金钱。那么，恭喜你。你把自己的兴趣和对公众的服务结合到了一起。据说，能够做到这一点的人，只占整个人口的百分之三。

  If one day, you say, this job gives me a peak experience, gives me a lot of fun, and even more incredibly, gives me money. So, congratulations. You combine your interests with service to the public. It is said that only three percent of the population is able to do this.不要小看了工作。工作是让我们觉得生命有意义的重要组成部分。如果你只把工作当成赚钱的工具，那么，你就丧失了人生极大的乐趣。一份喜爱的工作，让我们具体有了使命感，给了我们身份，是我们应答社会召唤的方式。我们的潜能得以在一个公众的平台上发挥，我们回报了社会，我们的内心收获了满足。

  Don't underestimate work. Work is an important part of making our lives feel meaningful. If you only use work as a tool to make money, then you will lose great joy in life. A job that we love gives us a sense of mission, gives us an identity, and is our way of responding to the call of society. Our potential is unleashed on a public platform, we give back to the society, and our hearts are satisfied.每样工作都有快乐，同理，每样工作也都有苦恼。现在的问题是——这快乐是否相宜于你？快乐也是有质量高下、持续长久之分的。有的快乐，只是好奇，当你知晓了其中的秘密，快乐就转变成了厌倦。有的快乐，却如醇酒，时间越长，你越感知到醉人的芳香。谈到苦恼，这可要认真琢磨一番。相比之下，苦恼比快乐更重要，因为这是你的底线。你是否可以接纳持之以恒的苦恼？你对苦恼的容忍程度到底怎样？你能容忍的时间是多久？你能为此做出多少改变呢？

  Every job has happiness, and in the same way, every job also has suffering. Now the question is – is this happiness suitable for you? Happiness is also of high quality and long-lasting. Some happiness is just curiosity, and when you know the secret, happiness turns into boredom. Some are happy, but like fine wine, the longer the time, the more you perceive the intoxicating aroma. When it comes to distress, you have to think about it carefully. In contrast, distress is more important than happiness because this is your bottom line. Can you accept persistent distress? How tolerant are you about distress? How long can you tolerate? How much can you change for this?人格对职业的影响力，远远不及兴趣。你要尽量拓展对某一行业的了解，它是什么？它做什么？它的行规是什么？这不是一项简单的功课，要知道，现在有超过两万种的职业在地球上开展。每一个行业都有行规，你如果不了解行规，贸然入行，很可能受不了。你不懂得游戏规则，游戏就会给人焦虑和压力。

  The influence of personality on the profession is far less than interest. You want to try to expand your understanding of a certain industry, what is it? What does it do? What are its rules? This is not a simple lesson, you know, there are now more than 20,000 professions on Earth. Every industry has its rules, and if you don't understand the rules and enter the industry rashly, you may not be able to stand it. If you don't understand the rules of the game, the game will give people anxiety and stress.行业里也有许多潜规则，你可曾知晓？我知道有一些潜规则是上不得台面的，但多少年来，他们一直在那个行业的激流之下存在着。如果你要接受这个行业，你就要了解它的全部：桌面上的和桌面下的。如果你有精神的需要，就要远离某种潜规则。你不可能一边控诉着，一边利用着，那你本人也成了潜在水面下的生物。

  There are also many unspoken rules in the industry, have you ever known? I know there are some unspoken rules that are not on the table, but they have existed under the torrent of that industry for many years. If you're going to embrace the industry, you need to know what it's all about: on the desktop and off-desktop. If you have spiritual needs, stay away from some kind of unspoken rule. You can't accuse and use it at the same time, then you yourself have become a potential creature under the surface.当你尝试着做一件充满了创造性的工作，应当更相信你无微不至的直觉，不必掺杂过多的理智。因为理智通常是通过已有的经验来做判断，但这一次，过多的理智会充当刺客。

  When you try to do a creative job, you should trust your meticulous intuition and not mix it with too much reason. Because reason is usually judged by existing experience, but this time, too much reason will act as an assassin.由于工作价值与生命意义的联系陷落和崩裂，现代的人们常常伸手不见五指地迷茫。工作占去了青葱岁月豆蔻年华，投入心血，殚精竭虑。当我们不再能从工作中找到快乐和意义的时候，负面的力量会来得如此之大，决然超过了你的预期。然而工作里越是找不到幸福感，我们越是要去寻找它。这就形成了最凶险的悖论。

  Due to the collapse and collapse of the connection between the value of work and the meaning of life, modern people often lose their hands and fingers. Work occupies the green years and cardamom years, and puts all his heart and soul into it. When we can no longer find joy and meaning in our work, the negative forces will come so much that they will definitely exceed your expectations. However, the more we can't find happiness at work, the more we have to look for it. This forms the most dangerous paradox.听过这样一句名言：世界上最幸运的人，是找到一份工，他不用工作。这话语有一点点拗口，说白了，就是你能把工作变成玩耍的一部分。在你工作的时候，完全不觉得这是被迫的事情，而是发自内心地喜爱。

  I have heard such a famous saying: The luckiest person in the world is to find a job, he does not have to work. This is a bit of a mouthful, to put it bluntly, you can turn work into a part of play. When you work, you don't feel forced at all, but love it from the bottom of your heart.

  工作就是爱自己、爱社会，是混合着生活素质和成就感的一杯鸡尾酒。如果你仅仅把工作变成了养家糊口的营生，那就不单对不起自己，也对不起工作。

  Work is to love oneself and society, and it is a cocktail of quality of life and a sense of accomplishment. If you just turn your work into a breadwinner, you're not only sorry for yourself, but also for your work.工作是可以换的，但事业不会。事业给生涯一个方向。事业是持续的，是和人生观、价值观挂在一起的。生涯更是一个宽广的概念。这就是工作和事业的不同。如果你能把工作和事业熔炼在一起，那就是天人合一了。

  Jobs can be changed, but careers cannot. Career gives a direction for career. Career is continuous and is linked to life outlook and values. Career is a broad concept. This is the difference between work and career. If you can smelt work and career together, it is the unity of heaven and man.所有的工作，都有它的神圣性，都有喜欢它的人存在。要力争把你的工作变成你的兴趣所在。这是一种纯美的境界。你做这件事，这件事让你快乐，让别人感到有帮助，人家还付酬金给你，你说这是不是多方共赢、皆大欢喜呢？这样的事，从天上掉下来的时候固然是有的，但肯定概率极低。所以，你要用心去寻找，以求达到幸福的高峰——有点像结婚。
All work has its sacredness, and there are people who like it. Strive to make your work your interest. This is a state of pure beauty. If you do this, this thing makes you happy, makes others feel helpful, and people pay you a reward, do you think this is a win-win situation for all parties and everyone is happy? Such things do happen when they fall from the sky, but the probability is extremely low. So, you have to look with your heart to reach the peak of happiness - a bit like getting married.

  12.首选护林员

  12. Preferred rangers

  我有一套表格，是根据一个人的性格、爱好、才能、本领等特征预测职业选择趋向。当然，结果仅供参考。朋友们知道了，有时会说，嘿，把你那沓表借咱使使，看看天生是从事何种职业的料子，现在还有没有转轨变形的可能性。我嫌麻烦，就说，人家国外都是给大学毕业生或待业青年找工作时才做这种测验，您都七老八十的喽，不说事业有成，也算轻车熟路了，怎么着，还真想重打鼓另开张啊？再说啦，这种表格是外国人设计的，统计数据也是海外的，简单移植过来，不一定准的。

  I have a set of tables that predict the trend of career choices based on a person's personality, hobbies, talents, skills and other characteristics. Of course, the results are for reference only. When friends know about it, they sometimes say, hey, lend us your stack of watches to see what kind of profession you were born to work in, and whether there is still a possibility of deformation. I think it's troublesome, so I said, people abroad are only doing this kind of quiz when looking for jobs for college graduates or unemployed young people, you are all seventy or eighty, not to mention that you have a successful career, you are also familiar with the road, what's wrong, do you really want to beat the drum again and open another one? Besides, this kind of table is designed by foreigners, and the statistics are also overseas, and it is not necessarily accurate when simply transplanted.我劝阻。但是，几乎没有一个人收回他们的要求，坚持着，从请求到恳求，甚至……哀求（假装的），直到我答应。我从中得到很惊奇的发现——现今社会中，有把握确认所从事的工作，正是自己爱好和擅长的人，少得令人叹息。现代人对于职业，普遍在一种不肯定、不确信的状态中游弋，懵懂茫然，期待着来自外界的确认或改变。

  I dissuade him. However, hardly a single person took back their demands, persisted, from pleas to pleas, and even ...... begging (pretending) until I agreed. I was surprised to find that in today's society, there are very few people who are sure to confirm that the work they are engaged in is exactly what they like and are good at. Modern people generally swim in a state of uncertainty and uncertainty about their careers, ignorant and dazed, looking forward to confirmation or change from the outside world.测验结果，令人瞠目。

  The test results are eye-popping.一位优秀企业家，他的最佳职业选择应是动物学家。

  An excellent entrepreneur, his best career choice should be a zoologist.一位兢兢业业的公务员，所得结果是民间艺人。

  A conscientious civil servant who gets a folk artist.一位电脑工程师，竟是农场主。

  A computer engineer is actually a farmer.一位警察，干脆提示他可以试试做个神职人员。

  A policeman simply suggested that he could try to be a clergyman.……

  凡此种种，南辕北辙，有的还不符合国情，闹得我对该表很没几分信心了。朋友们的反应，更叫人难以捉摸。不摇头也不点头，讪讪的，淡淡的。更有甚者，神秘莫测地笑笑，一反当初的诚恳和迫切，王顾左右而言他，好似我辛辛苦苦做出的结果和他不相干。

  All of these things are in line with national conditions, and some of them are not in line with national conditions, which makes me have little confidence in the watch. The reaction of friends is even more elusive. Don't shake your head or nod, scornful, lightly. What's more, he smiled mysteriously, contrary to his original sincerity and urgency, Wang looked around and talked about him, as if the results I had worked hard to make had nothing to do with him.次数多了，我也意兴阑珊。一天，一位很要好的朋友又求做这个表。我懒懒地说，做，可以。只是我做完了之后，无论那个结果怎样出乎你的意料，你都得把真实想法告诉我。她想了有25秒，说，好！

  The more times I have it, the more I feel like it's going to be low. One day, a very good friend asked to make this watch again. I lazily said, do, yes. It's just that after I'm done, no matter how unexpected the result is, you have to tell me what you really think. She thought for 25 seconds and said, okay!她是一家律师楼的合伙人。早年我一听到“合伙人”这个词就想笑，觉得像开卖瓜子的小杂货店，这两年不敢笑了。朋友在业内已声名卓著，物质也大大丰富了，出入香车。我到她郊外巍峨的别墅看过银河（北京城里通常是看不到星星的）。

  She is a partner in a law firm. In the early years, I wanted to laugh when I heard the word "partner", and I felt like a small grocery store selling melon seeds, and I didn't dare to laugh in the past two years. Friends have become well-known in the industry, and their materials have been greatly enriched, and they have come and gone in and out of the fragrant car. I went to her majestic villa on the outskirts to see the Milky Way (you usually can't see the stars in Beijing).用处理法律文书的严谨和节奏，她填完了表格。我把测验完成之后，先检查了两遍，然后盯着她问，还记着咱俩的约定吧？

  With the rigor and pace of handling legal documents, she filled out the form. After I finished the test, I checked it twice, then stared at her and asked, remember our agreement, right?

  她敲敲自己的头说，律师的脑壳，是电脑加文件柜。

  She knocked on her head and said that the lawyer's brain is a computer and a filing cabinet.我说，你可以兑现了。

  I said, you can cash in.我把测验结果递给她。在职业选择的顺序表上赫然列着——首选护林员。

  I handed her the test results. On the order of career choices, it is prominently listed - the preferred ranger.寂静。在我和她之间，犹如隔着一片被烈火焚烧过的旷地，没有林涛，也没有鸟鸣。我说，反悔了，是不是？我也不明白怎么得出了这结果。你是多么出色的律师啊。我见过你出庭，唇枪舌剑胜似闲庭信步。最大的可能性，是这个表错了。

  Quiescence. Between me and her, it was like a vast land burned by fire, without Lin Tao or birdsong. I said, I regret it, right? I don't understand how this result came about. What a great lawyer you are. I have seen you appear in court, and your words are better than walking in the court. The biggest possibility is that this table is wrong.女律师看着我，目光好似看一个嫌犯。她用我从来没听过的声调缓缓说，哦，那表没错，错的是我曾经的选择。刚才那一刻，只有一个念头，就是想掉眼泪。滚滚红尘中，没人知道我的心，包括我的父母和我的丈夫。远远的异国，却有一张不知何人打造出的表格直穿我胸襟，让我和我的灵魂有了一个突然而痛楚的接触，才知道这一生的真爱在百般打压之下依然安在。我愿被遮天蔽日的绿色掩埋，喜欢与世隔绝的静谧和亘古不变的安详。在与人屏蔽的大自然里，听蚯蚓爬过蘑菇根和蝴蝶须子拍破露珠的声响……我从来没对任何人说过这个心愿，以为成功地将它谋杀在少年时代。没想到，它如此鲜活地蛰伏在我内心最幽暗的水塘里，直到这张表钓它到阳光下。我每天忙着，为了许许多多的利益和功名，至今没有机缘走入原始森林一步。我能为树木所做的唯一的事，就是节省几张复印纸。哦，护林员，多好听的名字啊！念起它的时候，喉头充满了松脂的爽滑，连肺也像白纱裙一样鼓胀开来，可惜，我吸进的依然是城市嗅过千百次的废气……

  The female lawyer looked at me as if she were looking at a suspect. She said slowly in a tone I had never heard before, oh, that watch is right, what was wrong was my choice. At that moment just now, there was only one thought, which was to shed tears. In the rolling world, no one knows my heart, including my parents and my husband. In a foreign country far away, there was a form created by someone that pierced my chest, giving me a sudden and painful contact with my soul, only to know that the true love of this life was still alive under all kinds of suppression. I would like to be buried by the green that obscures the sky, and I like the silence of isolation and the eternal tranquility. In the nature shielded from people, listen to the sound of earthworms crawling over mushroom roots and butterfly whiskers beating dewdrops...... I never said this wish to anyone, thinking that I had successfully murdered it as a teenager. I didn't expect it to lie so vividly in the darkest pond in my heart until this watch caught it into the sun. I am busy every day, for the sake of many benefits and fame, I have not had the opportunity to step into the primeval forest so far. The only thing I can do for the trees is to save a few sheets of copy paper. Oh, ranger, what a nice name! When I read it, my throat was full of pine resin, and even my lungs were swollen like a white gauze skirt, but unfortunately, I still inhaled the exhaust gas that the city had smelled thousands of times......轮到我不知如何回应，唯有沉思。方明白一个人的职业，如果能和爱好契合，将是怎样的幸福。如果背道而驰，不管他依仗智力的超拔和人格的卓绝，凭借外力的援送和机遇的佳美，到达怎样精彩的高度，他内心总不能无拘无束地快活。一个苦苦的祈盼，在沉沉掩埋下愈老弥坚。如同三千年的古莲子，在枯燥中黑暗地坚守，期待有朝一日冲决而出重绽花朵。

  I didn't know how to respond, so I could only ponder. Only then do you understand what kind of happiness a person's career will be if it can match his hobbies. If he goes against him, no matter how wonderful he reaches by relying on his intellectual superiority and outstanding personality, with the help of external forces and the beauty of opportunity, he will never be able to be unrestrained and happy in his heart. A bitter prayer is getting older and stronger under the heavy burial. Like a 3,000-year-old ancient lotus seed, it sticks to the darkness in boredom, looking forward to one day breaking through and blooming again.你何时回你的森林？分手时我问。特别用了“回”这个字眼。那儿是她心灵的家园。

  When will you return to your forest? I asked when we broke up. The word "return" is used in particular. It was the home of her soul.以眼前这个忙法，等退休以后了。她捋捋满头的青丝，苦笑着说。又补充一句，找的人这么多，只怕退了也安生不下来。只有一个办法，把骨灰撒在白桦树下。

  With the busy method in front of me, I will wait until I retire. She stroked the green silk on her head and said with a wry smile. I added that there are so many people looking for me, I am afraid that I will not be able to live in peace even if I retire. There is only one way, scatter the ashes under the birch tree.魂灵也要看守森林，上车时她说。

  The spirit also has to guard the forest, she said when she got into the car.

  13.第6000次回答

  13. 6000th answer

  某机构驻北京办事处的首席代表，是一位外籍女华人。

  The chief representative of an institution's office in Beijing is a foreign Chinese.一次聊天，她说，本公司待遇优厚，事业发展很有前途，因此每次招聘白领，硕士、博士云集，真像一句北京土话形容的——可用簸箕论堆儿撮。好中选优，我的用人标准非常简单。开始阶段，完全唯文凭是举，而且一定要名牌大学的高才生。

  In a chat, she said that the company has a generous salary and a promising career development, so every time it recruits white-collar workers, masters and doctors gather, which is really like a Beijing dialect description - it can be used as a dustpan. My employment criteria are very simple. At the beginning, diplomas are completely the only ones, and they must be highly talented students from prestigious universities.我说，这样做是否有遗珠之憾？自学成才的也大有人在，俗话说包子有肉不在褶儿上，路遥知马力、日久见人心啊。

  I said, is there any regret in doing this? There are also many self-taught people, as the saying goes, the bun has meat and is not on the pleats, the road knows the horsepower, and the people see the hearts of the people over time.首席代表点头道，你讲得也有几分道理，但现代社会如此快节奏，哪儿有时间像个老农似的慢慢考察马的能力？我没有火眼金睛能看穿人的心肺，只有凭借他的历史。如果是匹千里马，早该穿云破雾战功赫赫。馅儿里藏着很多肉的包子，必会油汪汪、香气扑鼻，不能等咬了一口才知道。

  The chief representative nodded and said, you have some truth, but modern society is so fast-paced, where is the time to slowly examine the ability of horses like an old farmer? I don't have the golden eye to see through people's hearts and lungs, only by his history. If it was a thousand-mile horse, it should have broken through the clouds and fog long ago. Buns with a lot of meat hidden in the filling will be oily and fragrant, and you can't wait until you take a bite to know.名牌大学的学生，当然也非个个金刚不坏之身，但杰出人才的保险系数大一些。你想啊，重点大学的学生一般来自重点中学，重点中学来自重点小学……据说一个小学生大约要考500次试。念到博士毕业，便经历了成千上万次考试。都说现在学生压力大、精神负担重，能在大负荷下成绩优等，不曾考试昏倒，没有长期失眠，精神无分裂，身体未崩溃……不正说明了他毅力顽强、心理素质稳定，是可堪造就的人才吗？

  Of course, students from prestigious universities are not all indestructible, but the insurance coefficient of outstanding talents is larger. Do you think that students from key universities generally come from key middle schools, and key middle schools come from key primary schools...... It is said that an elementary school student takes about 500 exams. When I graduated with a doctorate, I went through thousands of exams. It is said that now students are under high pressure and mental burden, can achieve excellent grades under heavy loads, have never fainted in exams, have not suffered from long-term insomnia, have no schizophrenia, and have not collapsed...... Doesn't it mean that he has tenacious perseverance and stable psychological quality, and is a talent that can be cultivated?再者，我喜欢名牌大学学生的自信和优越感，那是一种从小积攒起来的雄厚功力和接受了某种训练培育出的虚张声势型自信，内在质量不一样。后面这种东西，一般的场合下还可凑合，但到关键时刻需要大胆魄、大气概时就易溃败瓦解。现代商战很残酷，谁能在气势上压倒对方，进退有度，坚持到最后一分钟，才能成为长远的赢家。当然，衡量人的整体素质，是综合指标，但我哪儿有那么多时间一一鉴定？只有忙中取巧，简化约分，把复杂的问题程式化。打仗时，大家挑选勇敢的人。和平年代，人们便用名牌大学这孔筛子，做用人的初步甄别。

  Furthermore, I like the self-confidence and superiority of students in prestigious universities, which is a kind of strong skills accumulated since childhood and bravado self-confidence cultivated by receiving some kind of training, and the inner quality is different. The latter kind of thing can be made do on ordinary occasions, but when it comes to critical moments, it is easy to collapse and collapse when boldness and grandiosity are needed. Modern business wars are cruel, who can overwhelm the opponent in momentum, advance and retreat, and persist until the last minute in order to become a long-term winner. Of course, measuring a person's overall quality is a comprehensive indicator, but where do I have so much time to identify them one by one? Only by taking advantage of the busyness, simplifying the division, and stylizing complex problems. When fighting, everyone chooses brave people. In peaceful times, people used the sieve of famous universities as a preliminary screening of people.我说，您的这套观点和现在的素质教育不符啊。人才应该是一个更广博的概念。

  I said, your set of views is inconsistent with the current quality education. Talent should be a broader concept.首席代表说，我也是无奈。除了分数，中国现在还有哪种比较公平公开而又负责任的评定指标可供用人单位参考？国外是有这种标准的。

  The chief representative said, I am also helpless. In addition to scores, what other fair, open and responsible evaluation indicators can employers refer to in China? There is such a standard abroad.我女儿和她的伙伴，都特别踊跃地参加志愿者服务队伍。工作是义务的，没有报酬，但登记处的表格摞得天高。孩子们要是得知申请获得接受，被指派了为公众服务的机会，会非常高兴。动机并不完全出于无私的爱心，关键在于活动结束后，用人部门会出示对志愿者能力和责任感的评语。此种经历和得分，对于就业极为重要。

  My daughter and her partner are particularly enthusiastic about participating in the volunteer service team. The work is compulsory and unpaid, but the registry is stacked with forms. The children will be happy to know that their applications have been accepted and that they have been assigned the opportunity to serve the public. The motivation is not entirely selfless, the key is that after the event, the employing department will show a comment on the volunteer's ability and sense of responsibility. This kind of experience and score is extremely important for employment.女儿领受任务回家，对着镜子不停咧着嘴笑。她平常性格内向，不大动表情。那一天，她直笑得腮帮上的肌肉都哆嗦起来，好像白天跑了太多的路，睡觉时小腿抽筋一样。我说，艾尼卡，你这是怎么啦？按照中国话说，是吃了笑婆婆的尿了吗？女儿说，妈妈，我被分到一家像迪士尼乐园样的游乐城，将穿着员工的制服站在一个岔路口为游人指路。经过测算，游人从进园玩到我所站立的地方，有三分之一的人会有需要方便的念头。虽然一路标有显著的卫生间指示牌，但仍有很多人会四处张望，向服务人员打听——洗手间在哪里？这个时候，我的工作，喏，就是一边打手势一边笑容满面地回答：请往这边走。

  The daughter received the task and went home, grinning in the mirror. She is usually introverted and doesn't move her expression. That day, she laughed so hard that the muscles on her cheeks trembled, as if she had run too much during the day and her calves cramped when she slept. I said, Anika, what's wrong with you? According to Chinese, did you eat the urine of the laughing mother-in-law? My daughter said, Mom, I was assigned to an amusement park like Disneyland, and I stood at a fork in the road in the uniform of an employee to show the way to the tourists. After calculation, one-third of the tourists will have the idea of convenience from entering the park to the place where I stand. Although there are prominent toilet signs along the way, there are still many people who will look around and ask the service staff - where is the toilet? At this time, my job, well, is to gesture and answer with a smile: Please go this way.

  工作基本就是如此，很简单，很单调，但是必不可少。今天，公园服务总管问我，你知道每天要说多少遍“请往这边走”吗？我说，不知道。总管说，要回答6000遍。这句话，我相信你在说第一遍的时候会亲切可人、温柔有加，说到1000次的时候也还算彬彬有礼，但你能保证在每天第6000次重复它时，脸上依旧是真切的笑意，口气中没有一丝厌倦的情绪吗？如果你做不到，现在离开还来得及。

  The work is basically like this, very simple, monotonous, but essential. Today, the park service manager asked me, do you know how many times a day you have to say "please go this way"? I said, I don't know. The manager said to answer 6,000 times. I believe you will be kind and gentle when you say this sentence the first time, and you will be polite when you say it 1,000 times, but can you guarantee that when you repeat it for the 6,000th time a day, there is still a real smile on your face and no trace of tiredness in your tone? If you can't do it, it's too late to leave.我心中一抽，女儿个性强，能承担如此乏味的工作和持续地善待他人吗？没有把握啊。忙问，艾尼卡，你怎样回答？女儿说，我想，这是一个培养爱心、锻炼耐力的好机会，再说为了得到一个就业参考的好分数，我就咬牙答应下来了。您没看我正在练习微笑吗？

  My heart twitched, can my daughter have a strong personality, take on such a tedious job and continue to be kind to others? I'm not sure. Busy asking, Anika, how do you answer? My daughter said, I think this is a good opportunity to cultivate love and exercise endurance, and in order to get a good score for employment reference, I gritted my teeth and agreed. Didn't you see that I was practicing smiling?艾尼卡真的说到做到了。我曾在游乐园快下班的时候偷窥过她，那大概已经是她当天的第5000多次微笑了，依旧纯真善良、举止到位，无一敷衍。以至义务劳动结束时，她说，妈妈，我已经忘记如何表示愤怒了。当然，她得到了很好的评语。

  Anika really did what he said. I once peeped at her when she was about to get off work at the amusement park, and it was probably her 5,000th smile that day, still innocent and kind, with good manners and no perfunctory. At the end of the volunteer labor, she said, Mom, I have forgotten how to express my anger. Of course, she got good reviews.听完首席代表的话，我说，您这样一讲，我是又明白又糊涂了。明白的是，艾尼卡是一个好孩子。糊涂的是，既然人的优良品质是培养出来的，这不又和您的天生自信学说矛盾了吗？

  After listening to the chief representative's words, I said, I understand and confused when you say this. What is clear is that Anika is a good boy. The confusing thing is that since people's good qualities are cultivated, isn't this contradictory to your natural self-confidence doctrine?首席代表笑起来说，不要钻我的空子啊。天生素质当然最好，如果不具备，就只好退而求其次。好比天然的大虾捕捞光了，人工养殖的也行啊。天才加上训练，就更棒啦！

  The chief representative laughed and said, don't take advantage of me. Of course, natural quality is the best, if you don't have it, you have to settle for the second. It's like natural prawns are caught, and artificially farmed ones are also fine. Genius plus training, it's even better!

未知

  14.你的身体里必有一颗成功的种子

  14. There must be a seed of success in your body

  在每个人的生命里，都有一个关于创造的秘密，等待着被发现。那将是你的第二次诞生。

  In everyone's life, there is a secret about creation, waiting to be discovered. That will be your second birth.你一定要相信，在你的身体里，有一颗种子，焦灼地盼望着阳光。至于它到底是一颗什么种子，在没有发芽之前，谁也不知道。

  You must believe that there is a seed in your body, anxiously looking forward to the sun. As for what kind of seed it is, no one knows until it germinates.你的责任就是给它浇水，保护它不被鸟雀啄食，不因为干渴而失去生机，不会被人偷走，也不会在你饥肠辘辘的时刻被你炒熟了充饥。如果那样做了，你虽可一时果腹，却丧失了长久发展的原动力。

  Your responsibility is to water it, protect it from being pecked by birds, not lose its vitality due to thirst, not be stolen by others, and not be cooked by you to satisfy your hunger when you are hungry. If you do that, you will be satisfied for a while, but you will lose the driving force for long-term development.那颗种子可能藏在你的耳朵里，你就有灵敏的听觉。可能藏在你的手指甲里，你就有非凡的触觉。也可能在你的眸子里，也可能在你的肌肉中。当然了，更可能在你的大脑中、心脏里、双手中……

  That seed may be hidden in your ears, and you have a keen sense of hearing. Probably hidden in your fingernails, you have an extraordinary sense of touch. It could be in your eyes, or it could be in your muscles. Of course, it is more likely to be in your brain, heart, and hands......每个人在属于个人的成长经历中，早已获得了解决问题的丰富宝藏。请信任我们的潜意识，它必定能在正确的时机产生恰当的回应。告诉你一句悄悄话——有时候，信息也将以非语言的方式揭露真相。

  Everyone has already obtained a rich treasure of solving problems in their personal growth experience. Trust our subconscious mind to respond appropriately at the right time. Whisper to you – sometimes, information will reveal the truth in a non-verbal way.找找吧。一定找得到！

  Look for it. You will definitely find it!身体里绝对有不少于一百种的功能，能保证你在浑然不觉中完成种种复杂的运作。但你不要以为功能们会一直老老实实地待在那里，它们是勤勤恳恳的，却不是任劳任怨的。如果你一直视它们的存在为理所当然，从来不照料它们，不维护和激励它们，或是过度使用，或置若罔闻，那么，它们不是反抗，就是消极怠工，也许集体突围，无声无息地溜走了，然而你误以为它们从来不曾居住在你的身体里。要知道，一辈子无意识地随波逐流，会导致你各种功能的退化。

  There are definitely no less than a hundred functions in the body, which can ensure that you can complete various complex operations without realizing it. But don't think that the functions will always stay there, they are diligent, but not hardworking. If you have always taken their existence for granted, never cared for them, not maintained and motivated them, or overused them, or turned a deaf ear, then they either resisted, or neglected their work, perhaps broke out collectively and slipped away silently, yet you mistakenly believe that they never inhabited your body. You know, unconsciously following the crowd all your life will lead to the degradation of your various functions.成功并不像想象的那样难。因为我们不敢做，它才变得难起来。

  Success is not as difficult as it seems. Because we don't dare to do it, it becomes difficult.

未知

  15.仇人的显微镜

  15. The microscope of the enemy

  人一生，会听到很多评价和意见，你不想听也不行。意见的来源，是个有趣的问题。

  In a person's life, you will hear a lot of evaluations and opinions, and you don't want to listen to them. The source of the opinion is an interesting question.说到意见的来源，最简单的可以分成两大类。一大类来自爱你的人，因为希望你进步，希望你好，希望你幸福，所以他们会指出你的不足。通常我们对这类意见，要么是重视过度，要么是过度地不重视。前者是因为亲人在我们眼中就是人间的上帝，句句是真理。后者也因为和凡间的上帝相处得太久了，反倒觉得老生常谈，把它当成了耳旁风。还有一大类意见，来自恨你的人。我说的这个“恨”，不是血海深仇，不是国恨家仇。在此文中，它统指对你印象不好的人、和你不对付的人、和你有过节儿且巴望着你倒霉的人。按时下年轻人的话讲，就是和你相克，也许是血型不符，也许是星座不合。那些和你暗中戗着茬儿的龌龊人，恕我简称为你的“仇人”。

  When it comes to the source of opinions, the simplest can be divided into two categories. A large category comes from people who love you because they want you to improve, want you to be good, and want you to be happy, so they will point out your shortcomings. Usually we either overvalue or do not attach too much importance to this kind of opinion. The former is because our relatives are the God of the world in our eyes, and every sentence is the truth. the latter also felt clichéd because he had been with the mortal god for too long, and took it as a deaf ear. There is also a large category of opinions from people who hate you. The "hatred" I am talking about is not a deep blood feud, not a national hatred and a family feud. In this text, it refers to people who have a bad impression of you, people who don't deal with you, and people who have a bad relationship with you and hope that you are unlucky. According to the words of young people nowadays, it is to be compatible with you, maybe it is a blood type that does not match, or it may be a constellation that does not match. Those filthy people who secretly quarrel with you, forgive me for simply calling you your "enemy".对待仇人的意见，有一句很经典的话，叫作“走自己的路，让别人说去吧”。这虽是一剂良药，但缺点是起效较慢。很多人试验过这法，有时好几个月甚至好几年之后，才能渐渐在想起仇人们的冷语时心境淡然。还有一个前提——你已经找到了一条路，正在走着，方向感明确，有主心骨，步履轻快。说这话的时候底气才较充足。倘若正在彷徨和苦闷中，雨雾迷蒙，路还不知在何方，或者干脆在路边崴了脚或被野兽啃伤了，创口流着血，那这句经典就稍嫌隔靴搔痒，有点近似精神胜利法了。

  There is a very classic saying about the opinions of enemies, called "Go your own way and let others tell you to go". Although this is a good medicine, the disadvantage is that it has a slower effect. Many people have tried this method, sometimes months or even years later, before they can gradually feel calm when they think of the cold words of their enemies. There is also a premise - you have found a path, you are walking, you have a clear sense of direction, you have a backbone, and you walk briskly. When he said this, he was more confident. If you are hesitating and distressed, in the rain and fog, and you don't know where the road is, or if you simply break your foot on the side of the road or be gnawed by a wild beast, and the wound is bleeding, then this classic is a little bit of an itch through the boots, a bit similar to the spiritual victory method.面对仇人的攻伐，如何是好？

  What should I do in the face of the enemy's attack?仇人的话，杀伤力之所以大，是因为那其中常常是有几分真实的。完全的谎话，其实倒并不可怕，因为除了极为弱智的人，一般都可识破。古语说“谣言止于智者”，现在资讯发达，人也吃了很多深海鱼油，智者可能比古时还要多些，所以对完全胡说八道的东西倒不必太过担心。如果仇人的话是完全的真实，我看是应该感激的，请你低下自己的头。这不是认输或领认了侮辱，而是真心实意地表达对真实的敬畏。只要他说得对，不必介意他的人品，只需看重他的意见。仇人的真知灼见，也许会让你因此得到终生受用的教诲，他在无意中就送了一个大礼给你，他就成了你的恩人。这就是很多人常常说，我最感激的是那些侮辱、攻击、放弃我的人，他们让我懂得了如何做人，才有了今天的成就云云……每逢我听到这种话，总觉得略微矫情了些。我不会感谢那些本来想侮辱我的人，他们不应该因为仇视和狭隘受到感激。仇恨和狭隘，常常是可以置人于死地的，你没有死，是因为你救了自己。你应该感谢的只有一个人，那就是你自己啊。

  The reason why the words of enemies are so lethal is because there is often some truth in them. A complete lie is actually not terrible, because except for extremely mentally retarded people, it can generally be seen.
