The old saying is that "rumors stop at the wise", now that information is developed, people have eaten a lot of deep-sea fish oil, and there may be more wise people than in ancient times, so there is no need to worry too much about complete nonsense. If the enemy's words are completely true, I think you should be grateful, please lower your head. This is not an admission of defeat or an insult, but a sincere expression of reverence for the truth. As long as he is right, don't mind his character, just value his opinion. The enemy's insight may give you a lifelong lesson, and he inadvertently gives you a big gift, and he becomes your benefactor. This is why many people often say that what I am most grateful for is those who insulted, attacked, and gave up on me, who taught me how to be a person, which is why I have achieved what I am today...... Whenever I hear this kind of thing, I always feel a little pretentious. I will not thank those who originally wanted to insult me, they should not be appreciated for hatred and narrow-mindedness. Hatred and narrow-mindedness can often put people to death, you didn't die because you saved yourself. There is only one person you should be thankful for, and that is yourself.

  即使你从仇人喷涌而来的污泥浊水中，荡涤出了金沙，你也可以依然保持你的仇恨，如同保持你脊骨的硬度，但这并不妨碍你思忖他们的意见。因为只有仇人，才会深深研究你的要害。因为他恨你，所以他时刻盯着你，对你观察得格外细致，思索得格外刻毒。试想一下，如果我们用显微镜看事物，那普天之下就没有一处洁净的地方了，到处都是繁殖的细菌、蠕动的螨虫……

  Even if you wash out the golden sand from the muddy water gushing out of your enemies, you can still maintain your hatred, as if you keep your spine hard, but this does not prevent you from thinking about their opinions. Because only enemies will study your key points deeply. Because he hates you, he always stares at you, observes you very carefully, and thinks particularly viciously. Just imagine, if we look at things with a microscope, there is no clean place in the world, full of breeding bacteria and wriggling mites......然而，依然有阳光。

  However, there is still sunshine.你的仇人，就是瞄准你的显微镜。

  Your enemy is aiming at your microscope.

未知

  16.布雷迪的猴子

  16. Brady's Monkey

  当心理医生的朋友给我讲过一个故事——布雷迪的猴子。

  When a friend of a psychiatrist told me a story - Brady's monkey.布雷迪不是一座山，也不是一片茂密的原始森林，而是一位科学家的名字。

  Brady is not a mountain or a dense primeval forest, but the name of a scientist.那是一个晴朗的日子，两只猴子各自坐在它们的椅子上，像平常一样开始了生活。但宁静仅仅维持了片刻，20秒钟后，它们猛地同时遭到一次电击。这当然是不愉快的感受了，猴子们惊叫起来。

  It was a sunny day, and the two monkeys each sat in their chairs and went about their lives as usual. But the silence lasted only a moment, and after 20 seconds, they were suddenly shocked at the same time. This was of course an unpleasant feeling, and the monkeys screamed.被仪器操纵的电源，毫不理睬猴子们的愤怒，均匀恒定地释放电流，每20秒钟准时击发一次。猴子们被紧紧地缚在约束椅上，藏没处藏，躲没处躲，只得逆来顺受。

  The power supply manipulated by the instrument ignored the anger of the monkeys and released current evenly and constantly, firing on time every 20 seconds. The monkeys were tightly tied to restraint chairs, hiding and hiding, and had to accept it.但猴子不愧为灵长目动物，开始转动脑筋。很快，它们发现各自的椅子上都有一根压杆。

  But the monkey, worthy of being a primate, began to turn its brain. Soon, they discover that each chair has a press bar.甲猴在电击即将来临的时候掀动压杆，电击就被神奇地取消了，它俩也就一同逃脱了一次痛苦的体验。

  The armored monkey lifted the pressure lever when the electric shock was about to come, and the electric shock was magically canceled, and they both escaped a painful experience together.乙猴也照样掀动压杆。很可惜，它手边的这件货色是摆样子的，压与不压，对电击没有任何影响。也就是说，乙猴在频频到来的打击面前束手无策。

  The B monkey also lifted the pressure rod in the same way. Unfortunately, the color of the goods it has at hand is in shape, pressed or not, and has no effect on electric shock. In other words, the Yi Monkey is helpless in the face of frequent blows.实验继续着。甲猴明白自己可以操纵命运，它紧张地估算着时间，在打击即将到来的前夕不失时机地掀动压杆，以避免灾难。当然，它有时成功，有时失败。成功的时候，它俩就有了短暂的休息；失败的时候，它们就一道忍受电流的折磨。

  The experiment continued. Knowing that it can manipulate fate, the armored monkey nervously estimates the time, and wastes no time in lifting the pressure rod on the eve of the imminent blow to avoid disaster. Of course, it sometimes succeeds, sometimes it fails. When they succeed, they have a short break; When they fail, they endure the torture of the current together.时间艰涩地流淌着，实验结果出来了。在同等频率、同等强度电流的打击下，那只不停掀动压杆、疲于奔命的甲猴，由于沉重的心理负担，得了胃溃疡。那只听天由命、无能为力的乙猴，安然无恙……

  Time flowed hard, and the results of the experiment came out. Under the blow of the same frequency and the same intensity of the current, the tired armored monkey kept lifting the pressure rod and suffered from a stomach ulcer due to the heavy psychological burden. The resigned and powerless Yi Monkey was safe and sound......假若是你，愿做布雷迪实验里的哪一只猴子？朋友问。

  If it were you, which monkey would you like to be in the Brady experiment? Friend asked.我说，我是人，我不是猴子。

  I said, I am a human, I am not a monkey.朋友说，这只是一个比方。其实，旋转的现代社会和这个实验有很多相同之处，频繁的刺激接踵而来，人们生活在目不暇接的紧张打击之中。大家拼命地在预防伤害，采取种种未雨绸缪的手段。殊不知，某些伤害正是在预防中发生，人为的干预常常弄巧成拙，适得其反……所以，人们有时需要无奈，需要阿Q，需要随波逐流，需要无动于衷、听其自然……

  Friends say it's just an analogy. In fact, the rotating modern society has many similarities with this experiment, with frequent stimuli and people living in a dizzying burst of tension. Everyone is desperately trying to prevent injuries and take all kinds of precautionary measures. As everyone knows, some injuries occur in prevention, and human intervention is often self-defeating and counterproductive...... Therefore, people sometimes need helplessness, need Ah Q, need to go with the flow, need to be indifferent and listen to nature......我说，我对于这个心理学经典的实验没有发言权。如果布雷迪先生只是借此证明强大的心理压力可以致病，无疑是正确的。

  I say that I have no say in this classic psychological experiment. If Mr. Brady simply uses this to prove that strong psychological stress can cause illness, he is undoubtedly right.停了一会儿，我对她说，你刚才问，假如是我，会在猴子中做怎样的选择。经过考虑，我可以回答你——我愿意做那只得了胃溃疡而仍在不断掀动压杆的猴子。

  After a pause, I said to her, you just asked, if it were me, what choice would you make among monkeys? After consideration, I can answer you - I am willing to be the monkey who has a stomach ulcer and is still constantly lifting the pressure bar.朋友惊讶地笑了，说为什么。她问过许多人，他们都愿做那只无助然而健康的猴子。

  The friend laughed in surprise and said why. She asked many people who would like to be the helpless but healthy monkey.我说，那只无助的猴子健康吗？每隔20秒钟准时到来的电击，是无法逃脱的、不以个人意志为转移的恶性刺激，日复一日，终有一天会瓦解意志和身体，让它精神失常或者干脆得上癌症。

  I said, is the helpless monkey healthy? The electric shock that comes on time every 20 seconds is an escapeable malignant stimulus that is not transferred by personal will, day after day, will one day disintegrate the will and body, making it insane or simply cancerous.它暂时还没有生病，那是因为它的同伴不断地掀动压杆，为它挡去了许多次打击。在别人的护翼下生活，把自己的幸运建筑在他人的辛苦与危险中，我无法安心与习惯。

  It is not sick yet, and that is because its companions keep lifting the lever, blocking many blows for it. Living under the protection of others, building my luck in the hardships and dangers of others, I can't feel at ease and get used to it.再说说那只无法逃避责任的甲猴，既然发现了可以取消一次电击的办法，它继续摸索下去，也许能寻找出更有效的法子，求得更长久的平安。压下去，拖延时间，也许那放电的机器会烧坏，通电的线路会折断，椅子会倒塌，地震会爆发……形形色色的意外都可能发生，只要坚持下去就有希望。

  Let's talk about the armored monkey that cannot escape responsibility, since it has found a way to cancel a shock, it continues to explore, and may be able to find a more effective way to seek longer-lasting peace. If you press down and delay time, maybe the discharging machine will burn out, the energized line will break, the chair will collapse, and the earthquake will break out...... All kinds of accidents can happen, as long as you persevere, there is hope.一百种可能性在远方闪光，避免一次电击，就积累了一次经验。也许实践会使它渐渐熟练起来，心情不再紧张、悲苦，把掀动压杆只当成简单的游戏……不管怎么说，行动比单纯的等待更有力量。一味地顺从与观望，办法绝不会从天上掉下来。

  A hundred possibilities flash in the distance, avoid a single electric shock, and accumulate an experience. Perhaps practice will make it gradually proficient, and the mood is no longer tense and sad, and the lifting of the lever is only a simple game...... In any case, action is more powerful than mere waiting. If you blindly obey and wait and see, the method will never fall from the sky.

  当然，最大的可能是无望，呕血的猴子无奈地掀动压杆到最后一刻……即使是这样，那我也绝不后悔。

  Of course, the biggest possibility is hopelessness, and the blood-vomiting monkey reluctantly lifts the press bar until the last moment...... Even so, I will never regret it.因为——

  Because——假如我和那只猴子是朋友，我愿意把背负的重担留给自己。

  If I were friends with that monkey, I would be willing to keep the burden to myself.假如我和那只猴子是路人，我遵照我喜爱探索的天性行事。

  If I and the monkey were passers-by, I would act according to my nature of exploration.假如我和那只猴子是敌手，我会傲然地处置自己的生命，不在对方的荫庇下苟活。

  If I and the monkey were enemies, I would proudly dispose of my life and not live under the shadow of the other person.所以，天造地设，我只能做那只得了胃溃疡的猴子。
Therefore, I can only be the monkey with stomach ulcers.

未知

  17.比树更长久的

  17. Those that last longer than trees

  人们对于生命比自己更长久的物件，通常抱以恭敬和仰慕；对于活得比自己短暂的东西，则多轻视和俯视。前者比如星空，比如河海，比如久远的庙宇和沙埋的古物。后者比如朝露，比如秋霜，比如瞬息即逝的流萤和轻风。甚至是对于动物和植物，也是比较尊崇那些寿命高的巨松和老龟，而轻慢浮游的孑孓和不知寒冬的秋虫。在这种厚此薄彼的好恶中，折射着人间对于时间的敬畏和对死亡的慑服。

  People usually have respect and admiration for objects that live longer than themselves; For things that live shorter than oneself, they despise and look down on them. The former is like the starry sky, such as rivers and seas, such as ancient temples and antiquities buried in the sand. The latter is like morning dew, autumn frost, like fleeting fireflies and light winds. Even for animals and plants, they also respect those giant pines and old turtles with high lifespans, while despise the floating lonely and autumn insects that do not know the cold winter. In this kind of love and dislike, it reflects the world's reverence for time and fear of death.妈妈说过，人是活不过一棵树的。所以我从小就决定种几棵树，等我死了以后，这些树还活着，替我晒太阳和给人阴凉，包括也养活几条虫子，让鸟在累的时候填饱肚子，然后歇脚和唱歌。我当少先队员的时候，种过白蜡和柳树。后来植树节的时候，又种过杨树和松树。当我在乡下有了几间小屋，有了一块属于自己的小园子之后，我种了玫瑰和玉兰，种了法国梧桐和迎春。有一天，我在路上走，看到一截干枯的树桩，所有的枝都被锯掉了，树根仅剩一些凌乱的须，仿佛一个倒竖的鸡毛掸子。我问老乡，这是什么？老乡说，柴火。我说，我知道它现在是柴火，想知道它以前是什么。老乡说，苹果树。我说，它能结苹果吗？老乡说，结过。我不禁愤然道，为什么要把开花结果的树伐掉？老乡说，修路。

  Mom said that people can't live a tree. So I decided to plant a few trees since I was a child, and when I died, these trees were still alive, basking in the sun and shading for me, including feeding a few insects so that the birds could fill their stomachs when they were tired, and then rest and sing. When I was a Young Pioneer, I planted ash and willow. Later, on Arbor Day, poplar and pine trees were planted. When I had a few cottages in the countryside and a small garden of my own, I planted roses and magnolias, French sycamore and Yingchun. One day, I was walking on the road and saw a dry stump, all the branches were sawn off, and only some messy whiskers were left at the roots, like an upside-down feather duster. I asked the fellow villagers, what is this? The fellow said firewood. I said, I know it's firewood now, wondering what it used to be. The fellow said that the apple tree. I said, can it produce apples? The fellow said that it was over. I couldn't help but say indignantly, why cut down the flowering and fruiting trees? The fellow said that the road was built.公路横穿果园，苹果树只好让路。人们把细的枝条锯下填了灶坑，剩下这拖泥带土的根，连生火的价值都打了折扣，弃在一边。

  The road crosses the orchard, and the apple trees have to give way. People sawed off the thin branches to fill the stove pit, leaving behind the roots that dragged the mud and soil, and even the value of starting a fire was reduced and abandoned.我说，我要是把这树根拿回去栽起来，它会活吗？老乡说，不知道。树的心事，谁知道呢？我惊，说，树也会想心事吗？老乡很肯定地说，会。如果它想活，它就会活。

  I said, if I take this root back and plant it, will it live? The fellow said he didn't know. Who knows what is on the tree's mind? I was shocked and said, does the tree also think about it? The fellow said with certainty, yes. If it wants to live, it will.我把“鸡毛掸子”种在了园子里，挖了一个很大的坑，浇了很多的水。先生说，根须已经折断了大部，根本就用不了这么大的坑，又不是要埋一个人，水也太多了，好像不是种树，是蓄洪。我说，坑就是它的家，水就是它的粮食。我希望它有一份好心情。

  I planted the "feather duster" in the garden, dug a big hole, and watered it a lot. The gentleman said that most of the roots had been broken, and there was no need for such a big pit, and there was no need to bury a person, and there was too much water, as if it was not planting trees, but flood storage. I said, the pit is its home, and the water is its food. I hope it has a good mood.种下苹果树之后的两个月，我一直四处忙，没时间到乡下去。当我再一次推开园子的小门，看到苹果树的时候，惊艳绝倒。苹果树抽出几十根长长短短的枝条，绿叶盈盈，在微风中如同千手观音一般舞着，曼妙多姿。

  For the first two months after planting the apple tree, I was busy and didn't have time to go to the countryside. When I opened the small door of the garden again and saw the apple tree, I was amazed. The apple tree pulls out dozens of long and short branches, with green leaves, dancing like a thousand-handed Guanyin in the breeze, graceful and colorful.我绕着苹果树转了又转，骇然于生命的强韧，甚至不敢去抚摸它紫青色的树干，唯恐惊扰了这欣欣向荣的轮回。此刻的苹果树在我眼中，非但有了心情，简直就有了灵性。

  I circled around the apple tree, amazed at the strength of life, and didn't even dare to touch its purple-blue trunk, for fear of disturbing this thriving reincarnation. At this moment, the apple tree is not only in the mood, but also in spirituality.当我看到云南个旧市老阴山上的文学林的时候，知道自己又碰上了一群有灵性的树。1983年的春天，丁玲、杨沫、白桦、茹志娟、王安忆等二十多位作家，在这里种下了树。21年过去了，我看到一棵高高的杉树，上面挂着一块铭牌，写着“李乔”。李乔是位彝族作家，已然仙逝。我没缘分见到他本人，但我看到了他栽下的树。以后当我想起他的时候，记不得他的音容笑貌，但会闪现出这棵高大的杉树。李乔已经把生命的一部分嫁接到杉树的枝叶里，这棵杉树从此有了自己的名姓。

  When I saw the literary forest on Laoyin Mountain in an old city in Yunnan, I knew that I had encountered a group of spiritual trees again. In the spring of 1983, more than 20 writers, including Ding Ling, Yang Mo, Bai Hua, Ru Zhijuan, and Wang Anyi, planted trees here. Twenty-one years later, I saw a tall fir tree with a nameplate hanging on it that read "Li Qiao". Li Qiao is a Yi writer who has passed away. I didn't have the chance to see him in person, but I saw the tree he planted. When I think of him in the future, I can't remember his voice and smile, but I will flash this tall fir tree. Li Qiao has grafted part of his life into the branches and leaves of the fir tree, and this fir tree has since had its own name.也许是考虑到每人一棵树，不一定能保证成活，也不一定能保证多少年后依然健在，这次聚会，栽树的仪式改为大家同栽一棵树。这是一棵很大的树，枝叶繁茂。我也挤在人群中扬了几锹土，然后悄悄问旁人，这是一棵什么树？

  Perhaps considering that each person's tree may not guarantee survival, nor may it guarantee that it will still be alive many years later, this gathering, the tree planting ceremony was changed to everyone planting a tree together. It is a large tree with lush branches and leaves. I also crowded into the crowd and raised a few shovels, and then quietly asked others, what kind of tree is this?是棕树的一种，国家二类保护树种呢！工作人员告诉我。

  It is a kind of palm tree, a national second-class protected tree species! The staff told me.这棵树能活多少年呢？我又追问。

  How many years can this tree live? I asked again.这个……不大清楚，想来，一百年总是有的吧。工作人员沉吟着。

  This one...... It's not clear, but if you think about it, there will always be a hundred years. The staff pondered.我看着那棵新栽下的棕树，心想不管它的寿命多么长久，总有凋亡的那一天。也许是被雷火劈中，也许是被山洪冲毁，也许是被冰霜压垮，也许是被盗木者砍伐……总之，一棵树也像一个人一样，有无数种死法，总之是不会永远长青的。

  I looked at the newly planted palm tree and thought to myself, no matter how long it lives, there will always be a day when it will die. Maybe it was struck by lightning, maybe it was washed away by flash floods, maybe it was crushed by frost, or maybe it was cut down by wood thieves...... In short, a tree is like a person, there are countless ways to die, in short, it will not be evergreen.在栽树的时候，去谋划一棵树的死亡，这近乎是刻毒了。我不想诅咒一棵树。鉴于一个人总是要死的，人们寄希望于那些比个体生命更悠远的事物。但一棵树也是会死的，即使像我捡来的苹果树那样顽强且有好心情的树，也是会死的。既然树木无望，我们只有寄托于精神的不灭。

  When planting a tree, planning the death of a tree is almost cruel. I don't want to curse a tree. Given that a person is always going to die, people hope for things that are farther than individual life. But a tree can die, even a tree as tenacious and in a good mood as the apple tree I picked up. Since trees are hopeless, we can only pin on the immortality of the spirit.一个人是活不过一棵树的，然而再古老的树也有尽头。在所有树的上面，飞翔着我们不灭的精神，而文学是精神之林的一片红叶。

  A person cannot live a tree, but no matter how old the tree is, there is an end. Above all the trees, our immortal spirit flies, and literature is a red leaf in the forest of spirits.

未知

  18.教养的证据

  18. Evidence of upbringing

  “教养”是个高频词。时下，如果说某人没教养，就是大批评大贬义了。如果说一个女人没教养，简直就如同说她是三陪小姐了。

  "Upbringing" is a high-frequency word. Nowadays, if someone is uneducated, it is a big criticism and a big derogatory meaning. If a woman is uneducated, it is like saying that she is a third escort lady.什么叫教养呢？词典上说是“文化和品德的修养”，但我更愿意理解为“因教育而养成的优良品质和习惯”。

  What is upbringing? The dictionary says "cultural and moral cultivation", but I prefer to understand it as "good qualities and habits developed through education".一个人可以受过教育，但他依然是没有教养的。就像一个人可以不停地吃东西，但他的肠胃不吸收，竹篮打水一场空，还是骨瘦如柴。不过，这话似乎不能反过来说——一个人没有受过系统的教育，他却能够很有教养。

  A person can be educated, but he is still uneducated. Just like a person can eat non-stop, but his stomach does not absorb it, and the bamboo basket is empty, and he is still skinny. However, it seems that this cannot be said the other way around - a person who is not systematically educated can be very educated.教养不是天生的。一个小孩子如果没有人教给他良好的习惯和有关的知识，他必定是愚昧和粗浅的。当然，这个“教”是广义的，除了指入学经师，也包括家长的言传身教和环境的耳濡目染。

  Upbringing is not innate. A child must be ignorant and superficial if no one teaches him good habits and knowledge. Of course, this "teaching" is broad, in addition to referring to the enrollment of the teacher, but also to the words and deeds of parents and the influence of the environment.教养和财富一样，是需要证据的。你说你有钱不成，得拿出一个资产证明。教养的证据不是你读过多少书，家庭背景如何显赫，也不是你通晓多少礼节规范，能够熟练使用刀叉，会穿晚礼服……这些仅仅是一些表面的气泡，最关键的证据可能有如下若干。

  Upbringing, like wealth, requires evidence. You say you can't make money if you have money, you have to show an asset certificate. The evidence of upbringing is not how many books you have read, how prominent your family background is, nor how much etiquette you know, how skillful you are in using knives and forks, and how to wear evening dresses...... These are just some superficial bubbles, and the most critical evidence may be as follows.热爱大自然。把它列为有教养的证据之首，是因为一个不懂得敬畏大自然、不知道人类渺小的人，必是井底之蛙，与教养谬之千里。这也许怪不得他，因为如果不经教育，一个人是很难自发地懂得宇宙之大和人类的微薄的。没有相应的自然科学知识，人除了显得蒙昧和狭隘以外，注定也是盲目傲慢的。之所以从小就教育孩子要爱护花草，正是这种伟大感悟的最基本的训练。若是看到一个成人野蛮地攀折林木，通常人们就会毫不迟疑地评判道：这个人太没有教养了。可见教养和绿色是紧密地联系在一起。懂得与自然协调地相处，懂得爱护无言的植物的人，推而广之，他多半也可能会爱惜更多的动物，爱护自己的同类。

  Love nature. It is listed as the first evidence of educated because a person who does not know how to revere nature and does not know the smallness of human beings must be a frog at the bottom of the well, a thousand miles away from the absurdity of upbringing. This may not be to blame him, because without education, it is difficult for a person to spontaneously understand the vastness of the universe and the meager nature of human beings. Without corresponding knowledge of natural sciences, in addition to appearing ignorant and narrow-minded, people are destined to be blind and arrogant. The reason why children should be taught to love flowers and plants from an early age is the most basic training of this great perception. If you see an adult climbing a tree savagely, people will usually judge without hesitation: This person is too uneducated. It can be seen that upbringing and green are closely linked. A person who knows how to live in harmony with nature and knows how to care for silent plants may also cherish more animals and love his own kind.一个有教养的人，应该能够自如地运用公共的语言，表达自己的内心和同他人交流，并能妥帖地付诸文字。我所说的公共语言，是指大家——从普通民众到知识分子都能理解的清洁和明亮的语言，而不是某种狭窄的土语俚语或者某特定情境下的专业语言。这个要求并非画蛇添足，在这个千帆竞发的时代，太多的人，只会说他那个行业的内部语言，只会说机器、仪器能听懂的语言，却不懂得和人亲密地交流。这不是一个批评，而是一个事实。和人的交流的掌握，特别是和陌生人的沟通，通常不是自发产生的，是要通过学习和练习来获得的。一个没有受过教育的人，他所掌握的词汇是有限的和贫乏的，除了描绘自己的生理感受，比如饿了、渴了、睡觉以及生殖的欲望之外，他们对于自己的内心感知甚为模糊，因为那些描述内心感受的词语，通常是抽象和长于比兴的。不通过学习，难以明确恰当地将它表达出来。那些虽然拥有一技之长但无法精彩地运用公共语言这种神圣的媒介来沟通和解读自我心灵的人，难以算是一个有教养的人。技术是用来谋生的，而仅仅具有谋生的本领是不够的。就像豺狼也会自发地猎取食物一样，那是近乎无须教育也可掌握的本能。而人，毫无疑问地应比豺狼更高一筹。

  An educated person should be able to use public language freely, express his heart and communicate with others, and be able to put it into words properly. By public language, I mean a clean and bright language that can be understood by everyone, from ordinary people to intellectuals, rather than some kind of narrow vernacular slang or professional language in a particular situation. In this era of thousands of sails, too many people can only speak the internal language of their industry, only the language that machines and instruments can understand, but they do not know how to communicate intimately with people. This is not a criticism, but a fact. Mastery of communication with people, especially with strangers, is usually not spontaneous, but is acquired through learning and practice. An uneducated person has a limited and poor vocabulary, and in addition to describing his physical feelings, such as hunger, thirst, sleep, and the desire to reproduce, they have a vague sense of their inner feelings, because the words that describe inner feelings are usually abstract and longer than Bixing. Without learning, it is difficult to express it clearly and appropriately. Those who have a skill but cannot use the sacred medium of public language to communicate and interpret their own minds are hardly educated. Technology is used to make a living, and it is not enough to have the ability to make a living. Just as jackals spontaneously hunt for food, it is an instinct that can be mastered almost without education. And man, without a doubt, should be a step higher than a jackal.一个有教养的人，对历史有恰如其分的了解，知道身而为人，我们走过了怎样曲折的道路。当然，教养并不能使每个人都像历史学家那样博古通今，但是教养能使一个有思考爱好的人知晓我们是从哪里来，要到哪里去。教养通过历史使我们不单活在此时此刻，也活在从前和以后，如同生活在一条奔腾的大河里，知道泉眼和海洋的方向。

  A well-educated person has a proper understanding of history and knows what kind of tortuous road we have gone through as human beings. Of course, education does not make everyone as knowledgeable as historians, but education can make a person with a hobby of thinking know where we come from and where we are going. Through history, we live not only in the present moment, but also in the past and in the future, as if we were living in a rushing river, knowing the direction of the spring and the ocean.一个有教养的人，除了眼前的事物和得失以外，他还会不由自主地想到他远大的目标。教养把人的注意力拓展了，变得宏大和光明。每一个个体都有沉没在黑暗峡谷的时刻，当你在跋涉和攀缘中，虽然伤痕累累，因为你具有的教养，确知时间是流动的，明了暂时与永久。相信在遥远的地方定有峡谷的出口，那里有瀑布在轰鸣。

  A well-educated person, in addition to the things in front of him and the gains and losses, will also involuntarily think of his lofty goals. Upbringing expands people's attention, becomes grand and bright. Every individual has a moment of sinking into a dark canyon, when you are trekking and climbing, although scarred, because of your upbringing, you know that time is flowing, and you know that it is temporary and permanent. It is believed that in the distance there must be an exit from the canyon, where a waterfall roars.

  一个有教养的人，特别是女人，对自己的身体有着亲切的了解和珍惜之情，知道它们各自独有的清晰的名称，明了它们是精致和洁净的，身体的每一部分都有着不可替代的功能，并无高低贵贱的区别。他知道自己的快乐和满足，有很大的一部分是建筑在这些功能灵敏的感知上和健全的完整上的。他也毫无疑义地知道，他的大脑是他的身体的主宰。他不会任由他的器官牵制他的所作所为，他是清醒和有驾驭力的。他在尊重自己身体的同时，也尊重他人的身体。在尊重自我的权利的同时，也尊重他人的权利。在驰骋自我意志的骏马时，也精心维护着他人的茵茵草地。

  A well-educated person, especially a woman, has a cordial understanding and cherishment of his own body, knows their own clear names, knows that they are delicate and clean, and that every part of the body has an irreplaceable function, and there is no difference between high and low. He knows that his happiness and satisfaction are largely built on the sensitive perception and sound integrity of these functions. He also knew without a doubt that his brain was the master of his body. He will not let his organs hold back what he does, he is sober and controlling. He respects his own body as well as the bodies of others. While respecting one's own rights, we also respect the rights of others. When galloping a horse of its own will, it also carefully maintains the grass of others.一个有教养的人，对人类种种优秀的品质，比如忠诚、勇敢、信任、勤勉、互助、舍己救人、临危不惧、吃苦耐劳、坚贞不屈……充满敬重、敬畏、敬仰之心。不一定每一个人都能够身体力行，但他们懂得爱戴和歌颂。人不是不可以怯懦和懒惰，但他不能把这些陋习伪装成高风亮节，不能由于自己做不到高尚，就诋毁所有做到了这些的人是伪善。你可以跪在泥里，但你不可以把污泥抹上整个世界的胸膛，并因此像煞有介事地说到处都是污垢。

  A well-educated person has all kinds of excellent qualities for human beings, such as loyalty, bravery, trust, diligence, mutual assistance, self-sacrifice to save others, fearlessness in the face of danger, hardship, and perseverance...... Full of respect, awe and admiration. Not everyone can do it themselves, but they know how to love and praise. It is not that man cannot be cowardly and lazy, but he cannot disguise these bad habits as noble and virtuous, and he cannot slander all those who have done these things as hypocrisy because they cannot be noble. You can kneel in the mud, but you can't smear the sludge on the chest of the whole world, and thus say that there is dirt everywhere.有教养的人知道害怕。知道害怕，是件有意义、有价值的事情。它表示明了自己的限制，知道世上有一些不可逾越的界限，知道世界上有阳光，阳光下有正义的惩罚。由于害怕正义的惩罚，因而约束自我，是意志力坚强的一种体现。

  Cultured people know to be afraid. Knowing that fear is a meaningful and valuable thing. It shows that there are some insurmountable boundaries in the world, that there is sunshine in the world, and that there is righteous punishment under the sun. Because of the fear of justice punishment, self-restraint is a manifestation of strong willpower.有教养的人知道仰视高山和宇宙，知道仰视那些伟大的发现和人格，知道对于自己无法企及的高度表达尊重，而不是糊涂地闭上眼睛或居心叵测地嘲讽。

  A well-educated person knows how to look up to the mountains and the universe, to those great discoveries and personalities, and to express respect for a high level beyond their reach, rather than closing their eyes in a daze or mocking with ulterior motives.

  教养是不可一蹴而就的。教养是细水长流的。教养是可以遗失也可以捡拾起来的。教养也具有某种坚定的流传和既定的轨道性。教养是一些习惯的总和，在某种程度上，教养不是活在我们的皮肤上，而是繁衍在我们的骨髓里。教养和遗传几乎是不相关的，是后天和社会的产物。教养必须有酵母，在潜移默化和条件反射的共同烘烤下，假以足够的时日，才能自然而然地散发出香气。教养是衡量一个民族整体素质的一张X光片子。脸面上可以依靠化妆繁花似锦，但只有内在的健硕才经得起冲刷和考验，才是力量的象征。
Upbringing cannot be achieved overnight. Upbringing is a long process. Upbringing can be lost or picked up. Education also has a certain firm circulation and established track. Upbringing is the sum of habits, and to some extent, upbringing does not live on our skin, but reproduces in our bone marrow. Upbringing and heredity are almost unrelated, acquired and social products. Upbringing must have yeast, and under the joint roasting of subtle and conditioned reflexes, it will naturally emit aroma with enough time. Education is an X-ray film to measure the overall quality of a nation. The face can rely on makeup to bloom, but only inner strength can withstand washing and testing, and is a symbol of strength.

未知

  19.格子布上的花

  19. Flowers on plaid

  好日子和坏日子，是有一定比例的。就是说，你的一生，不可能都是好日子——天天蜜里调油，也不可能都是坏日子——每时每刻黄连拌苦胆。必是好日子坏日子交叉着来，如同一块花格子布。如果算下来，你的好日子多，就如同布面上的红黄色多，亮堂鲜艳。如果你的坏日子多，那就是黑灰色多，阴云密布。

  There is a certain proportion of good days and bad days. That is to say, your life cannot be a good day - every day is mixed with honey, nor can it be a bad day - every moment is mixed with bitter gall. It must be a good day and a bad day intersect, like a flower plaid. If you count it, you have many good days, just like the red and yellow on the cloth, bright and bright. If you have a lot of bad days, it's a lot of black and gray and cloudy.什么是好坏日子的分水岭、试金石呢？钱吗？好像不是。有钱的人不一定承认他过的是好日子。钱少的人或者没钱的人，也不一定感觉他过的是坏日子。健康吗？好像也不是。无痛无灾的人不一定觉得他过的是好日子，罹病残疾的人也不一定承认他过的是坏日子。美丽和能力吗？似乎更不像了。看看周围，有多少漂亮能干的男人女人，锁着眉，苦着脸，抱怨着岁月的难熬啊……说了若干标准，都不是。那么，什么是好日子和坏日子的界限呢？

  What is the watershed and touchstone of good and bad days? Money? It doesn't seem to be the case. A rich person does not necessarily admit that he is living a good life. People with little money or no money may not necessarily feel that they are living a bad life. Is it healthy? It doesn't seem to be either. A painless person may not necessarily feel that he is living a good life, and a person with illness and disability may not necessarily admit that he is living a bad life. Beauty and ability? It seems even less like it. Look around, how many beautiful and capable men and women are locking their eyebrows, with bitter faces, complaining about the hardships of the years...... I said a few standards, but they are not. So, what is the line between good and bad days?不知他人的答案如何，我猜，是爱吧？

  I don't know what other people's answers are, I guess, it's love, right?有爱的日子，也许我们很穷，但每一分钱都能带给我们双倍的快乐。也许我们的身体坏了，每况愈下，但我们牵着相爱的人的手慢慢老去，旅途就不再孤独。也许我们是平凡和微小的，但我们竭尽全力做着喜欢的事，心中便充满温暖安宁。

  On days of love, maybe we are poor, but every penny can bring us double happiness. Maybe our bodies are broken and deteriorating, but we hold the hands of our loved ones and grow old, and the journey is no longer lonely. Maybe we are ordinary and small, but we do our best to do what we love, and our hearts are filled with warmth and peace.这是什么呢？这就是好日子了。你的那块花格子布上就绽开了鲜花。

  What is this? This is a good day. Flowers bloom on your flower plaid.

未知

  20.溪水金沙

  20. Stream Golden Sand

  人的天性如溪水，学习的本能就是金沙。它们潜伏在水中，浪花翻溅时一眼看不到它的颗粒，但因了它们的存在，水变得更有分量和价值。

  Human nature is like a stream, and the instinct to learn is golden sand. They lurk in the water, and their particles are not visible at a glance when the waves splash, but because of their presence, the water becomes more weighty and valuable.我相信那些不含有金沙的小溪已经干涸，因为人类生存的环境曾经并且还将是刺骨险恶，你一个人的经历是不丰富的，你同时代的借鉴是不全面的，你一个行业的规则是不完整的……如果不爱学习、不善于学习、不坚持学习的话，就会被层出不穷的打击和灾变来征伐与掩埋，这个人的遗传基因就昙花一现地湮灭了。

  I believe that those streams that do not contain golden sand have dried up, because the environment in which human beings live has been and will be bitterly sinister, your experience alone is not rich, your contemporaries are not comprehensive, and the rules of your industry are incomplete...... If you don't love to learn, are not good at learning, and don't persist in learning, you will be conquered and buried by endless blows and disasters, and this person's genetic genes will be wiped out in the pan.所以，乐观地说，我们每个人都是那些爱学习的人的后代，唯有这项潜藏在血液中的专擅，令我们比所有的动物都更繁荣递进。

  So, optimistically, each of us is a descendant of those who love to learn, and only this mastery hidden in our blood makes us more prosperous than all animals.学习是有很多种方法的，比如抬头望天，你可以学到星空的叙事是无与伦比的宏大，滋生出的渺小和畏惧感让你一生警醒、谦逊。比如低头俯地，你可以窥到万物葱茏、物竞天择、优胜劣汰、残酷、公平，激发出的紧迫和危机感让你不敢有一刻懈怠与放松。比如听妈妈讲那过去的事情，你会生出无限的柔情，不但绕指，更是绕心。比如看风光大片、科幻影像，你会惊骇莫名，有一种充满未知的狂喜和震撼……

  There are many ways to learn, such as looking up at the sky, you can learn that the narrative of the starry sky is incomparably grand, and the sense of insignificance and fear that breeds will make you vigilant and humble throughout your life. For example, bowing your head to the ground, you can see the lushness of everything, natural selection, survival of the fittest, cruelty, fairness, and the sense of urgency and crisis inspired by it makes you dare not slack and relax for a moment. For example, listening to your mother talk about the past, you will have infinite tenderness, not only around your fingers, but also around your heart. For example, when you watch a blockbuster or science fiction image, you will be shocked and inexplicable, and there is a kind of ecstasy and shock full of unknowns......然而，我以为最好的学习还是阅读。

  However, I thought the best learning was still reading.首先我们要感谢文字，因为有了文字，我们的情感血脉才有了附着的骨骼，我们的理论枝蔓才有了攀缘的篱笆，我们的科技成果才有了传袭的衣钵，我们的历史才有了一面面古镜矗立照耀。

  First of all, we must be grateful for words, because with words, our emotional bloodline has an attached bone, our theoretical branches have a climbing fence, our scientific and technological achievements have a mantle to be passed down, and our history has ancient mirrors standing and shining.时代进步，从布帛竹简到计算机液晶屏，书写变得越来越快，阅读变得越来越方便了。记得我小时候，看一本长篇小说要个把星期，那还算快的呢！借书给朋友，不过百八十页，半个月后要她还，她说，这才几天啊你就催，我还没看完呢，小气呀小气！

  With the progress of the times, from silk and bamboo slips to computer LCD screens, writing has become faster and faster, and reading has become more and more convenient. I remember when I was a child, it took me a week to read a novel, which was quite fast! Lending a book to a friend, but one hundred and eighty pages, half a month later asked her to pay it back, she said, it's only been a few days, you urge me, I haven't finished reading it yet, stingy, stingy!读书，一种是技艺之书，讲的是各行各业的特殊规则；还有一种是普遍的知识，比如文史哲。读行业之书的人多，读普遍知识的人少。有一年我到国内著名的一所医科大学授课，我说，你们这些未来中国最杰出的医生，有谁读过《红字》？有谁读过《罪与罚》？请举手。台下抬臂者寥寥。在感谢了这些博士生的诚实之后，我深表遗憾。一个医生，除了读医书以外，也要读艺术。因为你面对的不是一只装满了病痛脓血的破罐子，而是一个活生生的人。生死契阔啊，他们在最悲苦无助的时候和你狭路相逢，你要医治他，不仅仅是凭着你的精湛医术，而且要凭着你强大的人格和综合的力量。如果你想当一个名医而非庸医，请在读医书的同时也展读人文科学方面的书籍。提高了你的素养，是你的福气，是你爹妈、妻子、丈夫、孩子的福气，同时也造福了你的病患。

  Reading, one is a book of skills, which talks about the special rules of all walks of life; There is also a universal knowledge, such as literature, history, and philosophy. There are many people who read industry books and few people who read general knowledge. One year I went to a famous medical university in China to teach, and I said, who of you will read "The Red Letter"? Has anyone read Crime and Punishment? Please raise your hand. There are very few people in the audience who raise their arms. After thanking these doctoral students for their honesty, I deeply regret it. A doctor, in addition to reading medical books, also needs to read art. Because you are not facing a broken jar full of pain, pus and blood, but a living person. Life and death are at stake, they meet you in the most miserable and helpless time, and you have to heal him, not only with your superb medical skills, but also with your strong personality and comprehensive strength. If you want to be a famous doctor instead of a quack doctor, please read books on the humanities at the same time as reading medical books. Improving your literacy is your blessing, your parents, wife, husband, and children, and it also benefits your patients.我相信，一个读过很多专业以外书籍的建筑师盖出的楼房一定更漂亮和更实用。我相信，一个读过很多专业以外书籍的学者，授课传业的时候一定更风趣、更幽默、更旁征博引、口吐莲花。我相信，一个读过很多专业以外书籍的科学家，提出的设想和理论一定更曲径通幽、独树一帜。我相信，一个读过很多专业以外书籍的管理者，企业一定更具活力和创新精神。

  I believe that a building built by an architect who has read many books outside of his profession must be more beautiful and practical. I believe that a scholar who has read many books outside his profession must be more funny, humorous, more introductory, and more spitting lotus flowers when teaching. I believe that a scientist who has read many books outside his profession must put forward ideas and theories that are more convoluted and unique. I believe that a manager who has read many books outside of his profession must be more energetic and innovative.我们曾经有过阅读备感艰难的时代。高玉宝的“我要读书”就是明证。那时候的无法阅读，是因为贫困和压迫。后来又有过对知识的蔑视，阅读也被视为通向反动的阶梯。我上初中一年级的时候，正逢“文化大革命”。学校停课闹革命，图书馆也关闭了，任何人不得进入。得知可以不再读书的第一天，心情像焰火一样蓬松、绚烂。但日子一天天驰去，牙口徒长，知识却永远停留在十三岁的水平，那种渐进式的痛楚巨蚕噬桑般把意志镂空。后来，图书馆开了一道小小的门缝，说是可以借阅“毒草”了，代价是你看完一本之后要交出一篇大批判文章。还书的时候，批判稿须一并附上，如果审查合格，就可以继续借阅；如果你敷衍了事或者干脆交不出批判文章，便永久取消你的借阅资格。

  We have had times when reading was difficult. Gao Yubao's "I want to read" is proof of this. At that time, I couldn't read because of poverty and oppression. Later, there was a contempt for knowledge, and reading was also seen as a ladder to reaction. When I was in the first grade of junior high school, it coincided with the "Cultural Revolution". Schools were closed for revolution, and libraries were closed, and no one was allowed to enter. On the first day I learned that I could stop studying, my mood was as fluffy and brilliant as fireworks. But the days go by day by day, the teeth are long, but the knowledge will always stay at the level of thirteen years old, and the gradual pain of the giant silkworm devours the will like a mulberry. Later, the library opened a small crack in the door, saying that it was possible to borrow "poisonous weeds", but at the cost of handing over a big critical article after reading one. When returning the book, the critical manuscript must be attached, and if it passes the review, it can continue to be borrowed; If you are perfunctory or simply unable to submit a critical article, you will be permanently disqualified.大家蜂拥去借书。但几轮之下，就门可罗雀了。规则严苛，审查文稿者声色俱厉，拥有借阅资格的人越来越少。我面临一个悖论。我喜爱“毒草”的芬芳，可我不得不批判它们。为了能继续阅读，只有口是心非。记得我曾面对苍穹向大师们祷告，说，你们既然能创造出那么多心境复杂的人物，一定也能体谅一个中国女孩此时的难处，为了能亲近你们，就原谅我说你们的一点点坏话吧，请不要生气……

  Everyone flocked to borrow books. But after a few rounds, the door can be scattered. The rules are strict, the reviewers are fierce, and fewer and fewer people have the qualifications to borrow. I am faced with a paradox. I love the fragrance of "poisonous weeds", but I have to criticize them. In order to continue reading, there is only duplicity. I remember that I once prayed to the masters in front of the sky, saying, since you can create so many characters with complex feelings, you must also be considerate of the difficulties of a Chinese girl at this time, in order to get close to you, please forgive me for saying a little bad things about you, please don't be angry......

  现如今，很多人不再贫穷，也没有人压制阅读，可时间成了瓶颈，很多人苦恼的是总也找不到空闲来阅读。

  Nowadays, many people are no longer poor, and no one suppresses reading, but time has become a bottleneck, and many people are distressed that they can't find free time to read.那是因为有太多的诱惑。

  That's because there are too many temptations.阅读是没有香氛的，于是抵不过餐桌的美味。阅读是孤独的，于是没有觥筹交错的热闹。阅读是伴有思考和停顿的，于是没有游戏般的顺畅和惬意。阅读甚至是充满碰撞和痛楚的，因为有忏悔的顾盼和掘进的深入。

  Reading has no fragrance, so it cannot withstand the deliciousness of the table. Reading is lonely, so there is no staggered excitement. Reading is accompanied by thinking and pause, so there is no smoothness and comfort like a game. Reading is even full of collisions and pains, because there is a repentant and a deep digging.但是，优秀的阅读是有力量的，因为在阅读的时候，你不是一个人，而是和古今中外的先驱者们并行。
However, excellent reading is powerful, because when reading, you are not alone, but parallel to the pioneers of ancient and modern China and abroad.

未知

  21.拍卖你的生涯

  21. Auction off your career

  朋友参加过一堂很别致的讲座，对我详细地描绘了一番。

  A friend attended a very chic lecture and described it in detail.讲座叫作《拍卖你的生涯》。外籍老师发给每人一张纸，其上打印着数十行字。

  The lecture is called "Auction Your Career". The foreign teacher gave each person a piece of paper with dozens of lines printed on it.1.豪宅

  1. Mansion2.巨富

  2. Huge wealth3.一张取之不尽、用之不竭的信用卡

  3. An inexhaustible credit card4.美貌贤惠的妻子或英俊博学的丈夫

  4. A beautiful and virtuous wife or a handsome and learned husband5.一门精湛的技艺

  5. A superb skill6.一座小岛

  6. A small island7.一座宏大的图书馆

  7. A grand library8.和你的情人浪迹天涯

  8. Wander the world with your lover9.一个勤劳忠诚的仆人

  9. A hardworking and loyal servant10.三五个知心朋友

  10. Three or five close friends11.一份价值50万美元并每年可获得25%纯利收入的股票

  11. A stock worth $500,000 and a net profit income of 25% per year12.名垂青史

  12. Fame in history13.一张免费旅游世界的机票

  13. A free ticket to travel the world14.和家人共度周末

  14. Spend weekends with your family15.直言不讳的勇敢和百折不挠的真诚

  15. Outspoken bravery and indomitable sincerity……

  大家先是愣愣地看着这些项目，之后交头接耳地笑，感觉甚好。本来嘛，全世界的美事和优良品质差不多都集中在此了。

  Everyone looked at these projects in a daze, and then laughed at each other, which felt very good. Originally, the good things and good quality of the world were almost concentrated here.老师拿起一只小锤子，轻敲讲台，蜂房般的教室寂静下来。老师说（他能讲不很普通的普通话），我手里是一只旧锤子，但今天它有某种权威——暂时充当拍卖锤。我要拍卖的东西，就是在座诸位的生涯。

  The teacher picked up a small hammer and tapped the podium, and the beehive-like classroom fell silent. The teacher said (he can speak not very ordinary Mandarin) that I have an old hammer in my hand, but today it has some kind of authority - temporarily acting as an auction hammer. What I want to auction is the careers of all of you.课堂顿起混乱。生涯？一个叫人生出沧桑和迷茫的词语。我们大致明白什么是生存，什么是生活，但很不清楚什么是生涯。我们只是一天天随波逐流地过着，也许70岁的时候才恍然大悟，生涯已在朦胧中渐近尾声了。

  The classroom was in chaos. Career? A word that calls life vicissitudes and confusion. We roughly understand what survival is and what life is, but it is not clear what career is. We just go with the flow day by day, and maybe when we are 70 years old, we suddenly realize that our careers are coming to an end in a haze.老师说，一个人的生涯，就是你人生的追求和事业。它可以掌握在你自己手中。性格就是命运。生涯从属于你的价值观。通常当人们谈到生涯的时候，总觉得有太多的不可把握性埋藏在未知中。其实它并非想象中那般神秘莫测。今天，我想通过这个游戏，让大家比较清晰地看到自己的爱好，预测自己的生涯。

  The teacher said that a person's career is your life's pursuit and career. It can be in your own hands. Character is destiny. Career is subordinate to your values. Usually when people talk about career, they always feel that there is too much uncertainty buried in the unknown. In fact, it is not as mysterious as imagined. Today, I want to use this game to let everyone see their hobbies more clearly and predict their careers.大家听明白了，好奇地跃跃欲试。

  Everyone understood and was curious to try.我相信在每一个成人的内心深处，都潜伏着一个爱做游戏的天真孩童，只不过随着时光流逝，蒙上了世故的尘土。

  I believe that deep in the heart of every adult, there is a naïve child who loves to play games, but with the passage of time, it is covered with the dust of the world.成年以后的我们，远离游戏，以为那是幼稚可笑的玩闹。其实好的游戏，具有启蒙人的智慧，通达人的思维，启迪人的感悟，让人反省的力量。当我们做游戏的时候，就更接近了真我。

  As adults, we stay away from games and think it is a childish and ridiculous play. In fact, a good game has the power to enlighten people's wisdom, understand people's thinking, enlighten people's perceptions, and make people reflect. When we play games, we are closer to our true selves.老师说，我现在象征性地发给每人1000元钱，代表你一生的时间和精力。我会把这张纸上所列的诸项境况裁成片，一一举起，这就等于开始了拍卖。你们可以用自己手中的积蓄购买这些可能性。100元钱起叫，欢迎竞价。当我连喊三次，无人再出高价的时候，锤子就会落下，这项生涯就属于你了。注意，我说的是可能性，并非是真正的事实。它的意思就是——你用999元竞得了豪宅，但并不等于你真的拥有了仙境般的别墅，只是说你将穷尽一生的精力来为自己争取。相信只要你竭尽全力，把目标当成整个生涯的支撑点，实现的可能性甚大。

  The teacher said that I am now giving each person a symbolic 1,000 yuan, which represents your time and energy in your life. I will cut the situations listed on this paper into pieces and lift them up one by one, which is equivalent to starting the auction. You can buy these possibilities with your savings. 100 yuan is called, welcome to bid. When I shout three times in a row and no one pays a high price again, the hammer will fall, and this career will belong to you. Note that I am talking about possibilities, not real facts. What it means is that you bid for a mansion for 999 yuan, but it does not mean that you really own a fairyland-like villa, it just means that you will exhaust your life's energy to fight for yourself. I believe that as long as you do your best and take the goal as the support point of your entire career, it is very likely to be achieved.教室里的气氛，骚动之后有些沉重。这游戏的分量举轻若重，它把我们人生的繁杂目的约分并形象化了——拼此一生，你到底要什么？

  The atmosphere in the classroom was a little heavy after the commotion. The weight of this game is as light as ever, it divides and visualizes the complex purpose of our lives - what do you want for this life?

  老师举起了第一项拍卖品——拥有一座小岛。起价100元。

  The teacher held up the first auction item - owning a small island. Prices start at $100.全场寂静。一座小岛？它在哪里？南半球还是北半球？大西洋还是太平洋？面积多少？人口多少？有无石油和珊瑚礁？风光怎样？

  The audience was silent. An island? Where is it? Southern or Northern Hemisphere? Atlantic or Pacific? What is the area? What is the population? Is there oil and coral reefs? How is the scenery?疑声四起，大家迫切希望老师提供更详尽的资料，关于那座小岛，关于风土人情。老师一脸肃然，坚定地举着那个纸片，拒绝做更进一步的解说。

  Doubts arose, and everyone eagerly hoped that the teacher would provide more detailed information, about the island, about the customs. The teacher looked solemn, held the piece of paper firmly, and refused to give further explanations.于是，我们明白了。小岛，就是小小的、普普通通的一座无名岛。你愿不愿以一生作赌，去赢得这块海洋中的绿地？

  So, we get it. A small island is a small, ordinary nameless island. Would you rather gamble your life to win this green space in the ocean?终于，一个平日最爱探险、充满生命活力的女生大声地喊出了第一个竞价——我出200！

  Finally, a girl who loves adventure and is full of vitality shouted out loudly - I will pay 200!一个男生几乎是下意识地报出：500！他的心思在那一瞬很简单，买下荒凉岛屿这样的事就该是男子汉干的。

  A boy almost subconsciously reported: 500! His mind was very simple at that moment, buying a desolate island should be done by a man.但那名个子不高、意志顽强的女生志在必得，她涨红着脸，一下子喊出了……1000！

  But the girl who was not tall and tenacious was determined to win, and she blushed and shouted out all of a sudden...... 1000！这是天价了。每个人只有1000元钱的贮备，也就是说，她已下定以毕生的精力赢得这座小岛的决心，别的人只有望洋兴叹了。

  This is a sky-high price. Each person only has 1,000 yuan in reserve, that is, she has made up her mind to win this small island with her life's energy, and the others can only sigh at the ocean.那个男生有些悻悻地说，竞价应该一点点攀升，比如她要出600，我喊700……这样也可给别人一个机会。

  The boy said a little angrily that the bidding should rise little by little, for example, she wants to pay 600, I shout 700...... This can also give others a chance.老师淡然一笑说，我们只是象征性地拍卖，所以可能不合规矩。大家要记住，生涯也如战场，假如你已坚定地确认了自己的目标，就紧紧锁定它。机遇仿佛闪电。

  The teacher smiled indifferently and said that we were only auctioning symbolically, so it might not be in accordance with the rules. Remember, career is like a battlefield, if you have firmly confirmed your goal, lock it tightly. Opportunities are like lightning.大家明白了竞争的激烈，肃静中有了潜藏的紧迫感和若隐若现的敌意。

  Everyone understood the fierce competition, and there was a hidden sense of urgency and looming hostility in the silence.

  拍卖的第二项是美貌贤惠的妻子或英俊博学的丈夫。

  The second item in the auction is a beautiful and virtuous wife or a handsome and learned husband.我原以为此项会导致激烈的竞拍，没想到一时门可罗雀，也许因为它太传统和古板。被其他更刺激的生涯吸引，大伙儿不愿在刚开场不久就把自己的一生交付伴侣的怀抱。好在和美的家庭终对人有不衰的吸引力，在竞争不激烈的情形下，被一位性情温和的男子以700元买去。

  I thought this would lead to fierce bidding, but I didn't expect it to be a mess, maybe because it was too traditional and old-fashioned. Attracted by other, more exciting careers, everyone is reluctant to give their lives to their partners shortly after the opening. Fortunately, Hemei's family eventually had an enduring attraction to people, and in the case of fierce competition, it was bought by a gentle man for 700 yuan.我把指关节攥得紧紧，如果真有一沓钞票，大概会滴下浑浊的水来。到底用这唯一的机会买回怎样的生涯？扒拉一下诸样选择，自己属意的栏目有限，和同志们所见略同也说不准。定谋贵决，一旦确立了自己的真爱，便要直捣黄龙，万不可游移吝惜。要知道，拍的过程水涨船高步步为营。倘稍一迟缓，被他人横刀夺爱，就悔之莫及了。

  I clenched my knuckles tightly, and if there was really a stack of banknotes, it would probably drip murky water. What kind of career will you use this only opportunity to buy? Pulling out all the choices, the columns I like are limited, and I can't say for sure that they are similar to what my comrades see. Once you have established your true love, you must go straight to the yellow dragon and must not be stingy. You know, the process of filming is rising step by step. If you delay a little and are snatched away by others, you will regret it.拍到“取之不尽、用之不竭的信用卡”时，引起空前激烈的争抢。聪明人已发现，所列的诸项某些外延是交叉的，可互相替代。有同学小声嘀咕，有了信用卡，巨富不巨富的也不吃紧了，想干什么，还不如探囊取物？于是信用卡成了最具弹性和热度的饽饽。一时群情激昂，最后被一奋勇女将自重围中掳走。

  When the "inexhaustible and inexhaustible credit card" was photographed, it caused an unprecedented fierce competition. Wise people have discovered that some of the extensions listed are intersecting and can be substituted for each other. Some classmates muttered in a low voice, with a credit card, it doesn't matter if the rich are not rich, what do you want to do, why not reach into the bag to get something? As a result, credit cards have become the most flexible and popular treat. The crowd was excited for a while, and finally was abducted by a brave female general who was surrounded by him.其后的诸项拍卖，险象环生。有些简直可以说是个人价值取向甚至秘密的大曝光。一位众人眼中极腼腆内向的男同学，取走了免费旅游世界的机票，让人刮目相看。一位正在离婚风波中的女子选择了和情人浪迹天涯，于是有人暗中揣测，她是否已有了意中人？一位手脚麻利、助人为乐的同学，居然选了勤快忠诚的仆人，让全体大跌眼镜。细一琢磨，可能他总当一个勤快人已经厌烦，但又无力摆脱这约定俗成的形象，出于补偿的心理，干脆倾其所有买下对另一个人的指挥权吧。一旦咀嚼出这选择背后的意味，旁观者就有些许心酸。

  Subsequent auctions were full of dangers. Some can be said to be personal value orientation or even secret exposure. A very shy and introverted male classmate in the eyes of everyone took away a free air ticket to travel to the world, which was impressive. A woman in the midst of a divorce turmoil chose to wander the world with her lover, so some people secretly speculated whether she already had a lover? A student who is quick and helpful actually chose a diligent and loyal servant, which surprised everyone. If you think about it carefully, maybe he is always tired of being a diligent person, but he is unable to get rid of this conventional image, and out of the psychology of compensation, he simply gives everything he has to buy command over another person. Once the meaning behind this choice is chewed up, the onlookers are a little sad.一位爱喝酒的同人一锤定音买下了“三五个知心朋友”，让我在想象中立即狠狠掴了自己一掌。从前，我劝过他不要喝那么多的酒，他笑说，我喜欢和朋友在一起。我不死心，便再劝，他却一直不改。此番看了他的选择，我方晓得朋友在他的心中如此重要。我决定，该闭嘴时就闭嘴吧。

  A drinking fandom bought "three or five close friends" in one fell swoop, which made me slap myself immediately in my imagination. Once upon a time, I advised him not to drink so much, and he laughed and said, I like to be with friends. I didn't give up, so I persuaded him again, but he never changed. Looking at his choice this time, I know that friends are so important in his heart. I decided to shut up when it was time to shut up.光顾了看别人的收成，差点耽误了自己地里的活计。同桌悄悄问，你到底打算买何种生涯？

  patronizing other people's harvests, almost delaying his own work in the field. The tablemate quietly asked, what kind of career do you plan to buy?我说，没拿定主意啊。我想要那座图书馆。

  I said, I haven't made up my mind. I want that library.同桌说，傻了不是？我看你不妨要那份价值50万美元且年年递增25%的股票，要知道这可是一只会下金蛋的火鸡。只要有了钱，什么图书馆置办不出来呢？你要把图书馆换成别的资产就很困难了。如今信息时代，资料都储藏在光盘里，整个大英博物馆也不过是若干张碟的事。图书馆是落后的工业时代的遗物了……

  The tablemate said, stupid, aren't you? I think you might as well ask for that $500,000 stock that increases by 25% every year, you know that this is a turkey that lays golden eggs. As long as you have money, what library can't you buy? It's hard to trade your library for another asset. In today's information age, materials are stored on CDs, and the entire British Museum is just a matter of a few discs. Libraries are a relic of the backward industrial age......他话还没说完，老师举起了新的一张卡片。他见利忘友，立刻抛开我，大喊了一声，嘿，这个我要定了。1000！

  Before he finished speaking, the teacher held up a new card. When he saw that he forgot his friends, he immediately abandoned me and shouted, hey, I'm going to settle this. 1000！我定睛一看，他倾囊而出购买回来的是一门精湛的技艺。

  I took a closer look, and what he poured out to buy back was a superb skill.我窃笑道，你这才是游牧时代的遗物呢，整个一小农经济。

  I snickered, you are a relic of the nomadic era, the entire small peasant economy.他很认真地说，我总记着老爸的话，家有千金，不如一技在身。

  He said very seriously, I always remember my father's words, the family has a daughter, it is better to have a skill.我暗笑，哈，人啊，真是环境的产物。

  I laughed secretly, ha, people, it's really a product of the environment.好了，不管他人瓦上霜了，还是扫自己门前雪吧。同桌的话也不无道理。有了足够的钱，当然可以买下图书馆或是任何光碟。但你没有这些钱之前，你就干瞪眼。钱在前，还是图书馆在前，两者便有了原则的不同。我愿自己在两鬓油黑、耳聪目明之时，就拥有一座窗明几净、汗牛充栋、庭院深深、斗拱飞檐的图书馆。再说，光碟和图书馆哪能同日而语？我不仅想看到那些古往今来的智慧头脑留下的珍珠，还喜欢那种静谧幽深的空间和气氛，让弥漫在阳光中的纸张味道鼓胀自己的肺……这些，用钱买来的新书和光碟仿得出来吗？

  Okay, no matter how frosted the tiles are on others, it's better to sweep the snow in front of your own door. The words at the same table are not unreasonable. With enough money, of course, you can buy a library or any CD. But before you don't have the money, you just stare. Whether the money is in front or the library is in the front, there is a difference in principle between the two. I wish that when I had dark temples and clear ears, I would have a library with bright windows, sweaty cows, deep courtyards, and arched cornices. Besides, how can CDs and libraries be the same? I not only want to see the pearls left by the wise minds of the ages, but also like the quiet and deep space and atmosphere, which makes my lungs swell with the smell of paper filled with sunlight...... Can these new books and CDs bought with money be imitated?正这样想着，老师举起了“图书馆”，我也学同桌，破釜沉舟地大喊了一声，1000！

  Just thinking like this, the teacher raised the "library", and I also learned from my deskmates, and shouted in a hurry, 1000!于是，宏大的图书馆就落到了我的手中。那一刻，虽明知是个模拟的游戏，心中还是扩散起喜悦的涟漪。

  So, the grand library fell into my hands. At that moment, although I knew it was a simulation game, ripples of joy still spread in my heart.拍卖一项项进行下去，场上气氛热烈。我没有参加过实战，不知真正的拍卖是怎样的程序，但这一游戏对大家心灵的深层触动是不言而喻的。

  The auction went on one by one, and the atmosphere on the field was warm. I have not participated in actual combat, so I don't know what the real auction process is, but it is self-evident that this game has a deep touch on everyone's hearts.

  当老师说“游戏到此结束”时，教室一下静得不可思议，好像刚才闹哄哄的一干人都吞炭为哑或羽化成仙去了。

  When the teacher said, "The game is over here", the classroom was incredibly quiet, as if all the people who had been making a fuss just now had swallowed charcoal as dumb or became immortals.老师接着说，有人也许会在游戏之后，思索和检视自己，产生惊讶的发现和意外的收获。有一个现象，不知大家发现没有，有三项生涯，当我开价100元之后没有人应拍，也就是说不曾成交。这种卖不出去的物品，按规矩是要拍卖行收回的。但我决定还是把它们留下。也许你们想想之后，还会把它们选作自己的生涯目标。

  The teacher went on to say that some people may think and examine themselves after the game, and produce surprising discoveries and unexpected gains. There is a phenomenon, I don't know if you have found it, there are three careers, when I offered 100 yuan, no one responded to the auction, that is to say, there was no transaction. This kind of item that cannot be sold must be taken back by the auction house according to the rules. But I decided to leave them behind. Maybe after thinking about it, you will choose them as your career goals.这三项是：

  These three are:1.名垂青史

  1. Fame in history2.和家人共度周末

  2. Spend weekends with your family3.直言不讳的勇敢和百折不挠的真诚

  3. Outspoken bravery and indomitable sincerity同学大眼瞪小眼，刚才都只专注于购买各自的生涯，不曾注意被冷落的项目，听老师这样一说就都默然了。

  The classmates stared wide-eyed, just now they were only focused on buying their respective careers, and they didn't pay attention to the projects that were left out, and they were silent when they heard the teacher say this.我一一揣摩，在心中回答老师。

  I pondered them one by one and answered the teacher in my heart.和家人共度周末。

  Spend the weekend with your family.老师别恼。不曾购买它作为自己的生涯，原因可能是多方面的。有人以为这是很平淡的事，不必把它定作目标。凡夫俗子们估摸着自己就是不打算和家人共度周末也没有什么地方可去。一件被迫的、几乎命中注定的事，何必要选择？还有的人是一些不愿归巢的鸟，从心眼儿里不打算和家人共度周末。现今只有没本事的人才和家人共度周末，有本事的人是专要和外人度周末的。

  Teacher, don't be annoyed. The reasons for not buying it as your career may be manifold. Some people think this is a very ordinary thing, and there is no need to set it as a goal. Ordinary people guess that they just don't plan to spend weekends with their families, and there is nowhere to go. Why choose a forced and almost doomed thing? Others are birds that do not want to return to their nests and do not plan to spend weekends with their families from the bottom of their hearts. Nowadays, only those who have no ability spend weekends with their families, and those who have the ability are dedicated to spending weekends with outsiders.青史留名？

  Leaving a name in history?可叹现代人（当然也包括我）对史的概念已如此脆弱。仿佛站在一个修鞋摊子旁边，只在乎立等可取，只在乎急功近利。当我们连清洁的水源和绵延的绿色都不愿给子孙留下的时候，拥挤的大脑中如何还存得下一块森严的石壁，以反射青史遥远的回声？

  It is lamentable that modern people (including me, of course) have such a fragile concept of history. It seems to be standing next to a shoe repair stall, only caring about waiting for desirability, only caring about quick success. When we don't even want to leave clean water and green for our children and grandchildren, how can we still save a strict stone wall in our crowded brains to reflect the distant echoes of Qing history?勇敢和真诚？

  Brave and sincere?它固然曾经是人类骄傲的源泉，但如今怯懦和虚伪，更成了安身立命的通行证。预定了终生的勇敢和真诚，就把一把利刃悬在了颅顶，需要怎样的坚忍和稳定？我们表面的不屑是因为骨子里的不敢。我们没有承诺勇敢的勇气，我们没有面对真诚的真诚。

  Although it was once a source of human pride, cowardice and hypocrisy have now become a passport to settle down. After predestining a lifetime of bravery and sincerity, he hung a sharp blade on the top of his skull, what kind of perseverance and stability is needed? Our superficial disdain is because we don't dare in our bones. We do not have the courage to promise bravery, we do not face sincere sincerity.游戏结束了，不曾结束的是思考。

  The game is over, and what is not over is thinking.在弥漫着世俗气息的“我”之外，以一个“孩子”的视角重新剖析自己的价值观和生存质量，内心就有了激烈的碰撞和痛苦的反思。

  In addition to the "I" that is permeated with a secular atmosphere, re-analyzing one's own values and quality of life from the perspective of a "child", there is a fierce collision and painful reflection in my heart.在节奏纷繁的现代社会，我们一天忙得视丹成绿，很难得有这种省察自我的机会，这一瞬让我们返璞归真。

  In the fast-paced modern society, we are so busy that we see the elixir as green all day, and it is rare to have this opportunity to examine ourselves, and this moment allows us to return to the basics.人生的重大决定，是由心规划的，像一道预先计算好的轨道，等待着你的星座运行。如果期待改变我们的命运，请首先改变心的轨迹。
The major decisions in life are planned by the heart, like a pre-calculated track, waiting for your constellation to run. If expecting to change our destiny, change the trajectory of your heart first.

未知

  22.风的青睐

  22. The favor of the wind

  400年前的法国人蒙田，说过这样一句话——风不会对漫无目的者有所青睐。“青睐”是指一个人用黑眼珠子看着你。这是一句否定句，意思是假如你有了坚定的目标，整个大自然将帮助你。

  The Frenchman Montaigne 400 years ago said this - the wind will not favor the aimless. "Favor" is when a person looks at you with dark eyeballs. This is a negative sentence, meaning that if you have a firm goal, the whole of nature will help you.风是什么呢？风是一股看不见摸不着的力量。风吹的时候，影响着我们，逆风或是顺风，对我们的速度和方向都有强烈的影响。就连飞机的钢铁巨翅，也不敢对风等闲视之。

  What is wind? The wind is an invisible and intangible force. When the wind blows, it affects us, whether it is against the wind or downwind, it has a strong impact on our speed and direction. Even the steel giant wings of the plane did not dare to ignore the wind.人生的目的很重要。这个目的是谁给我们预定的呢？没有人。你的父母、你的师长、你的朋友，都可能参与你的目的的制定，但他们不是决定的力量。最后的赞成或是否决票，在你手里。如果你对自己说，我才不要什么人生的目的这种奇怪的东西，那么，你也是有一个目的了，那就是“虚无”。

  The purpose of life is very important. Who predestined this purpose for us? No one. Your parents, your teachers, and your friends may all be involved in the formulation of your purpose, but they are not the power of decision. The final yes or no vote is in your hands. If you say to yourself, "I don't want any strange thing like a purpose in life", then you also have a purpose, that is, "nothingness".一个没有方向感的人，如何行走呢？看看醉汉就明白了。踉踉跄跄、东倒西歪、昏乱嘟囔着，没有人知道他要到哪里去，更不知道他的归宿在何方……有着这种精神的吉卜赛人，终身流浪在灵魂的荒原。

  How can a person without a sense of direction walk? Just look at the drunkard. Staggering, stumbling, muttering in a daze, no one knows where he is going, let alone where he is going...... Gypsies with this spirit wandered the wasteland of the soul all their lives.还有一些人，把某种流行的腐朽说法或是误区当成了自己的目的。这种“镜花水月”的伪目标，只能引诱感官的堕落和本能的麻痹。

  There are also some people who regard some popular decadent sayings or misunderstandings as their own purposes. This kind of pseudo-goal of "mirror flowers and water moon" can only induce the degeneration of the senses and the paralysis of instincts.目的通常是阔大的、依稀的，但它确实存在着，一如晨曦。你从未摸到晨曦，但你每天都可以看到它。即使乌云蔽日的时候，你也坚忍不拔地确信，在高远之处，晨曦依然发出温暖的红色光芒。

  The purpose is usually broad and vague, but it does exist, like the morning light. You never touch the dawn, but you can see it every day. Even when the clouds obscure the sun, you are stoically sure that the morning light still emits a warm red light in the distance.一个有目的的人，走路的姿势是向前的。他们通常不会在跌倒之后太长久地抚摸伤痛，短暂的昏厥之后迅速清醒，用身边的树枝或是草叶捆扎好伤口，就蹒跚着上路了。他们走得慢，但很坚定，不会因为风险而避开既定的方向，也不会为路边一些小的花果而长时间地流连忘返。当然也有痴迷和混沌的时候，但他们能够重新恢复思考，从容向前……

  A purposeful person, the walking posture is forward. They usually don't touch the pain for too long after a fall, and after a short fainting, they quickly wake up, tie the wound with branches or blades of grass around them, and stagger. They walk slowly, but very determined, and will not avoid the established direction because of risks, nor will they linger for a long time for some small flowers and fruits on the side of the road. Of course, there are times of obsession and chaos, but they are able to regain their thinking and move forward calmly......风的青睐，是无价的礼物。只要你坚定地确立了自己的目标，努力下去，就会发现天地万物都来帮你了。

  The favor of the wind is a priceless gift. As long as you firmly establish your goals and work hard, you will find that everything in heaven and earth is coming to help you.

未知

  23.抵制“但是”

  23. Resist the "but"

  但是——是我们常常用到的一个词。我们原来有一个领导，就因为太爱使唤这个词了，外号就叫“老但”。

  But - is a word we often use. We originally had a leader, and because he loved to use the word too much, he was nicknamed "Lao Dan".“但是”的意思，主要是做连词，好像那把皮坎肩的碎皮子缀在一处的彩色丝线。多用在一句话的后半截，表示转折语气。

  The meaning of "but" is mainly to make conjunctions, like the colored silk thread that embellishes the broken skin of Pikan's shoulder in one place. It is mostly used in the second half of a sentence to indicate a turning tone.比方说：你这次的考试成绩不错，但是——不能骄傲自满。

  For example: you did well in this exam, but you can't be complacent.比方说：这地方的风景挺优美的，但是——离城里太远了点。

  For example: the scenery of this place is quite beautiful, but it is too far from the city.比方说：这女孩身材相当好，但是皮肤太黑了些。

  For example: this girl is in good shape, but her skin is too dark.等等。

  Wait a minute.我不知道“但是”这个词刚发明的时候，是不是对它的前半部和后半部的分量，一视同仁？也就是说，它只是一个公平的纽带，并不偏着谁向着谁。可惜在长期的运用过程中，“但是”这个词，成了类似音乐简谱中附点的标记，把后面半拍的节奏，挪到前面去了。当人们看到这个词的时候，无论在“但是”的前面，堆积了多少美好的说明，都像碰上盐酸的污垢，冒了些泡沫，就没了踪影。人们记住的总是“但是”后面的转折，如同好不容易爬上高坡，还没来得及喘口气，“但是”这个陡峭的下坡，不由分说把你掳住，一下就滑到了谷底。

  I don't know if when the word "but" was first invented, did it treat the weight of its first and second halves equally? In other words, it is just a fair bond, not biased towards whom. Unfortunately, in the long-term application process, the word "but" has become a mark similar to the dots in the music score, moving the rhythm of the last half beat to the front. When people see this word, no matter how many beautiful explanations are piled up in front of "but", it is like encountering the dirt of hydrochloric acid, bubbling up, and then disappearing. People always remember the turning point after the "but", as if they had finally climbed up a high slope and had not yet had time to catch their breath, "but" this steep downhill involuntarily captivated you and slipped to the bottom of the valley.于是，“但是”就几乎成了贬义的先兆。只要一出现，气氛就大变。它成了把人心捆成炸药包的细麻绳，成了马上有冷水泼面的前奏曲。“但是”让你打了个激灵，立马把“但是”前面的温暖忘了，只有抖擞起精神，准备迎击扑面而来的顿挫。

  As a result, "but" has almost become a precursor to derogatory connotations. As soon as it appears, the atmosphere changes drastically. It has become a thin hemp rope that binds people's hearts into explosive packs, and it has become a prelude to cold water splashing noodles immediately. "But" made you feel excited, and immediately forgot the warmth in front of "but", and only cheered up and prepared to face the setbacks that came to your face.“但是”便在这种频频警戒的气氛中，削减了平凡的联结之意，增添了沮丧的灰色意味。

  "But" cuts the meaning of ordinary connection and adds a gray meaning of frustration in this atmosphere of frequent vigilance.其实，所有的光明都有暗影，“但是”的本意不过是强调事情还有另一方面。可惜日积月累的负面暗示，使得“但是”这个预报一出现，就抹去了喜色，忽略了成绩，轻慢了进步，贬斥了攀升。

  In fact, all light has shadows, and the original meaning of "but" is just to emphasize that there is another side to things. Unfortunately, the accumulated negative hints have erased the joy, ignored the results, despised progress, and denigrated the climb as soon as the forecast of "but" appeared.

  一位心理学专家讲学时说，她主张大家从此不用“但是”，而改用“同时”。

  A psychology expert said in a lecture that she advocated that everyone should use "but" instead of "simultaneous" from now on.比如，我们形容天气的时候，早先是这样说：今天的太阳很好，但是风很大。

  For example, when we describe the weather, we said earlier: the sun is good today, but the wind is very strong.今后可以改成：今天的太阳很好，同时风很大。

  In the future, it can be changed to: the sun is good today, and the wind is strong.当你最初看这两句话的时候，好像没有多大的分别。你不要急，轻声地多念几遍，那分量和语气的差异，就体味出来了。

  When you first read these two sentences, there doesn't seem to be much difference. Don't be in a hurry, read it softly a few more times, and the difference in weight and tone will come out.但是风很大——会使人的情绪向糟糕那一面倾斜，注意力凝固在不利的因素上。觉着太阳好是件不值得太高兴的事情，风大才是关键。借助了“但是”的威力，风就把阳光打败了。

  But the wind is strong - it will tilt people's emotions to the bad side, and the attention will freeze on the unfavorable factors. Feeling that the sun is good is not worth being too happy about, and strong winds are the key. With the help of the power of "but", the wind defeats the sun.同时风很大——它更中性和客观，好似一个导游小姐，在指点我们注意了某一种情形之后，又把她手中的金属棒，向另一个方向示去。前言余音袅袅，后语也言之凿凿。不偏不倚，公允而平整。它使我们的心神安定，目光精准，两侧都观察得到，头脑中自有定夺。

  At the same time, the wind was strong - it was more neutral and objective, like a tour guide who, after pointing us to a certain situation, pointed the metal rod in her hand in the other direction. The aftermath of the prelude is lingering, and the latter is also clear. Impartial, fair and equitable. It makes our minds settled, our eyes are accurate, we can observe both sides, and our minds have their own decisions.一词之差，它的背后，是怎样看待世界和自身。

  The difference in one word, behind it, is how to see the world and oneself.我们绝不文过饰非，也不夸大其词。好比是花和虫子，一并存在。我们的眼光降落在哪里？

  We will never exaggerate or exaggerate. It's like flowers and bugs, which exist together. Where do our eyes land?降落在花丛中？降落在虫背上？

  Landing among the flowers? Landing on the back of a bug?

  “但是”，是一面偏光镜，把我们的目光聚焦在虫子上。花园里花朵很美丽，“但是”把虫子的影子放大。

  "But" is a polarizing mirror that focuses our eyes on the bug. The flowers in the garden are beautiful, "but" magnify the shadow of the bug.“同时”，是一个透明的水晶球，把我们均衡地分散在两方面。花园里花朵很美丽，“同时”，它也提示尚有虫子。

  "At the same time" is a transparent crystal ball that spreads us evenly between the two sides. The flowers in the garden are beautiful, and "at the same time", it also suggests that there are still insects.“但是”和“同时”，谁更持重和完整，更有利于我们对客观事物的评价和对主观判断的把持，想必会有公论。

  "But" and "at the same time", who is more cautious and complete, is more conducive to our evaluation of objective things and control of subjective judgments, there will surely be public opinion.如此讨论，仿佛和一个简单的连词过不去，有悖恕道。不过，这不单是如何连接上下两句话的问题，在词的背后隐伏着思维方式。

  Such a discussion seems to be incompatible with a simple conjunction, which is contrary to forgiveness. However, this is not only a question of how to connect the upper and lower sentences, but also the way of thinking hidden behind the words.当我尝试着用“同时”代替“但是”以后，一天两天，似也看不出多大的变化。可时间长了，我发现自己比较地多了勇气，因为我的精神得到了补给和呵护。我发现自己比较地对人友善，因为我更明确地发现了他人的长处和优异。我发现自己较为敏捷地从跌倒的地上爬起，因为我看到了沟坎也看到了辙印。我发现自己多了宽容和慈悲，因为每当我意识到不足的时刻，都同时给自己鼓励。
When I tried to replace "but" with "at the same time", I didn't seem to see much change in a day or two. But over time, I found that I had more courage because my spirit was replenished and cared for. I find myself being more kind to people because I have a clearer understanding of the strengths and strengths of others. I found myself getting up from the ground where I fell more agilely, because I saw the ditches and the ruts. I found myself more tolerant and compassionate, because whenever I realized my shortcomings, I encouraged myself at the same time.

未知

  24.指纹状的菌落

  24. Fingerprint-like colonies

  那时我是一个年轻的实习医生。在外科做手术的时候，最害怕的是当一切消毒都已完成，正准备戴上手套，穿上洁白的手术衣，开始在病人身上动刀操练的时候，突然从你的身后，递过来一只透明的培养皿。护士长不苟言笑地指示道，你留个培养吧。这是一句医学术语，解释成大众的语言就是——用你已经消完毒的手指，在培养基上抹一下。然后护士长把密闭的培养皿送到检验科，在暖箱里孵化培养。待到若干时日之后，打开培养皿，观察有无菌落生长，以检查你在给病人手术前，是否彻底消毒了你的手指。如果你的手不干净，就会在手术时把细菌带进腹腔、胸腔或是颅脑，引起感染。严重时会危及病人的生命。

  I was a young intern at the time. When performing surgery, the most fearful thing is that when all the disinfection has been completed, you are about to put on gloves, put on a white surgical gown, and start to practice on the patient, suddenly a transparent petri dish is handed over from behind you. The head nurse instructed unsmilingly, you can stay for training. This is a medical term, and the language interpreted by the masses is - use your disinfected finger and wipe it on the medium. Then the head nurse sent the airtight Petri dish to the laboratory department and hatched and cultured it in the warmer. After a certain period of time, open the petri dish and observe for any colony growth to check if you have completely sterilized your fingers before operating on the patient. If your hands are not clean, you can bring bacteria into the abdominal cavity, chest cavity or brain during surgery, causing infection. In severe cases, it can endanger the patient's life.我很讨厌这种抽查。要是万一查出你手指带菌，多没面子！于是我消毒的时候就格外认真。外科医生的刷手过程，真应了一句西谚：在碱水里洗三次。先要用硬毛刷子蘸着肥皂水，一丝不苟地直刷到腋下，直到皮肤红到发痛，再用清水反复冲洗，恨不能把你的胳膊收拾得像一根搓掉了皮、马上准备凉拌的生藕。然后整个双臂浸泡在百分之七十五的酒精桶里，度过难熬的五分钟。最烦人的是胳膊从酒精桶里拔出后，为了保持消过毒的无菌状态，不能用任何布巾或是纸张擦拭湿淋淋的皮肤，只有在空气中等待它们渐渐晾干。平日我们打针的时候，只涂一小坨酒精，皮肤就感到辛凉无比。因为酒精在挥发的时候，带走了体内的热能，是一种强大的物理降温过程。现在我们的上肢大面积裸露着，假若是冬天，不一会儿就冻得牙齿鼓点一般叩个不停。

  I hate this kind of spot check. If you find out that you have bacteria on your fingers, you will lose face! So I was extra careful when I disinfected. The surgeon's hand brushing process really corresponds to the Western proverb: wash it three times in lye water. First, use a stiff-bristled brush dipped in soapy water, meticulously brush straight to the armpits, until the skin is red to the point of pain, and then rinse repeatedly with water, wishing you could clean up your arms like a raw lotus root that has been rubbed off the skin and ready to be cold immediately. Then soak your entire arms in a 75% alcohol barrel for a difficult five minutes. The most annoying thing is that after the arm is pulled out of the alcohol barrel, in order to maintain the sterile state of disinfection, you cannot wipe the wet skin with any cloth or paper, but only wait for them to dry gradually in the air. When we inject on weekdays, we only apply a small amount of alcohol, and our skin feels extremely cold. Because when alcohol volatilizes, it takes away the heat energy in the body, which is a powerful physical cooling process. Now our upper limbs are exposed in large areas, and if it is winter, it will be cold and beat like a drumbeat in a short time.更严格的是在所有过程中，双臂都要像受刑一般高举着，无论多么累，都不能垂下手腕，更严禁用手指接触任何异物。简言之，从消毒过程一开始，你的手就不是你的手了，它成了一件有独立使命的无菌工具。

  More strictly, during all processes, both arms must be held high as if they were being tortured, no matter how tired they are, they cannot hang their wrists, and it is strictly forbidden to touch any foreign objects with their fingers. In short, from the very beginning of the disinfection process, your hand is not your hand, it becomes a sterile tool with an independent mission.我的同学是一位漂亮女孩，她的手很美，鸡蛋清一般柔嫩，但在猪毛刷子日复一日的残酷抚摸下，很快变得粗糙无光。由于酒精强烈的脱脂作用，手臂也像枯树干，失去了少女特有的润泽。单看上肢，我都像一个老太婆了。她愤愤地说。

  My classmate is a beautiful girl with beautiful hands, as soft as egg whites, but quickly becomes rough and dull under the cruel touch of the pig hair brush day after day. Due to the strong degreasing effect of alcohol, the arms are also like dead tree trunks, losing the unique moisture of girls. Just looking at the upper limbs, I look like an old woman. She said indignantly.以后的日子里，她洗手的时候开始偷工减料。比如该刷三遍，她一遍就草草过关。只要没人看见，她就把白皙的胳膊从酒精桶里解放出来，独自欣赏……有一天，我们正高擎双手，像俘虏兵投降一样傻站着，等着自己的臂膀风干时，她突然说，我的耳朵后边有点痒。

  In the days to come, she began to cut corners when she washed her hands. For example, when it was time to brush three times, she passed the test hastily once. As long as no one saw it, she freed her white arms from the alcohol barrel and admired it alone...... One day, when we were standing stupidly like captives surrendering with our hands held high, waiting for our arms to dry, she suddenly said, "The back of my ears is a little itchy."这是一件小事，但对于此时的我们来说，却是一件很难办的事。消过毒的手已被管制，我俩就像卸去双臂的木偶，无法接触自己的皮肤。按照惯例，只有呼唤护士，烦她代为搔痒。因手术尚未开始，护士还在别处忙，眼前一时无人。同学说痒得不行，忍不了。我说，要不咱们俩像山羊似的，脑袋抵着脑袋，互相蹭蹭？她说，我又不是额头痒，是脖子下面的凹处，哪里抵得着？我只好说，你就多想想邱少云吧。同学美丽的面孔在大口罩后面难受得扭歪了。突然，可能痒痛难熬，她电光石火地用消过毒的手，在自己耳朵后面抓了一把。

  This is a small thing, but it is a difficult thing for us at this time. The hands that have been disinfected have been controlled, and we are like puppets with our arms removed, unable to touch our own skin. According to the usual practice, you can only call the nurse and ask her to scratch the itch on her behalf. Because the operation had not yet begun, the nurse was still busy elsewhere, and there was no one in front of her for a while. The classmates said that it was so itchy that they couldn't bear it. I said, why don't we be like goats, head to head, rubbing each other? She said, it's not an itchy forehead, it's a hollow under the neck, where can I resist it? I have to say, just think more about Qiu Shaoyun. The beautiful faces of the classmates twisted uncomfortably behind the big masks. Suddenly, it may be itchy and painful, and she grabbed a handful behind her ear with her anti-poison hand with lightning speed.我惊愕得说不出话来，几乎不相信自己的眼睛。正在这时，护士长走了进来，向我和同学伸出了两个细菌培养皿……

  I was speechless in shock, almost believing my eyes. At this moment, the head nurse walked in and stretched out two bacterial petri dishes to me and my classmates......其实事情在这个份儿上，还是可以挽救的。同学可以直率地向护士长申明情况，说自己的手已经污染，不能接受检验。然后再重复烦琐的洗手过程，她依旧可以正常参加手术。但她什么也没有说，哆哆嗦嗦地探出手指，在培养基上捺了一下……那天是一个开腹手术，整个过程我都恍惚不安，好像自己参与了某种阴谋。

  In fact, things can still be saved in this regard. Students can bluntly declare the situation to the head nurse, saying that their hands are contaminated and cannot be tested. Then repeat the tedious handwashing process, and she can still participate in the operation normally. But she didn't say anything, trembling and sticking out her finger and pressing it on the medium...... It was an open surgery that day, and I was in a trance the whole time, as if I was involved in some kind of conspiracy.病人术后并发了严重的感染，刀口溃烂腐败，高烧不止，医护人员陷入紧张的治疗和抢救。经过化验，致病菌强大而独特。它是从哪里来的呢？老医生不止一次面对病历自言自语。过了几天，手术者的细菌培养结果出来了，我的同学抹过的培养基上，呈现出茂密的细菌丛，留下指纹状的菌落阴影，正是引致病人感染的险恶品种。

  The patient was seriously infected after the operation, the incision was ulcerated and rotten, and the fever was persistent, and the medical staff fell into intense treatment and rescue. After laboratory testing, the pathogenic bacteria are powerful and unique. Where did it come from? The old doctor said to himself more than once in the face of medical records. A few days later, the bacterial culture results of the surgeon came out, and the medium that my classmate wiped showed a dense bacterial flora, leaving a fingerprint-like colony shadow, which was the sinister variety that caused the patient's infection.那一刻，我的同学落下一串串眼泪。由于她的过失，病人承受了无妄之灾。她的手在搔痒的时候，沾染了病菌，又在手术过程中污染了腹腔，酿成他人巨大的痛苦。

  At that moment, my classmates shed strings of tears. Because of her fault, the patient suffered an unwarranted disaster. When her hands were itching, they were stained with germs and contaminated the abdominal cavity during the operation, causing great pain to others.病人的命总算挽救回来了，但这件事被我牢牢地记在心里，不敢忘怀。

  The patient's life was finally saved, but I kept this matter firmly in my heart and did not dare to forget it.随着年岁渐长，我从中悟出了许多年轻时忽略的道理。

  As I grew older, I realized many truths that I overlooked when I was young.

  首先是感染和腐败几乎是一种必然。牛奶放在那里，不加温不冷冻，随它去，就一定会变酸发臭。没有特殊的防腐措施，想在常温下保持牛奶的新鲜品质，是痴人说梦。铁会生锈，木头会腐烂，水面布满青苔，密闭的房屋长毛生霉，空气发出臭鸡蛋的味道……腐败几乎是无处不在，见缝下蛆。我那个同学只用手搔了一下耳后，千真万确，仅此一下，病菌潜伏到了她的手上，播种到手术刀口里，就引发了恶劣后果。细菌的生命力和感染力，真是不可思议的强大，任何侥幸心理都是万万要不得的。

  The first is that infection and corruption are almost inevitable. Milk is placed there, not warmed or frozen, and if it goes, it will definitely turn sour and smelly. Without special preservative measures, it is a fool's dream to maintain the fresh quality of milk at room temperature. Iron rusts, wood rots, water is covered with moss, closed houses are moldy, and the air smells like rotten eggs...... Corruption is almost everywhere, and maggots are sewn into the seams. My classmate only scratched the back of her ear with her hand, and it was true, and in this alone, the germs lurked in her hands and sowed into the scalpel mouth, which caused bad consequences. The vitality and infectivity of bacteria are really incredibly powerful, and any fluke mentality is absolutely necessary.二是防感染和腐败的措施，只要认真执行，是一定有效的。凡是认真执行了刷手要诀的人，每次细菌培养就都是阴性，他们的手术后感染率几乎是零。感染和腐败不是不可战胜的，只要有了切实可行、行之有效的措施，严格地执行用鲜血换来的经验教训，腐败和感染可以被制伏。

  Second, measures to prevent infection and corruption will be effective as long as they are carefully implemented. Anyone who carefully implements the brushing tips will be negative every time the bacterial culture is negative, and their infection rate after surgery is almost zero. Infection and corruption are not invincible, and corruption can be subdued as long as there are practical and effective measures and the lessons learned in exchange for blood are strictly enforced.三是同样的致病菌，每个人的抵抗力不同，结局也就有天壤之别。潜伏在同学耳朵旁的细菌，肯定已在她身上生存多时，相安无事。可是移植到了病人身上，就引发了骇人的后果，盖因彼此的素质不同，结果也就因人而异。同学是正常人，有良好的防御系统，所以病菌伤害不了她。但开刀的病人就不同了，自身抵抗力薄弱，雪上加霜，差点要了性命。当然我这样说，并不是要求病榻上的人要有运动健将一般的体魄，只是说加强自身的防御系统，是抵御病菌最有效的武器。一个人遭受细菌的感染不可避免，但有了足够的准备，即使敌人侵入，也可以在最短的时间内将其歼灭。

  Third, the same pathogenic bacteria, everyone's resistance is different, and the outcome is very different. The bacteria lurking next to her classmate's ears must have survived on her for a long time, and she was safe. However, transplanting to a patient causes terrible consequences, because the quality of each other is different, and the results vary from person to person. The classmate is a normal person and has a good defense system, so germs can't hurt her. But the patients who underwent surgery were different, their own resistance was weak, and to make matters worse, they almost lost their lives. Of course, when I say this, I do not require the people on the sickbed to have the physique of an athlete, but to strengthen their own defense system and be the most effective weapon against germs. It is inevitable that a person will suffer from a bacterial infection, but with adequate preparation, even if the enemy invades, it will be possible to annihilate it in the shortest possible time.最后是要找到一个黄金般的点。应该说抗感染的杀菌药物是十分有效的，医生把致病的细菌培养出来，它就成了靶子。把各种抗菌药物，以不同的浓度加到培养皿里，观察哪种药物杀菌最有效，然后对症下药，把病菌最敏感的药物压下去，力争在最短的时间里，取得胜利。记得老医生总是很仔细地计算用药的剂量，根据病情，反复测算。我看得不耐烦，说搞这么复杂干什么，不是治病救人吗，当然剂量越大效果越好。老医生说，任何药物都是有毒性的，正是为了治病救人，才要找到一个最恰当的剂量，既干净彻底地消灭了病菌，又最大限度地保护挽救病人，这是一门艺术。一个好医生的职责，就是要找到这个像黄金分割率一般宝贵的结合点……

  Finally, find a golden point. It should be said that anti-infective bactericidal drugs are very effective, and doctors cultivate disease-causing bacteria, and it becomes a target. Add various antibacterial drugs to the petri dish at different concentrations to observe which drug is the most effective in sterilization, and then prescribe the right medicine to suppress the most sensitive drugs of germs, and strive to win in the shortest possible time. I remember that the old doctor always carefully calculated the dosage of the medicine, and according to the condition, it was repeatedly calculated. I watched impatiently, saying why do you do such a complicated thing, isn't it to cure diseases and save people, of course, the higher the dose, the better the effect. The old doctor said that any drug is toxic, and it is precisely for the purpose of curing diseases and saving people that it is necessary to find the most appropriate dose, which not only cleanly and completely eliminates germs, but also protects and saves patients to the greatest extent, which is an art. The duty of a good doctor is to find this combination point as valuable as the golden ratio......我记住了他的话，但更深刻地领悟它，却是在年岁渐长，看到了许多医学领域以外的问题之后。

  I remembered his words, but realized it more deeply after I got older and saw many problems outside the medical field.病菌和微生物向我们撒下天罗地网，由它们引致的感染与腐败，每日每时都在发生。和形形色色的腐败菌做斗争，也许将贯穿经济和政治生活的整个历史。我们将会有更优秀的医生，我们将会有更强大的药品，我们将会有更严格的消毒手段，但加强自身的抵抗力，永远是最重要的。在旷日持久的战斗中，不断地完善自己、修复自己，人类才会保持蓬勃的生命力，欣欣向荣。
Germs and microorganisms are a net of infections and corruption that occur every day. The fight against all kinds of corruption will probably run through the entire history of economic and political life. We will have better doctors, we will have stronger drugs, we will have stricter disinfection methods, but strengthening our own resistance is always the most important. In the protracted battle, by constantly improving and repairing ourselves, human beings will maintain vigorous vitality and thrive.
