  25.腰线

  25. Waistline

  早年的卫生间只在壁上刷点白灰，像个从溪流里站起来的裸孩儿，斜披着毛巾。如今的房子，厨卫是重点，你再不讲究，也要贴上瓷砖才能说得过去。

  In the early years, the bathroom only painted the walls with white ash, like a naked child standing up from a stream, with a towel diagonally. In today's house, the kitchen and bathroom are the focus, no matter how much you don't pay attention to it, you have to put tiles on it to make sense.到建材市场挑选瓷砖，成了装修的必修课。砖铺像丝绸店，满眼花色闪闪烁烁，不知该挑哪一种好。顾图案更要看价钱，很快你就发现，精美瓷砖是没有止境的，但钱包是有大小的。到了最后，演变成先看价钱再定花色，流程进入量体裁衣看米下锅的局面。为了选瓷砖，我和丈夫甚至破了不当着外人争执的约定，不止一次吵得面红耳赤。一旁的店员漠然立着，连好奇的神色都不屑流露，想来因瓷砖而起的硝烟，她已见惯不怪。

  Choosing tiles in the building materials market has become a compulsory course for decoration. The brick shop is like a silk shop, full of sparkling colors, and I don't know which one to choose. Looking at the pattern, you should look at the price, and soon you will find that there is no end to exquisite tiles, but the wallet is of all sizes. In the end, it evolved into looking at the price first and then deciding on the color, and the process entered a situation of tailor-made rice and pot. In order to choose tiles, my husband and I even broke the agreement not to argue in front of outsiders, and we quarreled more than once. The clerk on the side stood indifferently, not even showing a curious look, thinking that she was used to seeing the smoke from the tiles.关于购买何种瓷砖，好不容易统一了意见，分歧又再接再厉地出现了。要不要花砖？要不要腰线？

  Opinions on what kind of tiles to buy finally came to a consensus, and disagreements continued to arise. Do you want to make tiles? Do you want a waistline?花砖是成套瓷砖的点睛之笔。瓷砖是淡绿调子的，花砖可能就是一丛披头散发的翠竹。瓷砖是棕黄调子的，如果是厨用，花砖上就有深驼色的咖啡杯盏，有袅袅的白气升起。如果是卫浴用，可能绘有几间木屋一丛野花，或许还有蜜蜂……有款砖叫作“海洋之心”，花砖镶着大朵的蔚蓝色椭圆形玻璃，假扮那块长眠在深海之下的无价钻石。

  Tiles are the finishing touch to a set of tiles. The tiles are light green, and the tiles may be a clump of green bamboo emitted by a disheveled. The tiles are brownish-yellow, and if they are for kitchen use, there are dark camel coffee cups on the tiles, and there is a lingering white gas rising. If it is for bathrooms, there may be a few wooden houses, a cluster of wildflowers, and maybe bees...... There is a brick called "Heart of the Ocean", which is inlaid with large azure oval glass, pretending to be a priceless diamond sleeping under the deep sea.更讲究的花砖像是一部有头有尾的小说呢。一款叫作“爱情鸟”的瓷砖，花砖就有几种格局。一块是两只水鸟相依为命，耳鬓厮磨的。这好理解，新婚宴尔啊。再一块就是三只鸟左顾右盼呼朋引伴的。这多出来的鸟，可不是什么非法闯入者，而是大鸟们辛辛苦苦孵出的小鸟。不知这两幅是不是全本，依此推下去，还可演变出多款情节。比如三只鸟展翅飞翔，比如四只鸟组成团队……

  The more elaborate tiles are like a novel with a beginning and an end. A tile called "lovebird", there are several patterns of flower tiles. One is two waterfowl dependent on each other, and their ears and temples are grinding. This is easy to understand, the wedding banquet. The other piece is the three birds looking left and right, looking for friends and companions. These extra birds are not illegal intruders, but small birds that the big birds have worked hard to hatch. I don't know if these two are the whole book, but if you push it forward, you can evolve into a variety of plots. For example, three birds spread their wings and fly, for example, four birds form a team......花砖之外，还有腰线。腰线并不像它的名字那样谦逊，它不是一条简单的线，而是由很多块精巧的长方形瓷砖连接而成的瓷砖带，缠绕在整壁瓷砖的中段。

  In addition to the tiles, there is also a waistline. The waistline is not as modest as its name, it is not a simple line, but a tile belt connected by many delicate rectangular tiles, wrapped around the middle of the entire wall of tiles.腰线是缩小了的花砖，有图案，甚至也有情节。比如上面说到的“爱情鸟”，腰线就是一只小鸟破壳而出，茸茸的羽毛和残缺的蛋壳，把爱情和繁衍拴在了一起。

  The waistline is a shrunken tile, with patterns and even plots. For example, the "lovebird" mentioned above, the waistline is a bird breaking out of its shell, with fluffy feathers and mutilated eggshells, tying love and reproduction together.

  腰线不便宜。瓷砖和瓷碗该是近邻吧？瓷碗是有曲线的，瓷砖却是完全不曾发育的平板，但一块腰线比一个普通的饭碗要值钱很多。腰线是很团结的，你不可能只贴一块，它们有着一荣皆荣一损俱损的气节。围着墙手拉手形成包围圈，统算下来，会吓得你的钱包一抖。若不镶，就一块都不能上，瓷砖的拼缝才能妥当。饭碗是生活的必需，而腰线则是锦上添花可有可无的，带着些许孤芳自赏的奢侈。

  The waistline is not cheap. Tiles and porcelain bowls should be close neighbors, right? The porcelain bowl is curved, but the tiles are completely undeveloped flat plates, but a waistline is much more valuable than an ordinary rice bowl. The waistline is very united, you can't just stick one piece, they have the integrity of glory and loss. Holding hands around the wall to form an encirclement, it will scare your wallet to shake. If it is not inlaid, it cannot be put on at all, and the tile seams can be properly jointed. The rice bowl is a necessity of life, and the waistline is the icing on the cake, with a little luxury of self-appreciation.母亲的新房子，割舍了所有的腰线。按说这点钱还是有的，但母亲坚决不肯，说有没有腰线是一样的，不花这个冤枉钱。

  My mother's new house gave up all her waistline. It is said that there is still this money, but the mother resolutely refused, saying that whether there is a waistline is the same and that it is not a waste of money.然而，有没有腰线是不一样的。就像上面说的“爱情鸟”，省去了雏鸟啄破蛋壳的那一幕，花砖上的两只鸟很突兀地变成了三只鸟，常常叫人疑心那小鸟的来历，甚至误会这是另外的一家了。“海洋之心”的腰线是一圈蓝白相间的小“钻石”，仿佛一挂悬垂的珠链。取消之后，墙壁上半截的莹白和下半截的蔚蓝，生硬地焊接在一起，丧失了柔和的过渡。孤零零的“巨钻”没来由地在白瓷板中闪烁，像一只莫名其妙的怪眼。

  However, it is not the same with or without a waistline. Just like the "love bird" mentioned above, the scene where the chick pecks through the eggshell is omitted, and the two birds on the tile suddenly turn into three birds, which often makes people doubt the origin of the bird, and even misunderstand that this is another family. The waistline of the "Heart of the Ocean" is a circle of small blue and white "diamonds", like a dangling bead chain. After the cancellation, the white of the upper half of the wall and the blue of the lower half were stiffly welded together, losing the soft transition. The lonely "giant diamond" flashed in the white porcelain plate for no reason, like an inexplicable strange eye.我觉得自己对不起母亲的新居，推而广之也对不起腰线。终于有一天，得了补偿的机会。我路过一家店铺，看到大肆甩卖腰线。

  I feel sorry for my mother's new home, and by extension, my waistline. Finally one day, I got the opportunity to compensate. I passed by a store and saw a big sale of waistlines.

  腰线的图案是很耀眼的玫瑰花蕾，夹杂着点点的金红，绮丽而烂漫。我不假思索地买了很多腰线，辛辛苦苦地搬回家，才面对一个严峻的问题——这些腰线嵌在哪里？

  The pattern of the waistline is a very dazzling rose bud, mixed with a little golden red, beautiful and gorgeous. I bought a lot of waistlines without thinking and moved home with great work before I was faced with a serious question - where are these waistlines embedded?腰线是美丽的，但许多腰线聚集在一起，除了让人眼花缭乱之外，就是安置它们的焦灼了。如同皮带是用最好的牛皮制造的，但你面对一堆皮带时，既不能把它们缝制成皮氅，也不能敲打成皮鞋。

  The waistline is beautiful, but many waistlines are gathered together, in addition to dazzling, it is the anxiety of placing them. Just as belts are made of the finest cowhide, but when you are faced with a pile of belts, you can neither sew them into a cloak nor beat them into leather shoes.失去了烘托和陪衬的腰线，也散失了精彩和雅致，剩下的是纷乱和拥挤。楼梯下有一间楔形的小房子，别家把它改造成了狗舍，我家堆积着杂物。早先一直是水泥墁地，如今我把腰线密集地砌在那里，闪闪花蕾只好在尘埃下皱缩。

  The waistline that sets off and foil has been lost, and the excitement and elegance have also been lost, and what remains is chaos and crowding. There is a small wedge-shaped house under the stairs, which has been converted into a kennel by other families, and my house is piled up with debris. In the past, it was a cement floor, but now I have densely built my waistline there, and the shining buds have to shrink under the dust.看到过一条关于人才的定律，说全由极高智商的人组成的团队，那效率和智慧却并非最高，反倒不如人才的阶梯状组合，方能发挥出最好的效力。仿佛腰线，顾名思义，只能是一面素墙美丽的统帅，而不能铺陈得漫山遍野。
I have seen a law about talents, saying that a team composed of people with extremely high IQs is not the highest, but it is not as good as the stepped combination of talents, so that it can exert its best effectiveness. It seems that the waistline, as the name suggests, can only be a beautiful commander with a plain wall, and cannot be spread all over the mountains.

  26.你不能要求没有风暴的海洋

  26. You can't ask for an ocean without storms

  痛苦和磨难是人生不可分割的一部分。只有接受这一事实，我们才能超越它，更加看清生命的意义。

  Pain and suffering are an integral part of life. Only by accepting this fact can we transcend it and see the meaning of life more clearly.你说你不要这些苦难，那么生命也就失去了框架。很多自杀的人，就是因为没有理会这种意义，一厢情愿地认为生命是应该只有甘甜没有挫败的。特别是在恋爱早期，那种汹涌的荷尔蒙带来的欢愉，让人把激情当成了常态。生命的常态，其实就是平稳和深邃，还有暗流。在最深刻的层面，我们不单与别人是分离的，而且与世界也是分离的，兀自踯躅前行。

  If you say you don't want these sufferings, then life will lose its framework. Many people who commit suicide are because they ignore this meaning and wishfully believe that life should only be sweet and not frustrated. Especially in the early days of love, the joy brought by the surging hormones makes people take passion as the norm. The normal state of life is actually stable and profound, and there are undercurrents. At the deepest level, we are not only separated from others, but also from the world, and we move forward on our own.生命的每一步都带着人们向死亡之境跌落，不要存在幻想，这才让你比较持久稳定，安然地居住在孤独中，胸中如有千沟万壑、千军万马。只有接受这一事实，我们才能超越死亡，腾起在空中，看清生命的意义。

  Every step of life takes people to the realm of death, don't have illusions, so that you can be more lasting and stable, live in loneliness peacefully, and have thousands of ravines and thousands of troops in your chest. Only by accepting this fact can we rise above death, soar into the air, and see the meaning of life clearly.有一次，到沙漠中间的一个城市去，临行之前和当地的朋友联络。她不停地说，毕老师，你可要做好准备啊，我们这里经常是黄沙蔽日。不过，这几天天气很不错，只是不知道它能不能坚持到你到来的那一天。

  Once, I went to a city in the middle of the desert and contacted a local friend before leaving. She kept saying, Teacher Bi, you have to be prepared, we are often covered by yellow sand here. However, the weather has been very good these days, but I don't know if it will last until the day you arrive.我有点纳闷。虽然人们常常说，“您的到来带来了好天气”，或者说，“天气也在欢迎您呢”，谁都知道，这是典型的客套。个体的人是多么渺小啊，我们哪里能影响到天气！

  I was a little puzzled. Although it is often said that "your arrival brings good weather" or "the weather is welcoming you", everyone knows that this is typical politeness. How small individual people are, how can we affect the weather!不过这位朋友反复地提到天气，还是让我产生了好奇。我说，不管好天气还是坏天气，我们都不能挑选。天气是你们那里的一部分，就是黄沙蔽日，也是你们的特色啊。

  However, this friend's repeated mention of the weather still made me curious. I said, good weather or bad weather, we can't pick and choose. The weather is part of you, that is, the yellow sand covers the sun, and it is also your characteristic.说者无意，听者有心。后来，这位朋友对我说，她听了我的话，就放下心来。我很奇怪，因为自觉这番话里，并没有多少劝人安心的含义啊。她说，我们这里天气多变，经常有朋友一下飞机就抱怨，闹得主客都很尴尬。

  The speaker has no intention, but the listener has a heart. Later, this friend told me that she listened to me and felt relieved. I am very strange, because I feel that there is not much meaning of persuasion in these words. She said that the weather here is changeable, and friends often complain when they get off the plane, which makes the host and guests very embarrassed.我说，坏天气也是大自然的一部分，就像每个人的生命中都必定会下雨，某些日子势必黑暗又荒凉。就像你不可能总是吃细粮，那样你就会得大肠癌。你一定要吃粗纤维。坏天气、悲剧、死亡、生病，都是生命中的粗纤维，我们只有安然接纳。

  I said that bad weather is also a part of nature, just like it is bound to rain in everyone's life, and some days are bound to be dark and desolate. Just like you can't always eat small grains, then you will get colorectal cancer. You must eat crude fiber. Bad weather, tragedy, death, and illness are all the coarse fibers of life, and we can only accept them safely.你不可能要求一个没有风暴的海洋。那不是海，是泥潭。

  You can't ask for an ocean without storms. It's not the sea, it's a quagmire.

  27.第二志愿

  27. Second choice

  人们常常把所有的注意力，都集中在第一志愿上。这些年，随着考试严酷性的不断升级，关于填报志愿的说法也越来越霸道了——那就是，全力以赴关注你的第一志愿。某些大学的录取人员公开宣布，我们是不会录取第二志愿的学生的。因为你的热爱不够专一，录来也学不好的。

  People often focus all their attention on their first choice. Over the years, with the continuous escalation of the severity of the exam, the statement about filling in the volunteer form has become more and more domineering - that is, go all out to pay attention to your first choice. Admissions officers at some universities have publicly announced that we will not admit students who are second choices. Because your love is not dedicated enough, you can't learn well when you record it.高考形势特殊，僧多粥少，对于学校的取舍，旁人不好议论是非。但我以为，如果把高考报志愿的经验推而广之，把第一志愿至上，扩展成人生选择的一大信条，就有商榷的必要了。

  The college entrance examination situation is special, there are more monks and less porridge, and it is not easy for others to discuss the trade-offs of schools. But I think that if we expand the experience of applying for the college entrance examination and apply for volunteering, and expand the first choice first into a major creed of life choices, it is necessary to discuss.人生的选择绝少是唯一的。

  Life choices are rarely the only one.听一位美国心理学家讲座，谈到男女青年挑选恋爱对象时，他说，如果你在读大学的时候，一眼扫去，本班级上的异性，有三分之一以上可以成为你的配偶候选人，那么……

  Listening to a lecture by an American psychologist, when talking about young men and women choosing a love partner, he said, if you scan at a glance when you are in college, more than one-third of the opposite sex in your class can become your spouse candidates, then......讲到这里，说是悬念也好，说是征询民意也好，他成心留出一个长长的停顿，用苍蓝色的眼珠扫视全场。台下发出汹涌的低语声，均说，那他就是一个神经病！

  At this point, whether it was suspense or public opinion, he made up his mind to leave a long pause and scan the audience with pale blue eyes. There were raging whispers from the audience, and they all said that he was a neurotic!异国的心理学家耸耸肩膀说，喏！那他或她，就是一个心理健康的人。

  The foreign psychologist shrugged his shoulders and said, "Hey! Then he or she is a mentally healthy person.这观点有点好玩，也有点耸人听闻，是不是？当然，他指的寻找伴侣，是在大学校园内，智商和背景有大的相仿，并不能波及整个社会，说某个男人觉得与世上三分之一的女人都可成为眷属，才属正常。

  This view is a bit funny and a bit sensational, isn't it? Of course, he was referring to finding a partner on a college campus, where IQ and background are very similar, and it cannot affect the whole society, and it is normal for a man to feel that he can be married to one-third of the women in the world.但这一论点也可以说明，既然结为夫妻这样严重的问题，都不妨有一手或是几手打算，那么，在其他场合的选择，当有更大的弹性。

  But this argument can also show that since there is such a serious problem as getting married, it is better to have one or several plans, so the choice on other occasions should be more flexible.

  当孤注一掷地把自己的命运押在某个“唯一”头上的时候，我们实际上是处于自我封闭和焦灼无序的状态。内心流淌的是自卑和虚弱。以为只有这狭窄的途径，才是抵达目的地的独木桥，无法设想在另外的情形下，还有道路尚可通行。某些人的信念虽执着但脆弱，难以容忍自己的不成功。由于太惧怕失败的阴影了，拒绝想象除胜利以外，事态还同时存有一千种以上暗淡的可能。他们能够采取的自卫措施，就是放下眼帘。以为只要不去想，不良的结果就可能像鬼魅，只能在暗夜中游走，不会真的在太阳下现身。

  When we desperately bet our fate on some "only" head, we are actually in a state of self-isolation and anxiety. What flows in the heart is low self-esteem and weakness. I thought that only this narrow path was the single-plank bridge to reach the destination, and I couldn't imagine that there would still be a road passable under other circumstances. Some people's beliefs are persistent but fragile, and they find it difficult to tolerate their own unsuccess. Afraid of the shadow of defeat, refusing to imagine that there are more than a thousand bleak possibilities in addition to victory. The self-defense measure they can take is to let go of their eyes. I thought that as long as I didn't think about it, the bad result might be like a ghost, I could only wander in the dark night, and I wouldn't really show up in the sun.于是每当选择的关头，我们可以看到那么多人鸵鸟似的奋不顾身、色厉内荏地跑跳着，到了没有退路的时候，就把小小的脑袋埋入沙荒。他们并不仅仅骗别人，首先的和更重要的，是用这种虚张的气势，为自己打气加力。他们拒不考虑第二志愿，觉着给自己留了退路，就是懦夫和逃兵。甚至以为那是一个不祥的兆头，好像夜啼的猫头鹰，早早赶走方平安。他们竭力不去前瞻那潜伏着的败笔和危险，好像不带粮草就杀入沙漠的孤军。即使为了应付局面多做准备，也是马马虎虎潦潦草草，虚与委蛇地写下第二、第三志愿……不走脑子，秋水无痕。不敢一针见血地问自己，假若第一志愿失守，能否依旧从容微笑？

  So whenever it comes to choosing, we can see so many people running and jumping like ostriches, and when there is no way out, they bury their small heads in the sand. They don't just lie to others, first and more importantly, they use this bravado to cheer themselves up. They refuse to consider a second choice, feeling that they have left a way out for themselves, that is, cowards and deserters. I even thought it was an ominous sign, like an owl crying at night, driving away Fang Ping'an early. They tried their best not to look forward to the lurking failures and dangers, as if they were a lone army entering the desert without food and grass. Even if you prepare more to cope with the situation, you are sloppy and write down your second and third choices...... It does not go to the brain, and there is no trace of autumn water. Don't dare to ask yourself to the point, if your first choice is lost, can you still smile calmly?可惜世上的事情，不如愿者十之八九。当冰冷的结局出现时，很多人就像遇到雪崩的攀缘者，一落千丈。

  Unfortunately, eight or nine out of ten things in the world are not as good as they want. When the icy ending appears, many people are like climbers who have encountered an avalanche and plummeted.此刻，你以前不经意间随手填写的第二志愿，就像保险绳一样，在你下坠的过程中，有力地拽住了你，还你一方风景。

  At this moment, the second choice you filled in inadvertently before is like a safety rope, which forcefully grabs you and gives you back the scenery in the process of your fall.惊魂未定的你，此时心中百感交集。被第一志愿抛弃的巨大失落，使百骸俱软，无暇顾及和珍视第二志愿的援手。你垂头丧气地望着崖下，第一志愿的游魂还在碎石中闪着虚光。有人恨不能纵身一跳，以七尺之躯殉了那未竟的理想。即便被亲人和世俗的利害劝得暂且委曲求全，那心中的苦郁悲凉，也经久不散。

  You are shocked, and you have mixed feelings in your heart at this time. The huge loss of being abandoned by the first choice makes all bones weak, and there is no time to take care of and cherish the help of the second choice. You look down the cliff dejectedly, and your first choice wandering spirit is still shining in the rubble. Some people wish they could jump and die for their unfinished ideals with their seven-foot bodies. Even if you are persuaded by relatives and worldly interests to compromise for the time being, the bitterness and sadness in your heart will last for a long time.第二志愿如同灰姑娘，龟缩在角落里，打扫尘埃，收拾残局，等待那不知何日才能莅临的金马车。

  The second choice is like Cinderella, huddled in the corner, cleaning up the dust, cleaning up the mess, and waiting for the golden carriage that will arrive when it doesn't know when it will come.其实人的才能是多方面的，守节般地效忠第一志愿，愚蠢不说，更是浪费。候鸟是在不断的迁徙当中，寻找自己的最佳栖息地，并在长途艰苦的跋涉中，锻炼了羽翼。在屋檐下盘旋的鸟，除了麻雀，还能想出谁。

  In fact, people's talents are multifaceted, and being loyal to their first choice is stupid and wasteful. Migratory birds are constantly migrating, looking for their best habitat, and exercising their wings during long and arduous treks. The bird hovering under the eaves, besides the sparrow, can you think of anyone.寻找第二志愿的过程，实质上是对自己的一次再发现。除了那最突出最显著的特点之外，我还有什么优长之处？第一志愿和第二志愿之间，可否像两位相得益彰的前锋，交互支援？我还有哪些潜藏着的特质，有待发掘和培养？平日疏忽的爱好，也许可在失落中渐渐显影？

  The process of finding a second choice is essentially a rediscovery of oneself. In addition to the most prominent and distinctive features, what other advantages do I have? Can the first choice and the second choice be mutually supported like two complementary strikers? What other latent traits do I have to discover and cultivate? Maybe the hobbies that are negligent on weekdays can gradually manifest in the loss?第二志愿的考虑和填写，也许比第一志愿更取舍艰难。惟妙惟肖地预想失败，直面败后的残局和补救的措施，绝非乐事，但却必须。尝试着在出征前就布置退却和迂回的路线，并在这种惨淡经营的设计当中，规划自己再一次崛起的蓝图，是一种经验，更是勇气。

  The consideration and filling of the second choice may be more difficult than the first choice. It is not a pleasure to vividly anticipate failure, face the endgame and remedy after defeat, but it is necessary. Trying to arrange retreats and detours before going out, and planning a blueprint for one's rise again in the design of this dismal operation, is an experience and courage.

  也许是因为害怕面对这种挫折的演习，有人敷衍了事般地拟下第二志愿，并不曾经历大脑深远的思考。他们以为这是勇往直前、背水一战的魄力，殊不知暴露的只是自己乏于坚忍和气血两虚。

  Perhaps because they are afraid of facing this setback exercise, some people perfunctory formulate a second choice without experiencing far-reaching thinking in the brain. They thought this was the courage to move forward bravely and fight with their backs to the water, but they didn't know that what was exposed was only their lack of perseverance and lack of qi and blood.

  不可搪塞第二志愿。它依旧是人生重要的选择，是你面对逆境的备份文件。它是进可以攻、退可以守的支撑点，它是无惧无悔的屏障，它是一个终结和起跑的双重底线。

  Don't prevaricate the second choice. It is still an important choice in life and a backup file for you in the face of adversity. It is a support point that can be attacked, retreated and defended, it is a barrier without fear and regret, and it is a double bottom line for finishing and starting.或许有人以为，有了第二志愿、第三志愿……人就易颓败，不进取。这是一个谬论。亡命之徒不可取，它使人铤而走险，一旦失利，便是绝望与死寂。不妨想想杂技演员。有了保险绳的时候，他们的表演会无后顾之忧，更精妙绝伦。

  Some people may think that there is a second choice, a third choice...... People are prone to decay and lack of progress. This is a fallacy. The outlaw is not advisable, it makes people take risks, and once they lose, they are despair and death. Think of acrobats. When they have a safety rope, their performances will be worry-free and more exquisite.在填写第一志愿的时候，把其后的每一个志愿也都认真地考虑，这是人生不屈不挠的法门之一。
When filling in the first choice, carefully consider every subsequent choice, which is one of the indomitable methods of life.

  28.莺鸟与铁星

  28. Warbler and iron star

  在南太平洋的岛屿中，飞翔着一种有着动听鸣叫的美丽小鸟，叫作莺鸟，它们长着形色各异的喙。岛屿上物产丰富的日子，莺鸟们靠吃多种草籽为生，活得优哉游哉。

  On the islands of the South Pacific, there is a beautiful bird with a beautiful song, called the warbler, which has a beak of different shapes. On days when the islands were rich in products, the warblers lived by eating a variety of grass seeds and lived a leisurely life.但是，饥馑来了。干旱袭击了岛屿，整个大地好像是刚刚凝固的炽热火山，赤红的土地，看不到一丝绿色。

  However, famine came. Drought hit the island, and the whole land seemed to be a hot volcano that had just solidified, and the red land could not see a trace of green.科学家找到一些从前研究过的莺鸟，它们的腿上拴着铁环。观测结果，发现莺鸟们的体重大减，挣扎在死亡线上。原因是食物奇缺，能吃的都吃光了，唯一剩下的是一种叫作蒺藜的草籽。它浑身是锋利的硬刺，锐不可当。在深深的内核里隐藏的种仁，好像美味的巧克力封死在铁匣中。

  Scientists have found some warblers that have been studied before, with iron rings tied to their legs. As a result of the observations, it was found that the warblers lost a lot of weight and struggled on the death line. The reason is that food is scarce, and everything that can be eaten is eaten, and the only thing left is a grass seed called tribulus terrestris. It was covered in sharp thorns and was unstoppable. The kernels hidden in the deep core are like delicious chocolate sealed in an iron box.蒺藜还有一个名字叫作铁星，象征着难以攻克。拉丁文的意思是“挤压和疼痛”。

  Tribulus also has a name called Iron Star, which symbolizes difficulty in overcoming. The Latin word means "squeeze and pain".莺鸟用自己柔弱的喙，啄开一粒铁星，先要把它顶在地上，又咬又扭，然后顶住岩石，上喙发力，下喙挤压，直到精疲力竭才能把外壳拧掉，吃到活命的粮草。

  The warbler uses its weak beak to peck open an iron star, first push it to the ground, bite and twist, and then hold the rock, exert force with the upper beak, and squeeze the lower beak until it is exhausted before it can twist off the shell and eat the living food and grass.岛上开始了残酷的生存之战。没有刀光剑影，唯一的声音就是嗑碎蒺藜的噼啪声。很多莺鸟饿死了，有些顽强地生存下来。科学家想，生和死的区别在哪里呢？

  A brutal battle for survival begins on the island. There was no sword light and sword shadow, the only sound was the crackling sound of shattering thistles. Many warblers starved to death, and some survived tenaciously. Scientists wondered, what is the difference between life and death?经过详尽研究，喙长11毫米的莺鸟，就能够嗑开铁星，而喙长10.5毫米的莺鸟，就望“星”兴叹，无论如何都叩不开生命森严的大门。

  After detailed research, a warbler with a beak of 11 mm can eat the iron star, while a warbler with a beak of 10.5 mm can sigh at the "star", and no matter what, it cannot knock on the door of life.0.5毫米之差，就决定了莺鸟的生死存亡。在丰衣足食的时候，一切都被温柔地遮盖了，但月亮并不总是圆的，事物的规律跌宕起伏。

  The difference of 0.5 mm determines the life and death of the warbler. When you are well-fed, everything is gently covered, but the moon is not always round, and the laws of things are up and down.我猜想，那些饿死的莺鸟在最后时分，倘能思索，一定万分后悔自己为什么没能生就一枚长长的利喙！短喙的莺鸟，是天生的，它们遭到了大自然无情的淘汰。但人类的喙——我们思维的强度，历练的经验，广博的智慧，强健的体力，合作的风采，幽默的神韵……却是可以在日复一日的积累中，渐渐地磨炼增长，成为我们度过困厄的支柱。

  I guess that those starving warblers in the last moments, if they can think about it, they will regret why they couldn't give birth to a long beak! The short-beaked warbler is natural, and they have been ruthlessly eliminated by nature. But the beak of human beings - the strength of our thinking, the experience we have, the vast wisdom, the strong physical strength, the style of cooperation, the charm of humor...... However, it can gradually temper and grow in the accumulation day after day, and become the pillar for us to overcome difficulties.

未知

  29.魔术师的铁钉

  29. The magician's iron nail

  有一位非常有名的魔术师，当记者问起他成功的秘诀时，他带着记者，来到他平日演出的宏大剧场门口。记者以为他会走进富丽堂皇的大门，没想到，他领着记者来到了马路对面的一个下水道口。

  There was a very famous magician, and when the reporter asked about the secret of his success, he took the reporter to the door of the grand theater where he usually performed. The reporter thought he would walk into the magnificent gate, but unexpectedly, he led the reporter to a sewer outlet across the road.你躺在这里，假设自己是在冬天的夜晚饥寒交迫，试试你能看到些什么？魔术师很和气地说。

  You lie here, assuming you are hungry and cold on a winter night, and see what you can see? The magician said very kindly.记者曲身躺在地上，他闻到了下水道发出的恶臭，他看到了香喷喷的饭店和华美的商场，还看到无数的人腿在向着剧场走动。另外，有一截突出的窗台就在头顶侧方悬着，如同丑陋的屋檐。他边看边报告着，魔术师说，很好，你看得很全面。只是，在窗台的水泥上，请你看得再仔细一点。你还可以有所发现。

  The reporter lay on the ground, he smelled the stench coming from the sewers, he saw fragrant restaurants and gorgeous shopping malls, and saw countless human legs walking towards the theater. In addition, there is a protruding window sill hanging from the side above the head, like an ugly eaves. He reported as he watched, and the magician said, "Very good, you see it comprehensively." However, on the cement on the windowsill, please look more closely. You can also find something.在魔术师的一再提示下，记者看到了窗台的下方，有一行模糊的字迹。他拼命瞪大眼睛，才辨识出那是魔术师的名字。

  Under the magician's repeated prompting, the reporter saw a line of blurry writing under the windowsill. His eyes widened desperately before he recognized the magician's name.魔术师说，很多年前，我是一个乡下来的孩子。冬天，我蜷着身子躺在这里。你知道下水道口尽管恶臭，但比较暖和，从来不会结冰的。我看到了满天的星斗，知道明天更冷。我看到了食品和衣物，但我身无分文。我还看到了无数的人到对面的剧场去看演出。我萌生了一个梦想，有一天，我也要到这座辉煌的剧院里去，不是去看演出，是让别人看我的演出。这样想了之后，我就从地上捡起一根铁钉，用冻僵的手指，把自己的名字刻在水泥窗台上了……你问我为什么会成功，就这么简单。我用一根生锈的铁钉，把我的梦想刻在这里，每当我没有信心的时候，我就来到这里。当我离开的时候，勇气就重新灌满了胸膛。

  The magician said, many years ago, I was a country kid. In winter, I curled up here. You know that the sewer outlet is warm and never freezes despite its stench. I saw the sky full of stars and knew that tomorrow would be colder. I saw food and clothing, but I was penniless. I also saw countless people go to the theater opposite to see the show. I had a dream that one day, I would also go to this glorious theater, not to see the performance, but to let others see my performance. After thinking about it, I picked up an iron nail from the ground and carved my name on the cement windowsill with my frozen fingers...... You ask me why I succeed, it's that simple. I used a rusty iron nail to engrave my dream here, and whenever I had no faith, I came here. When I left, courage filled my chest again.分手的时候，记者对魔术师说，能否让我看看您那神奇的铁钉？魔术师说，可以。说完，他随手从地上捡起一根铁钉，说，喏，就是它了。铁钉并不重要，重要的是亲手刻下你的名字。

  When they broke up, the reporter said to the magician, can you show me your magical iron nail? The magician said, yes. After saying that, he casually picked up an iron nail from the ground and said, "Oh, that's it." It doesn't matter what the iron nails are, the important thing is to engrave your name with your own hands.

未知

  30.美容师的作品

  30. The work of a beautician

  一家很有名的制造商，产品从服装到化妆品到无数精美的饰品。

  A well-known manufacturer with products ranging from clothing to cosmetics to countless exquisite accessories.一天，商家召开盛大的产品推销会，其中最有趣的项目是——造就绅士。他们聘用的高级美容师，从城市最肮脏的角落，找到了一个身材高大的流浪汉，衣衫褴褛面容晦暗。美容师先给他拍了照片，存档以观后效。接着便用芬芳的洗液为他冲沐理发，用名牌剃须泡给他刮胡子，敷上一层又一层含有药物成分的润肤品、面霜和眼霜……打理完毕后，根据他的身高和肤色，选配了最适宜的衬衣、西装、领带，甚至还有一根很棒的手杖和一顶昂贵的帽子……

  One day, the merchant held a grand product promotion meeting, and the most interesting project was to make a gentleman. The high-end beautician they hired found a tall homeless man with a ragged face from the dirtiest corner of the city. The beautician first took a photo of him and archived it to see the aftermath. Then he washed and cut his hair with a fragrant lotion, shaved him with brand-name shaving soaks, and applied layer after layer of emollients, creams and eye creams containing medicinal ingredients...... After taking care of it, according to his height and skin color, he chose the most suitable shirt, suit, tie, and even a great cane and an expensive hat......于是，众目睽睽之下，这个穷困潦倒颓败已极的莽汉，被商家的产品包装一新，成了仪表堂堂的绅士。在场的人叹为观止，公司的销售额飙升。

  As a result, in full view, this impoverished and decadent reckless man was repackaged by the merchant's products and became a dignified gentleman. Those present were amazed, and the company's sales soared.会后，某经理决定雇用这名容光焕发的绅士，约他第二天早晨报到，绅士点头答应了。但是，第二天早上，绅士没有来。经理决定耐心等下去，第三天、第四天……绅士还是没来。经理就去流浪汉聚集的地方，终于找到了他。

  After the meeting, a manager decided to hire the radiant gentleman and asked him to report the next morning, and the gentleman nodded in agreement. However, the next morning, the gentleman did not come. The manager decided to wait patiently, on the third and fourth days...... The gentleman still didn't come. The manager went to the place where the homeless gathered and finally found him.

  绅士脸上长出了白而短的乱须，身上散发着恶浊的气味，西服、领带以及华美的帽子全不见了，或许被他换了酒喝。此刻，他醉醺醺地躺在垃圾箱旁，只有那根手杖还枕在头下。

  The gentleman's face grew white and short whiskers, his body exuded a foul smell, and his suit, tie and gorgeous hat were all gone, perhaps he had changed his wine to drink. At this moment, he was lying drunkenly next to the trash can, with only the cane still resting under his head.经理把他叫醒，说，美容师改变了你的外貌，但是他没有改变你的内心。所以，你还是你啊。现在，你乐意跟我走吗？

  The manager woke him up and said, "The beautician has changed your appearance, but he has not changed your heart." So, you are still you. Now, are you willing to come with me?流浪汉站起身，跟着经理走了。后来，他终于从里到外成了新人。

  The tramp stood up and followed the manager. Later, he finally became a newcomer from the inside out.改变一个人的外貌，也许几个小时就够了。美容师没有错，但改变一个人的精神，绝不是化妆品和纺织品能够胜任的。只有劳动和信仰，才能真正改变我们。
To change a person's appearance, maybe a few hours will be enough. There is nothing wrong with a beautician, but changing a person's spirit is by no means something that cosmetics and textiles can do. Only labor and faith can truly change us.

未知

  31.闭阖星云之眼

  31. Close the eye of the nebula

  青年时代，我曾经有一段时间是一个悲观主义者，这也许是和我在西藏高原的经历有关。高原太辽阔了，人力太渺小了。雪峰太久远了，人生太短暂了。有时真是生出无尽的悲哀，觉得奋斗有什么用呢？百年之后，不还是一抔黄土？一个人的力量太微薄了，太平洋不会因为一杯沸水的倾倒而升高温度，这杯水却永远地消失了。

  In my youth, I was a pessimist for a while, perhaps related to my experience on the Tibetan plateau. The plateau is too vast and the manpower is too small. The snow peak is too long, and life is too short. Sometimes I really feel endless sorrow, and I feel that what is the use of struggle? A hundred years later, isn't it still a handful of loess? The power of one person is too meager, and the Pacific Ocean does not raise the temperature by the pouring of a glass of boiling water, which is gone forever.后来，我知道这种看世界的角度，被哲学家称为“银河”或“星云之眼”。从这个位置来看，我们和目所能及的所有生物都是微不足道，一切奋斗都显得荒凉和愚蠢，结局和发展都充满了不可言说的荒谬。一个人，和一只蚂蚁、一条蛆虫没有任何分别。从星云和银河的角度来看，人类轻渺如烟、无足挂齿。

  Later, I learned that this perspective on the world is called the "Milky Way" or "Nebula Eye" by philosophers. From this position, we and all living beings as far as the eye can see are insignificant, all struggles seem desolate and stupid, and the ending and development are full of unspeakable absurdity. A person is no different from an ant or a maggot. From the perspective of nebulae and the Milky Way, human beings are as light as smoke and insignificant.这只眼振振有词，在逻辑上几乎是无懈可击的。你若真要遵循了这只眼的视角，会从根本上使生命枯萎凋落。

  This eye is trembling and logically almost unassailable. If you really follow the perspective of this eye, you will fundamentally wither and wither life.一些好高骛远的人，在遭受失败的时候，会拾起这只眼为自己开脱。因为所有的努力和不努力都混为一谈，他的失败也就顺理成章。一些胸无大志的人，在沉沦和荒靡的时刻，会躲在这只眼后面为自己寻找借口。因为一切都在虚无中，他的荒废光阴也就有了理论支点。一些游戏人生放弃光明的人，在黑暗中也眨巴着这只眼，似乎一切都是梦，清醒和昏迷并无分别……

  Some ambitious people, when they suffer failure, will pick up this eye to excuse themselves. Because all efforts and non-efforts are confused, his failure is logical. Some ambitious people, in moments of sinking and desolation, hide behind this eye to find excuses for themselves. Because everything is in nothingness, his wasted time also has a theoretical fulcrum. Some people who give up the light in their game life also blink their eyes in the dark, as if everything is a dream, and there is no difference between waking up and being unconscious......

  你不要小看了这看似遥远而又神秘的星云之眼，如果你长期用这只眼注视世界，就会不由自主地灰心丧志。持久地沉浸其中，还有可能放弃生命。当我们从生活中抽离，成为袖手旁观的旁观者时，所有世俗的欢快和目标，就变得轻如鸿毛。

  Don't underestimate this seemingly distant and mysterious nebula eye, if you look at the world with this eye for a long time, you will involuntarily become discouraged. Persistent immersion in it and the possibility of giving up life. When we withdraw from life and become spectators who stand by, all worldly joys and goals become as light as a feather.闭阖星云之眼吧。因为那不是你的位置，那是神的位置。摒弃那高处不胜寒的孤寂，回到充满生机又复杂多变的人间吧。僭越是危险的，我们今生为人，是一种福气。珍惜我们明察秋毫的双眼，可以仰视星空，却不要让自己轻飘飘地飞起来，到达星云的高度。那里，据说很冷，很黑，很荒凉。

  Close the eyes of the nebula. Because that's not your place, that's God's place. Abandon the loneliness of the high place and return to the world full of vitality and complexity. Transgression is dangerous, and it is a blessing for us to be human in this life. Cherish our discerning eyes, we can look up at the starry sky, but don't let ourselves fly lightly and reach the height of the nebula. There, it is said to be cold, dark, and desolate.那些让我们感到有内涵、有勇气、有坚持力的人，我坚信他们是有理想的。人很怪，只有理想这种东西，才能够提供源源不断的动力。

  Those who make us feel connotative, courageous, and persistent, I firmly believe that they have ideals. People are very strange, only ideals can provide a steady stream of power.

未知
